政策法规国土安全部 (DHS)重要#执法·遣返·欺诈#入境与签证签发

美国国土安全部发布外国人登记表格及登记证明最终规则

发布日期 2026年6月29日生效日期 2026年6月29日

美国国土安全部(DHS)发布的最终规则已于2026年6月29日生效,该规则正式确定了2025年3月发布的临时最终规则,要求此前未登记的外国人使用通用登记表格,并更新了可作为登记证明的文件清单,包括I-94表格和边境通行证。

要点

  • 该最终规则自2026年6月29日起生效,确认了2025年3月发布的临时最终规则内容。
  • DHS修订了8 CFR 264.1条款,更新了可作为登记表格及登记证明的文件清单。
  • 此举落实了第14159号总统行政令的部分内容,旨在确保此前未登记的外国人遵守INA第261条至第266条规定的登记和指纹采集要求。
  • DHS还就登记与指纹采集相关法规的其他潜在修改公开征求意见,意见提交截止日期为2026年8月28日。
  • 原文未具体说明任何新的申请费金额或变化。

影响对象

此前未在DHS登记的外国人,以及为其提供协助的移民律师和雇主将受到影响。

建议行动

对拟议的其他修改有意见者,可在2026年8月28日前通过regulations.gov提交意见。

全文翻译

官方原文全文已于 2026-10-12 保存在本站。即使原链接变更或被删除，仍可在此查看全文。译文由 AI 逐段翻译，仅英文原文具有法律效力。

机构:
AGENCY:
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS),美国国土安全部(DHS)。
U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services (USCIS), Department of Homeland Security (DHS).
行动:
ACTION:
附征求意见的最终规则。
Final rule with request for comments.
摘要:
SUMMARY:
2025年3月12日,国土安全部发布了一项附征求意见的临时最终规则(IFR),修订国土安全部法规,指定一种新的登记表格,供外国人遵守法定外国人登记和指纹采集规定之用。未登记的外国人可使用此通用登记表格履行其法定义务。本最终规则回应了公众意见,修订了国土安全部法规,调整了可作为登记表格及外国人登记证明的表格和程序清单,并就与外国人登记和指纹采集相关法规的其他潜在修改征求意见。
On March 12, 2025, DHS issued an interim final rule (IFR) with request for comments amending DHS regulations to designate a new registration form for aliens to comply with statutory alien registration and fingerprinting provisions. Unregistered aliens may use this general registration form to satisfy their statutory obligations. This final rule responds to public comments, amends DHS regulations to adjust the lists of forms and processes that may serve as registration forms and evidence of alien registration, and seeks comments on other potential changes to the regulations relating to alien registration and fingerprinting.
日期:
DATES:
本最终规则自2026年6月29日起生效。
This final rule is effective on June 29, 2026.
征求意见的意见征询期:对本序言第五节“征求意见”中所述的与外国人登记和指纹采集相关的其他潜在修改,意见必须在2026年8月28日或之前提交。
Comment period for solicited comments: Comments on the other potential changes relating to alien registration and fingerprinting described in section V, Request for Comments, of this preamble must be submitted on or before August 28, 2026.
地址:
ADDRESSES:
征求补充意见的意见征询期:您可通过联邦电子规则制定门户网站https://www.regulations.gov,就本序言第五节“征求意见”中所确定的具体问题提交意见,提交至国土安全部档案编号USCIS-2025-0004。请按照网站说明提交意见。
Comment period for solicited additional comments: You may submit comments on the specific issues identified in section V, Request for Comments, of this preamble via Federal eRulemaking Portal at https://www.regulations.gov, to DHS Docket Number USCIS-2025-0004. Follow the website instructions for submitting comments.
凡未通过https://www.regulations.gov提交的意见,包括发送给本部官员的电子邮件或信函,均不予受理,本部亦可能不予答复。请注意,本部不接受任何手递或信使送达的意见。此外,本部不接受任何形式的数字存储媒介所载的意见,例如CD、DVD或USB存储设备。本部目前不接受邮寄意见。如您无法通过https://www.regulations.gov提交意见,请致电(240) 721-3000联系美国公民及移民服务局政策与战略办公室监管协调司代理司长John R. Pfirrmann-Powell,以获取其他提交方式说明。
Comments submitted in a manner other than via https://www.regulations.gov, including emails or letters sent to the Department's officials, will not be considered and may not receive a response from the Department. Please note that the Department cannot accept any comments that are hand-delivered or couriered. In addition, the Department cannot accept comments contained on any form of digital media storage, such as CDs, DVDs, or USB drives. The Department is not accepting mailed comments at this time. If you cannot submit your comment by using https://www.regulations.gov, please contact John R. Pfirrmann-Powell, Acting Chief, Regulatory Coordination Division, Office of Policy and Strategy, U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services, Department of Homeland Security, by telephone at (240) 721-3000 for alternate instructions.

Public Participation
提交意见的说明见“地址”标题项下。
Instructions for providing comments are in the ADDRESSES caption.
兹邀请有关人士就本序言第五部分“征求意见”所列具体问题，在“日期”标题项下规定的截止日期前，提交相关的书面资料、观点、意见和论据。为使国土安全部（DHS）获得最大帮助，意见应说明任何建议的理由，并包含支持所建议行动方案的数据、信息或依据。意见必须以英文提交，或须附英文译本。未按照上述说明方式提交的意见，包括发送给本部官员的电子邮件或信函，将不被视为对本规则的意见，本部可能不会作出回应。
Interested persons are invited to submit comments on the specific issues identified in section V, Request for Comments, of this preamble by submitting relevant written data, views, comments, and arguments by the deadline stated in the DATES caption. To provide the most assistance to DHS, comments should explain the reason for any recommendation and include data, information, or authority that supports the recommended course of action. Comments must be submitted in English, or an English translation must be provided. Comments submitted in a manner other than pursuant to the instructions, including emails or letters sent to the Department's officials, will not be considered comments on the rule and may not receive a response from the Department.
隐私事项：您在向本部提交的任何公开意见中，可能希望考虑限制所提供个人信息的数量。本部可能会将其认定可能影响个人隐私或具有冒犯性的意见内容，从公开查阅中予以隐去。欲了解更多信息，请参阅网址https://www.regulations.gov上的《隐私与安全声明》。
Privacy: You may wish to consider limiting the amount of personal information that you provide in any public comment submission you make to the Department. The Department may withhold information provided in comments from public viewing that they determine may impact the privacy of an individual or is offensive. For additional information, please read the Privacy and Security Notice at https://www.regulations.gov.
卷宗：如需查阅卷宗、阅读背景文件或已收到的意见，请访问https://www.regulations.gov，并注明DHS卷宗编号USCIS-2025-0004。您还可以在网上卷宗系统注册电子邮件提醒服务，以便在卷宗新增文件时获得通知。
Docket: For access to the docket and to read background documents or comments received, go to https://www.regulations.gov, referencing DHS Docket No. USCIS-2025-0004. You may also sign up for email alerts on the online docket to be notified when additional documents are added to the docket.
一、executive概要
I. Executive Summary
A. 依据与目的
A. Basis and Purpose
2025年3月12日，国土安全部（DHS）发布了附征求意见的临时最终规则（IFR）。见90 FR 11793（2025年3月12日）。该临时最终规则于2025年4月11日生效。该临时最终规则修订了DHS相关法规，为外国人指定了一种新的登记表格，以遵守法定的外国人登记与指纹采集规定。该临时最终规则部分实施了第14159号行政命令（E.O. 14159）《保护美国人民免受入侵》（2025年1月20日发布）第7条，90 FR 8443（2025年1月29日），该命令要求国土安全部部长（部长）等采取适当行动，确保所有此前未登记的在美外国人遵守法定登记要求。
On March 12, 2025, DHS issued an IFR with request for comments. See90 FR 11793 (Mar. 12, 2025). The IFR was effective on April 11, 2025. The IFR amended DHS regulations to designate a new registration form for aliens to comply with the statutory alien registration and fingerprinting provision. The IFR partially implemented section 7 of the Executive Order (E.O.) 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8443 (Jan. 29, 2025), which directed the Secretary of Homeland Security (Secretary), among other things, to take appropriate action to ensure that all previously unregistered aliens in the United States comply with the statutory registration requirements.
在认真审议与该临时最终规则相关的公众意见后，DHS现将该临时最终规则定稿，并根据公众意见对8 CFR第264.1条的法规文本作出额外的程序性和技术性修改。该临时最终规则对8 CFR第264.1条所作修改的理由，以及该临时最终规则序言中所提供的论述，除本最终规则另有区分说明外，依然有效。
After careful consideration of the public comments submitted in connection with the IFR, DHS is finalizing the IFR and making additional procedural and technical modifications to the regulatory text at 8 CFR 264.1 in response to the public comments. The rationale for the changes provided to 8 CFR 264.1 in the IFR and the reasoning provided in the IFR's preamble remain valid, except as distinguished in this final rule.
本最终规则的目的与整个临时最终规则所阐明的目的保持一致：改善特定外国人群体的登记结果，以更好地确保所有此前未登记的在美外国人遵守《移民与国籍法》（INA）第261条至第266条（8 U.S.C. 第1301条至第1306条）所规定的法定要求。该临时最终规则通过规定一种所有外国人无论身份均可使用的登记表格及相应的登记证明文件，填补了DHS法规体系中的空白。本最终规则将该临时最终规则确定为正式规则。本规则通过提供更全面的在美外国人位置信息，提高了DHS的执法效能，使DHS执法更加便捷和安全。此外，指纹采集要求遵从度的提高，将为DHS提供有关外国人犯罪记录的更多信息，包括该外国人是否为已知或疑似恐怖分子。
The purpose of this final rule remains the same as articulated throughout the IFR: to improve the registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens to better ensure that all previously unregistered aliens in the United States comply with the statutory requirements in sections 261 through 266 of the Immigration and Nationality Act (INA), 8 U.S.C. 1301 through 1306. The IFR filled a gap in the DHS regulatory regime by prescribing a registration form available to all aliens regardless of their status and corresponding evidence as proof of that registration. This final rule adopts the IFR as final. The rule improves DHS law enforcement efficacy, making it easier and safer for DHS to enforce the law by providing more comprehensive information about the location of aliens in the United States. Additionally, increased compliance with fingerprinting requirements will provide DHS with additional information about an alien's criminal record, including whether the alien is a known or suspected terrorist.
该最终规则还对现行法规作出了额外的技术性更正,以替换8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)中随时间推移而演变或变更的某些表格的名称、说明和编号,删除对外国人无法用于登记的某些长期(printed page 39250)过期表格的提述,并规定了一些额外表格作为登记证明。该最终规则还对8 CFR 264.1(e)和8 CFR 264.1(g)中与年满14岁外国人登记要求相关的指纹采集豁免条款,以及8 CFR 264.5(h)中与临时登记证明和合法永久居民(LPR)身份相关的条款作出技术性修订和更正。最后,该规则对8 CFR 264.6作出技术性更正。这些变更减少了混淆,提高了修订条款的可用性。这些技术性修订和更正不会对受监管公众产生实质性影响。
The final rule also makes additional technical corrections in the existing regulations to replace the name, description, and number of certain forms in 8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b) that have evolved or changed over time, eliminates references to certain long- ( printed page 39250) expired forms that aliens cannot use to register, and prescribes some additional forms as evidence of registration. The final rule also makes technical amendments and corrections to the fingerprinting waiver provisions of 8 CFR 264.1(e) and 8 CFR 264.1(g) related to the registration requirement of aliens who turn 14 years, as well as the provisions of 8 CFR 264.5(h) relating to temporary evidence of registration and lawful permanent resident (LPR) status. Finally, the rule makes technical corrections to 8 CFR 264.6. These changes reduce confusion and improve the usability of the revised provisions. These technical amendments and corrections do not substantively impact the regulated public.
B. 法律授权
B. Legal Authority
部长颁布本最终规则的授权见于《移民与国籍法》(INA)的各项规定,8 U.S.C. 1101及以下条款,包括INA第103(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1103(a),该条款授权部长管理和执行移民法律,并制定部长认为执行该授权所必需的法规。该授权还见于INA第262条至第266条,8 U.S.C. 1302至1306,这些条款规范外国人登记事宜,并授权部长编制用于外国人登记和指纹采集的表格。2002年《国土安全法》(HSA)第102(a)(3)条,6 U.S.C. 112(a)(3),将国土安全部(DHS)所有官员、雇员和组织单位的一切职能赋予部长。另见6 U.S.C. 202、271条;INA第214(a)(1)条,8 U.S.C. 1184(a)(1)。
The authority for the Secretary to issue this final rule is found in various provisions of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1101 et seq., including section 103(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1103(a), which authorizes the Secretary to administer and enforce the immigration laws and establish such regulations as the Secretary deems necessary for carrying out such authority. The authority is furthermore found in sections 262 through 266 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1306, which govern the registration of aliens and authorizes the Secretary to prepare forms for registration and fingerprinting of aliens. Section 102(a)(3) of the Homeland Security Act of 2002 (HSA), 6 U.S.C. 112(a)(3), vests all functions of all DHS officers, employees, and organizational units in the Secretary. See also6 U.S.C. 202, 271; INA sec. 214(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1184(a)(1).
C. 从临时最终规则到最终规则的变更摘要
C. Summary of the Changes From the Interim Final Rule to the Final Rule
在认真考虑了针对临时最终规则(IFR)收到的公众意见后,本最终规则对国土安全部的登记和指纹采集法规作出了额外变更。国土安全部不就这些变更征求公众意见。国土安全部在本最终规则中采取以下行动:
Following careful consideration of the public comments received in response to the IFR, this final rule makes additional changes to DHS's registration and fingerprinting regulations. DHS is not seeking public comments on these changes. DHS is taking the following actions in this final rule:
将临时最终规则对8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)所作的变更确定为最终版本。国土安全部将临时最终规则对8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)的修订不作改动地确定为最终版本。临时最终规则在8 CFR 264.1(a)表格中规定的登记表格清单中增加了对G-325R表格(传记信息(登记))的提述。临时最终规则还在8 CFR 264.1(b)表格的登记证明清单中增加了美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)G-325R登记外国人证明。
Adopting the changes to8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b) made by the IFR as final. DHS is adopting as final the IFR's amendments to 8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b) without change. The IFR added to the list of prescribed registration forms in the table of 8 CFR 264.1(a) a reference to the Form G-325R, Biographic Information (Registration). The IFR also added to the list of evidence of registration in the table of 8 CFR 264.1(b) the USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration.
修订8 CFR 264.1(a)中的表格,删除与I-67表格(检查记录,匈牙利难民(1958年7月25日法案))、I-691表格(临时居民身份批准通知)和I-700表格(临时居民身份申请)相关的条目。
Revising the table in8 CFR 264.1(a) by removing the entries related to the I-67, Inspection Record—Hungarian refugees (Act of July 25, 1958), I-691, Notice of Approval for Status as a Temporary Resident, and I-700, Application for Status as a Temporary Resident.
修订8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)中的表格,更新入境/离境记录表格编号和类别。在8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)中,国土安全部在I-94表格的条目中增加了I-94A/94W表格。国土安全部还在8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)中删除了提及“声称在1924年7月1日之前入境但无法核实,且已令人满意地确立自1924年7月1日之前在美国居住”这一类别的条目。
Revising the tables in8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b) by updating the Arrival-Departure Record Form Number and Class. In 8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b), DHS is adding Form I-94A/94W to the item for the Form I-94. DHS is also removing in 8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b) the entry referring to the class of “aliens whose claimed entry prior to July 1, 1924, cannot be verified, they having satisfactorily established residence in the United States since prior to July 1, 1924.”
修订8 CFR 264.1(a)中的表格,更新与I-181表格(创建合法永久居留记录备忘录)相关的条目。在8 CFR 264.1(a)中,国土安全部将该类别修订为“在境外临时访问期间出生、根据8 CFR 211.1(b)无需签证获准入境的合法永久居留外国人之子女”。
Revising the table in8 CFR 264.1(a) by updating the entry relating to Form I-181, Memorandum of Creation of Record of Lawful Permanent Residence. In 8 CFR 264.1(a), DHS is revising the class to “aliens born to an alien lawfully admitted for permanent residence during a temporary visit abroad admitted without a visa under 8 CFR 211.1(b).”
修订8 CFR 264.1(a)中的表格,更新与I-485表格和身份调整表格名称相关的条目。在8 CFR 264.1(a)中,国土安全部将I-485表格的表格名称修订为“登记永久居留或调整身份申请表,或其前身或后续表格”。
Revising the table in8 CFR 264.1(a) by updating the entry relating to Form I-485 and the Adjustment of Status Form Title. In 8 CFR 264.1(a), DHS is revising the form title for Form I-485 to “Application to Register Permanent Residence or Adjust Status, or its predecessor or successor form.”
修订8 CFR 264.1(a)中的表格,更新I-590表格的表格名称和外国人类别。在8 CFR 264.1(a)中,国土安全部将I-590表格的表格名称和外国人类别修订为“I-590表格,难民身份分类登记表或其后续表格,或其前身表格,根据INA第207条提出申请者及根据1960年7月14日法案第1条假释入境的难民逃亡者”。
Revising the table in8 CFR 264.1(a) by updating the form title and class of alien of the Form I-590. In 8 CFR 264.1(a), DHS is revising the form title and class of alien of Form I-590 to read as “Form I-590, Registration for Classification as Refugee or its successor form, or its predecessor form—Applicants under section 207 of the INA and Refugee-escapees paroled pursuant to section 1 of the Act of July 14, 1960.”
修订8 CFR 264.1(a)中的表格,更新与I-817表格(家庭团聚计划自愿离境申请表)相关的条目。在8 CFR 264.1(a)中,美国国土安全部(DHS)将I-817表格的表格名称修订为“家庭团聚福利申请表或其后续表格,或其前身表格”。
Revising the table of8 CFR 264.1(a) by updating the entry relating to Form I-817, Application for Voluntary Departure under the Family Unity Program. In 8 CFR 264.1(a), DHS is revising the form title of Form I-817 to “Application for Family Unity Benefits or its successor form, or its predecessor form.”
修订8 CFR 264.1(b)中的表格,删除对I-185表格(非居民外国人加拿大边境通行证)和I-186表格(非居民外国人墨西哥边境通行证)的提及。DHS将删除加拿大边境通行证(BCC)和墨西哥边境通行证的表格编号及名称,并在8 CFR 264.1(b)中增加DSP-150表格(B-1/B-2签证及边境通行证)或其后续表格,或其前身表格。
Revising the table in8 CFR 264.1(b) by removing the references to Form I-185, Nonresident Alien Canadian Border Crossing Card, and Form I-186, Nonresident Alien Mexican Border Crossing Card. DHS is removing the form numbers and titles of the Canadian Border Crossing Card (BCC) and the Mexican BCC and adding Form DSP-150, B-1/B-2 Visa and Border Crossing Card or its successor form, or its predecessor form in 8 CFR 264.1(b).
修订8 CFR 264.1(b)中的表格,增加I-860表格(快速遣返通知与命令书)和I-871表格(恢复先前遣返令的意向通知/决定书),以及每种表格所适用的外国人类别。DHS将增加“I-860表格,快速遣返通知与命令书”,适用于根据经修订的《移民与国籍法》第212(a)(6)(C)条或第(7)条被认定为不可入境,并根据经修订的《移民与国籍法》第235(b)(1)条被命令遣返的外国人类别;以及“I-871表格,恢复先前遣返令的意向通知/决定书”,适用于非法再次进入美国且其先前遣返令已根据经修订的《移民与国籍法》第241(a)(5)条予以恢复的外国人类别,作为在8 CFR 264.1(b)中登记的证明。
Amending the table in8 CFR 264.1(b) by adding Form I-860, Notice and Order of Expedited Removal, and Form I-871, Notice of Intent/Decision to Reinstate Prior Order and the class of aliens for each form. DHS is adding “Form I-860, Notice and Order of Expedited Removal” with the class of aliens who have been determined to be inadmissible under section 212(a)(6)(C) or (7) of the Immigration and Nationality Act, as amended, and ordered removed under section 235(b)(1) of the Immigration and Nationality Act, as amended, and “Form I-871, Notice of Intent/Decision to Reinstate Prior Order” with the class of aliens who reentered the United States illegally and whose prior order of removal has been reinstated under section 241(a)(5) of the Immigration and Nationality Act, as amended, as evidence of registration in 8 CFR 264.1(b).
修订8 CFR 264.1(b)中的表格,删除与I-862表格(出庭通知书)和I-863表格(移交移民法官通知书)相关条目中的“表格”一词。
Amending the table in8 CFR 264.1(b) by removing “Form” from the entry relating to the I-862, Notice to Appear, and the I-863, Notice of Referral to Immigration Judge.
修订8 CFR 264.1(b)中的表格,增加“美国海关与边境保护局(CBP)批准的文件或其电子等效文件,用于NEXUS、SENTRI、FAST和Global Entry可信旅行者计划,适用于最后一次经由NEXUS、SENTRI、FAST或Global Entry便捷通关程序入境美国的外国人。”
Amending the table in8 CFR 264.1(b) by adding “CBP-approved document or its electronic equivalent for the Trusted Traveler Programs NEXUS, SENTRI, FAST, and Global Entry—Aliens who were last admitted to the United States through NEXUS, SENTRI, FAST, or Global Entry facilitated processing.”
修订8 CFR 264.1(e)(1)中关于指纹豁免的规定。DHS将删除8 CFR 264.1(e)(1)中有关豁免“以外国政府官员和雇员身份获准入境的非移民外国人;国际组织代表、官员和雇员”指纹采集要求的措辞,并增加对22 CFR 41.26(a)(2)和《移民与国籍法》第101(a)(11)条的引用,用以描述持有外交签证的外国人。此外,DHS还将增加措辞以澄清,北大西洋公约组织(NATO)代表、官员和雇员的随从、仆役或私人雇员(NATO-7类非移民)本身不符合豁免资格,因为他们从未被归类为NATO代表、官员或雇员。
Amending8 CFR 264.1(e)(1) relating to fingerprint waiver. DHS is removing the language waiving the fingerprinting requirements for “nonimmigrant aliens admitted as foreign government officials and employees; international organization representatives, officers and employees” from 8 CFR 264.1(e)(1) and adding reference to 22 CFR 41.26(a)(2) and section 101(a)(11) of the Immigration and Nationality Act to describe aliens who are holders of diplomatic visas. Additionally, DHS is also adding language to clarify that the attendants, servants, or personal employees of North Atlantic Treaty Organization (NATO) representatives, officers, and employees (NATO-7 nonimmigrants) are not themselves eligible for the waiver because they are not and have never been classified as NATO representatives, officers, or employees.
重组8 CFR 264.1第(e)款的结构,将三项指纹豁免分别列为三个独立段落,并在适当之处增加“她”或“她的”等表述。
Restructuringparagraph (e) of 8 CFR 264.1 to separate the three fingerprint waivers into three paragraphs and adding “she” or “her” where appropriate.
修订8 CFR 264.1(g),在“根据本法”之后增加“或根据法律”字样。(印刷页第39251页)
Amending8 CFR 264.1(g) to add “or by law” after “under the Act.” ( printed page 39251)
修订8 CFR 264.1(g)第(g)(1)段,以澄清年满14岁的合法永久居民必须按照适用的表格说明及8 CFR 106.2规定的费用,在该外国人返回美国后30日内申请换发永久居民卡以办理登记;删除申请登记时须提交实体照片的要求;删除该条款第二句中“如为美国合法永久居民”的表述;并在适当之处增加“她”或“她的”等表述。
Amendingparagraph (g)(1) of 8 CFR 264.1(g) to clarify that an LPR who reaches 14 years old must apply for registration in accordance with the applicable form instructions and with the fee specified in 8 CFR 106.2 to replace a permanent residence card within 30 days of the alien's return to the United States; to remove the requirement of a physical photograph when applying for registration; to remove “if a lawful permanent resident of the United States in the second sentence of the provision; and to add “she” or “her” where appropriate.
修订8 CFR 264.5(h),规定美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)可向以下外国人签发临时登记及合法永久居民身份证明:已依规定提交换发永久居民卡申请或入籍申请的合法永久居民或有条件永久居民外国人、使用USCIS规定表格申请解除其居留条件限制的外国人,或USCIS根据表格说明另行认定的其他外国人。在8 CFR 264.5(h)中,DHS还澄清,当该外国人获发新的I-551表格时,无需将USCIS置于其护照内的临时登记证明交还USCIS。
Amending8 CFR 264.5(h) to state that USCIS may issue temporary evidence of registration and LPR status to an alien who is a “lawful permanent resident or conditional permanent resident alien who has properly filed an application for a replacement permanent resident card or for naturalization, petitioned for the removal of the conditions on his or her residence using the form prescribed by USCIS, or as otherwise determined by USCIS in accordance with the form instructions.” In 8 CFR 264.5(h), DHS is also clarifying that the temporary evidence of registration placed by USCIS in the alien's passport does not need to be surrendered to USCIS by the alien when the alien is issued a new Form I-551.
修订8 CFR 264.6,与非移民入境出境记录申请相关。国土安全部(DHS)正在修订8 CFR 264.6中的法规,作出技术性编辑更新,以删除过时的法规引用。
Amending8 CFR 264.6 related to the application for a nonimmigrant arrival-departure record. DHS is amending regulations in 8 CFR 264.6 by making technical editorial updates to remove obsolete regulatory references.
虽然国土安全部(DHS)在临时最终规则(IFR)中未征收费用,但国土安全部(DHS)曾就每位登记人可能征收30美元生物识别服务费一事征求公众意见。在对所收到的意见进行认真审议后,国土安全部(DHS)已确定目前不征收生物识别服务费,但未来可能征收申请费或生物识别服务费。
While DHS did not impose a fee as part of the IFR, DHS requested comments on adding a potential biometric services fee per registrant of $30. After careful consideration of the comments received, DHS has determined that it will not, at this time, impose a biometric services fee, but may impose an application or biometric services fee in the future.
此外,国土安全部(DHS)在临时最终规则(IFR)中就管理和预算办公室(OMB)批准的G-325R表格《生物信息(登记)》征求意见,目的是根据1995年《文书削减法》(PRA)(44 U.S.C. 3501及以下条款)发布为期60天的《联邦公报》通知。见90 FR 11793, 11799(2025年3月12日)。为《文书削减法》目的设置的意见征求期已于2025年5月12日结束。因临时最终规则(IFR)而就G-325R表格收到的任何公众意见,均已在2025年8月11日于90 FR 38655发布的、为获得管理和预算办公室(OMB)批准G-325R表格而设的为期30天的《联邦公报》通知中予以答复[1]。该信息收集文书及其说明和其他辅助文件,包括对作为60天《联邦公报》通知一部分提交的意见的答复,均可通过访问联邦电子规则制定门户网站:https://www.regulations.gov,在USCIS-2025-0005档案编号下查阅。
Additionally, within the IFR, DHS requested comments on the Office of Management and Budget (OMB)-approved Form G-325R, Biographic Information (Registration), for purposes of the 60-day Federal Register Notice under the Paperwork Reduction Act of 1995 (PRA), 44 U.S.C. 3501 et seq. See90 FR 11793, 11799 (Mar. 12, 2025). The comment period for purposes of the PRA ended on May 12, 2025. Any public comment received on Form G-325R as a result of the IFR has been responded to in the 30-day Federal Register Notice published for purposes of obtaining OMB approval of Form G-325R [1] on August 11, 2025, at 90 FR 38655. The information collection instrument with instructions and additional supporting documents, including responses to comments submitted as part of the 60-day Federal Register Notice, can be accessed by visiting the Federal eRulemaking Portal site at: https://www.regulations.gov under Docket ID number USCIS-2025-0005.
D. 对未来可能变更的意见征求
D. Request for Comments on Potential Future Changes
在本序言第五节中,国土安全部(DHS)就8 CFR第264部分现行法规中可能作出的某些修订征求意见,以提高可用性并减少未来规则中进行一致性修改的需要。国土安全部(DHS)正在就以下潜在的未来修订征求意见,包括:
In section V of this preamble, DHS solicits comments on certain potential amendments throughout the existing regulation in 8 CFR part 264 to improve usability and reduce the need for conforming changes in future rules. Potential future amendments for which DHS is seeking comments include:
规定更多的登记表格,包括但不限于I-90表格《更换永久居民卡申请书》、I-539表格《延长/变更身份申请书》,以及I-589表格《庇护和暂缓递解申请书》。
Prescribing additional registration forms, including but not limited to Form I-90, Application to Replace Permanent Resident Card, Form I-539, Application to Extend/Change Status, and Form I-589, Application for Asylum and Withholding of Removal.
从8 CFR 264.1(b)中删除某些规定的外国人登记证明表格,例如I-766表格《就业许可文件》;
Eliminating certain prescribed evidence of Alien Registration forms from8 CFR 264.1(b), such as Form I-766, Employment Authorization Document;
更新8 CFR 264.1(b)中以I-94表格作为登记证明的外国人类别。
Updating the classes of aliens for whom Form I-94 constitutes evidence of Registration under8 CFR 264.1(b).
在相关福利申请裁决之前即签发登记证明。
Issuing evidence of registration prior to the adjudication of related benefit requests.
更新并澄清8 CFR 264.1(e)中与豁免指纹采集要求相关的编辑内容。
Updating and clarifying edits related to the waiver of fingerprinting requirements in8 CFR 264.1(e).
E. 可分割性
E. Severability
在发布本最终规则时,国土安全部(DHS)的意图是本规则的各项条款应尽最大可能被视为彼此可分割。如果有管辖权的法院裁定本规则所修订的任何条款不适用于特定类别的个人或情形,国土安全部(DHS)的意图是法院应使本规则的其余部分对所有适用的人员和情形继续有效。国土安全部(DHS)的总体目标是,依照《移民与国籍法》(INA)的各项规定,改善此前未登记的外国人的登记结果。
In issuing this final rule, it is DHS's intention that the rule's various provisions be considered severable from one another to the greatest extent possible. If a court of competent jurisdiction were to hold that any of the provisions amended were not to be applied to a particular category of individuals or circumstances, DHS would intend for the court to leave the remainder of the rule in place with respect to all covered persons and circumstances. DHS's overarching goal is to improve the registration outcomes of aliens not previously registered consistent with the provisions of the INA.
F. 效益与成本
F. Benefits and Costs
本规则为外国人遵守《移民与国籍法》(INA)的外国人登记要求提供了另一种方法。本规则并未在INA已规定的义务之外施加新的登记或指纹采集义务。本规则通过提供一种通用登记选项,使未登记的外国人能够履行其登记要求并改善国土安全部(DHS)的执法效果,从而带来益处。DHS认识到,外国人遵守INA中登记要求是存在成本的。由于本规则并未在INA已有义务之外施加新的外国人登记或生物特征采集义务,这些成本是遵守该法规所固有的,并非本规则造成的结果。本规则可能会导致使用该选项的外国人合规成本增加。外国人的成本可能包括填写和提交登记表所需的时间,以及前往申请支持中心(ASC)、提交指纹和保存记录所花费的时间。目前,申请人提交表格或提交生物特征信息无需缴纳费用。本规则可能会因本规则产生的额外外国人登记而增加DHS的成本。DHS估计,截至2026年5月7日,根据本规则进行的现有登记和生物特征信息提交已使外国人产生约2130万美元的成本。该机构因收集和处理生物特征信息而产生的估计负担为60万美元。DHS已考虑到本规则(或许与其他政策相结合)可能产生某些间接影响的可能性,例如那些选择寻求法律援助者的法律费用增加,以及潜在的劳动力影响。我们目前没有足够的信息来量化这些影响。表1汇总了本规则相关的监管变化及预期影响的估计成本和效益。(印刷页第39252页)
This rule makes available another method for aliens to comply with the alien registration requirements of the INA. The rule does not impose new registration or fingerprinting obligations separate from the obligations already contained in the INA. The rule provides benefits by providing a general registration option to allow unregistered aliens to comply with their registration requirements and improve DHS enforcement. DHS recognizes that there are costs to aliens to comply with registration requirements in the INA. Because this rule does not impose new alien registration or biometric obligations separate from those already contained in the INA, the costs are inherent to compliance with the statute and are not a result of this rule. This rule may result in increased compliance costs for aliens that use this option. Costs to aliens may include the time to complete and file a registration form, as well as time spent traveling to an ASC, submitting fingerprints, and record retention. There is currently no fee for applicants to file the form or to submit biometrics. This rule may increase costs to DHS from additional alien registrations resulting from this rule. DHS estimates current registration and biometrics submissions under this rule have cost aliens approximately $21.3 million, as of May 7, 2026. The estimated burden to the Agency is $0.6 million from collecting and processing biometrics. DHS has considered the possibility that this rule, perhaps in combination with other policies, could have some indirect effects, such as increased legal costs for those who choose to seek legal assistance and potential workforce impacts. We do not have sufficient information to quantify these effects.Table 1 provides a summary of the regulatory changes and the estimated costs and benefits associated with the expected impacts. ( printed page 39252)

二、背景
II. Background
A. 《移民与国籍法》的外国人登记要求
A. Alien Registration Requirements of the Immigration and Nationality Act
1940年《外国人登记法》,又称《史密斯法》,于1940年6月28日颁布为法律。[2]《史密斯法》总体上要求所有在美国境内停留超过30天的外国人申请登记并采集指纹。国会后来将这些经修订的要求纳入1952年《移民与国籍法》(公法82-414号,66 Stat. 163)。登记和指纹采集要求目前经修订后载于《美国法典》第8编第12章第II分章第VII部分(8 U.S.C. 1301至1306)。在本序言全文中,我们将此类要求称为外国人登记要求或INA的外国人登记要求。
The Alien Registration Act of 1940, also known as the Smith Act, was enacted into law on June 28, 1940.[2] The Smith Act generally required all aliens in the country beyond 30 days to apply to register and to be fingerprinted. Congress later incorporated these requirements, as amended, in the Immigration and Nationality Act of 1952, Public Law 82-414, 66 Stat. 163. The registration and fingerprinting requirements currently appear, as amended, in part VII of subchapter II of chapter 12 of title 8, United States Code (8 U.S.C. 1301 through 1306). Throughout this preamble, we refer to such requirements as the alien registration requirements or the alien registration requirements of the INA.
根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)的外国人登记要求,除有限的例外情形(例如已通过签证申请完成登记和指纹采集的签证持有人,以及A类和G类签证持有人)外,[3]所有年满14岁且在美国停留30天或以上的外国人,必须在30天届满前申请登记并留取指纹。见INA第262(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1302(a)。同样,父母和法定监护人必须确保其未满14岁的子女完成登记。见INA第262(b)条,8 U.S.C. 1302(b)。外国人子女在其14周岁生日后的30天内,必须“亲自申请登记并留取指纹”。同上。部长可根据其自由裁量权,并基于对等原则,依照其可能制定的规章,对任何非移民豁免INA第262(a)条和(b)款,8 U.S.C. 1302(a)和(b)款规定的指纹采集要求。见INA第262(c)条,8 U.S.C. 1302(c)。正如下一节所述,部长已就某些非移民行使了该自由裁量权。
Under the alien registration requirements of the INA, with limited exceptions ( e.g., for visa holders who have already been registered and fingerprinted (through their application for a visa) and A and G visa holders),[3] all aliens above the age of 14 who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and be fingerprinted before the expiration of 30 days. See INA sec. 262(a), 8 U.S.C. 1302(a). Similarly, parents and legal guardians must ensure that their children below the age of 14 are registered. See INA sec. 262(b), 8 U.S.C. 1302(b). Within 30 days of reaching his or her 14th birthday, the alien child must “apply in person for registration and to be fingerprinted.” Id. The Secretary may, in his discretion and on the basis of reciprocity pursuant to such regulations as he may prescribe, waive the requirement of fingerprinting specified in section 262(a) and (b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(a) and (b), in the case of any nonimmigrant. See INA sec. 262(c), 8 U.S.C. 1302(c). As discussed in the next section, the Secretary has exercised this discretion with respect to certain nonimmigrants.
外国人故意不申请登记或不留取指纹,或拒绝申请登记或留取指纹的,可处以最高5,000美元的罚款或最长6个月的监禁,或两者并罚。[4]外国人的父母或法定监护人故意不为其登记或拒绝登记的,同样适用此规定。见INA第266(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1306(a)。任何外国人或外国人的父母或法定监护人,在提交登记申请时“做出其明知为虚假的陈述,或通过欺诈手段为自己或通过他人办理登记,或试图办理登记”的,应受刑事起诉。见INA第266(c)条,8 U.S.C. 1306(c)。另见18 U.S.C. 1001、1546。因欺诈性登记被定罪,构成INA第237(a)(3)(B)(i)条,8 U.S.C. 1227(a)(3)(B)(i)规定的可驱逐出境事由。
An alien's willful failure or refusal to apply to register or to be fingerprinted is punishable by a fine of up to $5,000 or imprisonment for up to 6 months, or both.[4] The same applies to an alien's parent or legal guardian's willful failure or refusal to register. See INA sec 266(a), 8 U.S.C. 1306(a). Any alien or any parent or legal guardian of an alien who files a registration application “containing statements known by him to be false, or who procures or attempts to procure registration of himself or through another person by fraud” is subject to criminal prosecution. See INA sec. 266(c), 8 U.S.C. 1306(c). See, e.g.,18 U.S.C. 1001, 1546. A conviction for fraudulent registration constitutes a ground of removal under section 237(a)(3)(B)(i) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1227(a)(3)(B)(i).
部长有权“编制外国人登记和指纹采集表格”,该表格“应包含关于下列事项的询问:(1)外国人进入美国的日期和地点;(2)其已经从事和打算从事的活动;(3)其预计在美国停留的时间长度;(4)该外国人的警方记录和犯罪记录(如有);以及(5)可能规定的其他附加事项。”见INA第264(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1304(a)。部长还有权“尽管有”INA第261条和第262条,8 U.S.C. 1301和1302的规定,为某些类别的外国人规定“特别的登记和指纹采集规章和表格”,包括“任何其他未依法获准在美国永久居留的外国人类别”。见INA第263(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1303(a)。尽管本规则完全符合INA第261条和第262条,8 U.S.C. 1301和1302及相关授权的规定,但部长在制定本规则所必需的范围内,亦援引INA第263(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1303(a)。
The Secretary has authority to “prepare forms for the registration and fingerprinting of aliens,” which “shall contain inquiries with respect to (1) the date and place of entry of the alien into the United States; (2) activities in which he has been and intends to be engaged; (3) the length of time he expects to remain in the United States; (4) the police and criminal record, if any, of such alien; and (5) such additional matters as may be prescribed.” See INA sec. 264(a), 8 U.S.C. 1304(a). The Secretary also has authority to prescribe “special regulations and forms for the registration and fingerprinting of” certain classes of aliens, including “aliens of any other class not lawfully admitted to the United States for permanent residence,” “[n]otwithstanding the provisions of” sections 261 and 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1301 and 1302. See INA sec. 263(a), 8 U.S.C. 1303(a). Although this rule is fully consistent with sections 261 and 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1301 and 1302 and related authority, the Secretary also invokes section 263(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1303(a) to the extent necessary to support this rulemaking.
根据INA外国人登记要求完成登记和指纹采集的每一名在美外国人,必须“按照[部长]所发布规章规定的形式、方式和时间,获发外国人登记证明书或外国人登记收据卡。”[5]年满18岁的每一名已登记外国人,必须随时随身携带其外国人登记证明书或外国人登记收据卡。不遵守该规定构成轻罪,可处以最高5,000美元的罚款或不超过30天的监禁,或两者并罚。见INA第266(b)条,8(印刷页第39253页)U.S.C. 1306(b);18 U.S.C. 3559(a)(8)、3571(b)(6)。
Every alien in the United States who has been registered and fingerprinted under the alien registration requirements of the INA must “be issued a certificate of alien registration or an alien registration receipt card in such form and manner and at such time as shall be prescribed under regulations issued by the [Secretary].” [5] Every registered alien 18 years of age and over must at all times carry and have in his or her personal possession any certificate of alien registration or alien registration receipt card. Noncompliance is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine of up to $5,000 or imprisonment for not more than 30 days, or both. See INA sec. 266(b), 8 ( printed page 39253) U.S.C. 1306(b); 18 U.S.C. 3559(a)(8), 3571(b)(6).
最后,根据《移民与国籍法》外国人登记要求须进行登记的每名在美国境内的外国人,必须在地址变更之日起10日内以书面形式将每次地址变更及新地址通知国土安全部(DHS),并提供部长可能通过法规要求的其他信息。参见《移民与国籍法》第265(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1305(a)。不遵守该规定构成轻罪,可处以最高5,000美元的罚款或最长30日的监禁,或两者并罚。此外,任何未能遵守8 U.S.C. 1305地址变更通知要求的外国人均可被驱逐出境,除非该外国人证明此种未遵守情形具有合理可原谅的理由或并非出于故意。参见《移民与国籍法》第237(a)(3)(A)条,8 U.S.C. 1227(a)(3)(A)。
Finally, each alien required to be registered under the alien registration requirements of the INA who is within the United States must notify DHS in writing of each change of address and new address within 10 days from the date of such change and provide such additional information as the Secretary may require by regulation. See INA sec. 265(a), 8 U.S.C. 1305(a). Noncompliance is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine of up to $5,000 or imprisonment for not more than 30 days, or both. In addition, any alien who has failed to comply with the change-of-address notification requirements of 8 U.S.C. 1305 is removable unless the alien establishes that such failure was reasonably excusable or was not willful. See INA sec. 237(a)(3)(A), 8 U.S.C. 1227(a)(3)(A).
B. 2025年临时最终规则之前的法规
B. Regulations Prior to the 2025 IFR
长期以来的法规规定,在美国境内未被豁免外国人登记的外国人,在年满14周岁后30日内,必须按照适用的表格说明申请登记和按指纹,除非根据8 CFR 264.1(e)条豁免按指纹要求。[6] 参见8 CFR 264.1(g)。
Longstanding regulations provide that within 30 days after reaching the age of 14 years old, an alien in the United States who is not exempt from alien registration must apply for registration and fingerprinting, unless fingerprinting is waived under 8 CFR 264.1(e),[6] in accordance with applicable form instructions. See8 CFR 264.1(g).
如果该外国人是美国的合法永久居民(LPR),且在年满14周岁时暂时不在美国境内,则该外国人必须在返回美国后30日内按照适用的表格说明申请登记并提供照片。参见8 CFR 264.1(g)(1)。如该外国人为美国合法永久居民,必须交回任何先前的外国人登记证明文件。同上。
If such alien is an LPR of the United States and is temporarily absent from the United States when he or she reaches the age of 14, the alien must apply for registration and provide a photograph within 30 days of his or her return to the United States in accordance with applicable form instructions. See8 CFR 264.1(g)(1). The alien, if an LPR of the United States, must surrender any prior evidence of alien registration. Id.
国土安全部的法规规定了满足外国人登记要求的表格。参见8 CFR 264.1(a)。该法规还指定某些表格可作为登记的证明文件。参见8 CFR 264.1(b)。
DHS regulations prescribe forms that satisfy the alien registration requirements. See8 CFR 264.1(a). The regulations also designate certain forms as constituting evidence of registration. See8 CFR 264.1(b).
在临时最终规则增设G-325R表之前,8 CFR 264.1(a)将以下表格列为登记表格:
Before the IFR added Form G-325R, 8 CFR 264.1(a) identified the following forms as registration forms:
I-67表,检查记录,匈牙利难民(1958年7月25日法案)。
I-67, Inspection Record—Hungarian refugees (Act of July 25, 1958).
I-94表,入境出境记录,以非移民身份获准入境的外国人;[7] 根据《移民与国籍法》第212(d)(5)条获准假释入境美国的外国人;其声称于1924年7月1日之前入境但无法核实的外国人,因其已圆满证明自1924年7月1日之前起即在美国居住;已合法获准永久居留美国但尚未登记过的外国人;获准在不启动驱逐程序的情况下离境的外国人,或正在对其启动驱逐程序的外国人。
I-94, Arrival-Departure Record—Aliens admitted as nonimmigrants; [7] aliens paroled into the United States under section 212(d)(5) of the Immigration and Nationality Act; aliens whose claimed entry prior to July 1, 1924, cannot be verified, they having satisfactorily established residence in the United States since prior to July 1, 1924; aliens lawfully admitted to the United States for permanent residence who have not been registered previously; aliens who are granted permission to depart without the institution of deportation proceedings or against whom deportation proceedings are being instituted.
I-95表,船员登岸许可证,经由船舶或飞机抵达的船员。
I-95, Crewmen's Landing Permit—Crewmen arriving by vessel or aircraft.
I-181表,合法永久居留记录设立备忘录,根据8 CFR 101.1被推定为合法获准进入美国的外国人。
I-181, Memorandum of Creation of Record of Lawful Permanent Residence—Aliens presumed to be lawfully admitted to the United States under8 CFR 101.1.
I-485表,永久居民身份申请书,根据经修订的《移民与国籍法》第245条及第249条,以及1957年9月11日法案第13条提出申请的申请人。
I-485, Application for Status as Permanent Resident—Applicants under sections 245 and 249 of the Immigration and Nationality Act as amended, and section 13 of the Act of September 11, 1957.
I-590表,难民逃亡者身份分类登记表,根据1960年7月14日法案第1条获准假释的难民逃亡者。
I-590, Registration for Classification as Refugee—Escapee—Refugee-escapees paroled pursuant to section 1 of the Act of July 14, 1960.
I-687表,临时居民身份申请书,根据经修订的《移民与国籍法》第245A条提出申请的申请人。
I-687, Application for Status as a Temporary Resident—Applicants under section 245A of the Immigration and Nationality Act, as amended.
I-691表,临时居民身份批准通知书,根据8 CFR 210.2及245A.2条调整为合法临时居民身份的外国人。
I-691, Notice of Approval for Status as a Temporary Resident—Aliens adjusted to lawful temporary residence under8 CFR 210.2 and 245A.2.
I-698表,由临时居民调整为永久居民身份申请书,根据经修订的《移民与国籍法》第245A条提出申请的申请人。
I-698, Application to Adjust Status from Temporary to Permanent Resident—Applicants under section 245A of the Immigration and Nationality Act, as amended.
I-700表,临时居民身份申请书,根据经修订的《移民与国籍法》第210条提出申请的申请人。
I-700, Application for Status as a Temporary Resident—Applicants under section 210 of the Immigration and Nationality Act, as amended.
I-817表,家庭团聚计划自愿离境申请书。[8]
I-817, Application for Voluntary Departure under the Family Unity Program.[8]
在临时最终规则增设“USCIS外国人G-325R登记证明”之前,8 CFR 264.1(b)将以下表格列为构成登记证明的文件:
Before the IFR added “USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration,” 8 CFR 264.1(b) listed the following forms as constituting evidence of registration:
I-94表,入境出境记录,以非移民身份获准入境的外国人;根据《移民与国籍法》第212(d)(5)条获准假释入境美国的外国人;其声称于1924年7月1日之前入境但无法核实的外国人,因其已圆满证明自1924年7月1日之前起即在美国居住;以及获准在不启动驱逐程序的情况下离境的外国人。
I-94, Arrival-Departure Record—Aliens admitted as nonimmigrants; aliens paroled into the United States under section 212(d)(5) of the Immigration and Nationality Act; aliens whose claimed entry prior to July 1, 1924, cannot be verified, they having satisfactorily established residence in the United States since prior to July 1, 1924; and aliens granted permission to depart without the institution of deportation proceedings.
I-95表,船员登岸许可证,经由船舶或飞机抵达的船员。
I-95, Crewmen's Landing Permit—Crewmen arriving by vessel or aircraft.
I-184表,外国船员登岸许可证及身份证,经由船舶抵达的船员。
I-184, Alien Crewman Landing Permit and Identification Card—Crewmen arriving by vessel.
I-185,《非居民外国人加拿大边境通行证》,适用于加拿大公民或居住在加拿大的英国臣民。
I-185, Nonresident Alien Canadian Border Crossing Card—Citizens of Canada or British subjects residing in Canada.
I-186,《非居民外国人墨西哥边境通行证》,适用于居住在墨西哥的墨西哥公民。
I-186, Nonresident Alien Mexican Border Crossing Card—Citizens of Mexico residing in Mexico.
I-221,《出庭理由通知书及听证通知》,适用于被启动驱逐出境程序的外国人。

Form I-863, Notice of Referral to Immigration Judge—Aliens against whom removal proceedings are being instituted.[9]
此外,根据8 CFR 264.1(b)项下的一项注释,外国护照上有效且未过期的非移民国土安全部(DHS)入境或假释章戳可作为登记证明。
In addition, under a note to 8 CFR 264.1(b), a valid, unexpired nonimmigrant DHS admission or parole stamp in a foreign passport constitutes evidence of registration.
C. 2025年3月临时最终规则
C. The March 2025 IFR
2025年3月12日,国土安全部(DHS)在《联邦公报》上发布了一项临时最终规则(IFR)并征求公众意见,见90 FR 11793(2025年3月12日)。该临时最终规则部分实施了2025年1月20日发布的第14159号行政命令《保护美国人民免受入侵》第7节的规定。参见90 FR 8443(2025年1月29日)。第7节指示国土安全部与国务院(State)及司法部长协调,采取一切适当行动,以:
On March 12, 2025, DHS published an IFR with request for comments in the Federal Register ,90 FR 11793 (Mar. 12, 2025). The IFR partially implemented section 7 of E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion, issued on January 20, 2025. See90 FR 8443 (Jan. 29, 2025). Section 7 directs the DHS, in coordination with the Department of State (State) and the Attorney General, to take all appropriate action to:
立即宣布并公开所有此前未登记的在美外国人必须遵守美国法典第8编第12章第II分章第VII部分(8 U.S.C. 1301至1306节)(第39254页)规定义务的相关信息;
Immediately announce and publicize information about the legal obligation of all previously unregistered aliens in the United States to comply with the requirements of part VII of ( printed page 39254) subchapter II of chapter 12 of title 8, U.S. Code (8 U.S.C. 1301 through 1306);
确保所有此前未登记的在美外国人遵守8 U.S.C. 1301至1306节;以及
Ensure that all previously unregistered aliens in the United States comply with8 U.S.C. 1301 through 1306; and
确保未能履行8 U.S.C. 1301至1306节所规定法律义务的行为被视为民事和刑事执法优先事项。参见90 FR 8443(2025年1月29日)。
Ensure that failure to comply with the legal obligations of8 U.S.C. 1301 through 1306 is treated as a civil and criminal enforcement priority. See90 FR 8443 (Jan. 29, 2025).
该行政命令发布后,国土安全部对8 CFR第264部分所载登记规定进行了审查,认定有必要在现行规定已列明的登记表格之外,再指定一种通用登记表格,以改善目前尚无指定登记表格的某些外国人群体的登记结果,例如未经许可入境或假释入境且此后未被国土安全部查获的外国人,或因商务或旅游目的入境但未获签发I-94表的加拿大非移民。参见90 FR 11793, 11795(2025年3月12日)。
Following the issuance of this E.O., DHS reviewed the registration regulations at 8 CFR part 264 and determined that it would be appropriate to designate a general registration form in addition to those already identified in the regulations to improve registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens who currently lack a designated registration form, such as aliens who are present without being admitted or paroled and have not otherwise been encountered by DHS, or Canadian nonimmigrants for business or pleasure who are not issued Form I-94. See90 FR 11793, 11795 (Mar. 12, 2025).
根据该行政命令及《移民与国籍法》(INA)关于外国人登记的要求,该临时最终规则指定新表格G-325R《生物特征信息(登记)表》作为适用于所有未登记外国人(无论其身份状态如何)的通用登记选项。参见90 FR 11793, 11795(2025年3月12日)。如该临时最终规则所述,外国人若欲使用此选项,须在myUSCIS网站(https://my.uscis.gov/)上为本人或其子女创建专属账户,然后填写G-325R表《生物信息(登记)表》。参见90 FR 11793, 11796(2025年3月12日)。此项服务不收取费用。
Consistent with the E.O. and the alien registration requirements of the INA, the IFR designated new Form G-325R, Biometric Information (Registration) as a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of their status. See90 FR 11793, 11795 (Mar. 12, 2025). As explained in the IFR, to use this option, aliens must create their own unique account, or an account for their child, in myUSCIS at https://my.uscis.gov/​ and then complete the Form G-325R, Biographic Information (Registration). See90 FR 11793, 11796 (Mar. 12, 2025). There is no fee.
在myUSCIS提交登记后,将启动该外国人在当地美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)申请支持中心(ASC)预约生物特征信息采集服务的程序,以采集包括指纹、照片和签名在内的生物特征信息。
Submission of the registration in myUSCIS initiates the process for the alien's biometric services appointment at a local USCIS Application Support Center (ASC) for the collection of biometrics, including fingerprints, photograph, and signature.
外国人在申请支持中心成功完成生物特征信息采集预约后,美国公民及移民服务局电子移民系统(ELIS)案件管理系统将触发生成《美国公民及移民服务局外国人G-325R登记证明》,该文件上印有唯一识别编号。对于那些虽须登记但指纹采集要求被豁免的外国人,例如加拿大非移民和14岁以下外国人,ELIS案件管理系统将在收到G-325R表后触发生成《美国公民及移民服务局外国人G-325R登记证明》。随后,该《美国公民及移民服务局外国人G-325R登记证明》文件将上传至该外国人的myUSCIS账户。在myUSCIS账户中,外国人可下载该文件的PDF版本并打印。该文件可作为该外国人根据《移民与国籍法》第264(d)节,即8 U.S.C. 1304(d)节完成登记的证明。
Once an alien successfully completes his or her biometrics appointment at an ASC, the USCIS Electronic Immigration System (ELIS) case management system triggers the creation of “USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration” with a unique identifier printed on the document. For those aliens, such as Canadian nonimmigrants and aliens under the age of 14 years old, required to register but for whom the fingerprint requirement is waived, the ELIS case management system triggers the creation of the “USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration” upon receipt of the Form G-325R. This “USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration” document is then posted to the alien's myUSCIS account. In the myUSCIS account, the alien is allowed to download a PDF version of the document and can print it. This document serves as evidence of the alien's registration for purposes of section 264(d) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(d).
《暂行最终规则》（IFR）通过制定一份适用于所有外国人（无论其身份如何）的登记表格，填补了现行监管体系的空白，该表格为已列明表格之外的补充表格。具体而言，《暂行最终规则》在8 CFR 264.1(a)中列明了该新表格，并在8 CFR 264.1(b)中列明了相应的登记证明。参见90 FR 11793, 11796（2025年3月12日）。
The IFR filled the gaps in the regulatory regime by prescribing a registration form available to all aliens regardless of their status, in addition to the other forms already listed. Specifically, the IFR listed the new form at 8 CFR 264.1(a) and listed the corresponding evidence of registration at 8 CFR 264.1(b). See90 FR 11793, 11796 (Mar. 12, 2025).
根据《移民与国籍法》（INA）第289条，即8 U.S.C. 1359的规定，美国国土安全部（DHS）对《移民与国籍法》第262条（即8 U.S.C. 1302）所规定的登记和指纹采集要求作出解释，将依据《移民与国籍法》第289条（即8 U.S.C. 1359）的授权而在美国境内的、具有至少百分之五十美洲印第安人血统的加拿大出生美洲印第安人排除在“所有外国人”之外，因为《移民与国籍法》第262条（即8 U.S.C. 1302）以及美国法典第8编第12章第二分章的其他条款，应结合其跨越美国边境的权利加以解释。[10] 因此，此类入境者无需登记，故《暂行最终规则》新增的登记表格不适用于此类入境者。[11]
Consistent with section 289 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1359, DHS interpreted the registration and fingerprinting requirements of section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 to exclude from “all aliens” American Indians born in Canada who possess at least 50 per centum of blood of the American Indian race who are present in the United States under the authority of section 289 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1359, as section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 and other provisions of subchapter II of chapter 12, title 8 of the U.S. Code are construed consistent with their right to pass the borders of the United States.[10] Therefore, the registration form added in the IFR is not used by these entrants because such entrants do not have to register.[11]
《暂行最终规则》并未施加任何有别于《移民与国籍法》现有规定的新登记或指纹采集义务。已依据8 CFR 264.1(a)完成登记的外国人，或持有符合8 CFR 264.1(b)规定的登记证明的外国人，通常无需再次登记，但此类外国人仍须遵守《移民与国籍法》第265(a)条（即8 U.S.C. 1305(a)）及8 CFR 265.1规定的持续地址变更报告义务，该义务与该外国人可能负有的其他任何地址报告义务相互独立、并行适用。
The IFR did not impose any new registration or fingerprinting obligations separate from the obligations already contained in the INA. An alien who has previously registered consistent with 8 CFR 264.1(a), or an alien who has evidence of registration consistent with 8 CFR 264.1(b), generally need not register again, although such an alien is subject to ongoing change of address reporting requirements under section 265(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1305(a) and 8 CFR 265.1, which are in addition to, and separate from, any other address reporting obligations the alien may have.
虽然国土安全部（DHS）未对提交G-325R表格设定费用，但国土安全部已在《暂行最终规则》中就是否增设每名登记人30美元的生物识别服务费（用于依据8 CFR 103.16和103.17采集、使用和存储生物识别信息）征求公众意见。
While DHS did not incorporate a fee for filing the Form G-325R, DHS requested comments in the IFR on the option of adding biometric services fee per registrant of $30, for the collection, use, and storage of biometric information, pursuant to 8 CFR 103.16 and 17.
III. 对《暂行最终规则》公众意见的回应
III. Response to Public Comments on the IFR
A. 《暂行最终规则》意见概述
A. Overview of Comments on the IFR
在发布《暂行最终规则》时，国土安全部（DHS）邀请公众通过提交发布后意见的方式参与本规则制定，意见范围涵盖该规则的各个方面。国土安全部还邀请公众就拟议的生物识别服务费（包括该费用的计算方式）发表意见。此外，为满足《文书削减法》（PRA，44 U.S.C. 3501及其后条款）项下为期60天的《联邦公报》通知要求，以及经行政管理和预算局（OMB）批准的G-325R表格《传记资料（登记用）》的要求，《暂行最终规则》还设置了第二个意见征集期。参见90 FR 11793, 11799（2025年3月12日）。国土安全部要求，就该信息收集提交的意见中应注明“OMB管理编号1615-NEW”字样。为《文书削减法》目的设置的意见征集期已于2025年5月12日结束。为取得OMB对G-325R表格的批准[12]，已于2025年8月11日在90 FR 38655发布了为期30天的《联邦公报》通知，针对G-325R表格收到的任何公众意见均已在该通知中予以答复。该信息收集文书及其说明，以及其他支持性文件（包括对60天《联邦公报》通知期间所提交意见的答复），均可通过访问联邦电子规则制定门户网站http://www.regulations.gov查阅，检索案卷编号为USCIS-2025-0005。
When issuing the IFR, DHS invited the public to participate in the rulemaking by submitting post-promulgation comments on every aspect of the rule. DHS also invited the public to comment on a potential biometric services fee, including on the calculation of the fee. Additionally, the IFR contained a secondary comment period for purposes of the 60-day Federal Register notice under the PRA, 44 U.S.C. 3501 et seq. and OMB approved Form G-325R, Biographic Information (Registration). See90 FR 11793, 11799 (Mar. 12, 2025). DHS requested that comments on the information collection included the term OMB Control Number 1615-NEW. The comment period for purposes of the PRA ended on May 12, 2025. Any public comment received on Form G-325R has been responded to as part of the 30-day Federal Register notice published for purposes of obtaining OMB approval of Form G-325R [12] on August 11, 2025, at 90 FR 38655. The information collection instrument with instructions and additional supporting documents, including responses to comments submitted as part of the 60-day Federal Register Notice, can be accessed by visiting the Federal eRulemaking Portal site at: http://www.regulations.gov under Docket ID number USCIS-2025-0005.
提交意见的主体和个人范围广泛，包括律师和法律服务提供者、申请人、个人评论者、专业组织、工会、倡导团体、宗教组织、研究机构和社区组织（印刷页第39255页），以及州和地方政府机构或民选官员。
Comments were submitted by a range of entities and individuals, including attorneys and legal service providers, applicants, individual commenters, professional organizations, unions, advocacy groups, religious organizations, research and community ( printed page 39255) organizations, and State and local government agencies or elected officials.
针对《暂行最终规则》，国土安全部（DHS）在为该规则及相关信息收集设定的初始意见征集期内（该期限于2025年4月11日截止）共收到4,198份公众意见。为信息收集目的设置的第二意见征集期于2025年5月12日截止，在2025年4月11日之后，案卷又收到71份补充意见。
In response to the IFR, DHS received 4,198 public comment submissions during the initial comment period that ended on April 11, 2025, for purposes of the IFR and the information collection. The secondary comment period for purposes of the information collection closed on May 12, 2025, with 71 additional submissions received to the docket after April 11, 2025.
国土安全部对所有意见和反馈表示感谢。少数评论者表示支持《暂行最终规则》或其部分内容，以及登记要求本身。大多数评论者反对法定登记要求，并因此反对该要求的实施，包括反对《暂行最终规则》、G-325R表格或该表格的内容，理由是其对各类人群或既往做法造成影响，但未提出具体建议。大多数评论者要求国土安全部撤销该规则。
DHS appreciates all comments and feedback. A few commenters expressed their support for the IFR or parts thereof, and the registration requirements. The majority of commenters opposed the statutory registration requirement and thus, the implementation thereof, including the IFR, Form G-325R, or the form's contents based on the impact on various populations or past practices but without providing specific suggestions. The majority of the commenters requested that DHS rescind the rule.
美国国土安全部(DHS)正在回应本最终规则中收到的反馈意见,但重申,大约85年来,美国的法律,包括长期实施的法规,一直要求大多数在美停留30天或以上的外国人进行登记,并且除基于年龄或非移民身份的若干例外情形外,还须按捺指纹。临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则均未修改这些法定要求。同样,临时最终规则和最终规则也未修改国会数十年前制定的、适用于外国人未能履行其登记及相关义务时的刑事处罚规定。本规则符合第14159号行政命令以及《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条至第265条(即8 U.S.C. 1302至1305)中规定的外国人登记要求,并为所有未登记的外国人建立了一项普遍适用的登记选项,无论其移民身份如何。美国国土安全部无权撤销该行政命令或《移民与国籍法》中的外国人登记要求。
DHS is responding to the feedback received in this final rule but reiterates that, for approximately 85 years, the laws of the United States, including longstanding regulations, have required most aliens present in the United States who remain for 30 days or longer to register and, with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted. The IFR and this final rule have not modified these statutory requirements. Similarly, the IFR and final rule do not modify the criminal penalties established by Congress many decades ago that apply when aliens fail to meet their registration and related obligations. This rule is consistent with E.O. 14159 and the alien registration requirements in sections 262 through 265 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1305, and establishes a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. DHS has no authority to rescind the E.O. or the alien registration requirements of the INA.
B. 对临时最终规则的支持意见
B. Support for the IFR
意见:多位意见提交者表示支持该临时最终规则。一位意见提交者指出,该临时最终规则是国土安全部和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)遵守第14159号行政命令的一种方式。该意见提交者认为,针对所有未登记外国人[13]的普遍登记表格,无论其身份如何,将改善登记成效,并支持移民法律法规的执行。该意见提交者补充说,这一新的登记表格将弥合“特定移民身份”与适当的登记程序和要求之间的监管缺口。另一位意见提交者认为“必须对外国人进行审查”,以保护美国公民的安全,包括了解“谁已进入我们的国家”,并鼓励国土安全部“通过”该法规。
Comment: Multiple commenters expressed support for the IFR. One commenter noted the IFR is a means for DHS and USCIS to comply with E.O. 14159. The commenter reasoned that a general registration form for all unregistered aliens,[13] regardless of status, would improve registration outcomes and support the enforcement of immigration laws and regulations. The commenter added that this new registration form would bridge the regulatory gap between “certain immigration statuses” and appropriate registration procedures and requirements. Another commenter reasoned that “aliens need to be vetted” to protect the safety of U.S. citizens, including by knowing “who has entered our country,” and encouraged DHS to “pass” this regulation.
回应:国土安全部同意,新的登记表格及登记证明将改善登记成效,并支持移民法律法规的执行。国土安全部感谢意见提交者的反馈及对国土安全部忠实执行国会制定的移民法律这一职责的支持。
Response: DHS agrees that the new registration form and evidence of registration will improve registration outcomes and support the enforcement of immigration laws and regulations. DHS appreciates the commenters' feedback and support for DHS obligation to faithfully execute immigration laws established by Congress.
C. 对临时最终规则的反对意见及政策异议
C. Opposition to the IFR and Policy Objections
1. 总体负面影响
1. General Negative Impacts
a. 关于过度扩权和侵犯隐私的担忧
a. Overreach and Invasiveness Concerns
意见:许多意见提交者对该临时最终规则所导致的联邦政府权力过度扩张表示担忧。意见提交者纷纷写道,该临时最终规则具有“侵入性”,“超出合理范围”,将造成“警察国家”,“在我们的民主法治体系中没有立足之地”,将违反法治原则,会允许过度的监控或政府控制,从而树立令人不安的先例,代表着权力滥用,并且是国家控制的危险扩张,将使政府能够监控数百万人。一位意见提交者表示,一旦实施,该临时最终规则可能为未来任何政府提供一种工具,用以对美国境内的外国居民采取有针对性的压制性措施。该意见提交者指出,权力一旦授予管理机构,就很少会被让渡或撤销,国土安全部应谨慎考虑该临时最终规则将确立的监管先例。另一位意见提交者表示,该临时最终规则施加的登记要求,类似于德国在第二次世界大战期间为控制不受欢迎群体而施加的登记要求。一位意见提交者补充说,尽管该登记要求已被编入美国法律,确保合规也是合法的,但该法律对那些多年来和平生活并为社会做出贡献的个人及其家庭,尤其是那些有待决移民救济或人道主义保护申请的人,造成了不成比例的影响。
Comment: Many commenters voiced concerns with Federal overreach as a result of the IFR. Commenters variously wrote that the IFR is “invasive,” “out of bounds,” would create a “police state,” “has no place in our system of democratic laws,” would violate the rule of law, would allow excessive surveillance or government control that sets a troubling precedent, represents an abuse of power, and is a dangerous expansion of state control that will enable the government to surveil millions of people. A commenter stated that once implemented, the IFR could provide a tool by which any future administration could enact targeted, repressive measures against alien residents of the United States. The commenter remarked that authority, once delegated to governing bodies, is rarely yielded or revoked, and that DHS should take care to consider the regulatory precedent the IFR would establish. Another commenter stated that the IFR imposes a similar registration requirement that was imposed by Germany during World War II as a means to control unpopular groups. A commenter added that while the registration is codified in the laws of the United States and it is lawful to ensure compliance, the law disproportionately impacts individuals and their families who have been living and contributing to society peacefully for years, especially those with pending immigration relief or humanitarian protections.
另一位意见提交者表示,将针对某一群体的大规模监控常态化树立了危险的先例,可能为所有人更广泛地侵蚀隐私和公民权利铺平道路。一位意见提交者表示,该临时最终规则赋予国土安全部部长不受制约的自由裁量权,以监控、登记和驱逐移民,因此“是对部长此前权力的全面修订”。
Another commenter stated that the normalization of mass surveillance for one group sets a dangerous precedent, potentially paving the way for broader erosions of privacy and civil rights for all individuals. A commenter stated that the IFR gives the Secretary of Homeland Security unchecked discretionary power to monitor, register, and remove immigrants and thus, was “a sweeping revision of prior powers to the Secretary.”
意见提交者对执法部门监控所带来的影响表示担忧。例如,一位意见提交者写道,监控措施削弱了整个社区的社会结构。其他意见提交者写道,监控范围的扩大最终可能涵盖所有美国公民,另有一人对该政策如此轻易地扩大监控范围并限制个人自由表示担忧。
Commenters expressed reservations about the implications of surveillance by law enforcement. For example, a commenter wrote that surveillance measures weaken the social fabric of entire communities. Other commenters wrote that the expansion of surveillance could eventually include all U.S. citizens, with another expressing concern about the ease with which the policy could expand surveillance and restrict individual liberties.
一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则(IFR)将创设“有失尊严的”监控要求,这将成为融入、经济参与和社区信任的障碍。另一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则是“绝对的丑闻”,因为它针对并诱捕那些为美国文化深度和经济活力作出贡献的人。一位评论者指出,这种手法依靠谎言来分化民众,而另一位评论者则表示“该计划”传递出不受欢迎的信息。
A commenter wrote that the IFR would create “undignified” monitoring requirements that would be a barrier to integration, economic participation, and community trust. Another commenter said the IFR is an “absolute abomination,” as it targets and entraps people who contribute to the cultural depth and economic vitality of the United States. One commenter stated that this tactic relies on lies to divide people, while another commenter said that “this program” sends an unwelcoming message.
另一位评论者表示担忧,该临时最终规则下收集的数据可能助长类似历史上强制登记计划中所见的侵犯人权行为。该评论者列举了若干例子,如第二次世界大战期间对日裔公民的拘禁、“9·11”事件后的国家安全入境出境登记系统(NSEERS)计划,以及大屠杀期间对犹太人的登记。该评论者指出,该临时最终规则中没有任何保障措施可以防止此类滥用行为,并建议撤销该规则以保护隐私并防止潜在的伤害。
Another commenter expressed concern that the data collected under the IFR could facilitate human rights abuses, similar to those seen in historical mandatory registration programs. The commenter cited examples such as the internment of Japanese citizens during World War II, the post-September 11 NSEERS (National Security Entry-Exit Registration System) program, and the registration of Jews during the Holocaust. The commenter remarked that there are no safeguards in the IFR to prevent such abuses and recommended rescinding the rule to protect privacy and prevent potential harm.
一位评论者写道,如果联邦政府打算对外国人实施“出示证件”规定,那么包括美国公民在内的美国境内每个人都必须随身携带其移民身份证明,否则将面临处罚。关于“随身携带证件”,一位评论者表示,已登记的移民如果不随身携带证件或未更新地址,将面临被起诉的风险,而外国人则面临被驱逐出境或刑事指控的风险。另一位评论者表示,某些州已经出现了移民执法行动(印刷页第39256页),并指出若实施全国性的“出示证件”法律,没有随身携带证明的美国公民或合法永久居民(LPR)同样面临被非法拘留甚至被驱逐出境的风险。该评论者表示,该规则及其登记程序不仅冒犯了公共安全,也冒犯了该程序本应维护的国家安全。
A commenter wrote that if the Federal Government intends to enforce a “show me your papers” rule against aliens, then every person in the United States, including U.S. citizens, must carry proof of their immigration status or face penalties. With regard to “carrying papers,” a commenter stated that registered immigrants risk prosecution if they do not carry them or update their address, while aliens face deportation or criminal charges. A different commenter said that certain States have already ( printed page 39256) seen immigration enforcement and said that with a nationwide “show me your papers” law, U.S. citizens without proof on them, or LPRs, are also at risk of being unlawfully detained and even deported. The commenter stated that the rule and registration processes are an affront to public safety as well as national security, which this process purports to uphold.
另一位评论者批评该临时最终规则是联邦权力的“严重越权”行为,尽管本届政府曾承诺将更多权力下放给各州。另一位评论者认为该临时最终规则与本届政府声称要减少监管的目标相矛盾。评论者还写道,该规则及强制登记强化了“深层国家”和监控国家的观念,即政府官员利用登记数据来追踪、针对和惩罚个人,这直接侵犯了人权和美国的各项权利。
Another commenter criticized the IFR as a “deep overreach” in Federal authority, despite the administration's promises to turn more power to the States. Another commenter viewed the IFR as contradictory to the administration's stated goal of reducing regulations. Commenters also wrote that the rule and forced registration reinforces the idea of a “Deep State” and surveillance state, where government officials use registration data to track, target, and punish individuals in direct violation of human rights and U.S. rights.
一位评论者表示,要求已登记的外国人向政府更新其现居住地,属于一种监控行为,其程度“堪比假释判决,而这是任何其他美国公民都无需经历的”。多位评论者表示,该临时最终规则和登记要求是迈向大规模监控及将“无证”社区定罪化的一步;这些要求具有“奥威尔式”色彩;并认为其存在政府越权和滥用数据以监视移民的潜在可能。
A commenter stated that requiring registered aliens to update the government with their current residence is surveillance “on par with a parole sentence that no other U.S. citizen has to go through.” Commenters stated that the IFR and the registration requirement are a step toward mass surveillance and criminalization of “undocumented” communities; that these requirements are “Orwellian;” and that they have the potential government overreach and the misuse of data to surveil immigrants.
众多评论者认为,该规则不符合美国精神或违背美国价值观,具有专制性、反民主性,将强化有害的刻板印象;或是“试图完成[一场]专制接管”,或建立独裁统治的企图。其他评论者表示,该临时最终规则违背了美国关于自由迁徙的价值观,是危险的、法西斯式的、惩罚性的、不人道的、不道德的,或具有剥削性质的。少数评论者写道,只有美洲原住民才不属于美国的移民,因此该临时最终规则的政策因此显得虚伪。
Numerous commenters suggested that the rule is un-American or runs contrary to U.S. values, authoritarian, un-democratic, that it would reinforce harmful stereotypes; or that it was an “attempt to complete [an] authoritarian takeover,” or establish a dictatorship. Other commenters stated that the IFR disagrees with the American value of free movement; is dangerous; fascist; punitive; inhumane; immoral; or exploitative. A few commenters wrote that only Native Americans are not immigrants to the United States, and that the IFR's policies are hypocritical as a result.
有评论者写道,临时最终规则(IFR)“残酷无情”,与自由女神像所代表的欢迎外国人并为其提供安全与自由的价值观相矛盾。类似地,少数评论者表示,美国是一个移民国家,应当包容多元化,对这些移民给予更多尊重;美国移民体系应当公平,并为合法身份提供可行的途径,而该规则并未促进这些目标的实现。其他评论者表示,美国不应恐吓或贬低外国人,美国应当恪守其接纳那些需要免受本国迫害、威胁、酷刑和死亡保护的外国人的声誉。一位评论者表示该规则施加了不当负担,并在社区中制造恐惧,称将那些为社会做出贡献的人定为罪犯是毫无道理的。该评论者在讨论其所在州的相关努力时指出,即使存在“加强边境安全的呼声”,也仍然可以尊重对待所有人,并承认移民对社会的价值。
Commenters wrote that the IFR is “cruel” and contradicts the values of welcoming aliens and providing them with safety and freedom, as represented by the Statue of Liberty. Similarly, a few commenters remarked that the United States is a nation of immigrants that should embrace diversity and be more respectful of those immigrants; that the U.S. immigration system should be fair and provide viable paths to legal status, and that the rule does not further those goals. Other commenters said that the United States should not intimidate or demean aliens and that the United States should honor its reputation of accepting aliens in need of protection from persecution, threats, torture, and death from their own country. Stating that the rule imposes an undue burden and creates fear in communities, a commenter said that making criminals out of people who are contributing to society was nonsensical. Discussing efforts in their State, the commenter noted that there can be “calls for border security” while still treating all people with respect and acknowledging the value of immigrants in society.
多位评论者称登记要求是对人权的侵犯。一位评论者还表示,该临时最终规则将为侵犯人权打开方便之门。评论者表示,该临时最终规则可能损害美国作为公平、人权和正当程序捍卫者的国际声誉。另一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则旨在“将移民贬低为不受欢迎的人”,这与宗教原则相矛盾,违背基督教价值观,并妨碍个人践行其宗教信仰。评论者还表示,该临时最终规则引发了道德和法律问题,令人联想起犹太历史上的黑暗岁月。
Several commenters called the registration requirement a violation of human rights. A commenter also said that the IFR would open the door for human rights abuse. Commenters stated that the IFR risks damaging the United States' international reputation as a champion of fairness, human rights, and due process. Another commenter wrote that the IFR aims to “reduce the immigrant to an undesirable person,” which contradicts religious principles, is against Christian values, and prevents individuals from practicing their religious beliefs. Commenters also stated that the IFR raises moral and legal questions and harkens back to dark days in Jewish history.
一位评论者表示,前一届总统政府曾多次承诺实行人道、充分考虑创伤影响的移民政策,特别是针对儿童和家庭的政策。该评论者表示,临时最终规则与这些价值观相矛盾,确立了基于恐惧的合规机制,却未提供通往安全、合法救济或稳定的途径,这在芝加哥等城市尤其令人担忧,因为那里仍有数千名寻求庇护者在等待工作许可、入学安置和基本住房。
A commenter stated that the Federal Government, under the previous Presidential administration, repeatedly committed to a humane, trauma-informed immigration policy, particularly for children and families. The commenters said that the IFR contradicts those values and codifies fear-based compliance mechanisms without providing pathways to safety, legal relief, or stability, which was particularly concerning in cities like Chicago, where thousands of asylum seekers are still awaiting work permits, school placements, and basic housing.
回应:对于评论者将该临时最终规则定性为越权、歧视性或以其他方式违背美国社会原则和价值观的说法,美国国土安全部(DHS)不予认同。美国国土安全部还不认同评论者提出的将登记计划等同于政府越权、大规模监控、侵蚀公民自由、惩罚、缺乏保障措施以及为对美国公民实施大规模监控开创先例的担忧。国会已指示并提供了必要的授权,以依据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条至第266条,即《美国法典》第8编第1302条至第1306条(8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1306)的规定,对外国人进行登记,包括登记的内容。制定政策和相应后果的是国会;美国国土安全部是依国会指示并致力于执行国会授权的机构。
Response: DHS disagrees with the characterization of the IFR as overreach, discriminatory, or otherwise contrary to the principles and values of American society, as raised by the commenters. DHS also disagrees with the concerns raised by commenters that equate the registration program with government overreach, mass surveillance, eroding civil liberties, punishment, lack of safeguards, and setting precedents for mass surveillance of U.S. citizens. Congress directed and provided the necessary authority for the registration of aliens, including the content of the registration in sections 262 through 266 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1306. It is Congress that sets the policy and consequences; DHS is directed by Congress and committed to carry out the congressional mandate.
特朗普总统指示美国国土安全部采取一切适当行动,确保此前未登记的在美外国人履行其向政府登记的义务。美国国土安全部发现现行监管体系存在空白,因此设立了一项新的一般登记选项,以便此前未登记的外国人能够遵守这项长期存在的法定要求。正如本规则制定过程中始终所述,登记制度已存在80多年;历届政府均依据8 CFR 264.1中长期存在的法规实施登记要求,该法规列明了登记所需的表格和证明文件,例如I-485表。该临时最终规则及本最终规则的范围有限,仅规定了一种针对未登记外国人(无论其移民身份如何)的额外登记表格和登记证明文件。一般登记表收集基本的个人生平信息以及《移民与国籍法》第264(a)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1304(a)条(8 U.S.C. 1304(a))所要求的信息,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)对此负有法律上的保密责任,与该局收集的其他表格类型相同。参见《移民与国籍法》第264(b)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(b)。
President Trump directed DHS to take all appropriate action to ensure that previously unregistered aliens in the United States comply with their duty to register with the government. DHS identified a gap in the regulatory regime and established a new general registration option so that previously unregistered aliens could comply with the longstanding statutory requirement. As explained throughout this rulemaking, registration has existed for over 80 years; administrations had implemented the registration requirement in the longstanding regulation at 8 CFR 264.1, listing forms and evidence of registration, such as Form I-485. The IFR and this final rule is limited in scope by prescribing an additional registration form and evidence of registration for unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. The general registration form collects basic biographic information and information required by section 264(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(a), which USCIS has a legal responsibility to safeguard, similar to the other form types collected by the agency. See INA sec. 264(b), 8 U.S.C. 1304(b).
b. 对递解、拘留和刑事定罪的影响
b. Effect on Removal, Detention, and Criminalization
评论:许多评论者批评该规则是企图诱使外国人陷入圈套的手段,迫使他们要么进行登记(从而面临被驱逐出境的可能),要么因不遵守规定而面临刑事处罚。另一位评论者将该规则描述为“不必要地复杂,显然是为了诱使和陷害民众而设计的”,而另一位评论者则将其描述为一项“恶意的‘政策’”,旨在通过混淆移民使其提供信息,而这些信息随后将被用于以非法或违宪的手段迫害他们。
Comment: Many commenters criticized the rule as an attempt to entrap aliens by forcing them to either register (causing them to face potential deportation), or face criminal penalties for failing to comply. Another commenter described the rule as “unnecessarily complicated and clearly designed to entrap and ensnare people,” while another described it as a “bad faith `policy' ” meant to confuse immigrants into providing information that would be used to persecute them by illegal or unconstitutional means.
一位评论者表示,时任部长诺姆在媒体采访中指出,登记的目的是便于将人员从美国遣返,登记者很可能面临拘留、驱逐程序以及最终被驱逐出境。一位评论者表示反对该临时最终规则(IFR)以及由此可能导致的外国人拘留问题,称移民拘留中心的条件可能致命,并指出有关于医疗虐待的报道。
A commenter stated that in media interviews, then-Secretary Noem indicated that the purpose of registration is to facilitate removal of people from the United States, and that those who register would likely face detention, removal proceedings, and eventual deportation. A commenter stated opposition to the IFR and the potential detention of aliens resulting from it, describing conditions in immigration detention centers as potentially deadly and noting reports of medical abuse.
其他众多评论者表示,该临时最终规则将导致不正当的拘留、将外国人在美国的存在定为犯罪行为、任意搜查和扣押、不正当的驱逐出境(包括对合法在美并等待法庭聆讯的外国人),将辛勤工作的移民当作替罪羊(印刷页第39257页),以及对无过错者施以惩罚。一位评论者表示担忧,认为该规则的主要目的是让国土安全部(DHS)更容易识别和驱逐外国人,并表示按照该临时最终规则的逻辑,类似措施也应适用于美国公民,因为如有必要对其进行逮捕,这样做会更容易识别他们。另一位评论者表示,将新的登记要求与一项要求国土安全部优先起诉违规和轻罪而非严重犯罪的行政命令相结合,凸显出此次登记的目的是“大规模驱逐出境”。一位评论者表示担忧,认为本届政府会将这一程序武器化,用来对付那些没有犯罪记录、在现行法律下也没有直接合法身份途径,但在其他方面是社会宝贵成员的人士。
Numerous other commenters stated that the IFR would lead to unjust detentions, the criminalization of aliens' presence in the United States, arbitrary searches and seizures, unjust deportations (including for aliens in the country legally and awaiting court hearings), scapegoating of hardworking ( printed page 39257) immigrants, and punishing people without merit. A commenter expressed concern that the main purpose of the rule is to make it easier for DHS to identify and deport aliens, and stated that following the IFR's logic, similar measures should be applied to U.S. citizens because it would be easier to identify them for arrest should it be deemed necessary. Another commenter stated that coupling the new registration requirement with an E.O. requiring DHS to prioritize prosecuting violations and misdemeanors, rather than serious crimes, highlights that the purpose of this registration is “mass deportation.” A commenter expressed concern that the current administration would weaponize the process against individuals who have no criminal history and no immediate path to legal status under the current law, but are otherwise valued members of society.
一位评论者写道,第14159号行政命令、外国人登记制度以及对驱逐出境的关注表明,联邦政府执行这些规定的主要目的是将该登记制度作为识别、拘留和驱逐“无证移民”的工具,或激励他们自行离境。其他评论者表示,该临时最终规则未能有意义地区分合法在美的外国人与无身份的外国人,他们认为这可能导致所有外国人,无论其法律身份或历史记录如何,都被视为潜在的执法对象。
A commenter wrote that E.O. 14159, alien registration, and the focus on deportation indicate that the primary aim of the Federal Government in enforcing these provisions is to use the registry as a tool to identify, detain, and deport “undocumented immigrants” or incentivize them to self-deport. Other commenters stated that the IFR fails to meaningfully distinguish between lawfully present aliens and those without status, which they said could lead to the treatment of all aliens as potential enforcement targets, regardless of their legal status or history.
其他评论指出,与“童年入境者暂缓遣返”(DACA)等计划不同,此登记程序不提供任何福利、任何法律保护、任何形式的移民救济,也不提供任何驱逐保护,并补充说该临时最终规则并未承诺通过此程序收集的数据不会被用于执法目的。另一位评论者表示,过去出于国家安全考虑而执行登记要求的做法中,曾明确保证遵守规定者将获得对移民救济的适当考虑,并表示若在不提供登记者寻求身份调整合法途径机会的情况下设定此类要求,将损害我们建立一个公正、高效、优先考虑长期居民福祉的移民体系的共同承诺。该评论者表示,这项政策非但不能激励人们遵守规定,反而惩罚了那些主动表明身份的人。
Other comments stated that, unlike programs such as Deferred Action for Childhood Arrivals (DACA), this registration process offers no benefits, no legal protections, no form of immigration relief, and no shield from deportation, and added that the IFR makes no promises that the data collected through this process would not be used for enforcement purposes. Another commenter stated that past attempts to enforce registration requirements in the interest of national security included explicit assurances that those who complied would be afforded due consideration for immigration relief, and stated that such a requirement without providing opportunities for registrants to pursue legal pathways toward status adjustment undermines our collective commitment to establishing a just, efficient immigration system that prioritizes the wellbeing of long-term residents. The commenter said that rather than incentivizing compliance, this policy punishes those who self-identify.
一位评论者指出,该规则将无法实现政府提高登记成效的政策目标,因为政府未能激励“无证移民”进行登记。该评论者在讨论1940年《外国人登记法》的历史背景时写道,国会曾授予时任司法部长罗伯特·H·杰克逊(Robert H. Jackson)权力,可免除登记者因非法入境而应承担的处罚。由此,加之司法部长成功开展的公众宣传,鼓励外国人进行登记,截至1941年1月,约有500万外国人向联邦政府进行了登记。相比之下,据该评论者称,本届政府已明确表示,计划利用登记程序推进其移民执法活动,包括驱逐出境,这未能激励“无证移民”进行登记。同样,另一位评论者也指出,这是政府首次将登记要求作为一项运动的一部分,旨在优先起诉移民违法行为并鼓励自我遣返,而不是像过去那样,向外国人保证登记可能有助于获得合法身份,或保证外国人无需担心因登记而产生不利后果。
A commenter indicated that the rule would fail to meet the administration's policy goals of improving registration outcomes because the administration fails to incentivize “undocumented immigrants” to register. Discussing the historical context of the Alien Registration Act of 1940, the commenter wrote that Congress had given then Attorney General Robert H. Jackson the power to relieve registrants of penalties for illegal entry. As a result, and because of the Attorney General's successful public messaging encouraging aliens to register, approximately 5 million aliens registered with the Federal Government by January 1941. In contrast, according to the commenter, this Administration made clear that it plans to use the registration process to advance its immigration enforcement activities, including removal, which fails to incentivize “undocumented immigrants” to register. Similarly, a commenter also indicated that it is the first time the government has required registration as part of a campaign to prioritize the prosecution of immigration offenses and encourage self-removal, rather than providing assurances, unlike in the past, that registration might lead to lawful status or that the alien does not have to fear adverse consequences for registering.
多位评论者表示担忧,国土安全部(DHS)可能会利用该规则,以1798年《敌对外国人法》为依据推动驱逐出境。
Several commenters expressed concerns that DHS would use the rule to facilitate deportations under the Alien Enemies Act of 1798.
答复:临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则的目标是通过制定一份适用于所有外国人的通用登记表格,来填补监管体系中的空白。该临时最终规则并未设立登记或随身携带登记证明的法定要求,也未设立国会数十年前就外国人未能履行登记及相关义务所规定的刑事处罚。
Response: The goal of the IFR and this final rule is to fill a gap in the regulatory regime by prescribing a general registration form available to all aliens. The IFR did not establish the statutory requirements to register or carry evidence of registration, or establish the criminal penalties established by Congress many decades ago that apply when aliens failed to meet their registration and related obligations.
国土安全部同意,登记不属于移民身份,登记文件也不能证明移民身份、不能证明就业授权,也不提供《移民与国籍法》(INA)或任何其他美国法律项下的任何其他权利或利益。
DHS agrees that the registration is not an immigration status, and the registration documentation does not evidence an immigration status, establish employment authorization, or provide any other right or benefit under the INA or any other U.S. law.
然而,法律规定,大多数14岁及以上、在申请美国签证时未进行登记和(如有要求)未进行指纹采集、且在美国境内停留30天或以上的外国人,必须申请登记并进行指纹采集。该法律对不遵守规定的行为规定了民事和刑事处罚。本规则的目的是为外国人提供一种简便的方式,以履行其法定登记义务,而非在《移民与国籍法》已有规定之外提供额外激励。
However, the statute provides that most aliens 14 years of age or older who were not registered and fingerprinted (if required) when applying for a U.S. visa and who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer, must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted. The statute imposes civil and criminal penalties for failure to comply. The purpose of this rule is to provide a straightforward way for aliens to meet their statutory obligations to register, rather than to provide incentives beyond those already contained in the INA.
时任司法部长罗伯特·H·杰克逊在向美国团结共同委员会(Common Council for American Unity)发表讲话时指出,“这些详细的个人记录使政府能够根据个人情况处理任何外国人问题,从而消除了采取不加区分的全面搜捕行动或集体行动的任何借口。”[14] 许多目前身处美国境内的外国人已经通过8 CFR 264.1条所列明的多种途径履行了登记义务。每一份G-325R表格登记申请都将经过individual审查,以确定该外国人是否已通过其他方式满足登记要求,从而无需提交G-325R表格,或者该外国人是否需要前往参加生物识别服务预约。
As then-Attorney General Robert H. Jackson stated in his address before the Common Council for American Unity, “[t]hese detailed individual records enable the government to deal with any alien problems on the basis of individual merits, and they take away any excuse for indiscriminating dragnet procedures or mass action.” [14] Many aliens who are present in the United States have already fulfilled their duty to register through a variety of pathways identified in 8 CFR 264.1. Each registration Form G-325R will undergo an individual review to determine if the alien has already complied with the registration requirements in some other way and does not need to submit Form G-325R or if the alien is required to appear for a biometric services appointment.
国土安全部认为,改善登记成效将通过提供更全面的外国人在美国境内所在位置信息,提高国土安全部的执法效能,从而使国土安全部执法工作更加便捷和安全。提高指纹采集要求的遵守率,将为国土安全部提供有关外国人犯罪记录的更多信息,包括该外国人是否为已知或疑似恐怖分子。国土安全部不认为登记与执法效能之间存在关联性存在任何问题。国会专门规定了可向联邦、州和地方执法机构共享外国人登记信息,国土安全部依法遵守国会正式通过的相关法律。[15]
DHS believes that improved registration outcomes will improve DHS law enforcement efficacy by providing more comprehensive information about the location of aliens in the United States, which makes it easier and safer for DHS to enforce the law. The increased compliance with fingerprinting requirements would provide DHS with additional information about an alien's criminal record, including whether the alien is a known or suspected terrorist. DHS does not believe that a nexus between registration and law enforcement efficacy is problematic; Congress specifically provided for the sharing of alien registration information with Federal, State, and local law enforcement, and DHS complies with the laws as duly passed by Congress.[15]
c. 对非移民群体的行政和经济影响
c. Administrative and Financial Impacts to Nonimmigrant Populations
评论:评论者反对该临时最终规则(IFR),理由是其给移民群体带来了经济和行政负担。一位评论者指出,登记要求将对生活状况发生变化的移民构成挑战,尤其是那些需要迅速搬迁的移民。该评论者指出,地址变更更新要求可能妨碍移民应对法院系统的能力,而官僚体系的积压可能错误地将流动性强的移民标记为不遵守登记要求。
Comment: Commenters opposed the IFR due to financial and administrative burdens on immigrant populations. A commenter stated that registration requirements would challenge immigrants experiencing changes in living situations, particularly those needing to relocate quickly. The commenter noted that change-of-address update requirements could impede immigrants' ability to navigate the court system, while bureaucratic backlogs might incorrectly label mobile immigrants as non-compliant with registration requirements.
一位评论者对如果国土安全部(DHS)对G-325R表格实施处理费可能带来的经济负担表示担忧,尤其是对因情况变化(印刷页第39258页)而需要多次提交登记的个人而言。这些行政要求将加重移民在应对法律诉讼、教育、家庭责任和数字障碍等方面已面临的挑战。许多评论者认为,要求提交G-325R表格将增加不必要的官僚和经济负担,因为移民已经提交了大量文件。
A commenter expressed concern about potential financial burdens if DHS implemented processing fees for Form G-325R, especially for individuals submitting multiple registrations due to ( printed page 39258) changing circumstances. These administrative requirements would compound existing challenges immigrants face while managing legal proceedings, education, family responsibilities, and digital barriers. Many commenters suggested that requiring the Form G-325R would add unnecessary bureaucratic and financial burdens, as immigrants already submit extensive documentation.
回应:该临时最终规则(IFR)及本规则的范围有限,旨在为外国人指定一份新的登记表格,以遵守法定的外国人登记和指纹采集规定。国土安全部(DHS)根据第14159号行政命令的指示,采取一切适当行动,确保外国人了解其法定义务,并通过新的一般登记程序和表格获得履行该义务的途径。本规则制定并未修改国会设立的外国人登记和指纹采集义务。同样,该临时最终规则(IFR)也未创设或修改国会数十年前设立的刑事处罚规定,这些规定适用于外国人未能履行登记及相关义务的情形,包括向国土安全部(DHS)通知地址变更的要求。国会制定的法律要求,需登记的外国人须在迁居后10日内向政府更新其地址。参见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第265条,8 U.S.C. 1305。
Response: The IFR and this rule are limited in scope and designate a new registration form for aliens to comply with the statutory alien registration and fingerprinting provisions. DHS was directed through E.O. 14159 to take all appropriate action to ensure that aliens understand their duty under the law and have a path to satisfy that duty through the new general registration process and form. This rulemaking has not modified the duty established by Congress for aliens to register and be fingerprinted.
