Similarly, this IFR did not create or modify the criminal penalties established by Congress many decades ago that apply when aliens fail to meet their registration and related obligations, including the requirement to notify DHS of change of address. The law created by Congress requires that aliens who are required to register update their address with the government within 10 days of moving. See INA sec. 265, 8 U.S.C. 1305.
一般登记表格收集基本的个人生物信息以及《移民与国籍法》(INA)第264(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1304(a)所要求的信息。该表格为数字表格,可通过互联网浏览器或个人手机轻松访问。此外,公共图书馆及其他公共和私人机构也提供互联网访问服务。完成G-325R表格每次平均所需时间负担评估为2.5小时。此外,许多在美外国人已按法律要求完成登记,无需再填写G-325R表格。因此,国土安全部(DHS)不认同该规则给公众造成不当负担或加重移民现有困境的说法。如果个人在登记过程中遇到困难,国土安全部(DHS)建议联系美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)联络中心获取现场协助。[16]
The general registration form collects basic biographic information and information required by section 264(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(a). The form is a digital form that can be easily accessed through an internet browser or a person's cell phone. Additionally, public libraries and other private and public institutions provide access to the internet. The burden assessed for the average hour burden per response for completion of Form G-325R is 2.5 hours. Also, many aliens in the United States have already registered, as required by law, and would not need to complete Form G-325R. DHS thus disagrees with the characterization that the rule imposes an undue burden on the public or compounds existing challenges immigrants are facing. If an individual encounters difficulties with registering, DHS recommends contacting the USCIS Contact Center for live assistance.[16]
国土安全部(DHS)已决定,在本最终规则中不对登记申请人收取生物识别服务费。本序言第III.F.5节提供了有关申请费确定的更多细节。
DHS has determined that it will not impose a biometric services fee for registration applicants as part of this final rule. Section III.F.5 of this preamble provides more details on the determination of the filing fee.
评论:评论者表示,额外的G-325R登记要求将给低收入外国人带来经济负担,原因在于与该临时最终规则(IFR)相关的法律及申请费用,以及其他相关费用,例如误工、误学、安排托儿服务,甚至为参加生物识别预约或获取特定文件而产生的交通费用,这些都对其有限的资源造成了不当压力,使得合规几乎无法实现。一位评论者指出,移民表格通常需要外部协助才能确保合规填写,鉴于不合规将面临处罚,预期登记人很可能会寻求外部支持。
Comment: Commenters said that the additional Form G-325R registration requirement would impose financial burdens on low-income aliens due to legal and application costs or other costs associated with the IFR, such as missing work or school, arranging childcare, and even travel for biometrics appointments, or to obtain specific documentation, which places an undue strain on their limited resources, all of which can make compliance unattainable. A commenter stated that immigration forms frequently require outside assistance to ensure compliant completion, and prospective registrants are likely to seek external support given the penalties for noncompliance.
回应:国土安全部(DHS)承认,本规则给登记人带来了一定负担。这些成本包括但不限于提交生物识别信息所需的时间成本,以及前往申请支持中心(ASC)所需的交通费用。有关本规则相关成本的更详细说明,见本序言第六节“法定和法规要求”部分。
Response: DHS acknowledges there are burdens to registrants associated with this rule. These costs include, but are not limited to, time burden to submit biometrics, and travel costs to go to an ASC. A more detailed breakdown of the costs associated with this rule is in section VI, the Statutory and Regulatory Requirements section of this preamble.
d. 健康影响
d. Health Impacts
评论:一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则(IFR)将危及社区的心理和身体健康。另一位评论者引用了一项研究,该研究发现限制性移民政策和监控措施与外国人较差的心理和身体健康结果相关,包括焦虑和抑郁情绪的加剧,以及医疗服务可及性的降低。还有一位评论者表示担忧,称该临时最终规则将因患者害怕遭遇美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)而限制其为患者提供医疗服务的能力,并进一步对外国人拘留设施中的健康状况表示关切,称“多人”因不人道、不安全和不卫生的条件在其中死亡。一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则给弱势群体带来的压力增加将导致急诊室、心理健康机构和初级保健提供者接触频率的上升。一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则给美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)处理中心带来的额外负担可能增加等待裁决的外国人的压力,从而导致心理和身体健康方面的挑战。
Comment: A commenter stated that the IFR would endanger communities' mental and physical well-being. Another commenter cited a study finding that restrictive immigration policies and surveillance measures correlate with poorer mental and physical health outcomes among aliens, including heightened anxiety and depression, as well as reduced access to healthcare services. Yet another commenter expressed concern that the IFR would limit their ability to provide medical care to patients due to patients' fear of encountering ICE, and further raised concerns about the health conditions in alien detention facilities, stating that “multiple people” have died in them due to inhumane, unsafe, and unsanitary conditions. A commenter wrote that the increased stress from the IFR to vulnerable populations would lead to higher contact with emergency rooms, mental health agencies, and primary health care providers. A commenter wrote that the additional burden from the IFR on USCIS processing centers could increase stress for aliens waiting for decisions, leading to mental and physical health challenges.
回应:80多年来,美国法律一直要求大多数在美停留30天或以上的外国人进行登记,并且除基于年龄或非移民身份的若干例外情形外,还须按捺指纹。
Response: For over 80 years, the laws of the United States have required most aliens present in the United States who remain for 30 days or longer to register and, with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted.
国土安全部(DHS)发现了现行监管制度中的一个空白,并建立了一种新的一般登记选项,供那些被要求登记但尚未登记的外国人使用,以满足其法定义务。该临时最终规则及本规则在范围上是有限的,其内容限于修订国土安全部的法规,以便为未登记的外国人(无论其移民身份如何)指定新的登记表格及登记证明。
DHS identified a gap in the regulatory regime and established a new general registration option that aliens, who are required to register but have not yet done so, may use to satisfy their statutory obligations. The IFR and this rule are limited in scope by amending DHS regulations to designate a new registration form and evidence of registration for unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status.
2. 对特定人群的影响
2. Impact on Specific Populations
a. 基于移民身份划分的群体
a. Groups Based on Immigration Status
评论:一位评论者表示,其所服务的难民、寻求庇护者、签证持有人和永久居民群体几乎完全由在原籍国遭受迫害的外国人组成,并补充说,一项新的登记要求使他们更有理由害怕这个他们本来前来寻求庇护和保护的地方。另一位评论者表示,该规则可能导致那些已经使用其他登记表格合法登记的外国人更加恐惧和不安。多位评论者补充说,该临时最终规则将严重影响合法移民。
Comment: A commenter said that the population of refugees, asylum seekers, visa holders, and permanent residents they serve almost exclusively comprises aliens who have been persecuted in their home countries, adding that a new registration requirement gives them more reason to fear the place they have come to for refuge and protection. Another commenter stated that the rule may cause aliens who are already legally registered using other registration forms to be increasingly fearful and uncertain. Multiple commenters added that the IFR would severely impact lawful immigration.
一些评论者表示,登记将被用来针对“无证”个人采取执法行动。一位评论者表示,“无证”群体中包括许多为美国作出贡献的勤劳个人,但他们却未能从自己的劳动成果中受益。该评论者总结认为,登记将伤害社区并制造恐惧。
Some commenters stated that the registration would be used to single out “undocumented” individuals for law enforcement actions. A commenter stated that the “undocumented” community includes hardworking individuals who contribute to the United States but do not see the benefits of their work. The commenter concluded that registration would harm communities and generate fear.
其他人指出,该临时最终规则将对那些为我们国家作出丰富贡献且对美国公民不构成威胁的个人产生负面影响,包括“童年入境者暂缓遣返计划”(DACA)受益人以及其他在年幼时抵达美国的个人。
Others indicated that the IFR would negatively impact individuals who contribute richly to our country and pose no threat to U.S. citizens, including DACA recipients and other individuals who arrived in the United States at a young age.
一位评论者表示,该组织的成员,包括未经检查入境的“非公民”,直接受到登记要求及不遵守规定可能产生的刑事处罚的影响。该评论者对那些有未决移民救济申请或暂缓遣返请求(如U签证或DACA)、已完成生物特征信息采集但缺乏可作为有效登记证明的工作许可文件(EAD)的成员表示担忧。该评论者表示,其曾协助在2020年至2021年间提交了大量DACA申请,但由于法院命令,这些申请从未得到裁决。
A commenter stated that the organization's members, including “noncitizens” who entered without inspection, are directly impacted by the registration requirements and potential criminal penalties for noncompliance. The commenter expressed concern for members with pending immigration relief applications or deferred action requests (such as U visas or DACA) who have completed biometrics appointments but lack EADs that would qualify as valid registration. The commenter said it had helped submit numerous DACA applications in 2020 to 2021 that were never adjudicated due to a court order.
一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则将针对并对那些长期居住在美国、正等待(印刷页第39259页)移民救济或人道主义保护的居民产生不成比例的影响。一位评论者质疑国土安全部作出此决定的理由,认为这为那些可能已经向政府提交过生物特征数据的弱势群体设置了额外的程序障碍。另一位评论者表示担忧,登记往往会导致负面后果,例如监控、隔离和入罪。该评论者指出,将某一群体identified为与其他人不同的群体,无论最初的意图如何,都可能产生严重后果。
A commenter wrote that the IFR would target and disproportionately affect long-term U.S. residents awaiting ( printed page 39259) immigration relief or humanitarian protections. A commenter questioned the rationale behind DHS's decision, suggesting that it creates an additional procedural hurdle for vulnerable populations who have likely already submitted their biometric data to the government. Another commenter expressed concern that registration often leads to negative outcomes, such as surveillance, segregation, and incrimination. The commenter remarked that identifying a group as separate from the rest can have serious consequences, regardless of the original intentions.
答复:临时最终规则(IFR)和本规则的适用范围仅限于建立一项面向所有未登记外国人(无论其移民身份如何)的一般性登记选项,以改善特定群体外国人的登记结果。已被签发8 CFR 264.1(b)条指定为登记证明文件之一的外国人已完成登记;已提交8 CFR 264.1(a)条指定表格之一并提供指纹(如未获豁免),但未被签发8 CFR 264.1(b)条所指定登记证明文件之一的外国人,已履行《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条、《美国法典》第8编第1302条规定的登记义务。如果外国人没有其他途径进行登记和按捺指纹,该外国人可提交G-325R表格,以履行其根据《移民与国籍法》第262条、《美国法典》第8编第1302条规定的义务。已登记并年满14岁的外国人,如无其他途径满足该要求,可使用新表格进行登记。
Response: The IFR and this rule are limited in scope to establish a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status to improve registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens. An alien who has been issued one of the documents designated as evidence of registration under 8 CFR 264.1(b) has already registered, and an alien who has submitted one of the forms designated at 8 CFR 264.1(a) and provided fingerprints (unless waived), but was not issued one of the evidence of registration designated at 8 CFR 264.1(b), has complied with the registration requirement of section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. If an alien does not have any other pathway to register and to be fingerprinted, the alien may file the Form G-325R to comply with their duty under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. Aliens who are registered and reached 14 years old may use the new form to register if they have no other pathway to satisfy this requirement.
国土安全部(DHS)意识到现行法规中存在可以改进的方面,包括修订8 CFR 264.1(a)条中规定的登记表格清单以及8 CFR 264.1(b)条中规定的登记证明文件清单。作为本最终规则的一部分,国土安全部正就修订国土安全部法规的各种方式征求意见,以改善《移民与国籍法》第262条、《美国法典》第8编第1302条所规定登记要求的落实情况。参见本序言第五节。
DHS is aware that there are areas of the existing regulations that could be improved, including amending the list of forms prescribed as registration forms in 8 CFR 264.1(a) and the list of forms constituted as evidence of registration in 8 CFR 264.1(b). As part of this final rule, DHS is requesting comments on various ways to amend the DHS regulation to improve implementation of the registration requirement under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. See section V of this preamble.
b. 家庭与儿童
b. Families and Children
评论意见:许多评论者表示,临时最终规则将对儿童和家庭造成伤害。多位评论者指出,该临时最终规则可能会阻碍家庭为子女办理入学、寻求医疗服务或举报犯罪。针对对儿童教育的影响,一位评论者表示,该登记制度可能导致受罚风险,家长可能会因担心相关信息被用于启动驱逐出境程序而犹豫是否为自己或子女进行登记。该评论者总结认为,这不仅破坏了与当地执法部门合作以减少犯罪所需的信任,也破坏了教育工作者、社会工作者和医疗保健合作伙伴有效服务这些家庭所需的信任。
Comment: Many commenters state that the IFR would harm children and families. Several commenters indicated that the IFR could deter families from enrolling children in school, from seeking medical care, or from reporting crimes. Speaking to the impact on children's education, a commenter stated that the registration system could lead to a risk of punishment and parents may be hesitant to register themselves or their children out of fear the information could be used to initiate removal proceedings. They concluded that this undermines the trust necessary not only to cooperate with local law enforcement to reduce crime, but for educators, social workers, and healthcare partners to serve these families effectively.
另有多位评论者指出,该临时最终规则未包含针对家庭的保障措施,并表示该规则可能导致父母或照料者被驱逐出境,从而造成家庭分离,包括身份混合家庭在内。一位评论者批评美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)假定潜在登记人会理解自己需要进行登记的做法,并将移民描述为“家庭分离、拘留或驱逐出境的目标”。
Several other commenters remarked that the IFR does not contain safeguards for families and stated that the IFR could lead to the deportation of parents or caregivers, and leading to family separation, including for mixed-status families. A commenter criticized USCIS' assumption that potential registrants would understand they need to register and described immigrants as “targets for family separation, detention, or deportation.”
一位评论者表示担忧,该临时最终规则将给那些缺乏稳定住房、法律顾问、数字素养或可靠互联网接入的有未成年子女家庭带来负担,且这些家庭若未能成功完成登记,还可能面临刑事处罚。
A commenter voiced concern that the IFR would create burdens for those families with minor children who lack access to stable housing, legal counsel, digital literacy, or reliable internet and potentially facing criminal penalties if not successful at registering.
另一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则未能充分考虑将快速驱逐出境适用于假释入境者对家庭和社区所产生的重大影响,并补充说,在未提供适当程序保护的情况下使其面临驱逐出境,可能给这些个人及其家庭带来严重的社会和经济后果。关于经济方面,评论者写道,许多“身份混合”家庭将因此受到伤害,尽管这些家庭对经济做出了重大贡献。
Another commenter stated that the IFR fails to consider the significant impact on its application of expedited removal to parolees would have on families and communities, adding that subjecting them to removal without appropriate procedural protections could result in severe social and economic consequences for these individuals and their families. With regard to the economy, commenters wrote that many “mixed-status” families would be harmed, despite having significantly contributed to the economy.
一位评论者写道,一些移民家庭目前正在寻求合法的移民救济,而该临时最终规则可能会在这一过程中带来混乱,并增加无意中不合规的风险。另一位评论者表示,该规则将导致移民家庭因担心被定罪而退出日常生活,从而对当地社区、经济和公共安全产生负面影响。
A commenter wrote that some immigrant families are already in the midst of pursuing lawful immigration relief and that the IFR could introduce confusion and risk of unintentional noncompliance into that process. Another commenter stated the rule would cause immigrant families to withdraw from everyday life for fear of criminalization, leading to negative impacts on local communities, economies, and public safety.
众多评论者表示,因临时最终规则(IFR)及登记要求而产生的对被驱逐出境的恐惧和家庭离散创伤,会给儿童造成长期的情感、发育、健康(包括心脏病、糖尿病、药物滥用和抑郁症)以及教育方面的损害。另一位评论者写道,临时最终规则将影响勤劳工作的家庭、纳税人以及能说会写英语的人士,并将损害外国人儿童的心理健康,导致部分儿童产生轻生念头。另一位评论者写道,本届政府正将移民(无论其身份如何)以及父母以“无证件”身份抵达美国的儿童,当作“权力斗争”中的“棋子”加以利用。另一位评论者表示,临时最终规则将使遭受贩运和虐待的儿童受害者更不愿意站出来向执法部门报告其遭遇。
Numerous commenters stated that the trauma of fear of deportation and family separation resulting from the IFR and from registration leads to long-term emotional, developmental, health (including heart disease, diabetes, substance abuse, and depression), and educational harm for children. Another commenter wrote that the IFR would impact hardworking families, taxpayers, and individuals who speak and write in English, and would harm the mental health of alien children, leading some to consider taking their own lives. Another commenter wrote that the administration is using immigrants, regardless of status, and children whose parents arrived to the United States “undocumented,” as “pawns” in a “power struggle.” Another commenter stated that IFR would make children who are victims of trafficking and abuse less likely to come forward and report their experiences to law enforcement.
答复:美国国土安全部(DHS)有义务忠实执行国会制定的法律,包括与外国人登记要求有关的规定。参见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第103(a)条,《美国法典》第8编第1103(a)条。
Response: DHS has an obligation to faithfully execute the laws established by Congress, including provisions related to the alien registration requirements. See INA sec. 103(a), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a).
特朗普总统指示国土安全部采取一切适当措施,确保此前未登记的在美外国人履行依法向政府登记的法定义务。国土安全部发现现行监管体系存在空白,因此设立了一个新的一般性登记选项,以改善特定类别外国人的登记结果。临时最终规则及本规则的范围仅限于为未登记的外国人(无论其移民身份如何)设立新的登记表格和登记证明。本次规则制定并未创设这些要求,也未修改国会为外国人设定的义务。
President Trump directed DHS to take all appropriate steps to ensure that previously unregistered aliens in the United States comply with the statutory duty to register with the government. DHS identified a gap in the regulatory regime and established a new general registration option to improve registration outcomes of certain groups of aliens. The IFR and this rule are limited in scope to establishing the new registration form and evidence of registration for unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. This rulemaking has not created these requirements or modified the duties established by Congress for aliens.
国土安全部指出,除有限的例外情形外,法律要求所有年满14岁且在美国境内停留满30天或以上的外国人,必须在30天期满前申请登记并接受指纹采集。同样,14岁以下外国人的父母或法定监护人必须确保该外国人完成登记。先前已登记的外国人,在其年满14周岁生日后的30天内,必须申请重新登记并接受指纹采集。
DHS notes that the statute requires, with limited exceptions, all aliens 14 years or older who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted before the expiration of 30 days. Similarly, parents and legal guardians of aliens below the age of 14 must ensure that those aliens are registered. Within 30 days of an alien reaching his or her 14th birthday, all previously registered alien must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted.
在临时最终规则出台之前,现行的长期规定已规定,凡不属于外国人登记要求豁免情形的在美外国人,在其年满14周岁后的30天内,必须申请重新登记并接受指纹采集,除非指纹采集被豁免。临时最终规则及本规则并未改变这些程序,而是通过增设一个适用于所有未登记外国人(无论其身份如何)的一般性选项,填补了监管空白,以改善特定类别外国人的登记结果。此外,许多在美外国人已依法完成登记。凡获发移民签证或非移民签证,且在其最近一次入境时凭借该签证获准进入美国的外国人,均视为已登记。参见《移民与国籍法》第221(b)条,《美国法典》第8编第1201(b)条(印刷页第39260页)。这包括以非移民学生和交流访问者身份获准入境的外国人。同上。
Before the IFR, longstanding regulations already provided that within 30 days after reaching the age of 14, any alien in the United States who is not exempt from the alien registration requirement must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted, unless fingerprinting is waived. The IFR and this rule do not change those procedures but fill a gap in the regulation by adding a general option available to unregistered alien, regardless of status to improve registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens. Also, many aliens in the United States have already registered, as required by law. An alien who was issued an immigrant or nonimmigrant visa and at his or her most recent arrival was admitted into the United States using that visa is registered. See INA sec. 221(b), 8 U.S.C. 1201(b). This includes aliens admitted ( printed page 39260) as nonimmigrant students and exchange visitors. Id.
基于上述理由以及此前答复中阐述的理由,国土安全部认为,尤其是在将临时最终规则或本规则与《移民与国籍法》所规定的外国人登记要求分开看待时,二者并不会给家庭和儿童造成负担,也不会对家庭、地方社区及公共安全产生负面影响。
For these reasons and the reasons articulated in previous responses, DHS does not believe that the IFR or this rule, particularly when viewed separate and apart from the alien registration requirements of the INA, creates a burden for families and children or otherwise leads to a negative impact on families, local communities and public safety.
评论:众多评论者对临时最终规则适用于未成年人和儿童表示反对。多位评论者敦促国土安全部基于登记要求可能对未成年人造成的影响,撤销或放弃该临时最终规则。
Comment: Numerous commenters expressed opposition to the IFR's application to minors and children. Several commenters urged DHS to rescind or abandon the IFR on the basis of potential impacts to minors subject to the registration requirements.
一位评论者表示,许多受临时最终规则影响的儿童曾经历创伤,例如遭受伤害或死亡威胁、虐待或忽视,而与创伤相关的困扰可能进一步削弱他们遵守临时最终规则众多且复杂要求的能力。
A commenter stated that many children affected by the IFR have experienced trauma, such as threats of harm or death, abuse, or neglect, and that trauma-related challenges can further impair their ability to comply with the numerous and complex requirements of the IFR.
一位评论者写道,年轻人往往因在原籍国经历过创伤而对指纹采集等程序特别感到恐惧。该评论者表示,如果他们在经过专门培训的法律顾问和支持下应对移民法律体系,他们能够在接受美国合法身份审查的过程中克服此类恐惧,但该评论者补充说,由于资金削减,许多此类儿童即将失去代理服务,因此将无法成功应对法律程序,因为他们将缺乏帮助来理解此类程序的目的及其相对安全性。一位评论者写道,登记所需的信息可能对儿童来说是无法获取或无法理解的,包括无人陪伴儿童,尤其是那些处于联邦政府拘留中的儿童。该评论者表示,特别是对于经历过重大创伤的儿童,最佳做法表明应为他们提供特殊考虑和创伤知情护理,而不是期望他们像成年人一样遵守可能涉及刑事责任的程序要求。
A commenter wrote that young people are often particularly fearful of procedures such as fingerprinting due to trauma they experienced in their home countries. The commenter said that if they are navigating the immigration legal system with specially trained legal advice and support, they are able to overcome such fears as part of the process of being scrutinized for lawful status in the United States but added that many of these children are set to lose representation due to funding cuts and thus are not able to successfully navigate the legal process as they will lack the help to understand the purpose and relative safety of such procedures. A commenter wrote that the information required for registration may be either unavailable or incomprehensible to children, including unaccompanied children and especially those in Federal Government custody. The commenter said that especially for children who have faced significant trauma, best practice indicates that they be provided special consideration and trauma-informed care, not an expectation to comply with the same requirements as adults for processes that potentially implicate criminal liability.
一位评论者对临时最终规则(IFR)要求14岁至18岁儿童接受登记、指纹采集和背景调查表示担忧,称该登记的侵入性要求未能考虑到自基础法律制定以来对儿童脆弱性认识的演变。该评论者指出,在普遍登记要求下,弱势移民,尤其是未成年人遭受剥削的情况有所增加,原因是存在欺诈者和诈骗者,包括冒充联邦特工的人。该评论者敦促国土安全部(DHS)重新考虑登记要求,以维护未成年人的公民权利和公共安全保护。同样,一位评论者对12岁至18岁可能没有驾照的儿童因肤色或缺乏英语能力而被针对表示担忧,因此该评论者反对携带身份证或登记卡的要求。
Expressing concerns about the IFR's requirement for children between the ages of 14 and 18 to submit to registration, fingerprinting, and background checks, a commenter stated that these registration's invasive requirements failed to account for evolving understanding of childhood vulnerabilities since the underlying law was written. The commenter noted the increased exploitation of vulnerable migrants, particularly minors, under a universal registration requirement, because on account of fraudsters and scammers, including those impersonating Federal agents. The commenter urged DHS to reconsider registration requirements to preserve civil rights and public safety protections for minors. Similarly, a commenter expressed concern for children between the ages of 12 and 18 who may not have a license being targeted for their skin color or lack of English language skills, and the commenter therefore opposed the requirement to carry ID or registration cards.
同样,评论者对登记要求对儿童和青少年的影响表示担忧。一位评论者表示,所考虑的刑事处罚也适用于14至18岁的儿童,使他们特别面临被定性和刑事定罪的风险,并指出一项1940年代的法规未能考虑到几十年来在未成年人刑事起诉方面所取得的改进。
Similarly, commenters expressed concern about the impact of the registration requirements on children and youth. A commenter stated that the criminal penalties contemplated also extend to children between the ages of 14-18, placing them at particular risk of profiling and criminalization, noting that a 1940-era statute does not account for the decades of improvement that have been made regarding the criminal prosecution of juveniles.
其他评论者表示,临时最终规则(IFR)对不登记的严重后果未作任何例外规定,将青少年与成年人同等对待。一位评论者表示,随着家庭拘留的恢复,青少年可能与父母一起被拘留,也可能独自被拘留,并面临可能被驱逐到其安全和福祉可能受到损害的国家的情况。该评论者引用了美国儿科学会(American Academy of Pediatrics)的立场,即“任何时长的拘留对儿童而言都不安全”,并引用了国土安全部一份咨询委员会报告的结论,即“拘留通常对家庭既不合适也无必要”,并且“绝不符合儿童的最佳利益”。
Other commenters stated that the IFR makes no exceptions for the severe consequences of not registering, treating youth the same as adults. One commenter stated that with the return of family detention, youth could potentially be held with their parents or alone, facing possible deportation to countries where their safety and well-being may be compromised. The commenter cited the American Academy of Pediatrics' position that “no amount of time in detention is safe for a child” and referenced a DHS advisory committee report concluding that “detention is generally neither appropriate nor necessary for families” and is “never in the best interest of children.”
另一位评论者表示,如果儿童的担保人和家庭成员因未登记或未随身携带登记证明而被刑事起诉,儿童将被拘留更长时间以等待团聚和释放,许多儿童将无人收留和照顾。
Another commenter stated that if children's sponsors and family members are criminally prosecuted for failing to register or not having proof of registration on their person, children would remain detained even longer awaiting reunification and release, and many would be left without anyone to house and care for them.
另一位评论者表示担心,该规则要求携带登记文件的规定可能被用作针对儿童和家庭采取执法行动的借口。该评论者指出,这可能导致对在执法人员看来可能显得“外国人”的人进行种族定性,评论者将此种做法描述为对“黑人和棕色人种”青少年及其家庭造成不成比例的伤害,无论其公民身份或移民身份如何。该评论者表示,未能登记或携带文件实质上使青少年默认地被刑事化,并加剧了对执法部门的不信任,使青少年和家庭更不愿意向执法部门报案或寻求帮助。该评论者认为,这可能特别影响无人陪伴的青少年,因为他们面临更高的童工剥削和其他虐待风险,而且他们可能更不愿意寻求帮助。另一位评论者表示,临时最终规则(IFR)将伤害那些因肤色或被认为的原籍国而遭受定性对待的儿童,以及贩运或其他犯罪的儿童受害者。
Another commenter expressed concern that the rule's requirement to carry registration documentation could be used as a pretext for law enforcement actions against children and families. The commenter stated this could lead to racial profiling of people who may appear “foreign” to law enforcement officials, a practice the commenter described as disproportionately harming “Black and Brown” youth and their families, regardless of citizenship or immigration status. The commenter said that failure to register or carry documentation essentially criminalizes youth by default and creates additional distrust of law enforcement, making it less likely that young people and families report crimes or seek assistance from law enforcement. The commenter suggested this could particularly impact unaccompanied youth who are at higher risk for child labor exploitation and other types of abuse, as they may be more reluctant to seek help. Another commenter stated that the IFR would harm children subjected to profiling based on skin color or perceived nation of origin, and children who are victims of trafficking or other crimes.
回应:该法规要求,除有限的例外情况外,所有年满14岁或以上并在美国停留30天或以上的外国人必须在30天期限届满前申请登记并接受指纹采集。同样,未满14岁的外国人的父母或法定监护人必须确保这些外国人完成登记。所有此前已登记的外国人,在其年满14周岁生日后的30天内,必须申请重新登记并接受指纹采集。该法规进一步要求,在美国境内年满18岁或以上且须登记的外国人,须随时随身携带其所获发的外国人登记证或外国人登记收据卡。国土安全部(DHS)通过本临时最终规则和最终规则,并未创设这些要求,也未修改国会为外国人设定的义务。
Response: The statute requires, with limited exceptions, all aliens 14 years or older who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted before the expiration of 30 days. Similarly, parents and legal guardians of aliens below the age of 14 must ensure that those aliens are registered. Within 30 days of an alien reaching his or her 14th birthday, all previously registered aliens must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted. The statute further requires aliens 18 years or older in the United States who are required to register must at all times carry with them and have in their personal possession any certificate of alien registration or alien registration receipt card issued to them. DHS, with the IFR and final rule, has not created these requirements or modified the duties established by Congress for aliens.
在本临时最终规则发布之前,长期有效的法规已经规定,凡在美国境内年满14岁且不豁免外国人登记要求的外国人,须在年满14岁后30天内申请重新登记并接受指纹采集,除非指纹采集被豁免。本临时最终规则和最终规则并未改变这些程序,而是通过规定一项适用于所有外国人(无论其身份如何)的一般登记选项及相应的登记证明文件,填补了现行监管体系中的空白。
Longstanding regulations before this IFR already provided that, within 30 days after reaching the age of 14, any alien in the United States who is not exempt from the alien registration requirement must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted, unless fingerprinting is waived. The IFR and final rule do not change those procedures but fill a gap in the regulatory regime by prescribing a general registration option available to all aliens regardless of their status and corresponding evidence as proof of that registration.
有关刑事条款执行情况的评论超出了本次规则制定的范围。本次规则制定不设定民事移民执法或刑事执法的政策或优先事项。14岁以下儿童不会因故意未登记或拒绝登记而面临刑事处罚。参见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第266(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1306(a)。
The comments regarding the enforcement of the criminal provisions are out of scope of this rulemaking. This rulemaking does not set civil immigration or criminal enforcement policies or priorities. Children under 14 years of age face no criminal penalties for a willful failure or refusal to register. See INA sec. 266(a), 8 U.S.C. 1306(a).
评论:有评论指出,该临时最终规则还将对残疾儿童、患有某些心理健康、发育或认知障碍的青少年,以及英语能力有限者(第39261页印刷版)造成不成比例的伤害,并补充说,患有某些心理健康、发育或认知障碍的青少年可能难以理解登记要求适用于他们、难以完成相关要求,或难以理解他们需要随时携带登记证明。另一位评论者表示,无法说或读英语的青少年也可能面临重大困难,特别是由于无人陪伴的青少年来自世界各地,其中许多人讲土著语言和其他不常见的语言,且往往缺乏获得翻译协助的渠道。另一条评论指出,在与执法部门互动时,患有某些心理健康、发育或认知障碍的青少年面临更高的不良后果风险,例如被逮捕、拘留,甚至遭受人身伤害。
Comment: Comments said that the IFR would also disproportionately harm children with disabilities, youth with certain mental health, developmental or cognitive disabilities, and those with ( printed page 39261) limited English proficiency, adding that youth with certain mental health, developmental or cognitive disabilities may face challenges in being able to comprehend that the registration requirement applies to them, complete the requirements, or understand that they need to carry proof of registration with them at all times. Another commenter said that youth who are unable to speak or read English may also face significant challenges, especially since unaccompanied youth come from all around the globe, many of whom speak indigenous languages and other uncommon languages and often lack access to interpretation assistance. Another comment stated that faced with law enforcement interactions, youth with certain mental health, developmental, or cognitive disabilities are at higher risk of worse outcomes, such as arrest, detention, or even physical harm.
评论者还指出,技术获取有限或在其他方面需要支持才能遵守该要求的儿童,可能会向教师和其他学校工作人员寻求指导,从而加重了教育系统的负担。
Commenters also stated that children with limited technology access or who otherwise need support to comply with the requirement may seek guidance from teachers and other school personnel, adding burdens to the education system.
另一位评论者表示,登记要求将使那些可能无法获得其移民文件或没有机会咨询律师的生活状况下的儿童受到不利影响。该评论者补充说,儿童可能没有任何理解英语的成年人,更不用说理解登记所需表格的复杂性质了。
Another commenter said that the registration requirement would prejudice children in living situations where they may not have access to their immigration paperwork or have not had the opportunity to speak to a lawyer. The commenter added that children may not have an adult who understands English, let alone the complex nature of the form required for registration.
回应:该法规规定,除有限的例外情况外,所有年满14周岁或以上并在美国停留30天或以上的外国人必须在30天届满前申请登记并接受指纹采集。见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条,8 U.S.C. 1302。同样,14岁以下外国人的父母或法定监护人必须确保这些外国人进行登记。所有此前已登记的外国人,在年满14周岁生日后的30天内,必须申请重新登记并接受指纹采集。见INA第262(b)条,8 U.S.C. 1302(b)。美国国土安全部(DHS)通过临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则,并未改变国会为外国人设定的要求。该临时最终规则和本规则的范围有限,仅为外国人指定了一份新的登记表格,以遵守法定的登记和指纹采集要求。
Response: The statute provides that, with limited exceptions, all aliens 14 years or older who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted before the expiration of 30 days. See INA sec. 262, 8 U.S.C. 1302. Similarly, parents and legal guardians of aliens below the age of 14 must ensure that those aliens are registered. Within 30 days of an alien reaching his or her 14th birthday, all previously registered aliens must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted. See INA sec. 262(b), 8 U.S.C. 1302(b). DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not changed the requirements established by Congress for aliens. The IFR and this rule are limited in scope, designating a new registration form for aliens to comply with the statutory registration and fingerprinting requirements.
国土安全部承认儿童可能遇到的挑战,特别是残疾儿童、有心理健康困难的儿童、英语能力有限的儿童,或基于社会经济状况处境不利的儿童。这些困难普遍适用于国会制定的任何法定义务,而长期存在的外国人登记要求在这方面并无特殊之处。为解决有关残疾和无障碍问题的关切,国土安全部根据现行法律提供了各种便利措施。国土安全部和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的电子和信息技术(IT)系统符合,并且在某些方面可能超过《康复法》第508条(29 U.S.C. 794d)及相关指南的要求。国土安全部和美国公民及移民服务局致力于以与非残疾人士相当的方式,使公众信息和数据可为残疾人士所获取和使用。美国公民及移民服务局依照《康复法》第504条提供便利措施。[17]
DHS acknowledges the challenges that children may encounter, in particular children with disabilities, mental health difficulties, limited English proficiency, or based on socio-economic situation. These difficulties apply generally to any statutorily imposed obligations codified by Congress, and the longstanding alien registration requirement does not distinguish itself in this manner. To address concerns about disability and access issues, DHS provides various accommodations, in accordance with current laws. DHS and USCIS electronic and information technology (IT) systems meet and in some respects may exceed the requirement of section 508 of the Rehabilitation Act (29 U.S.C. 794d) and related guidance. DHS and USCIS are committed to making the public information and data accessible and usable by individuals with disabilities in a manner that is comparable with individuals who do not have disabilities. USCIS provides accommodations consistent with section 504 of the Rehabilitation Act.[17]
美国公民及移民服务局还建立了一个专门网站,提供有关外国人登记要求(ARR)的信息,以及一个帮助外国人确定其是否必须登记的ARR工具。[18]该工具向外国人提出一系列问题,并根据外国人的回答,可帮助其确定是否必须登记。
USCIS also established a dedicated website with information on the Alien Registration Requirement (ARR) and an ARR Tool that help aliens determine if they must register.[18] The tool poses a series of questions to aliens and based on an alien's responses, may help an alien determine if they must register.
评论:一位评论者表示担忧,临时最终规则给通常缺乏独立收入来源的无人陪伴儿童造成经济负担。该评论者指出,儿童可能会感到被迫工作以遵守临时最终规则的要求,但由于缺乏工作许可或需要上学,许多儿童无法工作。该评论者列举了几项将使儿童承受负担的费用,包括申请费、技术获取费用、前往生物识别信息采集预约地点的交通费、文件费用、指纹采集费用、口译服务费用以及法律咨询费用。该评论者得出结论,认为这些经济障碍将阻止许多儿童遵守相关要求,从而可能使他们面临刑事责任。
Comment: A commenter expressed concern that the IFR creates a financial burden on unaccompanied children who typically lack independent income sources. The commenter stated that children might feel compelled to work to comply with the IFR, though many cannot, due to lack of work authorization or school attendance. The commenter identified several costs that would burden children, including application fees, technology access, transportation to biometrics appointments, document costs, fingerprinting fees, interpretation services, and legal consultation expenses. The commenter concluded that these financial barriers would prevent many children from complying with the requirements, potentially subjecting them to criminal liability.
回应:该法规要求,除有限的例外情况外,所有年满14周岁或以上并在美国停留30天或以上的外国人必须在30天届满前申请登记并接受指纹采集。同样,14岁以下外国人的父母或法定监护人必须确保这些外国人进行登记。所有此前已登记的外国人,在年满14周岁生日后的30天内,必须申请重新登记并接受指纹采集。
Response: The statute requires, with limited exceptions, that all aliens 14 years or older who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted before the expiration of 30 days. Similarly, parents and legal guardians of aliens below the age of 14 must ensure that those aliens are registered. Within 30 days of an alien reaching his or her 14th birthday, all previously registered aliens must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted.
国土安全部通过临时最终规则和最终规则,并未修改国会为外国人设定的义务。在临时最终规则出台之前,相关法规已经规定,任何不属于外国人登记要求豁免对象的在美外国人,在年满14周岁后的30天内必须申请重新登记并接受指纹采集,除非指纹采集被豁免。临时最终规则和最终规则并未改变这些程序,而是通过为外国人新增一项遵守现有法定登记要求的选项,填补了法规中的空白。国土安全部指出,部分无人陪伴儿童由于已通过I-862表格(出庭通知书)被置于遣返程序中,因而已经完成登记。
DHS, with the IFR and final rule, has not modified the duties established by Congress for aliens. Before the IFR, regulations already provided that within 30 days after reaching the age of 14, any alien in the United States who is not exempt from the alien registration requirement must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted, unless fingerprinting is waived. The IFR and final rule do not change those procedures but fills a gap in the regulation by adding an option for aliens to comply with the existing statutory registration requirements. DHS notes that some unaccompanied alien children will already be registered by virtue of having been placed in removal proceedings via the Form I-862, Notice to Appear.
c. 残疾人士
c. People With Disabilities
评论:评论者指出,临时最终规则(IFR)将对残疾人造成不成比例的伤害。同样,评论者表示,患有精神、发育或健康相关残疾的个人可能无法可靠地随身携带临时最终规则所述的文件。该评论者写道,如果没有家庭成员或其他人的帮助,这些人将面临更高的执法行动、监禁和被驱逐出美国的风险,而无法获得有意义的正当程序保障。
Comment: Commenters stated that the IFR would disproportionately harm people with disabilities. Similarly, commenters stated that individuals with mental, developmental, or health-related disabilities may be unable to reliably carry documentation as described in the IFR. The commenter wrote that without available assistance from family members or others, these individuals would face increased risk of law enforcement actions, incarceration, and removal from the United States without meaningful due process.
同样,评论者表示,临时最终规则将阻碍担保人和家庭成员为患有精神残疾的非合格被申请人计划(Non-Qualified Respondent Program)参与者提供支持。该评论者指出,移民法官在保释听证会上通常要求将患有精神残疾的个人释放交由家庭成员照管,以确保其参与法律程序。该评论者称,临时最终规则将因担心被申请人未登记可能产生的后果而使家庭成员不愿担任担保人。该评论者表示,这将延长患有精神残疾个人的拘留时间,他们可能因此失去支持系统,并无法有意义地参与移民程序。
Similarly, commenters stated that the IFR would deter sponsors and family members from providing support for Non-Qualified Respondent Program participants with mental disabilities. The commenter noted that immigration judges often require individuals with mental disabilities to be released into family members' care during bond hearings, ensuring their participation in legal proceedings. According to the commenter, the IFR would discourage family members from acting as sponsors due to concerns about potential consequences if the respondent fails to register. The commenter said this would prolong detention of individuals with mental disabilities, who may lose their support system and be unable to meaningfully participate in immigration proceedings.
同样,评论者表示,残疾人出于多种原因将无法有意义地获得登记程序的途径。评论者写道,患有某些精神健康、发育或认知障碍的人可能无法确定登记要求是否适用于他们。评论者表示,此外,(印刷页第39262页)登记需要接入互联网、使用计算机或智能手机以及拥有电子邮件地址,这对于各类残疾人士可能存在困难甚至无法实现,包括失明或低视力人士、因行动障碍而影响手部灵活性或使用技术能力的人士,以及智力或认知障碍人士。评论者补充说,登记程序还要求具备认知能力以遵循创建myUSCIS账户并完成登记的多步骤流程,包括双重身份验证流程。最后,一位评论者表示,登记者必须具备认知能力,在仅有极少指引的情况下回答一长串问题,否则将面临民事和刑事处罚的风险。
Similarly, commenters stated that people with disabilities would not have meaningful access to the registration process for several reasons. The commenters wrote that people with certain mental health, developmental, or cognitive disabilities may be unable to determine if the registration requirement applies to them. The commenters stated that additionally, ( printed page 39262) registration requires access to the internet, a computer or smartphone, and an email address, which may be difficult or impossible for people with various disabilities, including those who are blind or have low vision, those with mobility issues affecting manual dexterity or ability to navigate technology, and those with intellectual or cognitive disabilities. Commenters added that the registration process also requires cognitive capacity to follow a multi-step process for creating an account and registering for myUSCIS, including a two-factor authentication process. Finally, a commenter said that registrants must have the cognitive capacity to answer a long list of questions with minimal instructions or risk civil and criminal penalties.
一位评论者表示,要求残疾人遵守登记要求将给他们带来“不切实际且重大的负担”,其理由是,居住在社区中有健康需求的客户往往没有稳定的住所。具体而言,该评论者指出,由于这些人从一个过渡性住所辗转至另一个,文件经常遗失,而且财物也常常被盗。
A commenter said that expecting individuals with disabilities to comply with the registration requirement would place “an unrealistic and significant burden on them,” reasoning that clients with health needs living in the community are frequently without stable housing. Specifically, the commenter noted that as these individuals move from one transitional housing situation to another, papers are frequently lost, and, often, possessions are stolen.
回应:国土安全部(DHS)通过临时最终规则和本最终规则,并未改变国会为外国人设定的要求。临时最终规则和本规则的适用范围有限,仅为外国人指定了一种新的登记表格,以履行法定的登记和指纹采集要求。
Response: DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not changed the requirements established by Congress for aliens. The IFR and this rule are limited in scope, designating a new registration form for aliens to comply with the statutory registration and fingerprinting requirements.
在临时最终规则发布之前,美国境内大多数外国人已经履行了登记要求。数十年来,这些外国人已通过多种途径满足了该要求。然而,临时最终规则和本最终规则填补了监管体系中的一项空白,规定了一种适用于所有外国人的通用登记选项,无论其身份如何,并提供相应的证明作为登记证据。国土安全部认为,这一新选项可能改善某些外国人群体的登记结果。
Most aliens in the United States already complied with the registration requirement prior to the publication of the IFR. For many decades, these aliens have satisfied the requirement through a variety of pathways. However, the IFR and this final rule fills a gap in the regulatory regime by prescribing a general registration option available to all aliens regardless of their status and corresponding evidence as proof of that registration. DHS believes that this new option may improve registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens.
国土安全部(DHS)和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)致力于使电子和信息技术便于残疾人使用。myUSCIS网站及其技术经过设计,以确保残疾人能够以与非残疾人相当的方式获取和使用信息及数据。
DHS and USCIS are committed to making electronic and information technologies accessible to individuals with disabilities. The myUSCIS site and its technology are designed to ensure individuals with disabilities can access and use information and data in a way that is comparable to those without disabilities.
G-325R表格符合《康复法》第508条的规定;此外,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)依据《康复法》第504条提供合理便利。[19] 作为便利措施的一部分,我们鼓励个人在需要外国人登记方面的帮助时与USCIS联络中心联系。
The Form G-325R complies with section 508 of the Rehabilitation Act; additionally, USCIS provides reasonable accommodations in accordance with section 504 of the Rehabilitation Act.[19] As part of the accommodation, we encourage individuals to reach out to the USCIS Contact Center if they require help with alien registration.
国土安全部(DHS)指出,本规则制定并未改变如下要求:所有在美国境内年满18周岁且被要求登记的外国人,必须随时携带并亲自持有向其签发的外国人登记证或外国人登记收据卡。这些是《移民与国籍法》(INA)第264(e)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(e)项下的法定要求。
DHS notes that this rulemaking has not changed the requirement that all aliens 18 years or older in the United States who are required to register must at all times carry with them and have in their personal possession any certificate of alien registration or alien registration receipt card issued to them. These are statutory requirements under section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e).
丁. 犯罪和虐待行为的受害者
d. Victims of Crime and Abuse
意见:多位评论者表示,该临时最终规则(IFR)削弱并威胁了人口贩卖、家庭暴力和性暴力受害者的安全,而执法工作依赖社区合作以维护公共安全,当犯罪受害者害怕举报犯罪或与警方交谈时,这种合作将变得困难。一位评论者强调,移民女性,特别是“无证件”女性,极易遭受家庭暴力、性侵犯、人口贩卖和其他暴力犯罪的侵害。该评论者解释说,这些受害者在寻求安全方面面临重大障碍,因为施害者(通常是美国公民)利用受害者的移民身份来控制她们,并威胁如果她们寻求帮助将升级暴力行为。该评论者认为,该临时最终规则中扩大的登记要求在移民社区中造成了不必要的恐惧氛围,从而破坏了公共安全,因为犯罪受害者会因担心受到处罚、拘留或驱逐出境而避免联系警方。该评论者表示,该临时最终规则实际上使《反对妇女暴力法》(VAWA)为移民人口贩卖和家庭/性暴力受害者提供的现行法律保护形同虚设。该评论者写道,该临时最终规则使正在申请保护且已经与政府当局合作的移民受害者立即面临被驱逐出境或刑事起诉的风险,从而消除了移民暴力犯罪受害者寻求帮助的动力。该评论者进一步指出,新的登记程序为施虐伴侣伤害受害者提供了额外手段,他们将其描述为给移民幸存者设置了一个不可逾越的安全障碍。
Comment: Commenters stated that the IFR undermines and threatens the safety of victims of human trafficking and domestic and sexual violence, and law enforcement depends on community cooperation to maintain public safety, which becomes difficult when crime victims are afraid to report crimes or speak to police. A commenter emphasized that immigrant women, particularly those who are “undocumented,” are extremely vulnerable to domestic violence, sexual assault, human trafficking, and other violent crimes. The commenter explained that these victims face significant barriers to seeking safety because perpetrators, often U.S. citizens, exploit the victims' immigration status to control them and threaten escalated violence if they seek help. The commenter reasoned that the expanded registration requirements in the IFR create an unnecessary climate of fear in immigrant communities that undermines public safety, as crime victims would avoid contacting police due to fears of penalties, detention, or deportation. The commenter stated that the IFR effectively renders current Violence Against Women Act (VAWA) legal protections for immigrant victims of human trafficking and domestic/sexual violence meaningless. The commenter wrote that the IFR puts immigrant victims with pending applications for protection and those already working with government authorities at immediate risk for deportation or criminal prosecution, removing incentives for immigrant victims of violent crime to seek help. The commenter further noted that the new registration process creates an additional tool for abusive partners to harm victims by establishing what they described as an impossible barrier to safety for immigrant survivors.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则将导致人们在有机会获得可靠法律咨询之前就成为移民及海关执法局(ICE)的打击目标,其中包括人口贩卖幸存者,以及因遭受侵害而被授予特殊身份的青少年和儿童。该评论者写道,该临时最终规则将通过逮捕这些群体并强迫其出示证件而使他们再次遭受创伤。一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则引入了新的要求,这将阻碍幸存者站出来举报,增加再次遭受创伤的风险,并使施虐者和犯罪者更容易维持对受害者的控制。一位评论者特别关注该规则对已经获得或正在通过VAWA、T签证或U签证寻求人道主义保护的虐待幸存者的影响。
A commenter stated that the IFR would cause people to be targeted by ICE before they have had a chance to get trustworthy legal advice, including individuals who have survived human trafficking, and adolescents and young children who have been granted specialized status due to their victimization. The commenter wrote that the IFR would retraumatize these groups by arresting them and forcing them to show documentation. A commenter said that the IFR introduces new requirements that would deter survivors from coming forward, increase the risk of re-traumatization, and make it easier for abusers and perpetrators to maintain control over their victims. A commenter raised specific concerns regarding the impact on survivors of abuse who have received or are seeking humanitarian protection through VAWA, T, or U visas.
一个工会表示,该临时最终规则将遏制对严重工作场所违法行为的举报,并损害U类和T类非移民身份“申请人”的利益,这违反了《人口贩卖和暴力受害者保护法》(VTVPA)的国会立法意图。该评论者详细阐述了该临时最终规则对U签证和T签证申请人的各种影响,并表示国土安全部未对此予以考虑。该评论者评论了该临时最终规则中不必要且不合理的文书负担、隐私保护方面缺乏明确性,以及其所称的“不良政策”,认为该规则破坏了《人口贩卖和暴力受害者保护法》所确立的受害者和证人保护机制,从而引发不利的移民后果,对外国个体工人造成“无法容忍的”虐待,并损害所有美国工人的利益。
A union said that the IFR will chill reporting of serious workplace violations and harm “applicants” for U and T nonimmigrant status, in violation of congressional intent of the Victims of Trafficking and Violence Protection Act (VTVPA). The commenter elaborated, discussing various implications of the IFR for U and T visa applicants that the commenter said DHS did not consider. The commenter remarked on unnecessary and unjustified paperwork burdens, a lack of clarity around privacy protections, and “bad policy” set forth in the IFR that undoes the victim and witness protection scheme set forth in the VTVPA, triggering adverse immigration consequences, “intolerable” abuses against individual alien workers, and harms to all U.S. workers.
一位评论者对该临时最终规则在登记要求方面缺乏隐私和保密保护表示担忧。该评论者认为,这一缺陷可能使施虐者利用幸存者的恐惧心理,阻止其获得移民系统的帮助。多位评论者指出,施虐者可能通过干扰登记程序、阻止获取必要技术、妨碍生物特征信息采集预约,或阻碍获得法律援助等方式,操纵、控制、胁迫和恐吓幸存者。该评论者报告称,其所在机构已观察到客户在判断自己是否需要登记方面存在困难,并对登记程序感到恐惧和犹豫。
A commenter expressed concern about the IFR's lack of privacy and confidentiality protections associated with the registration requirement. According to the commenter, this deficiency could enable abusers to leverage survivors' fears to prevent their access to the immigration system. Multiple commenters remarked that abusers might manipulate, control, coerce, and intimidate survivors by interfering with the registration process, preventing access to necessary technology, obstructing biometrics appointments, or hindering access to legal assistance. The commenter reported that their organization has observed clients struggling to determine whether they need to register and experiencing fear and hesitancy around the registration process.
一些评论者同样描述了幸存者在提交移民福利申请时所面临的现有障碍（印刷页39263），例如：语言障碍；表格的篇幅和复杂性；收集必要文件和证据以支持其主张的能力；以及获取政府办公室和法律、社区及金融服务方面的地理障碍。评论者认为，临时最终规则（IFR）将加剧这一群体已经面临的障碍、恐惧和困惑。另一位评论者补充说，登记范围广泛且对谁必须登记缺乏明确界定，可能无意中为欺诈行为者提供可乘之机，利用弱势群体。该评论者指出，尽管美国国土安全部（DHS）正在持续开展打击移民服务诈骗的行动，但在缺乏明确指导的情况下，个人可能会轻信承诺协助登记程序的骗局，从而导致经济损失并进一步传播错误信息。
A few commenters similarly described the existing barriers survivors ( printed page 39263) experience while submitting requests for immigration benefits, such as: language access; the length and complexity of the forms; ability to gather the necessary documents and evidence to support their claims; and geographical barriers to accessing government offices and legal, community, and financial services. The commenters suggested that the IFR would exacerbate the barriers, fear, and confusion this population already faces. Another commenter added that the broad scope and lack of clear delineation regarding who must register may inadvertently create opportunities for fraudulent actors to exploit vulnerable populations. The commenter stated that despite DHS's ongoing initiatives to combat immigration services scams, in the absence of explicit guidance, individuals may fall prey to scams promising assistance with the registration process, leading to financial loss and further misinformation.
多位评论者指出，家庭暴力或性暴力的施害者可能操纵登记程序，错误地为幸存者登记，或干扰幸存者完成登记的能力，包括阻止其参加生物识别信息采集预约，从而使幸存者面临被定罪和执法的风险。其他评论者指出，施虐者还可能阻止幸存者随身携带登记证明，使其面临依据《移民与国籍法》（INA）第264(e)条，即8 U.S.C. 1304(e)条款的执法行动的风险。一位评论者建议，任何登记程序都应为幸存者提供灵活性，以纠正不一致之处，并在依据《移民与国籍法》第264(a)条，即8 U.S.C. 1306(a)条款采取执法行动之前，考虑受害经历可能对其合规性产生的影响。
Multiple commenters remarked that domestic or sexual violence perpetrators could manipulate the registration process by erroneously registering survivors or interfering with a survivor's ability to complete registration, including attending biometrics appointments, thus exposing them to criminalization and enforcement. Other commenters noted that abusers might also prevent survivors from carrying proof of registration, exposing them to enforcement under section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e). A commenter recommended that any registration process provide flexibility for survivors to correct inconsistencies and consider how victimization may impact compliance before conducting enforcement actions based on section 264(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1306(a).
一位评论者指出，移民相关的虐待是施虐者和犯罪者用来维持对受害者权力和控制的常见手段，并援引了全国家庭与性暴力中心（National Center for Domestic and Sexual Violence）提出的“移民权力与控制之轮”。该评论者解释说，幸存者往往在移民身份、住房、交通、收入和获取技术等方面依赖施虐的伴侣或雇主，从而形成施虐者有意维持的依赖关系，以孤立和控制幸存者。该评论者指出，在当今数字环境下，这些脆弱性被进一步加剧，并援引研究数据称，80%的跟踪骚扰受害者报告称曾通过技术手段被跟踪，其中超过三分之一的受害者是被现任或前任亲密伴侣跟踪的。该评论者表示，手机、应用程序和数字工具已成为监视、骚扰以及干扰幸存者逃离或寻求帮助的工具。该评论者认为，IFR的登记要求可能被施虐者操纵，例如阻断互联网访问、扣留必要文件、阻止幸存者参加生物识别信息采集预约，或故意误导幸存者有关合规要求的信息，使其在不知情的情况下未能履行合规义务。该评论者写道，如果没有专门针对幸存者的保障措施，登记程序可能成为胁迫的又一工具，使幸存者进一步面临被驱逐或受到伤害的风险。该评论者建议，如果美国国土安全部不撤销该IFR，则应纳入明确条款，允许幸存者纠正因施虐者造成的不一致之处、解释延误或空缺情况，并避免因遭受虐待而导致的不合规行为受到处罚。该评论者强调，任何幸存者都不应因曾经伤害过他们的人实施的胁迫、恐吓或操纵而承担移民后果。
A commenter noted that immigration-related abuse is a common tactic used by abusers and perpetrators of crime to maintain power and control over victims, citing the National Center for Domestic and Sexual Violence's “Immigrant Power and Control Wheel.” The commenter explained that survivors often depend on abusive partners or employers for their immigration status, housing, transportation, income, and access to technology, creating dependencies that abusers intentionally maintain to isolate and control survivors. The commenter stated that these vulnerabilities are compounded in today's digital environment, citing research indicating that 80 percent of stalking victims report being stalked using technology, with over one-third targeted by current or former intimate partners. The commenter said that phones, apps, and digital tools have become weapons for monitoring, harassing, and interfering with survivors' efforts to escape or seek help. The commenter reasoned that the IFR's registration requirements could be manipulated by abusers who might block internet access, withhold necessary documents, prevent survivors from attending biometrics appointments, or deliberately mislead survivors about compliance requirements, causing them to unknowingly fall out of compliance. The commenter wrote that without survivor-specific safeguards, the registration process could become another tool of coercion, placing survivors at further risk of removal or harm. The commenter recommended that if DHS does not rescind the IFR, it should include clear provisions allowing survivors to correct abuser-generated inconsistencies, explain delays or gaps, and avoid penalties for noncompliance resulting from abuse. The commenter emphasized that no survivor should face immigration consequences because of coercion, fear, or manipulation by someone who has already caused them harm.
一位评论者还指出，要求随时携带登记证明的规定将对暴力幸存者造成不成比例的伤害，这些幸存者可能正在逃离虐待，或其证件被施虐者控制，以此作为维持权力和控制的一种手段。该评论者敦促美国国土安全部在依据8 U.S.C. 1304(e)条款采取执法行动时，考虑诸如紧急情况、受害经历和健康状况等因素。
A commenter also stated that the requirement to possess proof of registration at all times would disproportionately harm survivors of violence who may be fleeing abuse or whose abusers control their documentation as a means of maintaining power and control. The commenter urged DHS to consider factors such as emergencies, victimization, and health conditions in its enforcement actions related to 8 U.S.C. 1304(e).
答复：美国国土安全部指出，IFR及本规则并未改变国会所确立的登记要求。本规则制定了一项适用于所有未登记外国人的通用登记选项，无论其移民身份如何，旨在改善特定外国人群体的登记结果。
Response: DHS notes the IFR and this rule have not changed the registration requirements established by Congress. This rulemaking establishes a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status to improve registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens.
《移民与国籍法》(INA)要求,除有限的例外情形外,大多数在美国停留30天或以上的外国人必须申请登记和捺指纹。在临时最终规则(IFR)发布之前,大多数外国人已经遵守了登记要求,但是,临时最终规则和本最终规则通过为未登记的外国人规定一种登记表格,填补了监管体系中的一个空白。
The INA requires that, with limited exceptions, most aliens in the United States who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and fingerprinting. Prior to the IFR, most aliens already complied with the registration requirements, however, the IFR and this final rule fill a gap in the regulatory regime by prescribing a registration form for unregistered aliens.
临时最终规则和本最终规则并未改变与有资格获得并已获得基于受害者的移民救济的外国人相关的任何现行程序或流程(具体而言,包括《妇女暴力防治法》(VAWA)自行申请人以及T类和U类非移民身份的申请人、请愿人和获得者)。此外,临时最终规则和本最终规则也未改变美国国土安全部(DHS)各机构为防止其收集、使用或保存的个人身份信息遭到未经授权披露而采取的程序或做法。
The IFR and this final rule have not changed any current procedures or processes related to aliens who are eligible for and recipients of victim-based immigration relief (specifically, VAWA self-petitioners as well as applicants and petitioners for, and recipients of, T and U nonimmigrant status). Also, the IFR and this final rule have not changed the procedures or practices of DHS agencies to protect against the unauthorized disclosure of personally identifiable information that it collects, uses, or maintains.
美国国土安全部指出,如果已登记的外国人未随身携带其登记证明,国土安全部各机构可以访问国土安全部数据库,以确认该外国人是否满足登记要求。
DHS notes that if a registered alien does not have immediate possession of his or her evidence of registration, DHS agencies have access to DHS databases to confirm whether an alien satisfies the registration requirement.
美国国土安全部认识到移民程序可能较为复杂,申请人,包括登记人,仍可能面临成为诈骗或欺诈受害者的风险。国土安全部鼓励申请人利用美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)网站上的信息,以避免成为常见诈骗、欺诈和不当行为的受害者。[20]
DHS recognizes that the immigration processes can be complex and that requestors, including registrants, may still be at risk of becoming victims of scams or fraud. DHS encourages requestors to use the information on the USCIS website to avoid becoming victims of common scams, fraud, and misconduct.[20]
e. 其他人群
e. Other Populations
评论意见:多位评论者指出,英语能力有限或获取技术或金融服务存在障碍的个人,将因无法以电子方式提交G-325R表格而面临惩罚性后果。另一位评论者还表示,临时最终规则完全没有提及其对英语能力有限或存在其他语言障碍的“非公民”的影响,并指出该临时最终规则未对登记表格或说明的翻译作出任何安排。
Comment: Several commenters stated that individuals with limited English proficiency or limited access to technology or financial access barriers would be exposed to punitive ramifications due to inability to file the Form G-325R electronically. Another commenter also said that the IFR makes no mention of its impact on “noncitizens” with limited English proficiency or other language barriers, and noted that the IFR does not account for any translation of the registration forms or instructions.
一位评论者表示担忧,英语能力有限的人可能在无需登记的情况下无意中进行了登记,从而可能引发错误的移民执法行动。其他评论者表示,该规则未提及其对英语能力有限或存在其他语言障碍的“非公民”的影响,也未对G-325R表格或该规则本身的翻译作出任何安排。
One of the commenters voiced concern that those with limited English proficiency might inadvertently register when not required to do so, potentially triggering erroneous immigration enforcement actions. Other commenters stated that the rule fails to mention its impact on “noncitizens” with limited English proficiency or other language barriers, nor does it account for any translation of Form G-325R or the rule itself.
回应:美国国土安全部指出,本规则制定并未改变如下要求,即所有在美国境内年满18岁且须登记的外国人,必须随时携带并在其人身上持有向其签发的任何外国人登记证或外国人登记收据卡。这些是《移民与国籍法》第264(e)条,8 U.S.C. 1304(e)规定的法定要求。此外,本规则制定并未施加(印刷页第39264页)任何不同于《移民与国籍法》中已有义务的新登记或捺指纹义务。
Response: DHS notes that this rulemaking has not changed the requirement that all aliens 18 years or older in the United States who are required to register must at all times carry with them and have in their personal possession any certificate of alien registration or alien registration receipt card issued to them. These are statutory requirements under section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e). In addition, this rulemaking does impose ( printed page 39264) any new registration or fingerprinting obligations separate from the obligations already contained in the INA.
美国国土安全部承认,登记人因须遵守法定登记要求而需承担一定成本。这些成本包括但不限于提交生物特征信息所需的时间负担,以及前往申请支持中心(ASC)所产生的交通费用。鉴于收到的评论意见,本序言第六部分“法定和监管要求”一节中载有与本规则相关成本的更详细分类说明。
DHS acknowledges there are costs to registrants associated with the statutory requirements to register. These costs include, but are not limited to, time burden to submit biometrics, and travel costs to go to an ASC. As a result of comments received, a more detailed breakdown of the costs associated with this rule is in section VI, the Statutory and Regulatory Requirements section of this preamble.
美国公民及移民服务局还专门建立了一个网站,提供有关外国人登记要求(ARR)的信息,以及一个外国人登记要求工具,可帮助外国人判断其是否必须登记。[21] 美国国土安全部还指出,G-325R表格是一种数字表格,可通过互联网浏览器或个人手机轻松访问。此外,公共图书馆以及其他私人和公共机构也可以提供互联网接入服务。
USCIS also established a dedicated website with information on the ARR and an ARR Tool that may help aliens determine whether they must register.[21] DHS also notes Form G-325R is a digital form that can be easily accessed through an internet browser or a person's cell phone. Additionally, public libraries and other private and public institutions can provide access to the internet.
美国国土安全部指出,美国公民及移民服务局的移民表格均以英文提供,也必须以英文提交。[22] 美国公民及移民服务局还提供G-325R表格的PDF版本,供外国人在填写表格前查阅。[23] 外国人也可以借助口译人员的协助来阅读新登记表格上的说明和问题。如果外国人使用口译人员,其在申请登记时必须提供该口译人员的联系信息,并上传该口译人员的认证声明和签名。
DHS notes that USCIS' immigration forms are offered and must be submitted in English.[22] USCIS also provides a PDF copy of Form G-325R for aliens to access before completing the form.[23] An alien may also use the assistance of an interpreter for reading the instructions and questions on the new registration form. If the alien uses an interpreter, he or she must provide the contact information of that interpreter and upload the interpreter's certification and signature when applying for registration.
3. 对外国人法律服务的影响
3. Impact on Aliens' Legal Services
评论意见:有评论者表示,作为向难民和流离失所者提供全面法律服务的机构,该临时最终规则(IFR)将对其客户群体产生不利影响,同时也会耗尽其资源,从而阻碍其扩大外国人和流离失所者获得保护、持久安全和正当程序的使命。另一位评论者表示,围绕该临时最终规则产生的困惑将导致他们耗费更多资源去调查哪些客户需要进行登记。一位评论者指出,其所在机构已收到众多社区成员询问是否需要登记的问询,这给其资源造成压力,而这些资源本可以更好地用于帮助人们申请移民救济。
Comment: Commenters stated that as providers of comprehensive legal services to refugees and displaced persons, the IFR would adversely impact their client communities as well as stretch their resources, hindering their mission to expand access to protection, lasting safety, and due process for aliens and displaced persons. Another commenter stated that confusion surrounding the IFR would cause them to expend more resources to investigate which of their clients would require registration. A commenter remarked that their organization has been receiving numerous inquiries from community members asking whether they need to register or not, creating a strain on their resources that could be better utilized helping people to apply for immigration relief.
一位评论者表示,他们将需要对接触到的每一个人进行筛查,这可能会改变接案流程、风险评估、咨询建议及培训方式,以确定登记要求对客户及其家庭成员的适用性。该评论者指出,提供称职的法律建议需要评估该临时最终规则所涉及的宪法问题,这就需要对每个案件进行专家审查和监督。该评论者表示,这些变化将需要额外的员工工时,从而使资源从服务更多客户上转移。该评论者预计客户将面临罚款和刑事处罚,这将需要法律咨询和快速应对审查。此外,该评论者对潜在的“公证人”(notario)虚假陈述和滥用行为表示担忧,这可能导致在错误的登记建议下行事的人员受到欺诈。该评论者最后指出,这将显著降低其为通常所服务的移民和寻求庇护者提供服务及争取永久性救济的能力。一位评论者表示,由于全国各地的服务提供者因资金削减和服务需求增加而不堪重负,他们及其他非营利组织不太可能有能力协助该规则下所需的登记工作,而这项登记对许多英语能力有限的外国人而言是必需的,这引发了一系列公平性方面的担忧。
A commenter said that they would need to screen every individual they encounter, potentially changing intake processes, risk assessment, advisal, and training to determine registration applicability for clients and their family members. The commenter noted that providing competent legal advice would require assessing the IFR's constitutional implications, necessitating expert review and supervision for each case. The commenter remarked that these changes would require additional staff hours, diverting resources from serving more clients. The commenter anticipated clients facing fines and criminal penalties that would require legal advice and rapid response review. Additionally, the commenter expressed concern about potential “notario” misrepresentation and abuse, which could defraud individuals acting under faulty registration advice. The commenter concluded this would significantly reduce their capacity to provide services and pursue permanent relief for immigrants and asylum seekers they would normally serve. A commenter said that since providers are overwhelmed across the country due to funding cuts and increased demand for services, it is unlikely that they and other nonprofit organizations would have capacity to assist with registration under the rule, which is needed for many aliens with limited English proficiency, raising a host of fairness concerns.
一位评论者同样写道,该临时最终规则给服务移民的机构带来了沉重的行政、时间和财务负担,因为它们必须对社区成员进行教育,并帮助其应对复杂的合规要求。该评论者将确定登记资格描述为“极其耗时”,且对于可能缺乏必要记录的个人而言尤为困难。该评论者将该临时最终规则置于诸多其他移民政策变化的背景下进行考量,援引了据称因撤销某些假释许可的行动而造成的困惑。该评论者表示,如果政府在未解决含糊不清之处的情况下实时更改身份认定,那么适当的通知便无从谈起,并由此得出结论:该临时最终规则的要求可能会在未充分考虑其对移民社区及支持机构影响的情况下发生变化。
A commenter similarly wrote that the IFR places heavy administrative, time, and financial burdens on organizations serving immigrants, as they must educate community members and help navigate complex compliance requirements. The commenter described determining registration eligibility as “extremely time-consuming” and difficult for individuals who may lack necessary records. The commenter contextualized the IFR within numerous other immigration policy changes, citing alleged confusion created by actions revoking certain grants of parole. The commenter stated that proper notice is impossible if the Administration changes status determinations in real time without resolving ambiguities, concluding that the IFR's requirements could change without due consideration of impacts on immigrant communities and supporting organizations.
一位评论者表示担忧,该临时最终规则(IFR)将造成繁重负担,并限制其履行服务纽约外国人社区使命的能力。该评论者表示,移民社区对该规则存在广泛的困惑,指出具有各种移民身份的个人就该规则对其具体情况的适用性提出了疑问。该评论者指出,该临时最终规则的复杂性和快速实施造成了易受虚假信息影响的脆弱性。据该评论者称,解决这种困惑需要与其成员进行广泛的一对一咨询,这超出了其组织的能力范围。该评论者表示,其法律团队的经验表明,确定登记要求既复杂,理解和遵守该规则也存在重大障碍。例如,该评论者解释说,确定外国人此前是否已登记,需要知道该外国人是否提交过I-485表格或I-765表格,以及该申请的结果。该评论者进一步指出,如果客户此前曾向美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)提交过大量文件,而这些文件根据相关法规不构成登记,则客户完全有理由对自己是否已登记感到困惑。该评论者表示,对于某些外国人而言,核实其登记状态并获取其现在必须依法随身携带的文件的唯一途径,将是提出信息自由法(FOIA)请求,而这是一个多步骤的程序,可能需要数月时间,耗费其组织的员工时间,并需要持续保持联系。其他评论者也指出,由于对谁需要完成新登记程序缺乏明确说明,该临时最终规则将导致信息自由法请求数量增加,并给移民律师提交信息自由法请求带来负担。
A commenter voiced concern that the IFR would be burdensome and limit their ability to fulfill their mission of serving alien communities in New York. The commenter expressed that there is widespread confusion about the rule among immigrant communities, stating that individuals with various immigration statuses have raised questions about the rule's applicability to their particular situations. The commenter indicated that the complexity and rapid implementation of the IFR have created vulnerability to misinformation. According to the commenter, addressing this confusion requires extensive one-on-one consultation with their members, which exceeds their organization's capacity. The commenter stated that their legal team's experience has demonstrated both the complexity of determining registration requirements and the significant barriers to understanding and complying with the rule. The commenter explained, for instance, that determining whether an alien has previously registered requires knowing whether the alien has filed a Form I-485 or Form I-765 and knowing the outcome of such application. The commenter further noted that clients could reasonably be confused about whether they had registered if the client had previously submitted extensive documentation to USCIS that does not qualify as registration under the regulations. The commenter said that for some aliens, the only way to verify their registration status and obtain the documents they must now carry by law would be through a Freedom of Information Act (FOIA) request—which is a multi-step process that can take months, consumes their organization's staff time, and requires maintaining contact. Other commenters also remarked that the IFR would lead to an increase in FOIA requests due to a lack of clarity about who is required to complete the new registration process and would place burdens on immigration attorneys to file FOIA requests.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则的复杂性和不一致性给其工作人员带来了重大挑战,他们在委员会会议和研讨会等通常有数十人参加的集体场合中,无法自信地就登记要求向成员提供建议。据该评论者称,登记要求令人困惑的性质,加上其几乎普遍适用的影响,破坏了其提供社区教育和“了解你的权利”讲座的工作模式。该评论者表示(见原文第39265页),鉴于其拥有数万名成员,其法律团队没有能力为所有可能受影响的个人提供个别咨询。一位评论者表示,该规则要求不明确,且美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)未能统一推行或开展公众教育,给其组织及其成员带来了巨大负担。该评论者表示,这也为欺诈行为创造了滋生的环境。
A commenter stated that the IFR's complexity and inconsistency pose significant challenges to their staff, who cannot confidently advise members on registration requirements in group settings such as committee meetings and workshops that often include dozens of attendees. According to the commenter, the confusing nature of the registration requirement, coupled with its nearly universal impact, undermines their model of providing community education and know-your-rights presentations. The commenter stated ( printed page 39265) that with tens of thousands of members, their legal team lacks the capacity to provide individual advice to all potentially affected individuals. A commenter stated that the rule's unclear requirements and lack of a concerted rollout or public education on the part of USCIS impose tremendous burdens on its organization and its members. The commenter said this also creates an environment ripe for fraud.
一位评论者表示,该组织将被迫优先帮助客户尽快获得登记文件,以避免执法行动,并补充说,其工作人员将不得不投入更多时间和资源来遵守该规则,这将分散用于协助提供其他重要法律服务和救济途径的能力。
A commenter said that it would be forced to prioritize helping clients obtain registration documents quickly to avoid enforcement actions, adding that its staff would have to devote more time and resources abiding by such a rule, which would divert capacity to assist with other vital legal services and pathways to relief.
一位评论者写道,密歇根州为家庭暴力和性暴力幸存者提供服务的专业人员将对幸存者登记后可能发生的情况“陷入困境”,并补充说,由于无法在安全的前提下提供指导并避免从事未经授权的法律执业活动,这些倡导者将难以帮助其客户保障自身及其子女的安全。该评论者还表示,密歇根州的警察将难以在调查中获得积极配合的证人,因为幸存者会因担心被驱逐出境而极度恐惧,从而不敢挺身而出。
A commenter wrote that Michigan professionals who serve survivors of domestic and sexual violence would be “left in the lurch” about what could happen to survivors when they register, adding that without being able to safely provide guidance and avoid unauthorized practice of law, such advocates would struggle to support their clients to stay safe and to keep their children safe. The commenter also stated that Michigan police officers would struggle to secure supportive witnesses in their investigations because survivors would be so fearful of removal that they would not come forward.
一位评论者对《临时最终规则》(IFR)对其所在类组织的影响表示担忧,指出其所在组织已经经历了来自现有客户关于该规则登记和证明要求方面信息咨询、法律建议和协助请求的显著增加。此外,该评论者报告称,来自更广泛社区的咨询和担忧也有所增加,这干扰了其提供移民法律服务这一核心职能。该评论者解释说,该规则的复杂性和“不一致性”,加上其几乎普遍适用的影响,意味着即便是可能被视为“已登记”的现有客户,也需要获得法律建议才能有把握地做出这一判断。该评论者补充说,对于有待决申请的客户或身份混合家庭中的客户而言,情况尤其如此,因为在谁需要履行该程序方面存在更大的不确定性。该评论者对《临时最终规则》给其所在组织的资金和运营带来的影响表示担忧。该评论者解释说,他们的很大一部分资金来自要求提供特定移民法律服务成果的拨款和合同,其中一些合同按“每案”付费,另一些则根据报告要求按周期付费。该评论者表示,就新规则向现有客户和社区成员提供咨询和协助,并不能作为这些拨款下的可交付成果计入,因为这些拨款是用于资助其他特定服务的。该评论者警告称,由于将资源转移以应对该临时最终规则而导致未能符合现行拨款指标和报告要求,可能导致该拨款项下剩余资金的损失。该评论者表示担忧,这可能危及该组织申请未来拨款的能力,进而可能导致员工裁员和其他削减成本的措施,最终降低其协助现有和未来客户的能力。
A commenter voiced concern about the IFR's impact on organizations like themselves, stating that their organization has already experienced a significant increase in requests for information, legal advice, and assistance from existing clients regarding the IFR's registration and proof requirements. Additionally, the commenter reported increased inquiries and concerns from the broader community, which has interfered with their core function of providing immigration legal services. The commenter explained that the complexity and “inconsistencies” of the rule, coupled with its nearly universal impact, mean that even existing clients who may be considered “registered” would need legal advice to confidently make that determination. The commenter added that this is particularly true for clients with pending applications or those in mixed-status households where there is greater ambiguity about who needs to comply with the process. The commenter raised concerns about the IFR's impact on their organization's funding and operations. The commenter explained that they receive a significant portion of their funding from grants and contracts that require specific deliverables of immigration legal services, with some contracts paid on a “per case” basis and others paid in cycles based on reporting requirements. The commenter said that advising and assisting existing clients and community members around the new rule would not qualify under these grants as deliverables, since those grants fund other specific services. The commenter warned that failure to comply with current grant metrics and reporting requirements due to the diversion of resources to address the IFR may result in the loss of remaining funds under those grants. The commenter expressed concern that this could jeopardize the organization's ability to apply for future grants, potentially leading to staff layoffs and other cost-cutting measures, ultimately reducing their ability to assist existing and future clients.
一位评论者写道,移民登记要求会给其所在组织及与之合作的辩护律师带来利益冲突,因为这将迫使律师建议“非公民”遵守一项很可能违反第五修正案反对自证其罪权利的法律。该评论者进一步表示,由于未登记可能作为轻罪被起诉,这将使准确就刑事案件处理结果的风险和利益提供建议变得更加困难,并使辩诉协商复杂化。另一位评论者表示,《临时最终规则》给律师带来了道德上的困扰,他们不愿因相关的法律义务而建议客户不要登记。
A commenter wrote that the immigrant registration requirement would present a conflict of interest for their organization and the defense attorneys they work with because it would force attorneys to advise “noncitizens” to comply with a law that likely violates the Fifth Amendment right against self-incrimination. The commenter further said that since failure to register can be prosecuted as a misdemeanor, it would be more difficult to accurately advise on the risks and benefits of criminal case dispositions and would complicate plea negotiations. Another commenter said that the IFR creates moral issues for attorneys, who are not going to want to advise their clients not to register because of the associated legal obligations.
答复:国土安全部(DHS)理解,各组织可能会经历来自客户社区咨询的增加,或者为提供关于外国人登记要求的额外协助而扩大其服务范围。然而,这种情况在新流程出台时往往会发生,并非本《临时最终规则》所独有。国土安全部不认同该《临时最终规则》及外国人登记给法律服务提供者和辩护律师带来利益冲突或道德困扰这一说法。法律服务提供者理应就《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条,即8 U.S.C. 1302条的规定,像对待任何其他法律义务一样向其客户提供建议。
Response: DHS understands that organizations may experience an increase in inquiries from client communities, or an expansion of an organization's services to include additional assistance about the alien registration requirement. However, this is often the case with new processes and is not unique to this IFR. DHS disagrees with the characterization that the IFR and alien registration presents legal organizations and defense attorneys with a conflict of interest or moral issues. Legal services providers would presumably advise their clients about the directives of section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, as they would any other legal obligation.
国土安全部还指出,该《临时最终规则》及本最终规则均未改变国会在数十年前确立的登记要求及相关义务。该法规要求,除有限的例外情形外,所有14岁或以上、未曾登记及(如有要求)未曾按捺指纹,且在美国停留30天或以上的外国人,均必须申请登记及按捺指纹。这些规则制定填补了国土安全部监管体系中的一项空白,即为所有身份的外国人建立一个普遍适用的登记选项。国土安全部认为,该选项可能改善某些外国人群体的登记结果。
DHS also notes that the IFR and this final rule have not changed the registration requirements and related obligations established by Congress many decades ago. The statute requires that, with limited exceptions, all aliens 14 years or older who were not registered and fingerprinted, if required, who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer, must apply for registration and fingerprinting. These rulemakings fill a gap in DHS's regulatory regime by establishing a general registration option available to all aliens, regardless of status. DHS believes that this option may improve registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens.
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)还建立了一个专门网站,提供有关外国人登记要求(ARR)的信息,以及一个ARR工具,可帮助外国人确定其是否必须登记。[24]
USCIS also established a dedicated website with information on the ARR and an ARR Tool that may help aliens determine whether they must register.[24]
作为本规则制定工作的一部分,国土安全部(DHS)同时就拟议修正案征求意见,该修正案旨在规定某些现有表格用于信息收集及全面的生物特征筛查与审查,供外国人使用,以改善特定外国人群体的登记结果,同时提高效率并减轻公众和政府的负担。参见本序言第V.A节。
As part of this rulemaking, DHS is also requesting comments on proposed amendments to prescribe certain existing forms with information collection and thorough biometric-based screening and vetting for use by aliens to improve registration outcomes for certain populations of aliens while increasing efficiency and reducing burden for the public and the government. See Section V.A of this preamble.
4. 对移民系统和政府运作的影响
4. Impact on the Immigration System and Government Operations
a. 对执法能力的益处不明
a. Unclear Benefits to Enforcement Capabilities
意见:许多评论者表示,该临时最终规则(IFR)未能提供充分证据表明现行制度执法不力,或拟议规则将有益于执法效力或效率、公共安全或国家安全。另有多位评论者认为该规则没有必要、成本高昂且无效,数位评论者呼吁废止该规则。一位评论者表示担忧,称该临时最终规则未能有意义地区分不同的移民身份,这可能导致混淆和重复登记。几位评论者警告称,类似的登记政策曾在第二次世界大战期间使用,并“始终未能增强安全”。一位评论者将该临时最终规则描述为“解决一个并不存在的问题”。另一位评论者推断,严重罪行的实施者不太可能遵守登记要求,并写道“9·11”事件后实施的类似“国家安全出入境登记系统”(NSEERS)登记要求并未产生实效,也没有证据表明该项目识别出任何恐怖分子。同样,一位评论者写道,大范围的登记制度“并不能让我们更安全”。该评论者指出,2001年9月11日之后,一项类似的项目(印刷版第39266页)(NSEERS)针对穆斯林移民,但未能促成一起恐怖主义定罪,反而造成了持久的伤害。他们补充说,真正的安全来自信任以及明智、有针对性的政策,而非基于恐惧的过度扩张。另一位评论者担忧该规则将耗费大量资源用于获取很可能质量低劣的数据。
Comment: Many commenters said that the IFR fails to provide sufficient evidence demonstrating that the current system is inadequately enforced or that the proposed rule would provide a benefit for enforcement effectiveness or efficiency, public safety, or national security. Multiple other commenters described the rule as unnecessary, highly costly, and ineffective, with several commenters calling for its rescission. A commenter expressed concern that the IFR does not meaningfully distinguish between different immigration statuses, which could lead to confusion and duplicated registrations. A couple of commenters warned that similar registration policies were used during World War II and “consistently failed to enhance security.” A commenter described the IFR as “addressing a problem that does not exist.” A different commenter reasoned that perpetrators of serious crimes would not be likely to follow the registration requirement, writing that the similar NSEERS registration requirement imposed after September 11th was not productive and that there was no evidence of any terrorist identified through the program. Similarly, a commenter wrote that sweeping registries “don't make us safer.” The commenter stated that after September 11, 2001, a similar program ( printed page 39266) (NSEERS) targeted Muslim immigrants and failed to produce a single terrorism conviction—while causing lasting harm. They added that true safety comes from trust and smart, targeted policies—not from a fear-based overreach. Another commenter expressed concern that the rule would consume vast amounts of resources for data likely to be of poor quality.
一位评论者表示,该规则将促使移民执法部门以被认为不遵守规定为由拘留更多人,包括美国公民,从而损害公共安全,并浪费本应用于应对真正安全威胁的执法资源。该评论者指出,该规则缺乏证据证明其对公共安全的益处足以抵消其成本,并援引研究指出,过去援引该移民法条款的做法并未提供此类证据。一些评论者表示,该规则将使更多资源被投入移民执法,致使法院充斥大量低级别行政案件,从而转移本应用于起诉危害公共安全的犯罪(如重罪及其他严重罪行)的资源,并限制机构在边境执法、欺诈防范和庇护裁决方面的资源。另一位评论者表示,该规则的影响将在一线移民执法人员身上进一步加剧,他们将被迫花费过多时间去寻找和审查登记文件,而非专注于优先执法任务,从而对国家安全和整体公共安全造成不利影响。
A commenter said that the rule would press immigration authorities to detain more individuals based on the perception of noncompliance, including U.S. citizens, undermining public safety and wasting enforcement resources that should target legitimate security threats. The commenter articulated that the rule lacks evidence to justify the costs to benefit public safety, citing studies indicating that past uses of this section of immigration law do not provide such evidence. Some commenter said the rule would apply more resources to immigration enforcement by flooding courts with low-level administrative cases, which would divert resources away from prosecuting crimes against public safety, such as felonies and other serious crimes and limit agency resources on border enforcement, fraud prevention, and asylum adjudication. Another commenter said the rule's effects would be compounded for immigration enforcement officers in the field, who would be required to spend disproportionate time seeking out and reviewing registration documents rather than focusing on priority enforcement tasks, with consequences for national security and overall public safety.
一些评论者同样写道,目前尚不清楚该规则将如何在现有措施之外显著改善国家安全或执法效力。其中一位评论者讨论了州级和联邦级现有的就业核实、驾驶执照核实和合法身份核实措施,并引用了移民政策研究所(Migration Policy Institute)的研究,以证明诸如“电子核实”(E-Verify)和“真实身份法”(REAL ID)等措施已经足够。一位评论者表示,国土安全部(DHS)已经掌握了其所接触的大多数个人的登记和生物特征数据,而与G-325R表格相关的背景调查与通过签证、庇护、临时保护身份(TPS)和工作许可申请已经进行的调查重复。该评论者表示,该规则非但不能提高国土安全部识别威胁的能力,反而可能因系统中充斥着冗余数据以及因移民社区担心因尝试合规而遭到报复从而不愿配合,从而妨碍执法。另一位评论者同样表示,现有的流程和系统已经在追踪与移民机构接触的个人,没有迹象表明现行追踪机制使国土安全部无法识别或逮捕构成真正公共安全或国家安全风险的个人,这使得这种“大规模扩张”既无必要又低效。同样,另一位评论者敦促本部门专注于改进现有系统,而不是制造不必要的行政复杂性,而另一位评论者则将登记要求描述为对移民制度的“不必要的彻底改造”。
A few commenters similarly wrote that it is unclear how the rule would significantly improve national security or law enforcement efficacy beyond existing measures. One of these commenters discussed the existing measures at both the State and Federal level to verify employment, driver's licenses, and legal status and cited research from the Migration Policy Institute to demonstrate that measures such as E-Verify and REAL ID are sufficient. A commenter stated that DHS already possesses registration and biometric data for most individuals it interacts with, and the background checks associated with the G-325R form replicate checks already done through visa, asylum, TPS, and employment authorization applications. The commenter stated that, rather than improving DHS's ability to identify threats, the rule may hamper enforcement by overloading systems with redundant data and discouraging cooperation from immigrant communities who fear retaliation for attempted compliance. A commenter similarly said that existing processes and systems already track individuals who interact with immigration agencies and there is no indication that current tracking mechanisms have left DHS unable to identify or apprehend individuals who pose real public safety or national security risks, making this “sweeping expansion” both unnecessary and inefficient. Similarly, another commenter urged the Department to focus on improving existing systems rather than creating unnecessary administrative complexities, while a different commenter described the registry requirement as an “unnecessary overhaul” of the immigration system.
一位评论者表示担忧,临时最终规则(IFR)将无法执行。另一位评论者建议国土安全部确保拟议的登记不会自动触发执法行动。
One commenter expressed concern that the IFR would be impossible to enforce. Another commenter recommended that DHS ensure the proposed registration would not automatically trigger enforcement actions.
回应:临时最终规则和最终规则并未改变现行程序,而是通过规定一种适用于所有外国人(无论其身份如何)的一般登记选项及相应的登记证明文件,填补了监管体制中的空白。预计该规则将通过提供关于外国人在美国所在位置的更全面信息,使国土安全部更容易、更安全地执法,从而提高执法效力。指纹采集要求合规率的提高,为国土安全部提供了关于外国人犯罪记录的更多信息,包括该外国人是否为已知或疑似恐怖分子。国土安全部还指出,大多数合法在美居留的外国人很可能已经完成了登记。最后,国土安全部指出,即使本规则制定对执法效力缺乏明确的净正面影响,国土安全部仍将依据其忠实执行《移民与国籍法》(INA)外国人登记要求以及总统第14159号行政命令指示的职责,推进本规则制定工作。
Response: The IFR and final rule do not change current procedures but fill a gap in the regulatory regime by prescribing a general registration option available to all aliens regardless of their status and corresponding evidence as proof of that registration. The rule is expected to improve DHS law enforcement efficacy by providing more comprehensive information about the location of aliens in the United States to make it easier and safer for DHS to enforce the law.
