The increased compliance with fingerprinting requirements provides DHS with additional information about an alien's criminal record, including whether an alien is a known or suspected terrorist. DHS also notes that most aliens lawfully present in the United States are likely already registered. Finally, DHS notes that even if this rulemaking lacked a clear net positive effect on law enforcement efficacy, DHS would pursue this rulemaking consistent with DHS's duty to faithfully implement the alien registration requirements of the INA and the President's direction in E.O. 14159.
b. 政府成本与政府资源的不当配置
b. Government Cost and Misallocation of Government Resources
评论:许多评论者对临时最终规则导致国土安全部成本增加表示担忧,并将其描述为政府资源的不当配置。例如,一些评论者反对使用税款资助对那些因其所认为的美国对外政策所致状况而逃离的个人进行治安管理和驱逐出境。另一位评论者建议不要将资源用于资助他们认为具有侵入性的治安管理做法。有几位评论者写道,政府资源应被用于保障基本人类需求。
Comment: Many commenters expressed concern regarding increased DHS costs arising from the IFR and described it as misallocation of government resources. For example, some commenters opposed the use of tax dollars to fund the policing and deportation of individuals fleeing conditions they attributed to U.S. foreign policy. Another commenter recommended against using resources to fund policing practices they viewed as invasive. A couple of commenters wrote that government resources should be directed toward ensuring basic human needs.
一位评论者表示,该规则将催生一个代价高昂、臃肿不堪的官僚机构,其目的是在邻居文书工作滞后时将其定为刑事犯罪,鉴于政府已经掌握所需的全部信息,这构成了资源的浪费。
A commenter stated that the rule would create an expensive, bloated bureaucracy in an effort to criminalize neighbors if they fall behind on their paperwork and constitutes a waste of resources given that the government already has all the information it needs.
一位评论者表示,国土安全部将需要建立一整套全新的系统来追踪这些登记,从而造成不当负担。一位评论者表示,按照目前的文本规定,任何合法永久居民(LPR)即使只是前往墨西哥进行一小时的旅行,也可能在每次再入境时被要求交出绿卡并重新采集指纹,这将导致“荒谬的结果”并浪费时间和资源。
A commenter stated that DHS would need to create a whole new system to keep track of these registrations, creating an undue burden. A commenter stated that, as currently written, any LPR who takes even a 1-hour trip to Mexico could be subject to having to tender their Green Card upon each re-entry and be re-fingerprinted, leading to an “absurd result” and waste of time and resources.
一位评论者写道,实施这一登记程序不会是资源的有效利用,并将其描述为“既与美国人的总体福祉无关,也与美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)已经提供的福利无关”。
One commenter wrote that it would not be an efficient use of resources to institute this registration process, describing it as “unrelated to the general welfare of Americans nor to the provision of the benefits USCIS already administers.”
其他评论者表示,该规则针对未遵守登记要求设定的刑事处罚,将给民事和刑事执法带来高昂成本。有评论者描述了当局在有效实施“国家安全出入境登记系统”(NSEERS)项目时所面临的困难及其财政成本,写道这段历史以及该项目各方面缺乏明确且可管理的实施方法,应当为本临时最终规则(IFR)及其潜在的财政影响提供参考,因为即将实施的登记要求将需要多个机构和部门来排查该项目各方面的问题。一些评论者写道,该临时最终规则将导致更多诉讼和滥用行为,其中一位评论者对国土安全部(DHS)可能产生的“律师费”表示担忧,称这将成为“纳税人资金的浪费”。
Other commenters said the rule's criminal penalties for failing to comply with registration requirements would create a high cost for civil and criminal law enforcement. Describing the difficulty authorities faced in effectively carrying out the NSEERS program and its financial costs, a commenter wrote that this history and the lack of a clear and manageable method of implementing the various aspects of the program should be informative to the IFR and its potential financial implications, as the impending registration requirement would require multiple agencies and authorities to troubleshoot the various aspects of the program. Some commenters wrote that the IFR would lead to more litigation and abuse, with an individual commenter expressing concern about “legal fees” DHS might incur and become a “waste of taxpayer money.”
回应:对于本临时最终规则及最终规则,国土安全部一如既往地努力做到公平并高效执行国会制定的法律。本规则制定旨在填补现行监管体系中的空白,确保所有外国人,无论其移民身份如何,都有途径履行其在法律下的义务,并改善整体登记成果。
Response: With this IFR and final rule, as always, DHS strives to be fair and efficiently execute the laws established by Congress. This rulemaking addresses a gap in the existing regulatory regime and ensures that there is a way for all aliens, regardless of their immigration status, to comply with their duty under the law and to improve overall registration outcomes.
为回应评论者关于资源和效率的担忧,G-325R表格的处理流程完全电子化。任何在入境或假释进入美国时已获发I-94或I-94W表格的人员,均已完成登记。合法永久居民(LPR)(第39267页)在短暂出境后重新入境美国时,通常已经完成登记,因为他们持有I-551表格(即“绿卡”),一般不需要再次申请入境许可。参见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第101(a)(13)(C)条,8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(13)(C)。
To address the resource and efficiency concerns of the comments, the Form G-325R process is entirely electronic. Anyone issued Form I-94 or I-94W upon their admission or parole to the United States is already registered. LPRs ( printed page 39267) who reenter the United States after a temporary absence abroad have generally already been registered as they are in possession of a Form I-551 (“a green card”) and are generally not seeking admission to the United States. See INA sec. 101(a)(13)(C), 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(13)(C).
该法规要求外国人在登记过程中提供指纹,除非获得豁免。总体而言,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)并未发现生物特征信息采集服务存在重大延误或效率低下的问题。本序言第VI.B.3节对本规则制定对该机构预计产生的成本作了进一步讨论。
The statute requires an alien to provide fingerprints, unless waived, as part of the registration process, and in general, USCIS has not seen any significant delays or inefficiencies in biometric collection services. Section VI.B.3 of this preamble provides further discussion on the estimated cost to the agency of this rulemaking.
就评论者所暗示的国土安全部不应全面执行和强制实施《移民与国籍法》中外国人登记要求这一观点,国土安全部对此持不同意见。此外,该规则并未要求对现行成文法实施额外的执法措施。国土安全部将依照其在《移民与国籍法》下的法定职责,以及总统的指示,管理、执行并切实履行这些法律,其中包括针对那些希望政府不执行这些法律的人所提出的质疑进行抗辩。国土安全部不认为,针对国会通过的移民法律的忠实实施进行法律抗辩是浪费政府资源的行为,相反,这正是政府职责的一部分。
To the extent that the commenters suggest that DHS should not fully administer and enforce the alien registration requirements of the INA, DHS respectfully disagrees. Furthermore, the rule does not obligate additional enforcement of the existing statute DHS will administer, enforce, and faithfully execute these laws consistent with DHS's statutory duties under the INA, and as directed by the President, which includes defending against challenges from those who would prefer that the government not enforce these laws. DHS does not believe that defending the faithful implementation of immigration laws, as passed by Congress, against legal challenges is a waste of government resources, but instead is part of the government's mandate.
评论:少数评论者表示普遍担忧,认为该临时最终规则将助长政府腐败及移民系统的不当使用。其中一位评论者写道,该要求可能导致移民系统内的官员更多地滥用职权,并认为政府只是试图表现得有效,而并非真正有效。
Comment: A few commenters expressed general concerns that the IFR would support government corruption and inappropriate usage of the immigration system. One of the commenters wrote that the requirement could lead to an increase in officers within the immigration system abusing their position of authority and that the government is trying to appear effective without being effective.
回应:作为第14159号行政命令的一部分,特朗普总统将按照法律执行登记要求列为优先事项,并要求公开宣传《移民与国籍法》第262条(8 U.S.C. 1302)所规定的外国人登记及(如要求)按捺指纹的义务,以及随附的携带登记证明和向国土安全部报告地址变更的相关要求。
Response: As part of E.O. 14159, President Trump made it a priority to enforce the registration requirement in accordance with the law, and to publicize information about the duty of aliens described in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, to register and be fingerprinted (if required), as well as the related requirements to carry such evidence of registration and notify DHS of changes of address.
本临时最终规则及最终规则的目标,是切实履行《移民与国籍法》中规定的外国人登记要求。国土安全部力求更好地确保外国人了解其在法律下的义务,并通过新的一般登记程序和表格为其履行该法定义务提供途径。关于政府官员未来可能滥用职权或存在其他不当行为的推测,超出了本规则制定的范围。本规则制定并未设定执法政策,也不可能合理地全面涵盖此类行为。
The goal of the IFR and final rule is to faithfully implement the alien registration requirements of the INA. DHS seeks to better ensure that aliens understand their duty under the law and have a path to satisfy that statutory duty through the new general registration process and form. Speculation about potential future misuse of authority or other malfeasance by government officials is beyond the scope of this rulemaking. This rulemaking does not set enforcement policy and cannot reasonably be expected to comprehensively account for such activity.
c. 重复现有流程并加剧案件积压
c. Duplicating Existing Processes and Exacerbating Backlog
评论:许多评论者写道,临时最终规则(IFR)未充分说明国土安全部(DHS)将如何应对大量涌入的登记申请,以及该规则所带来的相关行政和执法成本,也未说明其对现有积压案件和国土安全部其他重要职能的影响。许多评论者还表示担忧,新的登记制度将进一步加重已经不堪重负的系统的负担,并加剧美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的积压问题,从而导致诸如保护丧失、签证和申请处理延误以及工作许可中断等负面影响。
Comment: Many commenters wrote that the IFR does not adequately address how DHS would handle the massive influx of registrations and the associated administrative and enforcement costs of the rule, as well as impacts to the existing backlog and other essential DHS functions. Many commenters also expressed concern that the new registration system would further burden an overwhelmed system and exacerbate the USCIS backlog, resulting in negative effects, such as loss of protection, delays in visa and petition processing, and interruption in employment authorization.
许多评论者同样指出,要求数百万人进行登记并执行登记证明,将增加司法部(DOJ)和美国公民及移民服务局的行政成本,包括人员、培训和材料方面的费用;他们表示,后者机构在处理多个福利类别方面已经存在严重的积压。同样,一位评论者表示,建立、维护并执行一个庞大登记制度的合规工作将极其昂贵,需要新的官僚机构、加强的监控以及大规模的执法行动来处理所收集的数据,从而使国土安全部的资源不堪重负,并进一步加重现有积压案件的负担。一位评论者表示,美国公民及移民服务局在处理I-765表格申请方面已经面临重大挑战,如果将资源转移去处理数百万份G-325R表格,这些挑战只会进一步加剧,从而增加当前的就业许可文件(EAD)处理积压,并在此过程中损害工人、企业和整体经济利益。少数评论者鼓励国土安全部将重点放在其他优先事项上,例如提高现有系统内的效率,包括减少案件积压,而不是扩大外国人登记要求。
Many commenters similarly stated that requiring millions of people to register and enforcing proof of registration would increase administrative costs for DOJ and USCIS, including costs for personnel, training, and materials; they said the latter agency already has severe backlogs in processing several benefit categories. Likewise, a commenter stated that creating, maintaining, and enforcing compliance with a massive registration regime would be extraordinarily costly and require new bureaucracy, increased surveillance, and extensive enforcement operations to act on the data collected, thus overwhelming DHS resources and layering on to existing backlogs. A commenter stated that the significant challenges USCIS already faces in processing Form I-765 filings would only become more acute if resources are diverted to process millions of Form G-325Rs, thus increasing current EAD processing backlogs and harming workers, businesses, and the overall economy in the process. A few commenters encouraged DHS to focus on other priorities, including improving efficiency within the existing system, such as reducing the case backlog, instead of expanding alien registration requirements.
一位评论者表示,国土安全部没有足够的资源用于增加后勤保障、人员配备、培训以及开发和维护系统,并补充说,如果国土安全部目前确实有相关资金,政府本可以将其更好地用于政府内其他部门,”以改善服务、人员配备、培训等”。一位评论者还指出,该临时最终规则将在联邦政府裁员期间给国土安全部员工带来不必要的行政负担。多位评论者批评该规则未讨论美国公民及移民服务局当前的积压情况,也未讨论该规则将如何影响这种积压。评论者表示担忧,美国公民及移民服务局在处理各类申请方面已经存在严重积压,包括庇护申请、特殊移民青少年(SIJ)身份申请以及其他移民福利申请。评论者还批评该临时最终规则未讨论在已经不堪重负的美国公民及移民服务局基础上增加”大量”额外工作量可能带来的成本,并描述了积压问题在各福利类别中持续造成的经济损害。
A commenter stated that DHS does not have the necessary resources for increased logistics, staffing, training, and developing and maintaining a system, adding that if DHS does currently have the funding, the government could put it to better use in other departments within the government ” to improve services, staffing, training, etc.” A commenter also noted that the IFR would create unnecessary administrative burden for DHS employees during a period of Federal job reductions. Several commenters critiqued the rule for its lack of discussion on the current backlog at USCIS or how the rule would affect this backlog. Commenters expressed concern that USCIS is already experiencing significant backlogs in processing various applications, including asylum applications, Special Immigrant Juvenile (SIJ) status petitions, and other immigration benefits. Commenters also critiqued the IFR for its lack of discussion on the potential costs of adding “significant” additional workload to the overburdened state of USCIS, describing the ongoing economic damage of the backlog across benefit categories.
其中一位评论者写道,政府通过多个机构收集了大量的生物特征和个人身份数据。一些评论者表示,许多外国人已经完成了登记,包括已获得或已申请合法永久居留权的人员、在抵达美国时领取了I-94表格的人员、已获发就业许可文件(EAD)的人员,或在最近一次入境美国之前已获发非移民或移民签证的人员。同样,一位评论者表示,相关信息已经通过I-94、I-485、I-589和I-765等长期使用的表格收集。该评论者表示,此规则进一步使系统碎片化,使申请人和政府机构都感到困惑,造成与现有数据库和身份识别机制的重叠,从而直接违背了简化移民系统的呼声。该评论者敦促国土安全部开发与现有表格和数据库相整合的登记机制,而不是创建冗余且令人困惑的并行系统。
One of the commenters wrote that the government collects extensive biometric and biographic data through multiple agencies. Some commenters stated that many aliens are already registered, including individuals who have been granted or applied for lawful permanent residence, received a Form I-94 when arriving in the United States, were issued an EAD, or were issued a nonimmigrant or immigrant visa prior to their most recent arrival in the United States. Similarly, a commenter said relevant information is already collected via longstanding forms such as I-94, I-485, I-589, and I-765. The commenter stated that this rule fragments the system further, confusing both applicants and government agencies, creating overlap with existing databases and identification mechanisms, thus directly contradicting calls for immigration system streamlining. The commenter urged DHS to develop registration mechanisms that integrate with existing forms and databases, rather than creating redundant and confusing parallel systems.
多位评论者同样对要求庇护申请人、临时保护身份(TPS)持有人以及其他已申请人道主义福利但目前未被列入登记表格的人员提交G-325R表格一事表示担忧。一位评论者指出,这种重复要求在联邦政府裁员之际,给国土安全部员工带来了重大且不必要的负担。
Multiple commenters similarly expressed concern with the requirement for asylum applicants, Temporary Protected Status (TPS) holders, and others who have applied for humanitarian benefits not currently listed as a registration form, to submit the G-325R. A commenter noted that this duplication places a significant and unnecessary burden on DHS employees at a time when the Federal Government is cutting jobs.
有评论者表示,许多在接收阶段就要求采集生物识别信息作为申请流程一部分的移民表格,并未被列入可接受的登记表格清单,并写道这一遗漏表明此类强制性登记已经过时且没有必要。例如,(印刷页第39268页)该评论者指出,I-589表格未被列入可接受表格清单,但作为庇护程序的一部分,所有申请人都必须在I-589表格中披露大量的个人生平和人口统计信息,并且还必须完成生物识别采集程序。因此,该评论者认为,将庇护申请人视为“未登记”是不合理且武断的,同样的论点也适用于多种其他移民表格和申请程序,包括U签证和T签证、特殊移民青少年身份(SIJ)以及《防止对妇女施暴法》(VAWA)救济。该评论者得出结论认为,强迫庇护、U签证、T签证、SIJ和VAWA申请人使用G-325R进行登记将是对政府资源和时间的浪费,因为政府已经掌握了G-325R所要求的相同信息。
A commenter stated that numerous immigration forms that require biometrics upon receipt as part of the form's application process are not listed as acceptable forms of registration, writing that this omission shows that this type of mandatory registration is outdated and unnecessary. For example, ( printed page 39268) the commenter said Form I-589 is not included on the list of accepted forms, but as part of the asylum process, all applicants must disclose extensive biographical and demographic information on Form I-589 and also complete the biometrics process. Therefore, the commenter reasoned that to consider asylum applicants as “not registered” is unreasonable and arbitrary and that the same argument would hold for a variety of different immigration forms and application processes, including U and T visas, SIJ, and VAWA relief. The commenter concluded that to force asylum, U visa, T visa, SIJ, and VAWA applicants to register using the G-325R would be a waste of government resources and time since the government already has the same information that is being asked for in the G-325R.
答复:美国国土安全部(DHS)有义务忠实执行国会制定的法律,包括外国人登记要求。该法规要求大多数在美国停留30天或以上的外国人申请登记,并且除基于年龄或非移民身份的若干例外情形外,须接受指纹采集。DHS通过临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则,并未创设或修改《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条,即8 U.S.C. 1302条所规定的外国人向政府登记义务的法定要求。
Response: DHS has an obligation to faithfully execute the laws established by Congress, including the alien registration requirement. The statute requires most aliens who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer to apply for registration and with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted. DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not created or modified the statutory requirements of an alien's duty to register with the government under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302.
总体而言,临时最终规则(IFR)并未对美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)处理的其他移民福利事项造成任何重大处理延误或积压增加。DHS有意简化了未登记外国人进行登记并依照INA要求遵守法律的流程,以使外国人和USCIS均受益。G-325R表格的处理流程完全为电子化。与纸质申请不同,未登记外国人通过电子流程提交信息,并会自动收到提示,以提供完成登记所需的必要信息。外国人在申请登记后或在需要采集生物识别信息的情况下完成生物识别采集后,几乎可以立即获得登记证明(《USCIS外国人G-325R登记证明》)。外国人可在其myUSCIS账户中下载并打印该登记证明(《USCIS外国人G-325R登记证明》)的PDF版本,鉴于INA第264(e)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(e)条规定,所有在美国境内年满18周岁且须登记的外国人,必须随时随身携带其所获发的外国人登记证或外国人登记收据卡,这一点具有重要意义。G-325R表格的电子化处理省去了人工接收处理流程,包括拆开邮寄提交的信封、按照受理标准核对表格、以及将文件扫描转换为电子格式或将表格内容另行录入USCIS系统等环节。人工接收处理对该机构而言更为耗时且繁重,并可能导致处理延误和数据完整性问题。G-325R表格的电子化处理增强了面向未登记外国人和USCIS的自动化服务。该流程未造成任何重大处理延误,也未要求将资源从USCIS其他工作量中重新调配。
In general, the IFR has not caused any significant processing delays or an increase of backlogs on other immigration benefits processed by USCIS. DHS purposefully streamlined the process by which unregistered aliens may register and comply with the law as required by the INA to benefit aliens and USCIS. The Form G-325R process is entirely electronic. Unlike paper filings, an unregistered alien submits information through the electronic process and is automatically prompted to provide necessary information to complete his or her registration. An alien obtains evidence of registration (USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration) almost instantly after applying for registration or after providing biometrics, if required. In the alien's myUSCIS account, he or she will be able to download and print a PDF version of the evidence of registration (USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration), which is of significance in light of the requirement in section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e), that all aliens 18 years or older in the United States who are required to register must at all times carry with them and have in their personal possession any certificate of alien registration or alien registration receipt card issued to them. The electronic processing of the Form G-325R eliminates manual intake processing that includes opening envelopes of a mailed submission, checking forms against acceptance criteria, and scanning the documents to convert them into electronic format or otherwise entering form responses into USCIS systems. Manual intake processing is more time-consuming and burdensome for the agency and could lead to delays in processing and data integrity issues. The electronic processing of Form G-325R has enhanced automated services for unregistered aliens and USCIS. This process has not created any significant processing delays and has not required reallocation of resources from other workloads in USCIS.
目前,根据8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)款规定,已提交I-589表格(《庇护及暂缓递解申请书》)以及现行法规中未列明的其他表格的外国人,不被视为已完成登记。DHS指出,I-766表格(《就业授权文件》)已在8 CFR 264.1(b)款中被列为登记证明之一。许多获准庇护者、庇护申请人、临时保护身份(TPS)申请人及其他外国人已获得此类登记证明。
At this time, under 8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b), aliens who have filed the Form I-589, Application for Asylum and Withholding of Removal, and other forms that are not enumerated in the existing regulations, are not considered to be registered. DHS notes that Form I-766, Employment Authorization Document, is listed as evidence of registration at 8 CFR 264.1(b). Many asylees, asylum applicants, TPS applicants, and other aliens have received such evidence of registration.
国土安全部（DHS）意识到现行法规中存在可以改进的地方，包括修订8 CFR 264.1(a)中规定的注册表格清单，以及8 CFR 264.1(b)中构成注册证明的表格清单。作为本最终规则的一部分，国土安全部正在就以何种方式修订国土安全部法规以改进《移民与国籍法》（INA）第262条（8 U.S.C. 1302）项下注册要求的实施征求意见。参见本序言第五部分。
DHS is aware that there are areas of the existing regulations that could be improved, including amending the list of forms prescribed as registration forms in 8 CFR 264.1(a) and the list of forms constituted as evidence of registration in 8 CFR 264.1(b). As part of this final rule, DHS is requesting comments on various ways to amend the DHS regulation to improve implementation of the registration requirement under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. See section V of this preamble.
5. 对社区和公共安全的影响
5. Impact on Communities and Public Safety
a. 对执法和社区安全参与的影响及其意涵
a. Impacts and Implications for Law Enforcement and Participation in Community Safety
评论意见：许多评论者表示担忧，认为该规则将损害公共安全和执法效能。一位评论者指出，全国各地的社区都依赖与执法人员之间的牢固关系;该评论者称，由于错误逮捕的威胁或其认知度增加，临时最终规则（IFR）威胁到了这种关系，这将导致信任不断受损的恶性循环，同时影响美国公民和外国人。类似地，另一位评论者担忧执法机构将无法区分不同注册状态的移民，从而导致错误逮捕增加。关于信任问题，评论者写道，该政策将损害社区对执法部门的信任，使美国公民和合法在美外国人（包括合法永久居民，LPR）均面临错误逮捕和拘留的风险，并在未能带来实质性公共安全效益的情况下，给移民法律体系带来额外压力。一位评论者写道，现任政府已经采取了“杂乱无章且咄咄逼人的执法行动模式”，并称“此项临时最终规则只会助长本届政府普遍存在的令人担忧的执法做法”。与其所表达的公共安全担忧相关，一些评论者写道，外国人的犯罪率低于美国公民。
Comment: Many commenters expressed concerns that the rule would undermine public safety and law enforcement effectiveness. A commenter stated that communities across the country rely on a strong relationship with law enforcement officers; they said the IFR threatens that relationship due to the increased threat or perception of wrongful arrests, which would lead to a downward spiral of eroding trust impacting both U.S. citizens and aliens. Similarly, a different commenter expressed concern that enforcement agencies would be unable to distinguish between immigrants of different registration statuses and that there would be an increase in false arrests. With regard to trust, commenters wrote that the policy would damage community trust in law enforcement, subject both U.S citizens and lawfully present aliens, including LPRs, to wrongful arrests and detentions, and place additional strain on the immigration legal system without achieving meaningful public safety benefits. One commenter wrote that the current administration has already engaged in “haphazard and aggressive pattern of enforcement actions,” and that “this IFR will only fuel the alarming enforcement practices commonplace in this administration.” In connection with their stated concerns about public safety, some commenters wrote that aliens commit crime at lower rates than U.S. citizens.
另有评论者写道，该政策将为普遍存在的滥用行为开路，却无法改善社区安全，还有评论者补充称，该政策将妨碍与执法部门的合作，使社区变得不那么安全，并损害执法人员的安全。作为对临时最终规则的替代方案，一位评论者建议应集中精力与地方执法机构就有针对性的合作加强协调，并将资源集中用于应对最严重的国家安全和公共安全威胁，而不是不加区分地对数百万外国人进行登记和追查。另一位评论者建议，政策制定者应推行有利于经济发展和提升公共安全的移民政策，并优先将真正威胁公共安全者驱逐出境，而非针对没有犯罪记录的个人。
Separate commenters wrote that the policy would pave the way for widespread abuse without improving community safety, with others adding that it would impede cooperation with law enforcement, make communities less safe, and also undermine officer safety. As an alternative to the IFR, a commenter recommended concentrating on improved coordination with local law enforcement agencies on tailored cooperation and focusing resources on the most serious national security and public safety threats, rather than registering and pursuing millions of aliens indiscriminately. A different commenter suggested that policymakers should pursue immigration policies that benefit the economy and increase public safety and prioritize the removal of legitimate public safety threats over individuals without a criminal record.
一位评论者写道，预计该临时最终规则将使执法部门偏离其基本职责。该评论者警告称，被责令执行随身携带注册证明要求的地方警员将不得不面对“涉及第四和第五修正案复杂纠葛的互动，却没有获得适当的移民要求培训经费”，从而降低整体公共安全效能。关于警察，一位评论者指出，警察未接受过移民法方面的培训，可能难以辨别某人是否已正确完成注册，从而可能导致错误指控和诉讼。该评论者援引了戈德华特研究所（Goldwater Institute）2008年的一份报告，该报告指出，亚利桑那州马里科帕县警长办公室的执法效能“因优先事项错位而受到损害”，其中就包括移民执法。（印刷页第39269页）
A commenter wrote that the IFR is expected to divert law enforcement from essential duties. The commenter warned that local officers directed to enforce the requirement to carry evidence of registration would need to engage in “complex interactions with considerable fourth and fifth amendment entanglements without appropriate funding for training in immigration requirements,” reducing overall public safety efficacy. With regard to police, a commenter stated that they are not trained in immigration law and may struggle to differentiate if someone is properly registered, potentially leading to false charges and litigation. The commenter cited a 2008 report from the Goldwater Institute stating that the effectiveness of the Maricopa County, Arizona Sheriff's Department “was compromised by misplaced priorities,” including immigration enforcement. ( printed page 39269)
一位评论者表示，执法官员自身也对移民执法对社区信任的影响表示担忧，特别是当民众因不愿意或害怕而不报警、不报告受害情况或不愿作为证人配合调查时，这会增加城市中每个人遭受侵害的脆弱性。一位评论者表示，由于未完成注册将面临严厉处罚，移民将不愿与执法官员有任何接触，即使他们是受害者或目睹了犯罪行为也是如此。
A commenter stated that law enforcement officials themselves have expressed concern about the impact of immigration enforcement on community trust, in particular when people are unwilling to or fearful of calling the police, report victimization, or cooperate as witnesses, it increases the vulnerability of everyone in the city to victimization. A commenter said that because of the severe penalties for not having registration, immigrants would be reluctant to have any contact with law enforcement officials, even if they are victims or witnessed a crime.
许多评论者表示,强制登记可能会阻碍个人寻求基本服务、寻求帮助或举报犯罪。例如,其中一位评论者引用了一项研究,据该评论者称,该研究表明,在“安全社区”计划(Secure Communities)实施期间,西班牙裔社区成员举报犯罪的可能性降低了30%。“安全社区”计划是2008年设立的一项计划,该计划自动将所有被捕者的指纹转交给国土安全部(DHS)。同一位评论者指出,一项对地方政府配合联邦执法项目情况的审查发现,“没有一项举措能够减少暴力犯罪,恰恰相反,其中两项安排显著增加了一个人遭遇暴力犯罪的风险。”另一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则(IFR)通过为移民制造敌对环境,使社区变得更不安全,这既影响移民,也影响美国公民。该评论者表示,这可能导致过度执法,并指出强制登记可能会阻碍“非公民”举报犯罪,从而损害公共安全,导致犯罪得不到举报,进而影响更广泛的社区。
Many commenters stated that mandatory registration might deter individuals from seeking essential services, seeking help, or reporting crimes. For example, one of those commenters cited research that, per the commenter, showed that Hispanic community members were 30 percent less likely to report crimes during the implementation of Secure Communities, a 2008 program that automatically forwarded fingerprints of all arrestees to DHS. The same commenter noted that a review of local cooperation in Federal enforcement programs found that “none reduced violent crime [and] on the contrary, two of the arrangements significantly increased a person's risk of experiencing violent crime.” Another commenter wrote that the IFR makes communities less safe by creating a hostile environment for immigrants, affecting both immigrants and U.S. citizens. They said this could result in over-policing and stated that mandatory registration might deter “noncitizens” from reporting crimes, therefore undermining public safety and allowing crimes to go unreported, which impacts the broader community.
另一位评论者对此观点表示赞同,写道拘留和驱逐出境的数量将出现“急剧”上升。他们还写道,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)未能考虑到基于该规则进行的任意和歧视性搜查、扣押、拘留和驱逐出境所造成的影响,以及这些影响对“非公民”和美国公民的共同冲击。众多评论者担忧该临时最终规则会伤害移民社区或使其产生恐惧。一位评论者表示,该规则可能导致已经通过其他登记表格合法登记的外国人变得更加恐惧和不安。一位评论者写道,政府的“威胁性”言辞以及移民执法中所采用的手段正在加剧外国人社区中的恐惧情绪。众多评论者担忧,该临时最终规则所引发的恐惧将导致人们对移民体系信任度的下降,并可能对外国人获取或愿意使用公共服务、医疗保健、法律体系或合法移民程序产生寒蝉效应。例如,一位评论者讨论了人们可能担心登记会使个人面临未来执法行动、驱逐出境程序或歧视性待遇的风险,并表示该规则加剧了这些担忧,而不是以透明的方式解决合理的关切。其他评论者表示,该临时最终规则总体上会抑制公民参与,或限制人们获得社会服务的渠道,因为外国人被怀疑对待,而不是被视为社会的宝贵成员。
Expressing agreement with this viewpoint, another commenter wrote that there would be a “dramatic” spike in detention and deportation. They also wrote that USCIS fails to consider the impact of arbitrary and discriminatory searches, seizures, detentions, and deportations premised on the rule and the impacts on “noncitizens” and U.S. citizens alike. Numerous commenters expressed concern that the IFR would harm or instill fear in immigrant communities. A commenter stated that the rule may cause aliens who are already legally registered using other registration forms to be increasingly fearful and uncertain. A commenter wrote that fear among alien communities is being exacerbated by the administration's “threatening” rhetoric and by the tactics employed in immigration enforcement. Numerous commenters raised concerns that fear stemming from the IFR would lead to deterioration of trust in the immigration system and potential chilling effects on aliens' access or willingness to engage with public services, health care, the legal system, or legal immigration processes. For example, a commenter discussed potential fears that registering could expose individuals to future enforcement actions, deportation proceedings, or discriminatory treatment, and stated that the rule exacerbates fears, rather than addressing legitimate concerns transparently. Other commenters stated that the IFR would generally discourage civic participation or limit access to social services, because aliens are being treated with suspicion, rather than as valued members of society.
答复:国土安全部不认同该临时最终规则会对公共安全和社区安全参与产生负面影响,或对执法部门造成不当负担,或使其无法履行其基本职责这一观点。外国人登记要求并非新规定。在过去85年中,美国法律一直要求绝大多数在美停留30天或以上的外国人进行登记,并且除基于年龄或非移民身份的若干例外情形外,还须按捺指纹。国会在20世纪中期还确立了外国人须在迁移后10日内向政府登记并更新地址的要求,以及被签发登记证明的外国人须随身携带该证明的要求。[25] 国土安全部通过该临时最终规则及本最终规则,并未创设这些要求,也未修改国会为外国人设立的义务。同样,该临时最终规则也没有创设或修改国会数十年前确立的、适用于外国人未履行其登记及相关义务情形的刑事处罚规定。本规则与第14159号行政命令(E.O. 14159)以及《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条至第265条(即8 U.S.C. 1302条至1305条)中规定的外国人登记要求相一致,并为所有尚未登记的外国人设立了一种通用登记方式,无论其移民身份如何。改变法定登记要求属于国会的权限范围及政策选择。
Response: DHS disagrees that the IFR negatively impacts public safety and participation in community safety, puts an undue burden on law enforcement, or would divert them from performing their essential duties. The alien registration requirement is not new. For the last 85 years, the laws of the United States have required most aliens present in the United States who remain for 30 days or longer to register and, with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted. The requirement that aliens register and update their address with the government within 10 days of moving, and the requirement that aliens issued evidence of registration carry such evidence on their person, were also established by Congress in the middle of the 20th century.[25] DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not created these requirements or modified the duties established by Congress for aliens. Similarly, this IFR did not create or modify the criminal penalties established by Congress many decades ago that apply when aliens fail to meet their registration and related obligations. This rule is consistent with E.O. 14159 and the alien registration requirements in sections 262 through 265 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1305, and establishes a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. It is within Congress' control and its policy choice to change the statutory registration requirement.
此外,该规则并不要求加强对现行法规的执法。该规则也不要求将任何无合法身份的外国人驱逐出境。该规则规定,尚未通过其他方式进行登记的外国人,现在可以使用G-325R表格进行登记。正如临时最终规则(IFR)中所解释的那样,该规则预计将提高国土安全部(DHS)的执法效能,并提供有关外国人在美国所在位置的更全面信息。此外,这将使国土安全部更容易、更安全地执法,并提高外国人对法定指纹采集要求的遵守程度。这些生物识别信息将为该部门提供有关外国人犯罪记录的额外信息,包括该外国人是否为已知或疑似恐怖分子。
Furthermore, the rule does not obligate additional enforcement of the existing statute. The rule also does not oblige the removal of any unauthorized alien from the country. The rule establishes that those aliens that have not registered through other means can now register using Form G-325R. As explained in the IFR, the rule is expected to improve DHS law enforcement efficacy and to provide more comprehensive information about the location of aliens in the United States. Further, it will make it easier and safer for DHS to enforce the law and increase alien compliance with statutory fingerprinting requirements. These biometrics would provide the Department with additional information about an alien's criminal record, including whether the alien is a known or suspected terrorist.
b. 对社区的影响
b. Impacts on Communities
意见:有评论者表示,登记计划并不能使社区更安全,反而会破坏社区福祉,损害社区,迫使家庭躲藏起来,削弱社区而非加强社区。一位评论者在表达反对意见时笼统地写道,该规则将“攻击我们的社区”。
Comment: Commenters stated that registration programs do not make communities safer but instead undermine community wellbeing, harm the community, drive families into hiding, and weaken the community instead of strengthening it. While expressing opposition, a commenter generally wrote that the rule would “attack our community.”
评论者进一步写道,该规则将使人们害怕送孩子上学、离家外出工作以及为社区做贡献。例如,一位自称是教师的评论者表示,公开的移民登记簿将使其学生无法每天上学,并对该登记簿一旦实施后自身的人身安全表示担忧。
Commenters further wrote that the rule would make people afraid to send their children to school, leave their homes to go to work, and contribute to their communities. For example, a commenter, stating that they are a teacher, said that a public registry of immigrants would make it impossible for their students to attend school every day, and they expressed fear for their own safety if the registry were implemented.
多位评论者谴责所提议的登记程序不公正且令人愤怒,另有评论者表示这将使社区动荡不安,还有评论者批评该政策十分危险,称文书错误正在伤害无辜者的生活。评论者补充说,登记计划并不能使社区更安全,反而会导致公民参与度下降和社区投资减少。一位评论者表达了类似的观点,写道该临时最终规则将对移民工人施加不明确且带有惩罚性的官僚程序,并建立一种一刀切的监管结构,无法区分高风险和低风险个人,也无法区分受法律保护者和不受保护者。他们表示,这种做法会助长人们回避合法程序,从而损害公共安全和经济参与。
Multiple commenters condemned the proposed registration process as unjust and infuriating, while others said it would destabilize communities, with another criticizing the policy as dangerous, saying that paperwork errors are harming innocent peoples' lives. Commenters added that registration programs do not make communities safer but instead would lead to civic disengagement and community divestment. Expressing a similar opinion, a commenter wrote that the IFR would impose unclear and punitive bureaucracy on immigrant workers and establishes a one-size-fits-all regulatory structure that does not differentiate between high-risk and low-risk individuals or those with legal protections and those without. They said this approach encourages disengagement from lawful processes, undermining both public safety and economic participation.
评论者写道,人们应当享有在社区中安全生活的权利,一位评论者表示,移民是生活各个方面(印刷版第39270页)不可或缺的组成部分,包括他们所在的社区。一位评论者笼统地表示,他们反对外国人登记,因为“实施这样的任务”将是对美国人民的一种损害。
Commenters wrote that people deserve to live safely in their communities, with a commenter saying immigrants are integral to all corners of ( printed page 39270) life, including their neighborhood. A commenter generally stated that they oppose the alien registration as it would be a disservice to the American people to “implement such a task.”
一位评论者根据个人经历表示,他们居住在一个有许多外国人为经济做出贡献的城市。他们对新程序可能导致家庭和亲人分离表示担忧,认为这是不公正的。一位评论者写道,凭借其45年来促进学习和跨文化社区建设的经验,他们认识到,如果该临时最终规则得以实施,将造成广泛的伤害。另一位评论者在未提供具体细节的情况下表达反对意见,写道该规则是朝法西斯主义迈出的更深一步,将伤害社区和整个国家。
Referring to personal experience, a commenter stated they live in a city with many aliens who contribute to the economy. They expressed concern about the potential separation of families and loved ones due to the new process, which they regarded as unjust. A commenter wrote that with their 45 years of experience in fostering learning and intercultural community, they recognize the widespread harm that would result if the IFR were implemented. Expressing opposition and without specifics, another commenter wrote that the rule is a step deeper into fascism and would hurt communities and the country at-large.
评论者写道,他们重视移民作为其社区重要成员的地位,并支持他们在应对漫长而复杂的入籍程序期间继续留在美国的能力。
Commenters wrote that they value immigrants as important members of their communities and support their ability to remain in the United States while navigating the lengthy and complex citizenship process.
回应：本规则制定并未改变国会确立的现行法定登记要求。数十年来,美国法律一直要求在美国境内停留30天或以上的大多数外国人进行登记,并在基于年龄或非移民身份的若干例外情况之外,还须按捺指纹。该法规进一步要求,凡年满18岁且依法须登记的在美外国人,须随时随身携带其所获发的外国人登记证或外国人登记收据卡。在临时最终规则（IFR）及相关信息收集发布之前,美国境内大多数外国人已经遵守了外国人登记要求。临时最终规则（IFR）填补了美国国土安全部（DHS）监管体系中的一项空白,规定了一种可供所有外国人申请的登记表格（无论其移民身份如何）,以及作为该登记证明的相应证据。美国国土安全部（DHS）认为,这一通用登记选项可能改善某些外国人群体的登记结果。
Response: This rulemaking has not changed the existing statutory registration requirements established by Congress. For many decades, the laws of the United States have required most aliens present in the United States who remain for 30 days or longer to register, and with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted. The statute further requires that all aliens 18 years or older in the United States who are required to register must at all times carry with them and have in their personal possession any certificate of alien registration or alien registration receipt card issued to them. Most aliens in the United States already complied with the alien registration requirement prior to the publication of the IFR and related information collection. The IFR fills a gap in the DHS's regulatory regime by prescribing a registration form available to all aliens regardless of their immigration status and corresponding evidence as proof of that registration. DHS believes that this general registration option may improve registration outcomes for certain groups of aliens.
c. 对学术界的影响
c. Impact on Academic Communities
意见：关于大学社区,一些评论者写道,该规则将影响学生入学情况,其中一位评论者引用了有关限制性移民对教育影响的研究。另一位评论者表示,美国是寻求提升技能和参与学术交流人士的首选目的地之一,并补充说，“非公民”学生、毕业生和研究人员提升了美国的劳动力水平、经济竞争力和全球领导地位。该评论者写道,在大学校园中,无论是短期还是长期访客，都丰富了学习体验，推动了知识进步与创新，并带来了有益于社区和国家的新视角。此外，他们写道，登记要求可能会使包括移民学生、学者和工人在内的移民群体不愿在美国高等教育机构注册或寻求就业。他们继续指出，这种参与度的下降将对美国的创新和研究产生负面影响，损害全球竞争力。该评论者表示，拥有更可预测、更易理解的移民制度的国家将在吸引顶尖人才方面占据优势，这也是近期国际学生和毕业生选择竞争国家而非美国这一趋势的原因之一。此外，他们指出，这还将影响依赖学生、教职员工的经济和社会贡献的周边社区。他们总结说，美国面临失去“非公民”群体即时消费支出以及长期智力和社会贡献的风险。
Comment: With regard to university communities, some commenters wrote that student enrollment would be affected by the rule, with one of those citing research highlighting the impact of restrictive immigration on education. Another commenter said the United States is a top destination for individuals seeking to build skills and engage in intellectual exchange, adding that “noncitizen” students, graduates, and researchers enhance the U.S. workforce, economic competitiveness, and global leadership. The commenter wrote that on college campuses, both short- and long-term visitors enrich learning, advance knowledge, drive innovation, and offer fresh perspectives that benefit communities and the nation. Further, they wrote that the registration requirement could discourage immigrant populations, including immigrant students, scholars, and workers, from enrolling or seeking employment at U.S. higher education institutions. They continued, saying that this reduction in participation would negatively impact U.S. innovation and research, harming global competitiveness. The commenter said that countries with more predictable and readily intelligible immigration systems would have an advantage in recruiting top talent, contributing to recent trends that see international students and graduates choosing competitor countries over the United States. Additionally, they remarked that it would affect surrounding communities that rely on the economic and social contributions of students, faculty, and staff. They concluded that the United States risks losing both immediate consumer spending and long-term intellectual and social contributions from “noncitizen” communities.
回应：美国国土安全部（DHS）通过临时最终规则（IFR）和最终规则，并未改变国会为外国人确立的现行法定登记要求。这项由来已久的法规一直规定，大多数年满14岁、在美国境内停留30天或以上的外国人，必须在该30天期限届满前申请登记并按捺指纹。参见《移民与国籍法》（INA）第262条，8 U.S.C. 1302。
Response: DHS, with the IFR and final rule, has not changed the existing statutory registration requirements established by Congress for aliens. This longstanding statute has always provided that most aliens over the age of 14 who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted before the expiration of the 30 days. See INA sec. 262, 8 U.S.C. 1302.
美国国土安全部（DHS）指出，许多在美国境内的外国人已依法完成登记。任何获发移民签证或非移民签证、并在其最近一次入境时凭该签证获准进入美国的外国人，均已完成登记。这包括作为非移民学生或交流访问者的外国人。
DHS notes that many aliens in the United States have already registered, as required by law. Any alien who was issued an immigrant or nonimmigrant visa and at his or her most recent arrival was admitted into the United States using that visa is registered. This includes aliens who are nonimmigrant students or exchange visitors.
D. 法律问题与法定条款
D. Legal Issues and Statutory Provisions
1. 一般法律权限及临时最终规则（IFR）的合法性
1. General Legal Authority and Legality of the IFR
评论:许多评论者讨论并比较了《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条,8 U.S.C. 1302项下的外国人登记制度与“9·11”事件后实施的NSEERS计划,称过去执行类似政策的尝试导致了对美国居民的不成比例的针对性执法、拘留和驱逐,并加剧了歧视。[26]例如,一位评论者指出,虽然NSEERS很少导致刑事起诉,但起诉却是本临时最终规则(IFR)的一个主要组成部分。评论者还表示,NSEERS导致了大量基于种族、民族和宗教的歧视,以及许多在法律上存疑的后果,包括可能违反第一修正案、第十四修正案以及民权方面的规定。一位评论者指出,NSEERS导致超过13,000人被置于遣返程序之中,却未能带来任何恐怖主义定罪,这与该计划所宣称的目的相悖。将NSEERS与本IFR进行比较时,一位评论者表示,监察长办公室2012年的一份报告发现,NSEERS效率低下,且与现有的、更为可靠的信息收集方法存在重复。该评论者表示,本IFR的登记系统将面临类似的问题,例如由于系统故障、延误及其他技术故障,以及在其仅限在线的模式下缺乏可及性(例如技术和语言障碍),登记人将难以遵守相关要求。
Comment: Many commenters discussed and compared alien registration under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, to NSEERS, a program implemented after 9/11, stating that past attempts to enforce similar policies led to the disproportionate targeting, detention and deportation of U.S. residents, and increased discrimination.[26] For example, a commenter remarked that while NSEERS rarely resulted in criminal prosecution, prosecution is a major component of this IFR. Commenters also stated that NSEERS led to prolific racial, ethnic, and religious discrimination and many legally questionable outcomes, including possible First and Fourteenth Amendment and civil rights violations. A commenter remarked that NSEERS resulted in over 13,000 people being placed in removal proceedings, yet produced no convictions for terrorism, contrary to the purported purpose of the program. Comparing NSEERS to the IFR, a commenter stated that an Office of Inspector General report in 2012 found that the NSEERS was ineffective and duplicative of existing, more reliable methods of information gathering. The commenter stated that the IFR's registration system would face similar problems, such as difficulty for registrants to adhere to requirements due to system outages and delays and other technical glitches and lack of access ( e.g., technology and language barriers) in its online-only model.
回应:本IFR及本最终规则的目的与NSEERS不同,NSEERS主要是依据INA第263条,8 U.S.C. 1303,为特定群体的登记制定标准。相比之下,本IFR仅确定了一份与已经适用的一般登记要求相关的附加登记表格。本IFR及本最终规则的目标是确保外国人有一种简便明了的方式来履行其登记义务。G-325R表格适用于所有未登记的外国人,无论其移民身份、宗教、国籍或种族如何。关于在线G-325R表格技术困难的评论,将在本前言第III.F.2.b节中作更详细的说明。
Response: The IFR and this final rule serves a different purpose than NSEERS, which primarily established criteria for the registration of special groups under section 263 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1303. In contrast, the IFR merely identifies an additional registration form related to general registration requirements that already apply. The goal of the IFR and this final rule is to ensure that aliens have a straightforward way to satisfy their obligation to register. The Form G-325R is available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status, religion, nationality, or race. Comments regarding technical difficulties with the online G-325R are addressed in more detail in section III.F.2.b of this preamble.
2. 本临时最终规则的法律依据
2. Legal Basis for the IFR
评论:一位评论者指出,尽管INA包含登记规定,但本IFR将超出该法律预期的行政职能范围,并会将登记制度武器化以实现大规模驱逐。另一位评论者写道,本IFR超出了INA第262条至第264条所授予的权限,指出这些条款规范的是入境文件要求,而非对已经居住在美国境内人员的追溯性登记。(第39271页打印版)许多评论者批评本IFR是一种企图,旨在依据《敌对外国人法》[27]便利驱逐个人,他们表示总统对该法的援引属于非法或不当行为,其目的在于以驱逐和刑事处罚恐吓外国人,并仅基于原国籍或所谓威胁,在无正当程序的情况下驱逐和拘留个人。另一位评论者写道,在本届政府“遵守现行法律、规则、法规及法院命令”之前,该部不应实施任何登记要求。
Comment: A commenter indicated that while the INA includes registration provisions, the IFR would exceed the law's intended administrative functions and would weaponize registration to effect mass removals. Another commenter wrote that the IFR exceeds sections 262 through 264 of the INA authorities, remarking that those provisions govern entry documentation, not retroactive registration of ( printed page 39271) individuals already residing in the United States. Many commenters criticized the IFR as an attempt to facilitate the removal of individuals under the Alien Enemies Act,[27] which they stated the President has invoked illegally or improperly, and for the purpose of terrorizing aliens with removal and criminal penalties, and removing and detaining individuals without due process solely based on national origin or perceived threats Another commenter wrote that the Department should not implement any registration requirements until the administration “complies with existing laws, rules, regulations, and court orders.”
回应:国土安全部(DHS)不同意有关本IFR意图“将登记制度武器化以实现大规模驱逐”,或意图恐吓外国人或在无正当程序情况下将其驱逐的说法。本规则也与《敌对外国人法》无关。INA第262条,8 U.S.C. 1302,仅要求在美国境内停留30天或以上的外国人进行登记和按捺指纹,这完全属于本国主权范围内可要求事项。本规则弥补了现行监管体系中的一项空白,并确保所有外国人,无论其移民身份如何,都有途径履行其法律规定的义务。该部有责任落实登记要求,并对美国所有移民法律进行管理和执行。
Response: DHS disagrees with the assertion that the intent of the IFR is to “weaponize registration to effect mass removals,” or to terrorize aliens or remove them without due process. This rule is also unrelated to the Alien Enemies Act. Section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, simply requires registration and fingerprinting of aliens in the United States for 30 days or more, which is well within this nation's sovereign prerogative to require. This rule addresses a gap in the existing regulatory regime and ensures that all aliens, regardless of their immigration status, have a way to comply with their duty under the law. The Department has a duty to implement the registration requirements and administer and enforce all the immigration laws of the United States.
a. 关于本规则基于过时或种族主义法律的主张
a. Claims That the Rule Is Based on Outdated or Racist Laws
评论:多位评论者通过援引历史上使用登记制度的实例,表达了其对外国人登记的反对意见,以此说明他们为何认为该临时最终规则(IFR)将导致对移民社区及美国社区乃至联邦政府造成侵犯和损害,并说明为何登记是一种维护美国安全的无效方法。例如,一位评论者写道,在第二次世界大战期间,1940年《外国人登记法》要求外国人向联邦政府登记,否则将面临监禁或罚款。关于第二次世界大战,评论者特别指出,截至1942年初,美国司法部(DOJ)已逮捕了2,192名日本国民、1,393名德国国民和264名意大利国民,且该法律还导致超过120,000名日裔人士(包括美国公民)被拘禁。多位评论者同样对该部援引二战期间曾导致拘禁使用的法规来处理当代移民事务表示普遍担忧。评论者援引研究结果指出,美国在二战期间的拘禁行为造成了具有长期影响的创伤,且后来被证实并无军事必要性支持。评论者写道,种族主义、战争歇斯底里和政治领导的失败,给相关社区带来了严重的不公正。
Comment: Several commenters expressed their opposition to the registration of aliens by citing to examples of historic use of registration as an explanation as to why they believe that the IFR would result in violations and harm to the immigrant and U.S. communities, as well as the Federal Government, and why registration is an ineffective method of keeping the United States safe. For example, a commenter wrote that during World War II the Alien Registration Act of 1940 required aliens to register with the Federal Government or face imprisonment or fine. Specifically relating to World War II, commenters noted that by early 1942, the U.S. Department of Justice (DOJ) had arrested 2,192 Japanese, 1,393 German, and 264 Italian nationals, and that this law also led to the internment of over 120,000 individuals of Japanese descent, including U.S. citizens. Several commenters similarly expressed general concerns about the Department invoking the statute that led to internment use during World War II for modern immigration issues. Citing to research, commenters indicated that U.S. internments during World War II caused trauma with long-lasting effect, and were later found to not be supported by military necessity. Commenters wrote that racism, war hysteria, and failure of political leadership led to grave injustice to communities.
一位评论者指出,虽然最高法院最初在“是松诉美国案”(Korematsu v. United States,1944年)[28]中维持了二战期间对数千名日裔居民实施监禁的做法,但最高法院在“特朗普诉夏威夷案”(Trump v. Hawaii,2018年)[29]中推翻了该判决,当时第一届特朗普政府在一起涉及针对特定群体的旅行禁令诉讼案中援引了“是松案”进行辩护。[30]评论者还指出,“入侵”这一说法已成为排外、歧视和反移民言论的一部分,持续了一个多世纪,例如1873年的“中国人入侵”说法煽动了对华人移民的仇恨,并为1882年《排华法案》及1924年《移民法》铺平了道路。其他评论者表示,该临时最终规则重新启用了一项二战时期的政策,该政策最初是在战时援引使用的,他们认为该政策已不再符合现代多元社会的价值观和需求。
A commenter stated that while the Supreme Court originally upheld the practice of the imprisonment of thousands of Japanese residents during World War II in Korematsu v. United States (1944),[28] it repudiated that decision in Trump v. Hawaii (2018),[29] when the first Trump administration cited to Korematsu in its defense in a lawsuit over a travel ban on certain people groups.[30] Commenters also remarked that the “invasion” narrative has been part of xenophobic, discriminatory, and anti-immigrant rhetoric for over a century, citing to examples such as the “Chinese Invasion” in 1873 that incited hate against Chinese immigrants and paved the way for the Chinese Exclusion Act of 1882 or the Immigration Act of 1924. Other commenters stated that the IFR reanimates a World War II-era policy originally invoked during a time of war, which they said no longer aligns with the values and needs of a modern and diverse society.
回应:2025年1月20日,特朗普总统签署了第14159号行政令《保护美国人民免受入侵》,指示国土安全部(DHS)确保外国人履行国会规定的法定义务,即依照《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条至第265条(8 U.S.C. 1302至1305)向政府登记。见联邦公报90 FR 8443, 8444(2025年1月29日)。总统进一步指示国土安全部和司法部确保将不遵守登记要求的行为作为民事和刑事执法的优先事项。见联邦公报90 FR 8443, 8444(2025年1月29日)。
Response: On January 20, 2025, President Trump issued E.O. 14159, Protecting the American People Against Invasion, which directed DHS to ensure that aliens comply with their statutory duty, as provided by Congress, to register with the government under sections 262 through 265 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1305. See90 FR 8443, 8444 (Jan. 29, 2025). The President further directed DHS and DOJ to ensure that failure to comply with the registration requirement is treated as a civil and criminal enforcement priority. See90 FR 8443, 8444 (Jan. 29, 2025).
许多在美国境内的外国人已依法通过8 CFR 264.1所规定的各种途径,或通过向国务院申请签证的程序完成了登记。然而,仍有相当数量在美外国人,包括许多此前从未被国土安全部接触过的外国人,没有直接的途径来登记并履行《移民与国籍法》第262条(8 U.S.C. 1302)所规定的登记义务。尽管评论中提及了上述历史背景,但国会是在二战结束后很久的1952年《移民与国籍法》中专门列入外国人登记要求的。国会作出了要求登记的政策选择,国土安全部只是依照总统的指示,对长期存在的法律要求进行管理和执行。设立新表格G-325R《生物识别信息(登记)》的目的,并非针对特定种族群体或出于其他歧视性目的,而是为了建立一个在线流程,使未登记的外国人能够登记并遵守《移民与国籍法》中有关外国人登记的规定。正如该临时最终规则所述,国土安全部确实认为,设立一般登记表选项的好处之一在于能够改善外国人的登记结果,从而提高执法效能。见联邦公报90 FR 11793, 11797(2025年3月12日)。
Many aliens in the United States have already registered, as required by law, through a variety of pathways identified in 8 CFR 264.1 or through the visa application process with State. However, a significant number of aliens present in the United States, including many who have not previously been encountered by DHS, have no straightforward way to register and meet their registration obligations under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. Despite the history cited in the comments, Congress specifically included alien registration requirements in the INA of 1952, well after World War II had ended. Congress made the policy choice to require registration; DHS is merely administering and enforcing longstanding legal requirements, consistent with the President's direction. The purpose of establishing the new form, G-325R, Biometric Information (Registration), is not to target racial groups or for other discriminatory purposes, but to create an online process by which unregistered aliens may register and comply with the alien registration provisions of the INA. As explained in the IFR, DHS does believe that one of the benefits of the designation of a general registration form option is that it will improve the registration outcomes for aliens, which in turn will result in improved law enforcement efficacy. See90 FR 11793, 11797 (Mar. 12, 2025).
评论:许多评论者对该规则的法定依据表示担忧,许多人指出《移民与国籍法》(INA)中的外国人登记要求:(1)已经过时或与当前移民形势不相符;(2)源于不同的历史背景;(3)不适合解决当前的美国移民体系问题;或(4)与包容性、公平和人权等现代价值观不一致。评论者指出,美国在过去75年中实际上已经放弃了普遍登记制度,并且在1952年和1965年对联邦移民法进行全面修订后,美国政府已将登记事项转移至常规化的移民申请和执法程序中。
Comment: Many commenters expressed concern with the statutory basis for the rule, with many stating that the alien registration requirements of the INA: (1) are obsolete or misaligned with the current immigration landscape; (2) originated in a different historical context; (3) are not well suited to address the current U.S. immigration system; or (4) do not align with modern values of inclusivity, fairness, and human rights. Commenters stated that the United States has effectively abandoned universal registration for the past 75 years, and that after the overhaul of federal immigration law in 1952 and 1965, the U.S. Government shifted registration into regularized immigration applications and enforcement.
许多评论者认为,强制登记既不符合现代移民法律和实践,也无法反映其内涵,并且与我们现行法律的立法意图相悖。例如,评论者指出,该临时最终规则(IFR)实质上恢复了一项被认为不必要(印刷页第39272页)且与该国战后移民框架无关的政策。对该临时最终规则表示担忧的评论者指出,1940年《外国人登记法》(也称为《史密斯法》)自1940年以来一直处于休眠状态,从未如该临时最终规则这般大规模实施,而且该规则将带来负面的经济和社会成本,同时为美国人和美国带来的益处微乎其微。评论者同样指出,该临时最终规则标志着对过去80年间该登记法规狭义适用方式的显著背离,并且该法规所体现的第二次世界大战时期独立登记程序自1950年以来已被废弃。
Many commenters believed that mandatory registration is not commensurate with, nor reflective of, modern immigration law and practice, and is contrary to the intent of our current laws. For example, commenters remarked that the IFR essentially revives a policy deemed unnecessary ( printed page 39272) and irrelevant to the country's postwar immigration framework. Commenters, expressing concerns with the IFR, stated that the Alien Registration Act of 1940 (also known as the Smith Act) had been dormant, and not operationalized on such a large scale as the IFR since 1940, and that it would have negative economic and societal costs while providing minimal benefits to Americans and the United States. Commenters similarly remarked that the IFR marks a stark departure from the narrow application of the registration statute over the past 80 years and that the World War II-era independent registration process reflected in the statute has been abandoned since 1950.
其他评论者写道,本届政府正在“将曾经无法执行且已过时的法律武器化”,这些法律根植于战时的仇外心理,而该临时最终规则正在破坏民主、自由和人权,并助长“不人道且种族主义”的移民政策扩散。评论者建议国土安全部(DHS)应完全废除8 CFR第264部分,理由是“基础法律制度已经过时”。
Other commenters wrote that the administration is “weaponizing once unenforceable and obsolete laws” rooted in wartime xenophobia, and with the IFR, is undermining democracy, freedom, and human rights, and is proliferating “inhumane and racist” immigration policies. Commenters suggested that DHS remove 8 CFR part 264 entirely due to the “obsolescence of the underlying legal regime.”
一些评论者指出,在当前移民背景下适用《史密斯法》的方式是通过该临时最终规则来实现,这种做法并不恰当,因为美国目前并未处于战争状态,而《史密斯法》的宗旨和立法意图基于第二次世界大战时期的政策,旨在保护美国免受敌对势力潜在入侵,并防止寻求推翻美国政府的共产主义者移民入境。因此,评论者总结认为,《史密斯法》诞生于截然不同的历史背景,而当前的情况涉及的是多年来一直在美国生活并为社会做出贡献的个人和家庭,包括那些正在等待其庇护或难民身份得到解决的人员。鉴于这些担忧,许多评论者表示登记要求应予以废除或修改。一位评论者建议,国会应就此项及其他移民政策进行“适当的讨论”。
Some commenters stated that the IFR is an inappropriate way to apply the Smith Act in today's immigration context because the country is not at war and the Smith Act's purpose and intent, which was based on World War II policies, was to protect the United States from potential invasion by an enemy power and prevent communists who sought to overthrow the U.S. Government from immigrating. Thus, commenters concluded that the Smith Act was created in a vastly different context, while the current circumstances involve individuals and families who have been living and contributing to society for years, including those who are waiting for a resolution regarding their asylee or refugee status. In light of these concerns, many commenters stated that the registration requirement should be either rescinded or modified. A commenter suggested that Congress should have a “proper discussion” around this and other immigration policies.
一位评论者写道,虽然登记要求于1952年被纳入《移民与国籍法》,但政府此前从未利用这些条款来支持建立一套适用于所有外国人的独立的全国性登记程序。该评论者表示,相反,国土安全部的法规已明确列出多种可作为登记证明的移民表格,例如I-94表(入境/离境记录)、I-862表(出庭通知书,NTA)、I-766表(就业授权文件,EAD)以及I-551表(永久居民卡)等。该评论者表示担忧,在该临时最终规则出台之前,政府并未为未被上述列举表格覆盖的个人维持另一套登记程序,然而,该规则将要求数百万人,包括儿童,在没有指导或法律援助的情况下,理解该临时最终规则复杂的条款规定,并填写一份单独的表格,提供与其移民救济案件相关的信息。
A commenter wrote that while registration requirements were added to the INA in 1952, the government has not previously leveraged these provisions to support a separate, national registration process applicable to all aliens. Instead, the commenter said, DHS regulations have identified various immigration forms that constitute evidence of registration, such as Form I-94 (Arrival/Departure Record), Form I-862 (NTA), Form I-766 (EAD), and Form I-551 (Permanent Resident Card), among others. The commenter expressed concern that, prior to the IFR, the government did not maintain another process for individuals not covered by the enumerated forms to register, yet, this rule would require millions of people, including children, to interpret the IFR's complex provisions and complete a separate form requesting information with bearing on their cases for immigration relief, without guidance or legal assistance.
答复:自1940年以来,美国法律一直要求绝大多数在美逗留30天或以上的外国人进行登记,并且除因年龄或非移民身份而有若干例外情形外,须按捺指纹。美国国土安全部(DHS)同意该法律是在不同的历史时期制定的,但许多法律及《移民与国籍法》(INA)的许多条款均是如此。自国会最初颁布《史密斯法案》以来,国会已于1952年将外国人登记要求编入《移民与国籍法》,此后又多次修订《移民与国籍法》,但从未取消这些要求。仅举若干例子而非穷尽列举:国会于1994年修订了《移民与国籍法》第262条,即《美国法典》第8编第1302条,见1994年10月25日第103-416号公法第二编第219(n)条(技术性修正);于1988年修订,见1988年10月24日第100-525号公法第8(h)条(增加指纹豁免权限);于1986年修订,见1986年11月14日第99-653号公法第9条(技术性修正)。国会还于1996年修订了《移民与国籍法》第264条,即《美国法典》第8编第1304条,见1996年9月30日第104-208号公法第C分编第四编第415条(要求外国人提供社会安全号码(SSN)的权限);以及于1990年修订,见1990年11月29日第101-649号公法第五编第503(b)(2)条(增加对《移民与国籍法》第287(f)(2)条,即《美国法典》第8编第1357(f)(2)条项下信息共享的提及)。此外,作为2008年将美国移民法律扩展适用于北马里亚纳群岛联邦的法律的一部分,国会赋予部长额外的外国人登记权限,并明确澄清:“本款任何内容均不得修改或限制《移民与国籍法》第262条(《美国法典》第8编第1302条)或《移民与国籍法》中与外国人登记有关的其他条款。”见《美国法典》第48编第1806(e)(3)条。
Response: Since 1940, the laws of the United States have required most aliens present in the United States who remain for 30 days or longer to register, and with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted. DHS agrees that the law was enacted at a different time in history, but this is true of many laws and many provisions of the INA. Since the time Congress first enacted the Smith Act, Congress has codified alien registration requirements into the INA in 1952 and subsequently amended the INA many times without eliminating these requirements. To offer a non-exhaustive list, Congress amended section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, in 1994, see Public Law 103-416, title II, sec. 219(n) (Oct. 25, 1994) (technical correction); in 1988, see Public Law 100-525, sec. 8(h) (Oct. 24, 1988) (adding fingerprinting waiver authority); and in 1986, see Public Law 99-653, sec. 9 (Nov. 14, 1986) (technical correction). And Congress amended section 264 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304 in 1996, seePublic Law 104-208, Div. C, title IV, sec. 415 (Sept. 30, 1996) (authority to require aliens to provide their Social Security numbers (SSNs)), and in 1990, see Public Law 101-649, title V, sec. 503(b)(2) (Nov. 29, 1990) (adding reference to information sharing under section 287(f)(2) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1357(f)(2)). And as part of a 2008 law extending U.S. immigration laws to the Commonwealth of the Northern Mariana Islands, Congress provided the Secretary with additional alien registration authority and specifically clarified that “[n]othing in this paragraph shall modify or limit section 262 of the Immigration and Nationality Act (8 U.S.C. 1302) or other provision of the Immigration and Nationality Act relating to the registration of aliens.” 48 U.S.C. 1806(e)(3).
多年来,美国国土安全部(DHS)和美国国务院(State)持续为许多外国人提供多种登记途径,并对相关登记规定进行修改,以反映当前的程序和流程。然而,在第14159号行政命令发布后,美国国土安全部意识到现行监管体系存在缺口,致使部分外国人难以履行登记要求。总统负有确保法律得到忠实执行的职责,美国国土安全部通过建立这一通用登记表格和流程,正是在忠实执行这些法律。
DHS and State have continued over the years to provide ways for many aliens to register by providing several pathways to do so and made changes to the registration provisions in the regulations to reflect current procedures and processes. However, following the issuance of E.O. 14159, DHS was aware that there was a gap in the existing regulatory regime that made it difficult for some aliens to comply with the registration requirement. The President is charged with taking care that the laws be faithfully executed, and by establishing this general registration form and process, DHS is faithfully executing these laws.
评论意见:一位评论者列举了其认为与本临时最终规则(IFR)具有可比性且具有歧视性的其他美国法律或项目,包括1924年《约翰逊-里德法案》、1941年《亲属规则》,以及1917年至1921年和1942年至1964年的“布拉塞罗计划”。其他评论者在质疑本临时最终规则的法律和宪法依据的同时,将其比作国外历史上的歧视性法律制度,例如纳粹德国或种族隔离时期南非的相关制度。
Comment: A commenter listed other U.S. laws or programs that they compared to the IFR and that they said were discriminatory, including the Johnson-Reed Act (1924), the Relatives Rule (1941), and the Bracero programs (1917-21 and 1942-64). Other commenters, while criticizing the legal and constitutional basis for the IFR, likened it to historical discriminatory legal regimes abroad, such as those in Nazi Germany or apartheid-era South Africa.
答复:国外历史上的歧视性制度与外国人登记要求并无关联性。此外,美国国土安全部不同意评论者所引用的美国法律或项目与外国人登记要求具有可比性。与外国人登记要求不同,评论者所引用的法律或项目要么已被废止,要么从未被纳入《移民与国籍法》。外国人登记要求已被纳入《移民与国籍法》,并经国会定期更新,最近一次更新是在20世纪90年代。[31]
Response: The historically discriminatory regimes abroad do not correlate to the alien registration requirement. Furthermore, DHS disagrees that the U.S. laws or programs cited by the commenter are comparable to the alien registration requirement. Unlike the alien registration requirement, the referenced laws or programs were either repealed or were not incorporated into the INA. The alien registration requirement was incorporated into the INA periodically updated by Congress, with the most recent updates in the 1990s.[31]
b. 第14159号行政命令第7节
b. E.O. 14159, Sec. 7
评论意见:一位评论者反对本临时最终规则,称其依据的行政命令包含“宽泛的指令和煽动性的仇外言论”。一位评论者在质疑该规则的法律依据时表示,该规则所实施的行政命令是不合法的。该评论者写道,该行政命令试图将因迫害、战争、饥荒和自然灾害而产生的“合法且必要的”移民行为定性为“入侵”。该评论者补充说,如果政府的真实意图是驱逐暴力罪犯,则可以通过交叉核对各州刑事数据库并集中精力驱逐这些人员来实现这一目标。该评论者写道,相反,本临时最终规则将使难民和寻求庇护者沦为罪犯。其他评论者表示,该行政命令“从本质上源于基于种族的偏见”。
Comment: A commenter objected to the IFR, stating that it is based on an E.O. containing “broad mandates and inflammatory, xenophobic language.” Questioning the legal basis of the rule, a commenter stated that the E.O. that the rule implements is unlawful. The commenter wrote that the E.O. seeks to characterize “lawful and necessary” migration driven by persecution, war, famine, and natural disasters as an “invasion.” The commenter added that if the government's true intent were to remove violent criminals, it could accomplish this by cross-referencing State criminal databases and focusing on removing these individuals. Instead, the commenter wrote, the IFR would criminalize refugees and asylum seekers. Other commenters stated that the E.O. is “inherently founded in race-based prejudice.”
回应:国土安全部(DHS)不同意评论者的意见,并指出行政令中的措辞选择不影响执行行政令第7条的合法性,该条仅指示国土安全部和其他机构忠实执行(印刷页第39273页)国会制定的与外国人登记相关的法律。
Response: DHS disagrees with the commenters and notes that the language choices in the E.O. have no impact on the lawfulness of implementing section 7 of the E.O., which merely directs DHS and other agencies to faithfully execute ( printed page 39273) the laws established by Congress relating to alien registration.
c. 重大问题原则与国会意图
c. Major Questions Doctrine and Congressional Intent
评论:评论者指出,临时最终规则(IFR)涉及具有重大国家意义的问题,且没有明确的国会授权支持,因此法院在评估该规则的合法性时应适用“重大问题原则”。一位评论者认为,该临时最终规则施加的登记义务影响220万至320万人,其可能目的是驱逐个人并对未登记者施加刑事处罚。该评论者以及其他评论者表示,该临时最终规则实际上将非法身份刑事化,而非法身份历来属于民事违法行为。该评论者写道,国会曾在2005年《边境保护、反恐怖主义和非法移民控制法案》中考虑对在美国非法居留设立刑事处罚,但最终予以否决。
Comment: Commenters indicated that the IFR addresses a question of major national significance and is not clearly supported by clear congressional authorization, such that courts should apply the “major questions doctrine” when evaluating the rule's lawfulness. A commenter reasoned that the IFR imposes a registration obligation that impacts between 2.2 and 3.2 million people, with the likely purpose of removing individuals and conferring criminal penalties on those who fail to register. This commenter, as well as others, stated that the IFR effectively criminalizes unlawful status, which has historically been a civil offense. The commenter wrote that Congress had considered creating criminal penalties for unlawful presence in the United States in the Border Protection, Antiterrorism, and Illegal Immigration Control Act of 2005 but rejected doing so.
该评论者指出,国会据称未将非法居留刑事化,加之长期以来登记用途的普遍转变,表明该临时最终规则设立此类刑事处罚违背了国会意图和该法规的立法目的。该评论者援引West Virginia v. EPA案,597 U.S. 697(2022年),认为该临时最终规则利用一项过时且已搁置的国家安全条款来实施重大政策变更,违反了重大问题原则。因此,该评论者认为,此类问题应留待国会通过立法予以考虑。
The commenter observed that Congress' alleged failure to criminalize unlawful presence, combined with the general and long-standing shift in the use of registration, indicated that the IFR creating such criminal penalties is contrary to congressional intent and the purpose of the statute. Citing West Virginia v. EPA, 597 U.S. 697 (2022), the commenter opined that the IFR uses an outdated and dormant national security provision to effect a major policy change, violating the major questions doctrine. Therefore, the commenter concluded that its issues should be left to Congress to consider for legislation.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则创建了一种“双轨登记制度”,在该制度下,外国人要么通过提交G-325R表格承认非法入境的罪行,要么因未登记而获罪。该评论者认为,这种做法与“国会创建的单轨登记制度”不一致,在后者之下,国会设想外国人能够在无需承认任何罪行的情况下进行登记。
A commenter stated that the IFR created a “two-track registration system” under which an alien would either admit to the crime of illegal entry by submitting Form G-325R or be guilty of failing to register. The commenter suggested that this approach is inconsistent with “the single-track registration system created by Congress,” under which Congress contemplated that aliens would be able to register without needing to admit to any crimes.
少数评论者表示,该临时最终规则是对国会赋予美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)作为移民福利和裁决机构使命的“背叛”,因为登记是国土安全部和政府用于移民执法的工具。
A few commenters stated that the IFR represents a “betrayal” of the mission with which Congress charged USCIS as an immigration benefits and adjudications agency, insofar as registration is an immigration enforcement tool for DHS and the administration.
回应:关于重大问题原则,[32]国会在《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条至第265条,即8 U.S.C. 第1302条至第1305条中,明确授权并指示国土安全部[33]对外国人进行登记,并在《移民与国籍法》第264条,即8 U.S.C. 第1304条中,指示其为根据《移民与国籍法》第262条,即8 U.S.C. 第1302条进行的外国人登记及相关行为制定表格。[34]国会还明确指示国土安全部在外国人登记表格中列入对“外国人进入美国的日期和地点”的询问。《移民与国籍法》第264条(a)(1)款,8 U.S.C. 第1304条(a)(1)款。决定向必须登记者提供登记表格,并在该表格中列入国会要求国土安全部询问的问题,并不涉及重大问题原则,国土安全部不同意评论者将此规则定性为West Virginia案中所述具有重大政治或经济意义的行为。然而,即便此事项具有重大政治或经济意义,其也有明确的国会授权作为支持。
Response: In relation to the major questions doctrine,[32] Congress, in sections 262 through 265 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1305, specifically authorized and directed DHS [33] to register aliens, and in section 264 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304, to prescribe forms for the registration of aliens under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, and related actions.[34] Congress also specifically directed DHS to include, on alien registration forms, inquiries into “the date and place of entry of the alien into the United States.” INA sec. 264(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1304(a)(1). The decision to offer a registration form to those who must register, and to include on that form the questions that Congress required DHS to ask, does not implicate the major questions doctrine and DHS disagrees with the commenter's characterization of this rule as an action of major political or economic significance as described in West Virginia. However, even if the matter were of great political or economic significance, it would be supported by clear congressional authorization.
然而,正如临时最终规则序言中所解释的,自1940年以来,联邦法规通常要求在美国停留30天或以上的外国人进行登记并留取指纹。
However, as explained in the preamble to the IFR, Federal statutes have, since 1940, generally required aliens present in the United States for 30 days or more to register and be fingerprinted.
DHS还不认同本规则使在美国非法身份或非法居留行为入罪化的说法。移民与国籍法(INA)长期以来一直包含将各种与移民相关的行为入罪化的条款。参见,例如,INA第264(e)条、第366条、第272至278条,8 U.S.C. 1304(e)、1306、1322至1328条。因此,尽管评论者正确地指出移民违规行为通常作为民事事项处理,但评论者所担忧的任何刑事责任仍然是基于长期存在的法定条款而产生的。临时最终规则(IFR)中指定一份登记表格及登记证明作为个人履行法定登记要求的一般方式,并未不当偏离国会意图或该法规的明确含义。
DHS also disagrees that this rule criminalizes unlawful status or presence in the United States. The INA has long contained provisions criminalizing various forms of immigration-related conduct. See, e.g., INA secs. 264(e), 366, 272-278, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e), 1306, 1322-1328. Thus, although the commenter is correct that immigration violations are often handled as civil matters, any criminal liability feared by the commenter nevertheless exists by virtue of longstanding statutory provisions. Nothing in the IFR, by designating a registration form and evidence of registration as a general means for individuals to comply with the statutory registration requirement, impermissibly deviates from congressional intent or the plain meaning of the statute.
d. 关于授权及与授权性法规一致性的其他评论
d. Other Comments on Authority and Alignment With Authorizing Statutes
评论:一位评论者表示,“新的登记要求”属于“重复”,因为INA第262(a)条,8 U.S.C. 1302(a),已经要求受涵盖的外国人进行登记。一位评论者写道,自我登记对于执行该法定制度并非必要,并指出政府的利益,即“对未妥善登记的非公民予以入罪化”,并不重大,因为许多州已经为外国人提供身份证(ID)。评论者还表示,登记的目的并非发现和起诉犯罪外国人,而是使所有未持有G-325R表格的外国人入罪化。
Comment: A commenter stated that the “new registration requirement” is “redundant,” because section 262(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(a), already requires covered aliens to register. A commenter wrote that self-registration is not necessary to enforce the statutory scheme, stating that the government's interest—“the criminalization of non-citizens without proper registration”—is not significant, as many States already provide an identification card (ID) for aliens. Commenters also stated that the purpose of registration is not to detect and prosecute criminal aliens, but rather, to criminalize all aliens who do not have the Form G-325R.
回应:登记要求并非新设或重复。国会在INA第262条,8 U.S.C. 1302中规定了该要求,并指示DHS实施该要求。DHS指定新的登记表格,是为了解决现行关于登记的监管体系中存在的空白,使所有外国人,无论其身份如何,均能履行INA第262条,8 U.S.C. 1302项下的登记义务。无论各州提供何种其他身份证件,DHS均有义务忠实执行国会制定的法律,包括外国人登记要求。关于“所有未持有”新登记证明的外国人将被入罪化这一具体评论,该说法并不准确。美国境内许多外国人已经完成登记并持有登记证明,无需使用新表格及程序。
Response: The registration requirement is not new or redundant. Congress prescribed the requirement in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, and directed DHS to implement the requirement. DHS designated the new registration form to address a gap in the existing regulatory regime relating to registration and allow all aliens, regardless of their status, to fulfill their duty to register under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. DHS has the obligation to faithfully execute the laws established by Congress, including the alien registration requirement, regardless of other ID provided by States. In response to the specific comment about the criminalization of “all aliens who do not have” the new evidence of registration, this is inaccurate. Many aliens inside the United States are already registered and have evidence of registration without the need to utilize the new form and process.
e. 家庭暴力、性侵犯及人口贩运幸存者面临的法律问题
e. Legal Issues for Survivors of Domestic Violence, Sexual Assault, and Human Trafficking
评论:评论者讨论了为家庭暴力、性侵犯、人口贩运及其他严重犯罪的幸存者提供保护的立法历史,例如《妇女暴力防治法》(VAWA)和《受害者保护法》(VTVPA)。评论者指出,30多年前,两党一致的国会认识到移民幸存者(印刷页第39274页)所面临的风险,制定了VAWA,为遭受殴打或极端残忍对待的幸存者创设了移民保护措施,使其能够在不知情或未经施虐伴侣同意的情况下申请移民救济,为其提供通往安全与独立的途径。评论者继续指出,国会通过VTVPA加强了保护,该法为人口贩运受害者创设了T非移民身份(“T签证”),并为愿意配合执法部门的特定符合条件犯罪受害者创设了U非移民身份(“U签证”)。评论者批评该临时最终规则,称其无视这些法律的国会立法意图,使暴力幸存者在其案件得到解决之前便暴露于移民执法之下,剥夺了国会明确为其创设的保护措施。一位评论者强调了VAWA和VTVPA保护措施的历史重要性,并敦促DHS维护这些保护措施。
Comment: Commenters discussed the history of legislation providing protections for survivors of domestic violence, sexual assault, human trafficking, and other serious crimes, such as VAWA and the VTVPA. The commenters remarked that over 30 years ago, a bipartisan Congress recognized the risks faced by aliens who are ( printed page 39274) immigrant survivors when it enacted VAWA, which created immigration protections for survivors who experience battery or extreme cruelty that allow them to apply for immigration relief without the knowledge or consent of the abusive partner, giving them a path to safety and independence. The commenters continued, writing that Congress strengthened protections through VTVPA, which created the T nonimmigrant status (“T visa”) for victims of trafficking and the U nonimmigrant status (“U visa”) for victims of certain qualifying crimes who are willing to cooperate with law enforcement. The commenters criticized the IFR, stating that it disregards the congressional intent of these laws by exposing survivors of violence to immigration enforcement before their cases are resolved and deprives them of the protections Congress explicitly created for them. A commenter emphasized the historical importance of VAWA and VTVPA protections and urged DHS to uphold them.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则中有关地址变更要求的内容对外国人造成了不必要的困难,因为这将使其无法获得私密、安全的地址,从而无法在摆脱施虐者、迈向独立的过程中保护自己。
A commenter said that the IFR's content regarding change of address requirements creates needless hardships for aliens as it would deprive them of private, safe addresses to protect themselves from their abusers as they take steps toward independence.
一位评论者指出,《美国法典》第8编第1367条禁止国土安全部(DHS)使用施虐者提供的信息作出不利裁定,或披露有关外国人受害者的信息。该评论者表示关切,临时最终规则(IFR)并未说明如何维持这些隐私保护措施,特别是关于表格G-325R上的地址申报问题,并且IFR也未考虑到为保障符合条件的外国人安全而规定地址保密的家庭暴力法律。该评论者总结道,尽管IFR提到了“安全地址”,但并未解释该概念在登记背景下的含义,表格G-325R似乎也不允许地址保密。
A commenter remarked that 8 U.S.C. 1367 prohibits DHS from making adverse determinations using information furnished by abusers or disclosing information about alien victims. The commenter expressed concern that the IFR does not address how these privacy protections, particularly with respect to address reporting on Form G-325R, would be maintained, and that the IFR does not account for domestic violence laws that establish address confidentiality for eligible aliens for their safety. The commenter concluded that despite the IFR mentioning “safe addresses,” it does not explain what that means in the context of registration, nor does Form G-325R appear to allow for address confidentiality.
另一位评论者写道,IFR未能解决身处庇护所及不安全地址的外国人受害者的特殊情况;根据美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的特殊保护措施,申请妇女暴力受害者法(VAWA)、T类和U类福利的申请人有权使用安全邮寄地址,并依照严格的隐私规则处理其案件。该评论者表示,IFR要求此类外国人使用并非由“佛蒙特服务中心特殊申请人办公室”处理的普通登记表进行登记,这将违反上述隐私保护措施;并且与其他移民信息收集不同,IFR未包含任何与保护此类数据以符合VAWA强化保护要求的程序相关的规定。该评论者补充说,披露庇护所地址通常违反庇护所的政策,这样做可能导致外国人丧失在该庇护所居住的资格。
Another commenter wrote that the IFR failed to address the special circumstances of alien victims in shelters and at unsafe addresses; under USCIS special protections, applicants for VAWA, T- and U-based benefits are entitled to use safe mailing addresses and have their cases processed according to strict privacy rules. The commenter stated that the IFR would violate those privacy protections by demanding that such aliens register using a general registration form that is not processed by the “special applicants' office of the Vermont Service Center,” and that, unlike other immigration information collections, the IFR includes no provisions related to procedures that would be used to protect this data in conformity with heightened VAWA protections. The commenter added that disclosing shelter addresses is often against the policies of the shelter and doing so can result in aliens becoming ineligible for housing there.
回应:提交表格G-325R的外国人,并不因提交该表格而自动成为《美国法典》第8编第1367条所保护的外国人;但是,若该外国人基于其他理由已被认定为受保护外国人,USCIS将对通过表格G-325R程序提交的信息,提供与该受保护外国人所提供的其他信息同等的保护。表格G-325R收集实际地址信息,并允许外国人提供安全地址。与所有允许外国人提供安全地址的USCIS表格一样,若USCIS通过邮寄方式联系该外国人,将使用其所提供的安全地址。然而,目前G-325R的整个流程均通过电子方式进行。USCIS发送给外国人的所有通知均上传至该外国人的myUSCIS账户。与表格G-325R相关的所有通知均不通过邮寄方式发出。因此,不仅外国人可依照USCIS长期以来的做法提供安全地址,而且USCIS目前也不会就表格G-325R相关事宜通过邮寄方式发送任何文件。尽管要求外国人提供实际地址,DHS将继续确保工作人员遵守法定要求和保护措施。
Response: Aliens submitting a Form G-325R do not qualify as a protected alien under 8 U.S.C. 1367, by virtue of such filing; however, if the alien is otherwise designated as a protected alien on another basis, USCIS would maintain the same protections for information submitted through the Form G-325R process as it does for other information provided by such protected aliens. Form G-325R collects a physical address and allows aliens to provide a safe address. As with all USCIS forms in which an alien may provide a safe address, if USCIS contacts the alien through the mail it will use the safe address that he or she provides. However, the G-325R process is entirely electronic at this time. All notices sent from USCIS to an alien are uploaded to the alien's myUSCIS account. None of the notices correlating to a Form G-325R are issued via mail. Therefore, not only may aliens provide a safe address, consistent with longstanding USCIS practice, USCIS does not at this time send any documents through the mail in connection with Form G-325R. While an alien is required to provide a physical address, DHS will continue to ensure that personnel adhere to statutory requirements and protections.
3. 与IFR相关的宪法问题
3. Constitutional Concerns Related to the IFR
评论:多位评论者对IFR或登记要求的合宪性表达了总体关切。例如,一位反对IFR的评论者敦促当权者停止这种“公然违反”美国宪法的行为,并表示参与此过程但未试图加以阻止的相关人员未尽到其对美国宪法应尽的职责。评论者批评IFR,称其“未能考虑其对公民和非公民宪法权利所造成的广泛影响”。
Comment: Several commenters expressed general concerns related to the constitutionality of the IFR or registration requirement. For example, a commenter expressing opposition to the IFR urged those in power to stop the “blatant violation” of the U.S. Constitution and indicated that those involved in this process who do not attempt to stop it are in dereliction of their duty to the U.S. Constitution. Commenters criticized the IFR, saying that it “fails to account for its sweeping impact on the constitutional rights of citizens and noncitizens alike.”
回应:DHS不认同评论者对本规则违宪的笼统定性。本规则提供了一种新机制,供外国人依照国会指示,遵守长期存在的法定登记和指纹采集要求。忠实执行国会制定的法律是行政部门的宪法职责。DHS将在本最终规则后文中,就评论中提出的具体合宪性关切作出回应。
Response: DHS disagrees with commenters' broad characterization of this rule as unconstitutional. This rule makes available a new mechanism by which aliens may comply with longstanding statutory registration and fingerprinting requirements, as directed by Congress. It is the Executive's constitutional duty to faithfully execute the law created by Congress. DHS responds to comments raising specific constitutionality concerns later in this final rule.
关于美国政府要求境内外国人进行登记的一般权力,国土安全部(DHS)指出,一个国家拥有控制其边境的主权特权。参见平诉美国案,130 U.S. 581, 603-04(1889年)(“美国政府通过立法部门的行为将外国人排除在其领土之外,这一主张我们认为并无争议之处。对本国领土享有此种管辖权是每个独立国家固有的特性,是其独立性的一部分。倘若一国不能排除外国人,则在此范围内该国即受制于另一权力。”)。国会早在很久以前就为履行这一至关重要的职责而立法,其中包括设立法定的登记和指纹采集要求。此外,美国宪法将移民事务的控制权同时赋予国会和行政部门;最高法院一个多世纪以来一直承认,总统广泛的外交事务权力延伸至移民事务。参见美国宪法第二条第二款第二项;冯越婷诉美国案,149 U.S. 698, 713(1893年)(认定移民管理属于“影响国际关系的权力”,因此理应“归属于政府的政治部门”)。这些广泛的宪法权力进一步支持了法定的外国人登记要求,以及总统指示国土安全部忠实执行此类要求的权力。它们为本临时最终规则(IFR)提供了充分的权力依据,该规则仅是指定了一种新表格,供外国人用以遵守长期存在的法定要求。
With respect to the U.S. Government's general authority to require aliens within its borders to register, DHS notes that a nation has a sovereign prerogative to control its borders. Cf. Ping v. United States, 130 U.S. 581, 603-04 (1889) (“That the government of the United States, through the action of the legislative department, can exclude aliens from its territory is a proposition which we do not think open to controversy. Jurisdiction over its own territory to that extent is an incident of every independent nation. It is a part of its independence. If it could not exclude aliens it would be to that extent subject to the control of another power.”). Congress long ago legislated in pursuit of this utmost responsibility by, among others, setting statutory registration and fingerprinting requirements. What is more, the U.S. Constitution vests both Congress and the Executive with control over immigration matters; the Supreme Court for over a century has acknowledged that the President's broad foreign affairs power extends to matters of immigration. U.S. Const. Art. II, § 2, cl. 2; Fong Yue Ting v. United States, 149 U.S. 698, 713 (1893) (finding because the regulation of immigration is “a power affecting international relations,” it appropriately “is vested in the political departments of the government”). These broad constitutional authorities further support the statutory alien registration requirements and the President's authority to direct DHS to take care to faithfully execute such requirements. They provide ample authority for the IFR, which merely designates a new form by which aliens can comply with longstanding statutory requirements.
a. 反对自证其罪的权利
a. The Right Against Self-Incrimination
评论意见:评论者普遍担忧,本临时最终规则所要求的披露事项,例如报告既往犯罪活动的要求,将违反宪法第五修正案中反对自证其罪的权利,会使外国人处于为执法目的而自我暴露身份的境地,或总体上违反第五修正案权利。一位评论者表示,本临时最终规则“在表面上即属违宪”,因为它要求外国人承认犯罪行为及违反刑事药物滥用法律的行为,并要求未持有合法证件在美国境内的外国人进行登记( 第39275页印刷版),“尽管本届政府认定无证移民属于非法”。
Comment: Commenters expressed general concern that the disclosures required by the IFR, such as the requirement to report past criminal activity, would violate the Fifth Amendment right against self-incrimination, would place aliens in a position of identifying themselves for purposes of enforcement, or would generally violate Fifth Amendment rights. One commenter stated that the IFR is “unconstitutional on its face,” because it would ask aliens to confess to crimes and violations of criminal substance abuse laws and requires aliens who are present in the United States without documentation to register ( printed page 39275) “even though the current administration regards undocumented immigrants to be illegal.”
评论者表示,登记要求及未登记可能面临的处罚,以多种方式涉及第五修正案的保护,包括:
