Commenters stated that the requirement to register and potential penalties for non-registration implicate Fifth Amendment protections in several ways, including by:
实质上强迫外国人承认在美国非法居留,或提供其他可能使其面临刑事起诉或驱逐出境风险的信息;以及
Effectively compelling aliens to admit unlawful presence in the United States or provide other information that could potentially expose them to criminal prosecution or removal; and
造成一种“进退两难”的局面,即登记将因承认非法身份而面临自证其罪的风险,而不登记则将因不遵守规定而触发处罚。
Creating a “Catch-22” situation in which registering would risk self-incrimination through acknowledging unlawful status, while not registering would trigger penalties for noncompliance.
鉴于上述担忧,评论者敦促国土安全部撤回或不实施该规则,或者重新考虑登记要求,制定一种尊重反对自证其罪宪法保护的替代方案。
In light of these concerns, commenters either urged DHS to withdraw or not implement the rule, or to reconsider the registration requirement and develop an alternative approach that respects constitutional protections against self-incrimination.
一位评论者论证认为,由于反对自证其罪的特权,该要求将无法执行,也不可能因外国人故意不登记而对其定罪,并援引格罗索诉美国案,390 U.S. 67, 70(1968年)。该评论者认为,该案的裁定要求国土安全部修改表格,加入一项保证,即表格所要求的信息绝不会被用于对登记人提起刑事犯罪的起诉。该评论者进一步指出,《外国人登记法》本身并不存在这种自证其罪的问题,因为其所规定的表格对所有外国人统一适用,并不要求承认犯罪行为。
A commenter reasoned that because of the privilege against self-incrimination, the requirement would be unenforceable and that it would be impossible to convict an alien for willful failure to register, citing Grosso v. United States, 390 U.S. 67, 70 (1968). The commenter reasoned that the finding in this case requires that DHS amend the form to include a guarantee that information required by the form would never be used to prosecute a registrant for criminal offenses. This commenter further remarked that the Alien Registration Act itself did not have such a self-incrimination problem, because the form it imposed was universal for all aliens and did not demand admission of a crime.
有评论援引判例法指出,反对自证其罪的特权不仅保护个人免受仅凭其回答即可支持定罪的情形,还保护个人免受为起诉联邦罪行所需证据的影响。[35] 一位评论者得出结论认为,由于最高法院否定了“无罪辩解”原则,[36] 受本临时最终规则(IFR)约束的外国人援引反对自证其罪特权的唯一方式,要么是不进行登记,要么是在登记时不完整填写登记表。该评论者specifically指出第1.11项(地址历史)、第1.12项(最近一次入境)、第1.13项(I-94信息)、第3.2项(未被逮捕的犯罪行为)和第3.5项(管制物质违法行为)问题,认为这些问题从自证其罪角度看存在问题。
Citing to case law, a commenter stated that the privilege against self-incrimination protects not just against answers that would alone support convictions, but also against evidence needed to prosecute for a Federal crime.[35] A commenter concluded that because of the Supreme Court's rejection of the “exculpatory no,” [36] the only way an alien subject to the IFR can invoke their privilege against self-incrimination is either not to register, or to register without fully completing the registration form. The commenter specifically identified questions 1.11 (address history), 1.12 (last arrival), 1.13 (I-94 information), 3.2 (crimes committed without arrest), and 3.5 (controlled substance violations) as problematic from a self-incrimination perspective.
另一位评论者讨论了第五修正案反对自证其罪特权的“本质上属于监管性质”的例外情形,该评论者称,当政府行为针对广大公众,且与本质上非刑事性、监管性质的调查相关时,该例外允许强制披露作为全面监管计划一部分的证言性信息。该评论者指出,本临时最终规则不符合该例外情形,因为它针对的是涉嫌犯罪行为的人员,其性质并非本质上属于监管性质。
Another commenter discussed the “essentially regulatory” exception to the Fifth Amendment privilege against self-incrimination, which the commenter said permits compelled disclosure of testimonial information as part of a comprehensive regulatory scheme when the government action is directed at the public at large and related to an essentially noncriminal and regulatory inquiry. The commenter stated that the IFR does not qualify for this exception because it targets those suspected of criminal conduct and is not essentially regulatory in nature.
回应:国土安全部(DHS)不同意本规则违反外国人根据第五修正案享有的反对自证其罪特权的观点。第五修正案并不禁止政府提出问题,也不禁止政府接受答案。参见Nat'l Fed'n of Fed. Emps. v. Greenberg, 983 F.2d 286, 291 (D.C. Cir. 1993)。第五修正案也不妨碍对有效的民事监管制度的执行。参见Rajah v. Mukasey, 544 F.3d 427, 442 (2d Cir. 2008)。法院“长期以来承认驱逐或排除外国人的权力,是政府政治部门行使的一项基本主权属性,在很大程度上不受司法控制”。参见Fiallo v. Bell, 430 U.S. 787, 792 (1977)。登记的性质属于监管性质而非刑事性质,因为它并非“充斥着刑事法规,且该报告制度服务于实质性的非起诉利益”。参见Rajah, 544 F.3d at 442。具体而言,登记的主要目的是通过对寻求进入并留在美国的外国人进行监管,来加强移民法的执法。诚然,对于故意不进行登记以及未就地址变更通知国土安全部的行为,存在刑事处罚,但该法律框架的目的在于对在本国的外国人行使监管权力。此外,法院一贯承认,第五修正案并不免除个人向联邦政府作出某些提交的法定义务。参见United States v. Oliver, 505 F.2d 301, 307 (7th Cir. 1974)(指出第五修正案并不免除纳税人全面申报收入的法定义务,包括来自非法活动的收入)(该判决在其他方面已被推翻)。
Response: DHS disagrees that this rule violates aliens' Fifth Amendment privilege against self-incrimination. The Fifth Amendment does not forbid the government from asking questions and it does not forbid the government from taking the answers. See Nat'l Fed'n of Fed. Emps. v. Greenberg, 983 F.2d 286, 291 (D.C. Cir. 1993). Nor does it impede the enforcement of a valid civil regulatory regime. Rajah v. Mukasey, 544 F.3d 427, 442 (2d Cir. 2008). Courts “have long recognized the power to expel or exclude aliens as a fundamental sovereign attribute exercised by the Government's political departments largely immune from judicial control.” Fiallo v. Bell, 430 U.S. 787, 792 (1977). The nature of registration is regulatory rather than criminal because it is not “permeated with criminal statutes and [ ] there is a substantial non-prosecutorial interest served by the reporting regime.” Rajah, 544 F.3d at 442. Specifically, the primary purpose of registration is to enhance immigration law enforcement through the regulation of aliens seeking to enter and remain in the United States. Certainly, there are criminal penalties for willful failure to register and failure to notify DHS of a change in address, but the purpose of the statutory framework is to exercise authority over the regulation of aliens in this country. Furthermore, courts consistently acknowledge that the Fifth Amendment does not relieve individuals of their statutory obligations to make certain submissions to the Federal Government. See United States v. Oliver, 505 F.2d 301, 307 (7th Cir. 1974) (observing Fifth Amendment does not relieve taxpayers of statutory obligation to report income in full, including income from illegal ventures) (overruled on other grounds).
因此,第五修正案并不保护外国人免于提供与登记相关的信息,而该登记是外国人在美国境内居留的一项条件,包括来自护照、其他文件的信息,或关于其移民身份的陈述。参见Rajah, 544 F.3d at 441(涉及NSEERS登记政策)；另见,例如,United States v. Sacco, 428 F.2d 264, 271 (9th Cir. 1970)；Matter of Chen, 15 I&N Dec. 480, 482 (BIA 1975)；Matter of Yau, 14 I&N Dec. 630, 635 (BIA 1974)。最后,第五修正案反对自证其罪的主张,只有在实际提出该特权主张之后才具有可审性。参见,例如,Carman v. Yellen, 112 F.4th 386, 404 (6th Cir. 2024)。
Thus, the Fifth Amendment does not protect an alien from having to provide information relevant to the registration that is a condition of the alien's presence in the United States, including information from passports, other documents, or statements regarding his or her immigration status. See Rajah, 544 F.3d at 441 (relating to NSEERS registration policy); see also, e.g., United States v. Sacco, 428 F.2d 264, 271 (9th Cir. 1970); Matter of Chen, 15 I&N Dec. 480, 482 (BIA 1975); Matter of Yau, 14 I&N Dec. 630, 635 (BIA 1974). Finally, a Fifth Amendment self-incrimination claim is not ripe until a claim of the privilege is actually asserted. See, e.g., Carman v. Yellen, 112 F.4th 386, 404 (6th Cir. 2024).
b. 正当程序
b. Due Process
评论:许多评论者还表示,临时最终规则(IFR)将违反或导致违反第五修正案的正当程序保护,例如针对移民执法、逮捕、拘留或遣返等任意或不当政府行为的保护。评论者还表示,鉴于语言障碍和缺乏律师协助理解临时最终规则等可及性方面的挑战,这种情况尤其可能发生。其他评论者就以下方面表达了正当程序方面的担忧:针对具有友好政策的城市和州采取执法行动;违反具有约束力的和解协议的无证逮捕(连带逮捕);以及遣返包括美国公民在内的整个家庭。一位评论者敦促国土安全部(DHS)修订临时最终规则,以纳入“正当程序保障措施,包括明确的资格标准、豁免程序和上诉机制”。该评论者未提出具体的标准、程序或上诉依据。
Comment: Many commenters also indicated that the IFR would violate or result in violations of Fifth Amendment due process protections, such as protections against arbitrary or wrongful government actions like immigration enforcement, arrests, detentions, or removals. Commenters also indicated that this is particularly likely given accessibility challenges such as language barriers and lack of access to counsel of understand the IFR. Others expressed due process concerns relating to the targeting of cities and States with welcoming policies for enforcement actions; warrantless collateral arrests that violate binding settlement agreements; and removal of whole families that include U.S. citizens. A commenter urged DHS to revise the IFR to include “due process safeguards, such including clear eligibility guidelines, waiver processes, and appeal mechanisms.” The commenter did not suggest specific guidelines or processes, or bases for appeal.
一位评论者援引Mathews诉Eldridge案,424 U.S. 319(1976)中评估正当程序要求的三部分检验标准,表示外国人在避免刑事起诉方面具有重大的私人利益;由于语言障碍和贫困,存在重大的错误剥夺风险;且政府在本案中的利益并非真正的安全考量,而是利用移民问题引发的恐惧达到政治目的。该评论者还表示,根据登记要求,个人在政府行为剥夺其重大利益之前,未能获得Goldberg诉Kelly案,397 U.S. 254(1970)所要求的有意义的申辩机会,因为国土安全部未向登记人提供合理的机会以遵守其严格的截止日期或获得法律顾问的协助,从而给外国人在不切实际的时间框架内提交所需文件造成了过度负担。(印刷页第39276页)
Citing to the three-part test for evaluating due process requirements from Mathews v. Eldridge, 424 U.S. 319 (1976), a commenter stated that aliens have a significant private interest in avoiding criminal prosecution; that due to language barriers and poverty there is a significant risk of erroneous deprivation; and that the government's interest in this case is not in genuine security but in leveraging fear over immigration for political purposes. This commenter also stated that with the registration requirement, individuals are not afforded a meaningful opportunity to be heard before the governmental action deprives them of a significant interest as required by Goldberg v. Kelly, 397 U.S. 254 (1970), because DHS is not providing registrants reasonable opportunities to comply with its stringent deadlines or to access legal counsel, placing undue burdens on aliens to submit required documentation within an unrealistic time frame. ( printed page 39276)
回应:国土安全部不同意本规则违反了外国人根据第五修正案享有的正当程序权利,并且拒绝就登记事宜建立额外的资格标准、豁免程序和上诉机制。
Response: DHS disagrees that this rule violates aliens' Fifth Amendment right to due process and also declines to establish additional eligibility guidelines, waiver processes, and appeal mechanisms associated with the registration.
该临时最终规则仅修订了相关法规,以指定一种新的登记表格,供外国人遵守法定登记要求之用。《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条,8 U.S.C. 1302所规定的登记要求是由国会制定实施的;对登记义务本身提出的任何正当程序异议,实质上是对该法规本身的异议,而非对本规则的异议。本规则的唯一效果是创建一种登记表格,以确保所有外国人都有办法进行登记,这与该法规是一致的。而且无论如何,就违反美国移民法并无限期地逃避发现,或未能按照法定义务进行登记而言,并不存在可被承认的正当程序利益。长期以来的判例均认定,无论外国人身份如何,他们都享有某些宪法保护,但该保护受到国会在移民管控方面广泛权力的限制。例如参见Ahmed诉Gonzales案,447 F.3d 433, 439(第五巡回上诉法院,2006年)(在NSEERS登记制度中确认国会在移民管控方面的广泛权力)(援引Rodriguez-Silva诉INS案,242 F.3d 243, 246, 247(第五巡回上诉法院,2001年))。国土安全部还指出,登记义务及其后果与因其他使个人具有可遣返性的行为而可能导致的遣返后果无关,并且在登记要求方面,国土安全部并未针对特定类别的外国人、城市或州。相反,国会普遍规定了适用于所有外国人的登记要求,且国土安全部提供的一般登记选项适用于所有此前未登记的外国人,无论其身份如何。
The IFR simply amended the regulations to designate a new registration form for aliens to comply with the statutory registration requirements. The registration requirement of section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 was implemented by Congress; any due process objection to a registration obligation in general amounts to an objection to the statute rather than this rule. This rule's only effect is to create a registration form that ensures all aliens have a way to register, consistent with the statute. And in any event, there is no cognizable due process interest in violating U.S. immigration law and remaining undetected indefinitely, or in failing to register consistent with one's statutory obligations. It has long been held that aliens enjoy some constitutional protections regardless of their status, but the protection is limited by Congress' broad powers to control immigration. See, e.g., Ahmed v. Gonzales, 447 F.3d 433, 439 (5th Cir. 2006) (recognizing, as part of NSEERS registration, Congress' broad power to control immigration) (citing Rodriguez-Silva v. INS, 242 F.3d 243, 246, 247 (5th Cir. 2001)). DHS also notes that the duty to register and its consequences is unrelated to the consequences of potential removal because of other actions that render individuals removable and that with the registration requirement, DHS neither is targeting certain classes of aliens, cities, or States. Rather, Congress universally prescribed the registration requirement to all aliens, and the general registration option provided by DHS is available to all previously unregistered aliens regardless of their status.
关于评论者所述某些外国人英语能力有限或获取法律资源渠道有限的陈述,美国国土安全部(DHS)指出,移民表格仅以英语提供。[37] 由于外国人不理解英语但能够使用其他语言发声而无法说英语,这并不构成《康复法》项下需要合理便利措施的残疾。[38] DHS还指出,任何可能受DHS法规影响的个人,无论是外国人还是公民,理论上都可能在获取法律资源以帮助其理解该法规及其可能产生的影响方面受到限制。DHS并无义务向外国人提供法律资源,但本规则并不妨碍外国人寻求律师协助。因此,DHS认为评论者对获取法律资源的担忧并不超过本机构忠实执行法定登记和指纹采集要求的利益。基于上述所有原因,本规则完全避免了侵犯第五修正案所保障的反对自证其罪的特权以及正当程序权利和法律平等保护权利。
Regarding commenters' statements about certain aliens' limited proficiency in English or access to legal resources, DHS notes that immigration forms are only offered in English.[37] Inability to speak English because the alien does not understand English, but can vocalize other languages, is not a disability warranting a reasonable accommodation under the Rehabilitation Act.[38] DHS also notes that any individual—alien or citizen—potentially affected by a DHS regulation may theoretically have limited access to legal resources to help them understand that regulation and how it may affect them. DHS is not required to provide aliens with legal resources, but this rule does not prevent an alien from seeking counsel. DHS accordingly does not view commenters' concern about access to legal resources as outweighing the agency's interest in faithfully executing the statutory registration and fingerprinting requirements. For all these reasons, this rule steers well clear of infringing upon the Fifth Amendment privilege against self-incrimination and right to due process and equal protection of law.
c. 儿童和无人陪伴未成年人的正当程序
c. Due Process of Children and Unaccompanied Minors
评论意见:一家组织评论称,要求外国儿童遵守本规则的要求表明该登记法规已经过时,并指出自该法规颁布以来,我们对儿童期和青春期的理解已发生重大演变,后续立法已承认儿童和青少年的差异性和脆弱性。另一位评论者表示,这一关键变化试图将未成年人从刑事法律体系中转移出去。
Comment: An organization commented that subjecting alien children to the requirements of the rule belies the outdated nature of the registration statute and remarked that since its enactment, our understanding of childhood and adolescence has significantly evolved, with subsequent legislation recognizing the differences and vulnerabilities of children and teenagers. Another commenter stated that the key change attempts to divert juveniles away from the criminal legal system.
另一位评论者表示,国会在2008年《威廉·威伯福斯反人口贩运保护重新授权法》(TVPRA)中为无人陪伴外国儿童(UAC)设立了具体的程序保护,参见例如8 U.S.C. 1232,以承认无人陪伴外国儿童的特殊脆弱性,并防止其被遣返回人口贩运及其他伤害之中。该评论者认为,临时最终规则(IFR)及第14159号行政命令是否符合TVPRA值得质疑,因为该临时最终规则将使14岁以上的外国人受其要求约束,但似乎并未考虑外国儿童独特的发育需求、对移民法律有限的理解,以及需要从可信赖的成年人那里获得支持和照顾的需求,而无人陪伴外国儿童面临的障碍更大。该评论者进一步阐述了对无人陪伴外国儿童的担忧,写道不遵守该临时最终规则可能导致对其实施拘留,但该临时最终规则并未说明此类拘留将如何符合TVPRA、《弗洛雷斯和解协议》或其他少年司法改革的要求。该评论者总结认为,该临时最终规则将允许对外国儿童实施简易拘留、遣返或其他处罚,因此违背了TVPRA确保脆弱的外国儿童能够公平获得法律保护以防止其被遣返回人口贩运及其他伤害的宗旨,并补充说没有理由认为国会曾预期行政部门会恢复一项全面的登记计划,并以威胁外国儿童正当程序保护的方式加以实施。
Another commenter stated that Congress created specific procedural protections for unaccompanied alien children (UAC) in the William Wilberforce Trafficking Victims Protection Reauthorization Act of 2008 (TVPRA), see, e.g.,8 U.S.C. 1232, in recognition of UAC's particular vulnerability and to prevent their return to trafficking and other harm. The commenter reasoned that the IFR's and E.O. 14159's compliance with the TVPRA is questionable, because the IFR would subject aliens over the age of 14 to its requirements, but it does not appear to consider alien children's distinct developmental needs, limited understanding of immigration law, and need for support and care from trusted adults, with greater barriers for UACs. The commenter elaborated on concerns for UACs, writing that noncompliance with the IFR could lead to detention of them, but that the IFR does not explain how such detentions would comport with TVPRA, the Flores Settlement Agreement, or other juvenile justice reforms. The commenter concluded that the IFR would allow for the summary detention, removal, or other penalties for alien children, and therefore contravenes TVPRA's aim of ensuring vulnerable alien children are able to fairly access legal protections to prevent their return to trafficking and other harm, adding that there is no reason to think that Congress anticipated the Executive Branch would revive a comprehensive registration program and apply it in a manner that would threaten alien children's due process protections.
一位评论者列举了对该临时最终规则影响外国儿童的多项担忧,敦促DHS重新考虑登记要求,以维护外国儿童的公民权利和公共安全保护。该评论者表示,“如该临时最终规则所提议的”对外国人实行普遍登记要求将“增加弱势移民,特别是未成年人,可能被不法分子利用的渠道”。该评论者说明,他们曾与一名19岁的外国人合作,该外国人在被迫作为童工从事家庭佣工劳动后逃离危地马拉,即使在抵达美国之后,该外国人获取必要信息的渠道也十分有限,以至于她本可能成为“企图利用不熟悉该临时最终规则要求的新来者的潜在骗子的理想目标”。另一位评论者敦促DHS保护那些在儿童时期被带到美国的外国人。
Citing multiple concerns about impacts of the IFR on alien children, a commenter urged DHS to reconsider registration requirements to preserve civil rights and public safety protections for alien children. The commenter stated that a universal registration requirement for aliens “as the IFR proposes” would “increase the vectors by which vulnerable migrants, particularly minors, may be exploited by bad actors.” The commenter explained that they had worked with an alien who is 19 years old and fled Guatemala after having been forced to work as a child domestic servant, and that even after arriving in the United States, this alien had such limited access to necessary information that she would have been “an ideal target for would-be cozeners seeking to prey on newcomers who are unfamiliar with the requirements of the IFR.” Another commenter urged DHS to protect aliens who were brought to the United States as children.
答复:国土安全部(DHS)不同意登记要求威胁外国人儿童正当程序权益的观点。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)网站载有帮助所有外国人了解登记要求的信息。所有外国人,包括无人陪伴未成年人(UAC),都必须遵守《移民与国籍法》(INA)的规定,该法要求所有外国人,除有限的例外情形外,凡年满14周岁且将在美国境内停留30天或以上者,均须申请登记并留取指纹。
Response: DHS disagrees that the registration requirement threatens due process interests of alien children. The USCIS website contains information to help all aliens understand the registration requirement. All aliens, including UACs, must follow the INA, which requires all aliens, with limited exceptions, to apply for registration and fingerprinting if they are age 14 or older and will remain in the United States for 30 days or longer.
丁、第一修正案、言论、集会与结社
d. First Amendment, Expression, Assembly, and Association
评论意见:许多评论者普遍担忧,临时最终规则(IFR)将要求外国人出示登记证明,这也将导致种族定性、对被拘留的恐惧,并最终抑制包括集会权和言论自由在内的第一修正案权利。一位评论者援引研究指出,相关研究发现,广泛的监视或定性与被针对的社区成员中第一修正案活动受限之间存在直接联系。其他评论者特别担忧,在特朗普政府更广泛的移民执法政策背景下,IFR所产生的寒蝉效应,包括非暴力反对可能具有歧视性影响的政府政策的权利,以及反对任意执法行为的权利。(印刷页第39277页)一位评论者称,IFR将通过为所有外国人提供近乎普遍性的集中身份信息,助长该届政府基于言论对个人进行针对的议程。
Comment: Many commenters expressed a general concern that the IFR would require aliens to show proof of registration, which would also lead to racial profiling, fears of detention, and ultimately chill First Amendment liberties, including the right to assembly and freedom of expression. Citing to research, a commenter said that studies have found a direct link between widespread surveillance or profiling and restricted First Amendment activity among members of the community being targeted. Others expressed particular concerns for the IFR's chilling effect in the context of the Trump administration's broader immigration enforcement policies, including the right to nonviolently object to government policies that have a ( printed page 39277) potential discriminatory effect and to arbitrary enforcement practices. A commenter stated that the IFR would facilitate the administration's agenda of targeting individuals based on speech by providing near-universal centralized identifying information for all aliens.
另一位评论者反对该IFR,理由是外国人登记制度将被用于迫害那些行使言论自由权、对某届政府或某政党政策表示不同意见的人。
Another commenter opposed the IFR because alien registration would be used to persecute those who exercise their freedom of speech to disagree with the policies of an administration or political party.
多位评论者表示担忧,IFR已经并将继续对外国人的言论和表达自由产生寒蝉效应,原因是他们担心被拘留和驱逐出境。一位评论者表示,IFR侵犯了宗教自由和言论自由,其做法是与撤销《敏感场所备忘录》'相互配合,侵扰美国居民的宗教自由,使许多人因担心被定性或因行使言论自由而担心即便是被错误地也可能成为驱逐出境的目标,从而不敢前往参加宗教活动。'关于言论权利,一位评论者表示,现任政府一直在基于言论撤销移民身份,包括学生签证,而IFR将通过为所有外国人提供集中身份信息来助长这一议程,从而对第一修正案权利保护产生寒蝉效应。
Several commenters voiced concern that the IFR would have, and already has had, a chilling effect on free speech and expression for aliens, due to fear of detention and deportation. A commenter stated that the IFR trespasses on freedom of religion and speech by working “hand-in-hand with the rescinding of the Sensitive Locations Memos to intrude upon the religious freedom of U.S. residents by discouraging many from attending religious services for fear of being profiled or to exercise their freedom of speech for fear of being targeted for deportation, even erroneously.” Regarding speech rights, a commenter stated that the current administration has been revoking immigration status, including student visas, based on speech and that IFR would facilitate this agenda by providing centralized identifying information for all aliens, creating a chilling effect on First Amendment protections.
一位评论者表示担忧,该机构中最活跃、最引人注目的成员无论是否登记,都可能成为执法目标,这将直接影响其为低收入群体和移民社区进行倡导的工作。该评论者指出,其所在机构的倡导工作通常依赖于集会等线下活动来推动政策提案,而IFR对个人参与此类倡导形式的意愿产生了寒蝉效应。该评论者表示,IFR将使其社区需要加强监视和监控,并报告称他们已经目睹了类似登记政策对政治言论及该机构倡导活动的负面影响,例如其寻找并支持愿意向媒体、立法者和社交媒体分享自身故事的成员的能力受到影响,而这些工作原本有助于建立基层支持并推进该机构的政策目标。此外,该评论者表示,在集会活动期间,组织者需要花费大量时间来处理这些担忧。
A commenter expressed concern that the organization's most visible and active members could become targets for enforcement whether they register or not, directly impacting their work of advocating for low-income and immigrant communities. The commenter remarked that their organization's advocacy often relies on in-person events such as rallies to advance policy proposals and that the IFR creates a chilling effect on individuals' willingness to participate in these forms of advocacy. The commenter stated that the IFR would necessitate greater surveillance and monitoring of their community, and reported that they have already witnessed the negative impact of registration-like policies on political speech and the organization's advocacy activities, such as their ability to find and support members willing to share their stories for use with the press, legislators, and social media in support of building grassroot support and advancing the organization's policy goals. Additionally, the commenter stated that during rallies organizers spend significant time addressing these concerns.
答复:公民和外国人当然可以对各类法规、规章和政策提出异议,并公开表达此类异议。本规则并未削减言论自由、新闻自由、结社自由或和平集会自由。根据第一修正案提出的强制执行前表面抗辩,只有在个人证明其面临“可信的起诉威胁”,且该威胁是依据“一项似乎使[该]个人可能受保护的言论成为非法的法规[或规则]”提出的情况下,方可成立。参见Am. Library Ass'n诉Barr案,956 F.2d 1178, 1194(哥伦比亚特区巡回上诉法院,1992年)。本案情形并非如此,也不可能如此。移民与国籍法(INA)第262条,即《美国法典》第8编第1302条,以及本规则,均未将任何形式的言论定为非法。移民与国籍法第262条,即《美国法典》第8编第1302条,仅要求在美国境内停留30天或以上的外国人进行登记和指纹采集,这完全属于本国主权范围内可以要求的事项。本规则旨在弥补现行监管体系中的空白,为所有外国人,无论其移民身份如何,提供一种履行其法律义务的途径。
Response: Citizens and aliens of course may object to various statutes, regulations, and policies and express those objections publicly. This rule does not abridge the freedom of speech, of the press, or of association or peaceful assembly. A pre-enforcement facial challenge under the First Amendment cannot lie unless an individual establishes that he or she faces a “credible threat of prosecution” “under a statute [or rule] that appears to render the [individual]'s arguably protected speech illegal.” Am. Library Ass'n v. Barr, 956 F.2d 1178, 1194 (D.C. Cir. 1992). That is not and cannot be the case here. Neither section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, nor this rule renders illegal any form of speech. Section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 simply requires registration and fingerprinting of aliens in the United States for 30 days or more, which is well within this nation's sovereign prerogative to require. This rule addresses a gap in the existing regulatory regime and provides a way for all aliens, regardless of their immigration status, to comply with their duty under the law.
美国国土安全部(DHS)指出,根据《联邦法规汇编》第8编第264.1条所列表格可知,登记活动一直以来都在依据这项近百年历史的法定要求进行。G-325R表格上的问题与第8编第264.1条(a)款所指定的其他登记表格上的问题类似,包括I-485表格,即《申请登记永久居留或调整身份申请表》。
DHS notes that registration activity has always taken place, as evidenced by the forms listed in 8 CFR 264.1, in accordance with the nearly century-old statutory requirement. The questions on the Form G-325R are similar to questions on other registration forms designated at 8 CFR 264.1(a), including the Form I-485, Application to Register Permanent or Adjust Status.
e. 平等保护、种族定性与歧视
e. Equal Protection, Racial Profiling, and Discrimination,
评论意见:许多评论者普遍担忧,该临时最终规则(IFR)将导致种族主义、仇外心理、种族定性或歧视。
Comment: Many commenters expressed a general concern that the IFR would lead to in racism, xenophobia, racial profiling, or discrimination.
一些评论者指出,该临时最终规则将导致种族定性,进而导致对美国公民和合法永久居民(LPR)的错误逮捕。一位评论者表示,该规则从根本上改变了数百万人的权利和利益,包括可能在该登记规则所制造的任何带有歧视性的大范围排查中被要求出示“证明”其公民身份的美国公民,并补充说这一程序是针对移民社区更大规模打压行动的一部分。
Some commenters indicated that the IFR would lead to racial profiling which, in turn, would lead to wrongful arrests of U.S. citizens and LPRs. A commenter said that the rule fundamentally alters the rights and interests of millions of people, including U.S. citizens who may be asked to show “proof” of their citizenship in any discriminatory dragnet created by this registration rule, adding that this process is part of a larger attack on immigrant communities.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则将特别针对外国人,不仅依据其原籍国,还将依据种族和族裔,以提高逮捕统计数字从而完成配额。该评论者引用了《华盛顿邮报》的一篇报道,称政府已指示移民与海关执法局(ICE)官员增加逮捕人数以完成每日配额,每个外勤办公室须每天完成75人次逮捕,未能完成目标的管理人员将被追责。该评论者及其他人认为,在一个鼓励执法人员在毫无正当理由的情况下要求出示登记证明的体系中,种族定性是不可避免的必然结果。评论者表示,执法人员可能会依据诸如个人的族裔、肤色、不常见的姓名、语言能力或被认为带有外国口音等因素要求出示登记证明。
A commenter stated that the IFR would particularly target aliens based not only on national origin but also on race and ethnicity in order boost arrest statistics to meet quotas. The commenter referenced a Washington Post report stating that the administration has directed ICE officials to increase arrests to meet daily quotas, with each field office required to make 75 arrests per day and managers being held accountable for failing to meet these targets. This commenter and others reasoned that racial profiling is an unavoidable certainty in a system where law enforcement is encouraged to demand proof of registration without justification. Commenters said that law enforcement could ask for proof of registration based on factors such as an individual's ethnicity, skin color, uncommon name, language proficiency, or perceived foreign accent.
另一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则的执行将依赖于受执法人员审查的个人的原籍国,这“必然会导致种族定性、错误逮捕以及对美国公民和其他具有合法身份人士的拘留”。该评论者写道,在特朗普政府执政期间,美国公民已经在移民执法中受到种族定性的影响,而该临时最终规则将加剧这一问题。该评论者及其他人援引了州一级“出示证件”法律的例子,例如亚利桑那州参议院第1070号法案,即《支持我们的执法与安全社区法》,该法引发了关于针对拉丁裔和亚裔美国人的种族定性是否符合宪法的诉讼。多位评论者表示,曾有报道称,美洲原住民及其他美国公民因种族定性而被移民当局拘留。
Another commenter stated that enforcement of the IFR would rely on the national origin of individuals under scrutiny by law enforcement officials, which would “undoubtedly lead to racial profiling, wrongful arrests, and detention of U.S. citizens and other individuals with lawful status.” This commenter wrote that U.S. citizens are already being racially profiled in immigration enforcement under the Trump administration and that the IFR would exacerbate this problem. This commenter and others cited examples of State-level “show me your papers” laws, such as Arizona Senate Bill 1070, the “Support Our Law Enforcement and Safe Neighborhoods Act,” that resulted in lawsuits over the constitutionality of racial profiling against Latino and Asian Americans. Several commenters stated that there have been reports of Native Americans and other U.S. citizens who have been detained by immigration authorities due to racial profiling.
另一位评论者表示,临时最终规则(IFR)将加剧种族和民族归类问题,而这种问题已成为美国移民执法的“基石”,该评论者特别引用了美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)第287(g)条项目工作组模式,该模式“允许被授权的官员在例行交通拦截中询问被拦截人员的移民身份,并仅以联邦移民理由为依据进行逮捕”。一位评论者写道,基于民族血统的歧视在美国不应合法。另一位评论者担忧该临时最终规则将影响合法移民和美国公民。例如,一位评论者提供了一个轶事性描述,写道有美国公民因讲西班牙语而遭逮捕。一位评论者表示,基于民族血统的歧视在美国不应合法,而且该临时最终规则没有足够的防止政府滥权的保障措施,无法通过宪法审查。该评论者还表示,即便涉及国家安全问题,也不能因此赋予联邦政府侵犯外国人权利的许可。(印刷页39278)
Another commenter stated that the IFR would exacerbate the problems that result from racial and ethnic profiling being a “cornerstone” of U.S. immigration enforcement, specifically citing the U.S. Immigration and Custom Enforcement (ICE's) 287(g) Program Task Force Model, which “allows deputized officers to ask about immigration status of individuals stopped during routine traffic stops and make arrests based solely on federal immigration grounds.” A commenter wrote that discrimination on account of national origin should not be legal in the United States. Another commenter raised concern that the IFR would affect lawful immigrants and U.S. citizens. For example, providing an anecdotal account, a commenter wrote that U.S. citizens were arrested for speaking Spanish. A commenter stated that discrimination on account of national origin should not be legal in the United States and that the IFR did not have enough government-overreach-safeguards to pass constitutional muster. The commenter also stated that even if national security were at issue, it would not give the Federal Government a license to violate the rights of aliens. ( printed page 39278)
另一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则将外国人身份定为刑事犯罪,其背后带有种族歧视动机的“出示证件制度”,将对“黑人和棕色人种”及拉丁裔社区以及整体有色人种社区造成不成比例的伤害,这些社区本已不成比例地遭受监禁以及警察拦截、盘问和搜查。该评论者表示,这些政策将导致有色人种的美国公民也需要携带公民身份证明,以避免在该规则下被卷入执法和刑事定罪之中。
Another commenter wrote that the IFR's criminalization of alien status and the IFR's underlying racially discriminatory motive and “show-your-paper regime” would disproportionately harm “Black and Brown” and Latino communities, as well as communities of color generally, which are already disproportionately incarcerated and subjected to police stops, questioning, and searches. The commenter said that these policies would lead to a situation in which U.S. citizens of color would need to have proof of citizenship to avoid being swept into the enforcement and criminalization under the rule.
一位评论者表示,尽管有人可能试图以加强国家安全审查的必要性为由为该规则的实施辩护,但该规则必然依赖于民族血统,这将不可避免地导致种族归类以及对美国公民和合法在美外国人(包括合法永久居民)的错误逮捕和拘留。该评论者表示,所谓基于民族血统进行歧视的意图,反映的是针对移民社区的政治言论和种族偏见,而非对国家安全或美国公众福祉的合法关切。据该评论者称,第14159号行政命令“本质上建立在基于种族的偏见之上,其通过该临时最终规则的实施是基于这样一种前提,即非公民的移民正在‘入侵’美国。”该评论者写道:“[对]这一军事战争用语的滥用,将移民、移民群体和寻求庇护者等同于一种带有敌意且武装的群体入侵。”另一位评论者表示,这种做法同样与针对移民社区的政治言论和种族偏见有关,而非出于合法的国家安全或公共关切,并援引特朗普总统的言论作为证据,其中包括关于“不良基因”以及移民“毒害我们国家血统”的说法,以及关于移民“入侵”的说法。
A commenter stated that while some may attempt to justify the implementation of the rule due to the necessity for increased vetting for the purposes of national security, the rule will necessarily rely on national origin, which will inevitably result in racial profiling and wrongful arrests and detention of U.S. citizens and lawfully present noncitizens including lawful permanent residents. The commenter stated that the alleged intent to discriminate based on national origin reflects political rhetoric against immigrant communities and racial prejudice, not a legitimate concern about national security or the U.S. public well-being. According to the commenter, E.O. 14159 “is inherently founded in race-based prejudice and its implementation through the IFR is based on the premise that immigrants that are not citizens are `invading' the United States. The commenter wrote that “[t]he misuse of a military term of war equates migrants, immigrants, and asylum seekers with a group incursion that is hostile and armed.” Another commenter stated that such an approach is also linked to political rhetoric against immigrant communities and racial prejudice, rather than legitimate national security or public concerns, referencing, as evidence, President Trump's statements, including claims about “bad genes” and immigrants “poisoning the blood of our country,” as well as an immigrant “invasion.”
许多评论者表示担忧,该规则将导致种族归类和侵犯公民权利的行为,从而损害公共安全。一位评论者表示,该规则规定未随身携带“登记证明”即构成刑事处罚,这将助长移民官员和执法人员对其认为是移民的人员(包括儿童和家庭)进行种族归类以及任意搜查、扣押、拘留或逮捕。
Many commenters expressed concern that the rule would lead to racial profiling and civil rights violations that would undermine public safety. A commenter stated that the rule's criminal penalty for failing to carry “proof of registration” on one's person at all times encourages racial profiling and arbitrary searches, seizures, detention or arrest by immigration officials and law enforcement officers of individuals whom they perceive to be immigrants, including children and families.
评论者表示,该临时最终规则将造成“敌对环境”,针对任何被认为是外国人的人员,并对外国人和美国公民均造成伤害,导致过度执法。另有几位评论者写道,该规则的实施可能会导致歧视性的搜查和扣押,以及错误逮捕,这将同时影响美国公民和“非公民”。
Commenters stated that the IFR would create a “hostile environment” and target anyone perceived as foreign, and harm both aliens and U.S. citizens, leading to over-policing. Another couple of commenters wrote that the implementation of this rule would likely result in discriminatory searches and seizures, as well as wrongful arrests that would impact U.S. citizens and “non-citizens” alike.
一位评论者写道,临时最终规则(IFR)的要求将对弱势群体产生不成比例的影响,例如低收入移民和获得法律援助渠道有限的人群。一位评论者指出,该临时最终规则将使亚裔、夏威夷原住民和太平洋岛民社区成员心生恐惧,使其无法获得学校教育、医疗保健、就业及其他福利。同样,数位评论者写道,该临时最终规则使亚裔和太平洋岛民社区的重要成员心生恐惧或将其定性为犯罪,而这些人占“无证移民”总数的七分之一,该规则仅因其原籍国而将其作为替罪羊,使其成为种族归类(racial profiling)的对象,并由于这些社区中英语能力有限者比例较高而使合规变得困难。
A commenter wrote that the requirements of the IFR would disproportionately impact vulnerable groups, such as low-income immigrants and those with limited access to legal assistance. A commenter indicated that the IFR would instill fear in Asian, Native Hawaiian, and Pacific Islander community members, preventing them from accessing schools, healthcare, employment, and other benefits. Similarly, a couple of commenters wrote that the IFR instills fear in or criminalizes integral members of the Asian and Pacific Islander community, who make up one in seven “undocumented immigrants,” and scapegoats them simply for their national origin, targets them for racial profiling, and makes compliance difficult due to high rates of limited English proficiency in these communities.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则未考虑其将对少数族裔社区造成的社会和经济影响,并援引了纽约州据称存在歧视性移民执法的例子。另一位评论者写道,移民政策和执法强化了对所有拉丁裔群体均有影响的有害刻板印象和社会不平等,无论其身份是否合法;该评论者补充说,尽管永久居民拥有合法工作许可,但他们在寻求资源时常常遭遇歧视。一位评论者表示,加利福尼亚州拉丁裔农场工人比例失衡的农村社区已经成为移民执法的目标,该评论者称国土安全部(DHS)将此错误地描述为针对有犯罪记录的外国人。另一位评论者写道,移民政策和执法是白人至上主义的工具,其理由是该政策限制了拉丁裔和美国其他移民获得资源、服务和机会的渠道。
A commenter stated that the IFR did not account for the social and economic impacts that the IFR would have on minority communities, citing examples of alleged discriminatory immigration enforcement in New York. Another commenter wrote that immigration policy and enforcement reinforce harmful stereotypes and social inequities that impact all Latinos, regardless of their legal status; they added that although permanent residents have legal work authorization, they often encounter discrimination when seeking resources. A commenter stated that rural communities in California with disproportionate Latino farm workers are already being targeted for immigration enforcement, which the commenter said DHS falsely characterizes as targeting aliens with criminal records. Another commenter wrote that immigration policy and enforcement is a tool of white supremacy, reasoning that it limits access to resources, services, and opportunities among Latinos and other immigrants in the United States.
一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则和国土安全部将“无证”和未登记的“非公民”与帮派成员和罪犯混为一谈。该评论者表示,尽管美国的公共安全和国家安全是重要关切,但该规则源于对“非公民”所犯罪行的错置且夸大的关注。该评论者援引了2025年1月21日司法部(DOJ)的一份备忘录,该备忘录将“跨国帮派”和“非法外国人”与“在全国迅速升级”的“残暴且不可容忍的暴力犯罪”联系在一起。该评论者表示,这种描述未能区分轻罪与更严重的犯罪活动。该评论者写道,个别“非公民”实施的暴力犯罪常被用来为诸如《莱肯·莱利法案》(Laken Riley Act)的通过等行动提供正当理由,但指出这些事件在呈现时并未就外国人实际犯罪水平提供适当的背景说明。该评论者援引学术研究表明,“非公民”的犯罪活动与美国总体人口相比并不存在不成比例的情况。
A commenter stated that the IFR and DHS conflate “undocumented” and unregistered “noncitizens” with gang members and criminals. The commenter stated that, while U.S. public safety and national security are important concerns, the rule stems from a misplaced and exaggerated focus on crime committed by “noncitizens.” The commenter referenced a January 21, 2025, DOJ memo that linked “transnational gangs” and “illegal aliens” to “brutal and intolerant violent crime” that is “escalating rapidly across the country.” The commenter said that this characterization fails to distinguish between misdemeanors and more serious criminal activities. The commenter wrote that individual violent crimes by “noncitizens” are often highlighted to justify actions like the passage of the Laken Riley Act, but suggested these incidents are presented without proper context regarding the actual level of crime committed by aliens. The commenter cited academic research suggesting that criminal activity by “noncitizens” is not disproportionate to the U.S. population at large.
一位评论者表达了略有不同的担忧,写道该临时最终规则未能考虑或采取措施防范创业负担、纳税人支出和公共安全成本。此外,另一位评论者写道,该临时最终规则将扩大国土安全部任意逮捕和驱逐更多寻求庇护者使其身陷危险的能力,并补充说,尽管国土安全部无合法权利驱逐美国公民,但随着该临时最终规则的实施,这种情况很可能日益频繁地发生。
Expressing a slightly different concern, a commenter wrote that the IFR fails to consider or take steps to safeguard against entrepreneurial burdens, taxpayer expenditures, and public safety costs. Further, another commenter wrote that the IFR would broaden DHS's ability to arbitrarily arrest and deport more asylum seekers to danger, adding that though DHS has no lawful right to deport U.S. citizens, this is likely to become a daily occurrence with the implementation of this IFR.
同样,一位评论者反对拟议的“非公民”登记政策,认为这将导致歧视性的执法行为。该评论者表示,要求“非公民”随时携带登记文件将带来执法上的挑战,因为国土安全部官员没有实际可行的方法来识别谁应受此要求约束,除非诉诸于种族归类(profiling)。
Similarly, a commenter opposed the proposed “noncitizen” registration policy, arguing it would lead to discriminatory law enforcement practices. The commenter stated that requiring “noncitizens” to carry registration documents at all times would create enforcement challenges, as DHS officers would have no practical way to identify who would be subject to this requirement without resorting to profiling.
另一位评论者进一步写道,行政当局将在该政策下采用任意的方法来识别“非公民”。据一位评论者称,尽管该临时最终规则所述目的是加强国土安全部的执法以保护公共安全并减少暴力犯罪,但必须牢记,法律之下的平等保护意味着犯罪活动是由个人行为而非群体身份或地位所致,另一位评论者则表示,试图识别和拘留涉嫌外国人的做法将对社区造成无法弥补的伤害。
Another commenter further wrote that the administration would employ arbitrary methods of identifying “noncitizens” under the policy. According to a commenter, while the IFR's stated purpose is to enhance law enforcement by DHS to protect public safety and reduce violent crime, it is important to remember that equal protection under the law means criminal activity is caused by individual actions, not group identity or status, with a different commenter stating that communities would be irreparably harmed by attempts to identify and detain suspected aliens.
有评论者表示担忧,执法做法甚至可能在合法永久居民和美国公民中造成恐惧,从而导致(印刷页第39279页)公民社会参与度下降。作为例证,该评论者引用了一项关于亚利桑那州严格移民法律的研究,并称该研究发现此类政策尤其对拉丁裔青少年产生负面影响。有评论者写道,这一程序否定了美国应代表的一切价值,不应在这个国家被“恢复”。评论者还表示,临时最终规则(IFR)的执法做法助长了种族定性,甚至在合法永久居民和美国公民中造成恐惧和焦虑。评论者引用了有关严格移民法律的研究,具体是亚利桑那州的《支持我们的执法和安全社区法》(Support our Law Enforcement and Safe Neighborhoods Act),该研究发现,助长种族定性的政策对拉丁裔青少年的心理健康和学业成就产生负面影响。评论者表示,尽管临时最终规则估计登记要求将直接影响200万至300万人,但可能发生的种族定性将对美国超过6300万拉丁裔居民这一更广泛人群产生不利影响。
A commenter expressed concern that enforcement practices can create fear even among lawful permanent residents and U.S. citizens, which leads to ( printed page 39279) reduced participation in civil society. As an example, they cited a study on Arizona's stringent immigration laws and said that it found that such policies negatively impact Latino youths in particular. A commenter wrote that this process negates everything the United States should stand for and should not be “reinstated” in this country. Commenters also stated that the IFR's enforcement practices enable racial profiling and create fear and anxiety even among lawful permanent residents and U.S. citizens. The commenters cited research on stringent immigration laws, specifically Arizona's Support our Law Enforcement and Safe Neighborhoods Act, which found that policies promoting racial profiling negatively impact Latino youths' mental health and academic achievement. The commenters stated that while the IFR estimates the registration requirement would directly impact 2 to 3 million people, the racial profiling likely to occur would have detrimental effects on the broader population of over 63 million Latino residents in the United States.
有评论者表示担忧,若无明确的保护措施,登记程序可能被滥用,以不公平的方式针对特定人群,尤其是对某些国家的移民给予优于其他国家移民的优待。其他评论者在讨论此前“国家安全出入境登记系统”(NSEERS)项目下所谓的歧视性做法时,也表示扩大外国人登记要求可能再次导致带有偏见的执法做法,侵蚀移民社区与政府机构之间的信任,使移民非人化,并侵犯外国人的基本权利。
A commenter voiced concern that without clear protections, the registration process could be misused to target certain populations unfairly, particularly with giving preference to immigrants from specific countries over others. While discussing allegedly discriminatory practices under the former NSEERS program, other commenters also stated that expanding alien registration requirements could once again lead to biased enforcement practices, eroding trust between immigrant communities and government agencies, dehumanizing immigrants, and infringing on aliens' basic rights.
答复:国土安全部(DHS)强烈不同意将临时最终规则(IFR)定性为与针对移民社区的政治言论和种族偏见相关联,而非出于落实法定授权以及国家和公共安全关切等正当考量的表述。美国法律普遍要求外国人进行登记,无论是作为签证程序的一部分,还是在美国境内停留30天或以上时。临时最终规则和本最终规则的范围有限,旨在为外国人建立一个通用的登记选项,以便其按照适用情形履行登记和按捺指纹的法定义务。本规则制定并未对任何群体施加任何新的义务,也未基于种族偏见制造差别待遇。同样,临时最终规则和本规则并未新设或修改国会数十年前所确立的刑事处罚,该处罚适用于未能履行登记及相关义务的外国人。本规则也未要求将任何外国人驱逐出境。登记要求适用于所有年满14周岁、尚未登记和按捺指纹且在美国境内停留30天或以上的外国人。如果外国人没有其他途径进行登记和按捺指纹,该外国人可以提交G-325R表以履行《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条,即8 U.S.C. 1302条规定的义务。预计本规则将提高国土安全部的执法效能,并提供有关外国人在美国所在位置的更全面信息,从而使国土安全部更容易、更安全地执行法律。对法定指纹要求的更高合规率,将为国土安全部提供有关外国人犯罪记录的额外信息,包括该外国人是否为已知或疑似恐怖分子。最后,国土安全部重申,《移民与国籍法》中的外国人登记要求是针对外国人的,而非美国公民。
Response: DHS strongly disagrees with the characterization of the IFR as linked to political rhetoric against immigrant communities and racial prejudice, rather than legitimate concerns of implementing a statutory mandate, as well as national and public security concerns. The laws of the United States generally require aliens to register—either as part of the visa process or upon being present in the United States for 30 days or more. The IFR and this final rule are limited in scope to establish a general registration option for aliens to comply, as applicable, with their statutory duty to register and to be fingerprinted. This rulemaking does not impose any new obligations on any population, or create disparate treatment motivated by racial prejudice. Similarly, the IFR and this rule do not create or modify the criminal penalties established by Congress many decades ago that apply when aliens fail to meet their registration and related obligations. The rule also does not oblige the removal of any alien from the country. The registration requirements apply to all aliens who are 14 years or older, have not been registered and fingerprinted, and remain in the United States for 30 days or longer. If an alien does not have any other pathway to register and to be fingerprinted, the alien may file the Form G-325R to comply with their duty under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. The rule is expected to improve DHS law enforcement efficacy and to provide more comprehensive information about the location of aliens in the United States to make it easier and safer for DHS to enforce the law. The increased compliance with statutory fingerprinting requirements would provide DHS with additional information about an alien's criminal record, including whether the alien is a known or suspected terrorist. Finally, DHS reiterates that the alien registration requirements of the INA require aliens—not U.S. citizens—to register.
评论:两位评论者反对在临时最终规则中使用“外国人”(alien)一词,认为该词具有冒犯性和主观性,且不准确,并建议作为提供保障措施的一部分更改该用语。
Comment: Two commenters objected to the use of the word “alien” in the IFR as offensive and subjective, as well as inaccurate and suggested, as part of providing safeguards, to change the term.
答复:“外国人”(alien)一词是《移民与国籍法》(INA)中定义的法律术语,用于描述任何非美国公民或国民的人员。[39]此外,“外国人”一词被用于《移民与国籍法》第261条至第266条,即8 U.S.C. 第1301条至第1306条,这些条款正是规定外国人登记要求的条款。
Response: The term “alien” is a legal term defined in the INA that is used to describe any person who is not a citizen or national of the United States.[39] Moreover, the term alien is used in sections 261 through 266 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1301 through 1306, the sections that describe the alien registration requirements.
评论意见:一位评论者表示,1940年《外国人登记法》所确立的全面登记政策在其通过后的几十年间已被放弃,部分原因在于其社会和经济成本,并指出执行该登记政策不仅无法实现本届政府的目标,反而会导致基于感知的种族或族裔对美国公民和“非公民”进行歧视性针对。
Comment: A commenter said the comprehensive registration policy that was established in the Alien Registration Act of 1940 was abandoned in the decades following its adoption due, in part, to its social and economic costs, and stated that the enforcement of this registration policy would fail to achieve the goals of the administration while leading to discriminatory targeting of U.S. citizens and “noncitizens” based on their perceived race or ethnicity.
另一位评论者在讨论前“国家安全出入境登记系统”(NSEERS)项目下的歧视性做法时指出,扩大外国人登记要求可能再次导致带有偏见的执法行为,从而侵蚀移民社区与政府机构之间的信任。
While discussing discriminatory practices under the former NSEERS program, another commenter remarked that expanding alien registration requirements could once again lead to biased enforcement practices, eroding trust between immigrant communities and government agencies.
回应:本规则符合第14159号行政命令以及《移民与国籍法》第262条(8 U.S.C. 1302)所规定的外国人登记要求。本规则制定了一项适用于所有未登记外国人的一般性登记选项,无论其移民身份如何,旨在改善特定外国人群体的登记结果。本规则并未改变或修改现行法定要求,大多数外国人仍须遵守现行法定登记要求。
Response: This rule is consistent with E.O. 14159 and the alien registration requirements in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. This rulemaking establishes a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens, regardless of immigration status, to improve the registration outcomes of certain groups of aliens. This rulemaking does not change or modify the existing statutory requirements that most aliens must comply with the existing statutory registration requirements.
评论意见:多条评论普遍担忧,该临时最终规则(IFR)将侵犯《宪法第五修正案》正当程序条款所保障的平等保护权利。评论者援引最高法院判例法,[40]指出该规则将对来自低收入、非英语社区的外国人造成不成比例的影响,因此可能违反平等保护原则,应受到严格审查。一位评论者警告称,该临时最终规则引发平等保护方面的担忧,因为其复杂的要求对正规教育程度有限或英语能力不足的外国人而言将难以承受、令人困惑。评论者对该临时最终规则所隐含的“出示证件”要求所导致的平等保护侵犯和种族定性问题表示特别关切。
Comment: Multiple comments expressed a general concern that the IFR would violate equal protection rights guaranteed under the Fifth Amendment's Due Process Clause. Citing to case law from the Supreme Court,[40] commenters indicated that the rule would disproportionately impact aliens from low-income, non-English-speaking communities and that it, therefore, may violate equal protection and should be subject to strict scrutiny. A commenter warned that the IFR raises equal protection concerns because its complex requirements would be overwhelming and confusing for aliens with limited formal education or English proficiency. Commenters expressed particular concern for equal protection violations and racial profiling resulting from the IFR's implicit “show your papers” requirement.
一位评论者指出,该临时最终规则将违反平等保护原则,因为G-325R表仅提供英文版本,并指出,尽管原告在提出平等保护主张时须证明存在歧视性意图,而该临时最终规则在表面上是中立的,但本届政府已作出若干声明并采取其他行动,例如宣布英语为美国官方语言,这些均支持如下论点,即政府选择不翻译G-325R表,系作为限制政府服务语言获取渠道这一更大计划的一部分。
A commenter indicated that the IFR would violate equal protection principles because Form G-325R is only available in English, remarking that while a plaintiff must prove a discriminatory purpose to prove in an equal protection claim and the IFR is facially neutral, the administration has made statements and taken other actions, such as declaring English as the official U.S. language, that would support the contention that the government elected not to translate Form G-325R as part of larger scheme to limit language access in government services.
一位评论者表示,尽管政府出于对居民信息的了解存在正当利益的合理论据,但该临时最终规则将对受其条款约束的外国人造成不成比例且有害的负担,因而超出了该政府利益的范围,从而构成平等保护方面的侵犯。(印刷页第39280页)
A commenter stated that while there are bona fide arguments for government interest in information about residents, the IFR would create a disproportionate and deleterious burden on aliens subject to its terms, and therefore exceeds that government interest, crossing into equal protection violations. ( printed page 39280)
回应:国土安全部(DHS)不同意本规则违反平等保护原则的说法。《移民与国籍法》第262条(8 U.S.C. 1302)是一项表面中立的法律,要求“现在或此后在美国境内的每一名外国人,凡(1)年满十四周岁,(2)尚未依据[《移民与国籍法》第211(b)条]登记并捺指纹,且(3)在美国境内停留三十天或以上者,均须在该三十天期满前申请登记并捺指纹。”8 U.S.C. 1302(a)(着重号为引用者所加)。本规则同样设立了一项附加机制,使任何此前未登记的外国人均可据此履行其法定义务,而不论该外国人的国籍为何。[41] 因此,本规则并未违反平等保护原则。
Response: DHS disagrees that this rule violates equal protection. Section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, is a facially neutral law, requiring “ every alien now or hereafter in the United States, who (1) is fourteen years of age or older, (2) has not been registered and fingerprinted under [section 211(b) of the INA], and (3) remains in the United States for thirty days or longer, to apply for registration and to be fingerprinted before the expiration of such thirty days.” 8 U.S.C. 1302(a) (emphasis added). This rule likewise creates an additional mechanism by which any previously unregistered alien may comply with his or her statutory duty regardless of the alien's country of nationality.[41] It thus does not violate equal protection.
f. 第四修正案相关问题
f. Fourth Amendment Issues
评论意见:多位评论者普遍担忧,该临时最终规则将导致侵犯《宪法第四修正案》权利的情形,例如对美国公民和外国人实施任意搜查和扣押。一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则在缺乏明确且严格限定的执法指南的情况下予以实施,将增加违反第四修正案的风险,因为执法人员可能仅为核实登记状态,在缺乏合理怀疑或合理根据的情况下拦截并拘留个人。该评论者认为,这将形成一种与种族挂钩的、违宪的“非法推定”,而这种做法早已被联邦法院所否定。
Comment: Several commenters expressed a general concern that the IFR would lead to violations of Fourth Amendment rights, such as arbitrary searches and seizures of U.S. citizens and aliens alike. A commenter stated that the IFR's implementation without clear and narrowly tailored enforcement guidelines would increase the risk of Fourth Amendment violations, as officers may stop and detain individuals without reasonable suspicion or probable cause solely to verify registration status. This commenter reasoned that this would create an unconstitutional presumption of illegality tied to race, a practice long rejected by Federal courts.
答复:国土安全部不同意该临时最终规则将导致侵犯外国人第四修正案权利的观点。国会长期以来一直要求《移民与国籍法》第262条、《美国法典》第8编第1302节所述的外国人进行登记并按要求留取指纹,同时随身携带登记证明并将地址变更情况告知国土安全部(DHS),而执行这些法律是国土安全部的职责。参见《移民与国籍法》第103条(a)款、《美国法典》第8编第1103节(a)款;另参见第14159号行政命令第7条《保护美国人民免受入侵》(2025年1月20日),《联邦纪事》第90卷第8443页,第8444页(2025年1月29日)。该临时最终规则和最终规则的目标在于确保外国人了解其法律义务,并通过新的一般登记程序和表格获得履行该义务的途径。该临时最终规则并未改变国土安全部与外国人接触方面已有的授权。
Response: DHS disagrees that the IFR would lead to violations of an alien's Fourth Amendment rights. Congress has long required aliens described in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, to register and be fingerprinted (if required) as well as carry evidence of registration and notify DHS of a change in address, and it is the duty of DHS to carry out those laws. See INA sec. 103(a), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a); seeE.O. No. 14159 sec. 7, Protecting the American People Against Invasion (Jan. 20, 2025), 90 FR 8443, 8444 (Jan. 29, 2025). The goal of the IFR and final rule is to ensure that aliens understand their duty under the law and have a path to comply with that duty through the new general registration process and form. The IFR has not changed the existing authority provided to DHS on engagement with aliens.
g. 第八修正案问题
g. Eighth Amendment Issues
评论意见:一位评论者表示担忧,称使“无证件人员”对其原籍国的潜在后果以及移民与海关执法局(ICE)的“残酷对待”产生恐惧,可能构成对其第八修正案权利的侵犯。另一位评论者表达了担忧,认为该临时最终规则将导致不人道和非法的域外或无限期监禁。一位评论者表示,该临时最终规则对外国人不遵守规定所施加的处罚与所指控的违法行为不成比例,尤其是在违规行为属于技术性或无意性质的情况下。该评论者援引特罗普诉杜勒斯案(Trop v. Dulles, 356 U.S. 86 (1958)),该案认定处罚必须与罪行相称,并据此得出结论称,该临时最终规则因对程序性或技术性违规行为施加处罚而违反了这一原则,评论者称这构成残酷和非常规的处罚。
Comment: A commenter raised concerns that causing “undocumented people” to live in fear of both potential consequences in their country of origin and “cruel treatment” from ICE could constitute a violation of their Eighth Amendment rights. Another commenter expressed concern that the IFR would lead to inhumane and illegal extraterritorial or indefinite imprisonment. A commenter stated that the penalties aliens would face under the IFR for noncompliance are disproportionate to the alleged offense, particularly where the violation is technical or inadvertent. Citing Trop v. Dulles, 356 U.S. 86 (1958), which found that punishment must be proportionate to the offense, the commenter concluded that the IFR violates this principle by imposing penalties for procedural or technical violations, which the commenter said would constitute cruel and unusual punishment.
答复:国会已就与移民相关的各类违法行为设立了民事和刑事处罚。尽管某些外国人可能担心因其自身选择和行为而受到相关处罚,但本规则并未设立任何此类后果或违法行为。本规则对在美外国人可获得的宪法保护也没有任何影响。《移民与国籍法》第262条至第265条(《美国法典》第8编第1302节至第1305节)所规定的因未能登记或未能满足其他相关要求而产生的刑事处罚,是由国会设立的,也不在本规则的范围之内。
Response: Congress has established civil and criminal penalties for a variety of offenses relating to immigration. While certain aliens may be afraid of being subjected to the penalties connected to their choices and actions, this rule does not establish any such consequences or offenses. This rule also has no impact on the constitutional protections available to aliens within the United States. The criminal penalties for failure to register or meet the other associated requirements under sections 262 through 265 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302-1305, were established by Congress and also fall outside the scope of this rule.
h. 隐私权
h. Privacy Rights
评论意见:一位评论者写道,根据现行法律和法规,移民与海关执法局(ICE)不能将本登记信息用于其执法行动,因为根据《美国法典》第8编第1357节(f)款第(2)项,移民与海关执法局是一个民事执法机构,而非执法机关。该评论者还表示,该临时最终规则不包含司法部长作出的任何指定,以扩大可共享登记和指纹记录的人员范围,而这是《美国法典》第8编第1304节(b)款所要求的。
Comment: A commenter wrote that the information collected by this registration information cannot be used by ICE under existing law and regulation, for its enforcement actions because under 8 U.S.C. 1357(f)(2), ICE is a civil enforcement body, not a law enforcement agency. The commenter also stated that the IFR does not contain any designations by the Attorney General to expand the persons with whom registration and fingerprint records may be shared, as required under 8 U.S.C. 1304(b).
答复:约85年来,美国法律一直要求在美国境内停留30天或以上的大多数外国人进行登记,并且除基于年龄或非移民身份的若干例外情形外,还须留取指纹。国土安全部通过该临时最终规则和本最终规则,并未改变国会为外国人设立的现行法定登记要求,而仅仅是规定了一种供外国人按照法律要求进行登记的一般方式。外国人须登记、须在迁居后10日内向政府更新其地址,以及须随身携带登记证明等要求,同样是国会在20世纪中期设立的。同样,该临时最终规则也没有创设或修改国会数十年前设立的、适用于外国人未能履行登记及相关义务时的刑事处罚。本规则符合第14159号行政命令以及《移民与国籍法》第262条至第265条(《美国法典》第8编第1302节至第1305节)所规定的外国人登记要求,并为所有未登记的外国人设立了一种不论其移民身份如何均可使用的一般登记方式。
Response: For approximately 85 years, the laws of the United States have required most aliens present in the United States for 30 days or longer to register and, with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted. DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not changed the existing statutory registration requirements or established by Congress for aliens but merely prescribed a general means for aliens to register, as required by law. The requirements that aliens are required to register, update his or her address with the government within 10 days of moving, and carry evidence of registration on their person, were also established by Congress in the middle of the 20th century. Similarly, this IFR has not created or modified the criminal penalties established by Congress many decades ago that apply when aliens fail to meet their registration and related obligations. This rule is consistent with E.O. 14159 and the alien registration requirements in sections 262 through 265 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1305, and establishes a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status.
关于涉及依据《移民与国籍法》第264条(b)款、《美国法典》第8编第1304节(b)款共享信息的评论意见,国土安全部不同意评论者关于登记和指纹记录不能在国土安全部内部为移民执法目的进行共享的观点,也不同意评论者关于移民与海关执法局总体上并非执法机关的观点。
In regard to the comment addressing sharing of information pursuant to section 264(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(b), DHS disagrees with the commenter's view that registration and fingerprint records cannot be shared within DHS for immigration enforcement purposes, as well as the commenter's view that ICE is not a law enforcement agency in general.
此外,根据《移民与国籍法》第264(b)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(b)款,所有登记和指纹记录“应予保密,且仅得向以下对象提供”:(1)依据《移民与国籍法》第287(f)(2)条,即8 U.S.C. 1357(f)(2)款(即该评论者所援引的条款);以及(2)部长可能指定的此类人员或机构。[42]该制定法并未指示仅由美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)负责外国人登记及规定登记表格,或将外国人登记记录予以保密。该制定法将这些权力赋予部长,而USCIS、美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)以及国土安全部(DHS)其他组成部门的所有权力均归属于部长。[43]若将保密条款解释为要求部长对ICE和CBP(二者均为DHS下属机构)保密外国人登记信息,则几乎毫无意义,尤其是考虑到部长拥有向其可能指定的“此类人员或机构”(印刷页第39281页)提供外国人登记及指纹记录的全权。
Moreover, under section 264(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(b), all registration and fingerprint records “shall be confidential, and shall be made available only”: (1) pursuant to section 287(f)(2) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1357(f)(2) ( i.e., the provision cited by the commenter); and (2) to such persons or agencies as may be designated by the Secretary.[42] The statute does not direct USCIS alone to register aliens and prescribe registration forms, or to hold alien registration records confidential. The statute vests these authorities in the Secretary, in whom all authorities of USCIS, ICE, and other DHS components are vested.[43] It would make little sense to interpret the confidentiality provision to require the Secretary to hold alien registration information confidential as against ICE and CBP, which are subagencies within DHS, particularly in light of the Secretary's plenary authority to make alien registration and fingerprint records available “to such ( printed page 39281) persons or agencies” as he may designate.
无论如何,该临时最终规则(IFR)并未改变DHS在信息(包括外国人登记信息)的维护、收集和使用方面的做法;即便在该IFR出台之前,此类信息也已可供ICE和CBP使用。[44]
In any event, the IFR did not change DHS practices related to the maintenance, collection, and use of the information, including alien registration information; such information was available to ICE and CBP even before the IFR.[44]
评论意见:多位评论者表示普遍担忧,认为该IFR将侵犯个人隐私权。一位评论者认为,强制登记(包括现居地址)对1974年《隐私法》及格里斯沃尔德诉康涅狄格州案(Griswold v. Connecticut, 381 U.S. 479 (1965))所保障的隐私权构成威胁。该评论者指出,对过度监控的担忧已多次导致普遍登记要求被推迟或否决,包括2005年《真实身份法》(REAL ID Act)实施过程中长期的延迟。[45]该评论者补充说,由于该IFR未针对年龄设定登记要求的例外情形,仅对14岁以下外国人作出程序性宽限规定,因此隐私方面的担忧更为突出。该评论者表示,对此类信息保护不足,会引发对未成年人及家庭暴力幸存者安全的担忧。
Comment: Several commenters expressed general concern that the IFR would violate individuals' privacy rights. A commenter reasoned that mandatory registration, including current addresses, poses threats to privacy rights guaranteed by the Privacy Act of 1974 and Griswold v. Connecticut, 381 U.S. 479 (1965). The commenter remarked that fears of excessive surveillance have repeatedly led to the deferral or rejection of universal registration mandates, including the long-running delays in the implementation of the REAL ID Act of 2005 (REAL ID Act).[45] The commenter added that the privacy concerns are heightened because the IFR makes no exception for registration requirements based on age, only procedural allowances for aliens under the age of 14. The commenter said that the inadequate privacy protection for this information raises considerations for the safety of both minors and survivors of domestic abuse.
一位评论者指出,该IFR对与登记要求相关的隐私及保密保护事宜未作说明,并表示DHS根据该IFR收集的信息如涉及披露,必须符合8 U.S.C. 1367款及相关指南的规定。
A commenter stated that the IFR is silent on privacy and confidentiality protections associated with the registration requirement, remarking that any DHS disclosures of information collected under the IFR must comply with 8 U.S.C. 1367 and related guidance.
一位评论者表示担忧,认为该IFR未就其隐私影响或其与现行法律(包括8 U.S.C. 1367款及相关指南,如《DHS第002-02-001号指令(第00.1版修订)》《DHS第215-01号指令》《DHS第215-01-001号指令》及《DHS第215-01-002号指令》)的符合情况提供明确信息。该评论者指出,由于缺乏明确性,个人(包括受8 U.S.C. 1367款保护的人员或“童年入境者暂缓遣返”(DACA)受益人)将难以充分理解其信息将如何依照现行法律被使用和共享。
A commenter expressed concern that the IFR does not provide clear information regarding its privacy impact or how it comports with existing law, including 8 U.S.C. 1367 and related guidance such as DHS Instruction 002-02-001, Revision 00.1; DHS Directive 215-01; DHS Instruction 215-01-001; and DHS Instruction 215-01-002. The commenter remarked that the lack of clarity would make it difficult for individuals, including those subject to 8 U.S.C. 1367 protections or Deferred Action for Childhood Arrivals (DACA) recipients, to fully understand how their information would be used and shared in compliance with existing law.
回应:提交G-325R表格的外国人,并不会仅因提交该表格而被认定为8 U.S.C. 1367款所保护的外国人;但是,若该外国人基于其他理由已被认定为受保护外国人,USCIS将对其给予同等保护,包括对其安全地址的保护。G-325R表格设有供外国人填写其邮寄地址或安全地址的栏目,若其实际居住地址与邮寄地址或安全地址不同,还需填写该实际居住地址。与所有允许外国人提供安全地址的USCIS表格一样,若USCIS通过邮件方式联系该外国人,USCIS将使用其提供的安全地址。然而,目前G-325R的处理流程完全以电子方式进行。USCIS发送给外国人的所有通知均上传至该外国人的myUSCIS账户。与G-325R表格相关的通知,均不通过邮寄方式发出。因此,不仅外国人可依照USCIS长期以来的做法提供安全地址,而且目前USCIS在处理G-325R表格事务时并不通过邮寄方式发送任何文件。
Response: Aliens submitting G-325R do not qualify as protected aliens under 8 U.S.C. 1367, by virtue of such filing; however, if the alien is otherwise designated as a protected alien on another basis, USCIS maintains the same protection for the alien, including for the safe address. Form G-325R provides fields for the alien's mailing or safe address, and if different from his or her mailing or safe address, the alien's physical address. As with all USCIS forms in which an alien may provide a safe address, if USCIS contacts the alien through the mail, USCIS will use the safe address that he or she provides. However, the G-325R process is entirely electronic at this time. All notices sent from USCIS to an alien are uploaded to the alien's myUSCIS account. None of the notices correlating to Form G-325R are issued via mail. Therefore, not only may aliens provide a safe address, consistent with longstanding USCIS practice, USCIS does not at this time send any documents through the mail in connection with Form G-325R.
本规则同样未改变DHS及其下属机构为防止其收集、传播、使用或保存的个人身份信息遭未经授权披露而采取的任何程序或做法。
This rule also does not change any procedures or practices of DHS and its subagencies to protect against the unauthorized disclosure of personally identifiable information that DHS collects, disseminates, uses, or maintains.
一、行动自由
i. Freedom of Movement
评论:一位评论者指出,临时最终规则(IFR)所设定的与登记及更新地址要求相关的经济处罚和刑事处罚,将威胁个人的行动自由权,而该项权利已经最高法院在Paul诉Virginia案(8 Wall. 168 (1868))和Saenz诉Roe案(526 U.S. 489 (1999))中予以确认。关于该临时最终规则序言中所述“任何未能遵守8 U.S.C. 1305条规定的地址变更通知要求的外国人,均可被驱逐出境,除非该外国人能够证明该未遵守行为具有合理的可原谅性或并非故意”(援引8 U.S.C. 1227(a)(3)(A))的说法,评论者表示,该论述不够充分,因为它“未提供任何框架供登记人或联邦官员据以解释该条款,使登记人面临不当的主观性判断风险。”
Comment: A commenter stated that the financial and criminal penalties created by the IFR, related to the requirement to register and update addresses, would threaten individuals' freedom of movement, a right affirmed by the Supreme Court in Paul v. Virginia, 8 Wall. 168 (1868), and Saenz v. Roe, 526 U.S. 489 (1999). With respect to the statement in the IFR's preamble that “any alien who has failed to comply with the change-of-address notification requirements of 8 U.S.C. 1305 is deportable unless the alien establishes that such failure was reasonably excusable or was not willful” (citing 8 U.S.C. 1227(a)(3)(A)), the commenter stated that this discussion is insufficient because it “provides no framework by which this clause may be interpreted by either registrants or federal officials, exposing registrants to undue subjectivity.”
回应:本临时最终规则既未限制外国人的行动自由[46],也未创设或修改国会数十年前所确立的、适用于外国人未能履行其登记及相关义务时的刑事处罚。外国人须进行登记、须在迁居后10日内向政府更新其地址、以及须随身携带登记证明的要求,也并非新设要求。国土安全部(DHS)通过本临时最终规则及本最终规则,并未创设这些要求,也未修改国会为外国人设定的义务。向国土安全部提供最新地址是每位受管辖外国人自身的责任。
Response: This IFR neither restricts an alien's freedom of movement [46] nor creates or modifies the criminal penalties, established by Congress many decades ago, that apply when aliens failed to meet their registration and related obligations. The requirements that aliens are required to register, update their address with the government within 10 days of moving, and carry evidence of registration on their person are also not new. DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not created these requirements or modified the duties established by Congress for aliens. Providing a current address to DHS is the responsibility of each covered alien.
j. 权力分立
j. Separation of Powers
评论:一位评论者援引宪法第一条第八款、INS诉Chadha案(462 U.S. 919 (1983))以及布朗诉教育委员会案(347 U.S. 483 (1954)),指出该临时最终规则违反权力分立原则,并指出对移民权利产生重大影响的法规应以国会立法为依据,而不应仅依赖行政规则制定。
Comment: Citing Article I, Section 8 of the Constitution, INS v. Chadha, 462 U.S. 919 (1983), and Brown v. Board of Education, 347 U.S. 483 (1954), a commenter stated that the IFR violates the separation of powers doctrine, and that regulations that significantly affect immigrants' rights should be grounded in congressional legislation, not solely in administrative rulemaking.
回应:该临时最终规则确实牢固地建立在国会立法基础之上;它实施了《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条至第266条(即8 U.S.C. 1302条至1306条)所规定的外国人登记要求。此外,《移民与国籍法》第103(a)条(即8 U.S.C. 1103(a)条)以及前述登记条款,均为实施这些登记要求提供了广泛的法定授权。这些法律均经国会正式制定。国土安全部不同意评论者关于该临时最终规则存在任何权力分立缺陷的观点。
Response: The IFR is firmly grounded in congressional legislation; it implemented the alien registration requirements of sections 262 through 266 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1306. Additionally, section 103(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1103(a), as well as the registration provisions cited previously, provide broad statutory authorization to implement these registration requirements. These laws are duly enacted by Congress. DHS disagrees with the commenters that the IFR suffers from any separation-of-powers defect.
4. 临时最终规则对国际法的遵守情况
4. The IFR's Compliance With International Law
评论:一位评论者指出,该临时最终规则将违反美国对难民及寻求庇护者所负有的国际法律义务,这些义务源自《1967年关于难民地位的议定书》,并已通过《1980年难民法》(第39282页)纳入美国国内法,且已经最高法院在INS诉Cardoza-Fonseca案(480 U.S. 421, 426 (1987))中作出解释。该评论者指出,在国际上,登记要求旨在促进对难民的保护,但该临时最终规则似乎意图将登记作为针对个人实施驱逐的手段。该评论者写道,第14159号行政命令所宣称的目标是“忠实执行美国移民法律”,但该命令并未承认这些法律包括《难民法》,而忠实执行这些法律应当包括酌情提供庇护及其他人道主义保护。该评论者批评该临时最终规则未能将此类保护纳入考量。
Comment: A commenter stated that the IFR would violate U.S. international legal obligations to refugees and asylum seekers, obligations stemming from the 1967 Protocol Relating to the Status of Refugees that have been implemented in domestic U.S. law through the Refugee ( printed page 39282) Act of 1980 and that have been interpreted by the Supreme Court in INS v. Cardoza-Fonseca, 480 U.S. 421, 426 (1987). The commenter remarked that, internationally, registration requirements are intended to facilitate the protection of refugees but that the IFR appears to intend to use registration as a method to target individuals for removal. The commenter wrote that E.O. 14159's purported aim is to “faithfully executing the immigration laws of the United States,” but it does not acknowledge that those laws include the Refugee Act, and that such faithful execution includes providing asylum and other humanitarian protections as appropriate. The commenter criticized the IFR, stating that it fails to take such protections into account.
具体而言,该评论者援引了1951年《关于难民地位的公约》第31条第1款,该条款规定,难民如直接来自其生命或自由受到威胁的领土,未经许可即进入或逗留于缔约国领土,只要毫不迟延地向当局报到,并说明其非法入境或居留的正当理由,除特定情形外,一般不得因其非法入境或居留而受到惩罚。该评论者指出,临时最终规则(IFR)对于该规则是否适用以及如何适用于庇护申请人、已获庇护者以及尚未申请庇护的有保护需求人员缺乏明确说明,且未对其个人情况的考量作出规定。该评论者据此认为,若将临时最终规则规定的民事和刑事处罚适用于难民,根据国际法,通常将构成此类不被允许的惩罚。最后,该评论者表示担忧,登记要求可能直接导致难民在违反不驱回原则的情况下被遣返,或者对于正处于庇护申请程序中或已完成该程序的外国人而言,未遵守登记要求的处罚可能导致庇护酌情裁定不利,或在日后干扰其身份调整或入籍程序。
More specifically, the commenter cited Article 31(1) of the 1951 Convention Relating to the Status of Refugees, which establishes that refugees generally may not be penalized for their illegal entry or presence when they come directly from territories where their life or freedom is threatened, present themselves without delay to the authorities, and show good cause for their irregular entry or presence save under certain specified circumstances. Stating that the IFR lacks clarity with respect to whether and how it applies to aliens who are asylees, asylum applicants, and people with protection needs who have not yet applied for asylum, and that it makes no provision for consideration of their individual circumstances, the commenter concluded that the IFR's civil and criminal penalties, if applied to refugees, would generally constitute such impermissible penalties according to international law. Finally, the commenter expressed concern that the registration requirement could lead directly to refugees being removed in violation of non-refoulement requirements, or, for aliens who are in or have completed the asylum application process, that registration noncompliance penalties could lead to either a negative discretionary asylum determination or interfere later with their adjustment of status or naturalization.
其他评论者指出,临时最终规则将通过对隐私权、家庭权和住宅权的任意干涉而侵犯人权。评论者指出,关于保护机密信息的说明不足。评论者虽承认政府有权监管移民事务,但将该临时最终规则定性为一种意图恐吓外国人的举措,存在侵犯人权的风险。
Other commenters stated that the IFR would infringe on human rights through arbitrary interference with privacy, family, and home law. The commenters noted inadequate information on safeguarding confidential information. While acknowledging the government's authority to regulate immigration, the commenters characterized the IFR as an effort to intimidate aliens that risks violating human rights.
答复:该临时最终规则及本规则的适用范围有限,仅为所有未登记的外国人(不论其移民身份如何)设立一种通用的登记方式。已获得8 CFR第264.1(b)条所指定的登记证明文件之一的外国人,已完成登记;已提交8 CFR第264.1(a)条所指定表格之一并提供指纹(如未获豁免)、但尚未获发8 CFR第264.1(b)条所指定登记证明文件的外国人,已履行《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条(8 U.S.C. 1302)规定的登记义务。如外国人没有其他途径办理登记和采集指纹,则可提交G-325R表格,以履行《移民与国籍法》第262条(8 U.S.C. 1302)规定的义务。
Response: The IFR and this rule are limited in scope to establish a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. An alien who has been issued one of the documents designated as evidence of registration under 8 CFR 264.1(b) has already registered, and an alien who has submitted one of the forms designated at 8 CFR 264.1(a) and provided fingerprints (unless waived), but was not issued one of the evidence of registration designated at 8 CFR 264.1(b), has complied with the registration requirement of section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. If an alien does not have any other pathway to register and to be fingerprinted, the alien may file the Form G-325R to comply with their duty under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302.
此外,1951年《难民公约》及其1967年《议定书》均未禁止缔约国要求难民向移民主管机关登记。事实上,该公约第2条规定:“每一难民对其所在国负有义务,此项义务特别要求难民遵守该国的法律和规章,以及为维持公共秩序而采取的措施。”并且,联合国难民事务高级专员公署(UNHCR)鼓励寻求难民保护的外国人及时办理登记。[47]
Moreover, neither the 1951 Refugee Convention nor its 1967 Protocol preclude state parties from requiring refugees to register with immigration authorities. Indeed, Article 2 of the Convention states, “Every refugee has duties to the country in which he finds himself, which require in particular that he conform to its laws and regulations as well as to measures taken for the maintenance of public order.” And the United Nations High Commissioner for Refugees (UNHCR) encourages the prompt registration of aliens seeking refugee protection.[47]
E. 适用范围
E. Applicability
1. 一般适用性
1. General Applicability
评论:多位评论者表示担忧,该规则及新的登记程序在谁须登记、谁可豁免方面造成混乱。评论者指出,该临时最终规则及登记要求造成混乱且缺乏明确性,因此该临时最终规则将导致错误,致使合法移民因未随身携带登记证明而被错误处罚。评论者同样表示担忧,该规则在谁被视为已登记方面存在混乱,对于已提交移民表格并可能已参加美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)生物识别信息采集预约的个人而言,缺乏明确说明。
Comment: Several commenters expressed concern that the rule and the new registration process creates confusion surrounding who is required to register and who is exempt. Commenters stated that the IFR and registration requirements caused confusion and lacked clarity and, therefore, the IFR would lead to errors and result in legal immigrants being wrongly penalized for not carrying proof of registration. Commenters similarly expressed concern that the rule is confusing about who is considered registered and lacks clarity for individuals who have already filed immigration forms and may have attended USCIS biometrics appointments.
另一位评论者写道,《临时最终规则》(IFR)本身具有“令人困惑”的性质,加之本届政府加强移民执法行动所带来的焦虑与恐惧,可能导致错误信息的传播,甚至对不属于该要求适用对象的个人也会产生“严重”后果。该评论者写道,不了解如何登记的困惑与合法身份突然被取消相结合,构成了一种诱骗陷阱。其他评论者表示,日益增多的官僚障碍可能会阻碍或使合规变得更加复杂。有几位评论者表示,《临时最终规则》给本已复杂的移民体系增添了不必要的复杂性。一位评论者写道,《临时最终规则》造成了混乱和恐惧,尤其是对那些可能已经通过其他程序被视为已登记的移民而言更是如此。另一位评论者也认为,《临时最终规则》在那些认为自己已经提交了登记证明的人群中造成了混乱,而《临时最终规则》中遗漏了某些常见的移民文件,进一步加剧了这种混乱的可能性。一位评论者表示,通过施加带有刑事处罚的登记要求以惩戒不合规行为,无论个人的合法身份或过往经历如何,该规则都将移民视为执法打击的对象。
Another commenter wrote that the “confusing” nature of the IFR, coupled with the anxiety and fear, driven by the administration's enhanced immigration enforcement efforts, could lead to the spread of misinformation, with “drastic” consequences even for individuals not subject to the requirement. The commenter wrote that the combination of confusion on how to register with the sudden removal of legal status is entrapment. Other commenters stated that increased bureaucratic hurdles may discourage or complicate compliance. A couple of commenters stated that the IFR adds unnecessary complexity to an already complex immigration system. A commenter wrote that the IFR creates confusion and fear, particularly for immigrants who may already be considered registered through other processes. Another commenter echoed that the IFR creates confusion among those who believe they have already submitted proof of registration, and the omission of certain common immigration documents within the IFR exacerbates the potential for confusion. A commenter stated that, by imposing registration requirements with criminal penalties for noncompliance, it treats immigrants as enforcement targets, regardless of their legal status or history.
一位评论者表示担忧,《临时最终规则》在登记要求方面对移民表格的处理方式不充分,将给那些本应被视为已登记的合法在美人员带来压力、恐惧和困惑。该评论者还表示,他们从社区成员处了解到,一些不法分子正在利用这种混乱局面,承诺通过登记程序获得永久居留权,并故意混淆“登记簿(Registry)”与“登记(Registration)”两个概念,以此诈骗弱势群体。
A commenter expressed concern that the IFR's approach to immigration forms for registration requirements was inadequate and would create stress, fear, and confusion among lawfully present individuals who should be considered registered. The commenter additionally stated that they had heard from community members that unscrupulous actors were exploiting this confusion by promising permanent residency through the registration process and deliberately conflating “Registry” with “Registration” to defraud vulnerable individuals.
具体而言,评论者表示担忧,由于《临时最终规则》缺乏指导说明,个人将难以准确判断自己此前是否已经登记,从而导致个人未能提交G-325R表。评论者特别担忧所列举的登记表格清单以及构成登记证明的表格清单,评论者指出,这些表格清单并未涵盖所有似乎符合《移民与国籍法》(INA)规定的登记及指纹采集门槛要求的表格。一位评论者表示,那些已提交冗长详细的移民申请表并接受指纹和生物识别信息采集的人,理应合理地认为自己已经登记。(印刷版第39283页)
Specifically, commenters expressed concern that it would be difficult for individuals to accurately assess whether they have previously registered because of the lack of guidance in the IFR, and, therefore, would lead to individuals failing to file Form G-325R. Commenters expressed particular concern about the enumerated lists of registration forms and forms that constitute evidence of registration; commenters stated that these lists of forms do not include all forms that would appear to meet the threshold requirements for registration and fingerprints as prescribed in the INA. A commenter said that those who have submitted lengthy and detailed immigration application forms and undergone fingerprinting and biometrics would reasonably believe that they are already registered. ( printed page 39283)
一位评论者写道,个人可能不知道其在美国边境获准入境是否依据假释(parole)程序,而且鉴于美国南部边境案件处理方式差异很大,他们可能也没有简便的方法判断自己是否被签发过出庭通知书(NTA)。该评论者表示,其成员和客户通常不知道在边境被签发了哪些文件,许多人在抵达居住地之前就已经遗失了这些文件。该评论者还提出了关于混乱的其他担忧,例如,并非所有被签发出庭通知书的成员都在该过程中被采集了指纹。他们表示,该规则所暗示的,即他们无需再做任何其他事情即可被视为“已登记”,没有提供任何指导,也使人对他们是否还需要满足指纹采集要求产生疑问。该评论者就提交I-485表的个人提出了同样的问题,即尚不清楚他们此前是否曾被采集指纹,或者此前可能是数十年前采集的指纹是否足以满足登记要求。其他评论者表示,《临时最终规则》中含糊不清的措辞使相关要求难以理解,对于持有“童年入境者暂缓遣返”(DACA)身份或“临时保护身份”(TPS)的人而言尤其如此,这些人在技术上已在美国政府处登记,但如果他们没有工作许可,则必须重新登记。
A commenter wrote that individuals may not know whether their admission at a U.S. border was pursuant to parole or not, and they may not have an easy way to tell whether an NTA was issued to them, given the wide variation in case processing at the southern U.S. border. The commenter said that its members and clients generally do not know which documents were issued to them at the border and many have lost their documents by the time they reach their place of residence. The commenter raised additional concerns about confusion stating, for instance, that not all of their members who were issued NTAs were fingerprinted in the process. They said that the rule's suggestion that they do not need to do anything else to be considered “registered” leaves no guidance and questions about whether they also need to comply with the fingerprinting requirement. The commenter raised the same question for individuals who submit Form I-485 and for whom it is unclear whether they were previously fingerprinted or if any prior fingerprinting, potentially decades ago, is sufficient for purposes of the registration. Other commenters said that opaque language in the IFR makes the requirements difficult to understand, particularly for those with DACA or TPS status who are technically registered with the U.S. Government but would have to re-register only if they did not have a work permit.
回应:在关于某外国人是否已履行其登记义务存在混淆的情况下,该临时最终规则(IFR)对此种混淆不负有责任,且实际上可能有助于消除此种混淆。该临时最终规则有助于确保所有外国人均有一种简明直接的方式来履行其外国人登记义务。该临时最终规则并未创设外国人登记义务,也未对外国人未能履行该等义务施加新的后果;登记的要求、随身携带登记证明的要求以及未能履行该等要求的后果,均源自制定法。且总统已指示美国国土安全部(DHS)和美国司法部(DOJ)优先全面实施和执行该法律。如果某外国人在该临时最终规则发布之前对其登记状态不确定,或已丢失履行《移民与国籍法》外国人登记要求所需的文件,该外国人如今可能认为有必要澄清其登记状态或申请补发文件;但这并非该临时最终规则所致。
Response: To the extent that confusion exists regarding whether a given alien has met their registration obligations, the IFR is not responsible for such confusion, and may in fact help abate it. The IFR helped ensure that all aliens have a straightforward way to comply with their alien registration obligations. The IFR did not create alien registration obligations or impose new consequences on aliens for failing to meet those obligations; the requirement to register, the requirement to carry evidence of registration, and the consequences for failing to do so are all creatures of statute. And the President directed DHS and DOJ to prioritize full implementation and enforcement of the law. If an alien was unsure about their registration status prior to the IFR or had lost documents necessary to comply with the alien registration requirements of the INA, the alien might today see a need to clarify his or her registration status or seek a replacement document; but that is not a function of the IFR.
除发布该临时最终规则以公开在美未登记外国人履行登记要求的法律义务外,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)还建立了一个专门网站,提供有关外国人登记要求(ARR)的信息,并创建了一个ARR工具,可帮助外国人确定其是否必须登记。48
In addition to the IFR that publicized the legal obligation of unregistered aliens in the United States to comply with the registration requirements, USCIS established a dedicated website with information on the ARR and created an ARR Tool that may help aliens determine whether they must register.48
原则上,国土安全部同意,如果某申请表格包含8 U.S.C. 1304(a)所述外国人登记所需的数据要素,且该外国人已满足相关指纹要求,国土安全部应考虑根据8 CFR 264.1(a)和(b)款将此类申请表格及相关登记证明予以指定。在本序言第V.A节中,国土安全部就其未来可能指定的若干潜在表格征求意见。
In principle, DHS agrees that if an application form contains the data elements for alien registration described in 8 U.S.C. 1304(a) and the alien has satisfied relevant fingerprinting requirements, DHS should consider designating such application form and related evidence of registration under 8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b). In section V.A of this preamble, DHS seeks comment on a number of potential forms that DHS may designate at a future date.
评论意见:一位评论者同样指出,该部未能考虑该临时最终规则对已登记的更广泛外国人群体所产生的影响,留下了“一系列令人担忧且未得到解决的实际问题”。该评论者质疑,合法永久居民(LPR)是否须随时随身携带其绿卡实体副本,或复印件是否即可。该评论者进一步质疑,如果绿卡遗失或被盗,个人应如何处理,因为补发绿卡的处理时间超过两年。此外,该评论者询问,通过提交福利申请(例如I-485表格)而被视为已登记的人员,是否须随身携带该24页申请表的副本。此外,对于那些登记状态取决于其是否已被置于移民法庭驱逐出境程序中的人员,该评论者询问,即使该程序已在多年或数十年前结束,他们是否仍须随身携带出庭通知书(NTA)的副本。
Comment: A commenter similarly wrote that the Department failed to consider the effects of the IFR on the broader pool of aliens who have registered, leaving a “troubling array of practical issues unaddressed.” The commenter questioned whether LPRs would be required to carry a physical copy of their Green Card at all times, or whether a photocopy would suffice. The commenter further questioned what individuals should do if a Green Card is lost or stolen, as processing times for replacing a Green Card are over 2 years. Additionally, the commenter asked whether those deemed registered by submitting a benefits application, such as a Form I-485, would be required to always carry a copy of the 24-page application on their person.
