Additionally, for those whose registration hinges on their having been placed into immigration court removal proceedings, the commenter asked whether they must carry a copy of an NTA, even if those proceedings concluded years or decades ago.
回应:该临时最终规则并未改变已获发登记证明的外国人须随身携带该证明的要求。该临时最终规则填补了该法规中的一处空白,为此前没有简明方式履行现行制定法登记要求的未登记外国人增加了一种通用选项。此类外国人可使用G-325R表格进行登记,并随身携带USCIS外国人登记证明G-325R表格或其后续表格的副本。
Response: The IFR did not change the requirement that aliens issued evidence of registration carry such evidence on their person. The IFR filled a gap in the regulation by adding a general option for unregistered aliens who previously did not have a straightforward way to comply with the existing statutory registration requirements. Such aliens may register using the Form G-325R and carry a copy of the USCIS Proof of Alien G-325R Registration, or its successor form.
该临时最终规则对合法永久居民(LPR)没有影响,因为合法永久居民被视为已登记,并获发永久居民卡作为登记证明。[49]此类人员不受该临时最终规则将G-325R表格规定为登记表格这一规定的影响。国土安全部指出,根据制定法要求,年满18岁的合法永久居民必须随身携带其登记证明。见《移民与国籍法》第264(e)条,8 U.S.C. 1304(e)。如果合法永久居民的永久居民卡遗失或被盗,且该合法永久居民提交I-90表格申请补发其卡片,则该合法永久居民可获得临时合法永久居民身份证明,例如外国人证件识别及电信(ADIT)印章(也称为I-551印章)。见8 CFR 264.5(h)。国土安全部各机构可查阅国土安全部数据库,其中载有关于待决I-90表格的信息。同样,国土安全部各机构亦可查阅国土安全部数据库,以确认待决的身份调整申请,以及该外国人是否已作为该申请程序的一部分进行了指纹采集。
The IFR had no effects on LPRs, because LPRs are considered registered and are issued a permanent resident card as evidence of registration.[49] Such persons are not affected by the IFR's prescribing of the Form G-325R as a registration form. DHS notes that, as required by statute, LPRs 18 years of age or older must carry their evidence of registration on their person. See INA sec. 264(e), 8 U.S.C. 1304(e). If an LPR's permanent resident card is lost or stolen and the LPR files the Form I-90 to replace his or her card, the LPR could obtain temporary evidence of LPR status, such as an Alien Documentation Identification and Telecommunication (ADIT) stamp (also known as an I-551 stamp). See8 CFR 264.5(h). DHS agencies have access to DHS databases, which contain information regarding a pending Form I-90. Similarly, DHS agencies have access to DHS database to confirm a pending adjustment of status application and whether an alien has been fingerprinted as part of that application.
如果外国人的登记证明是I-862表(《出庭通知书》),该外国人必须随身携带该登记证明,并按照法律要求始终将该证明置于其个人随身携带之中。参见《美国法典》第8编第1304(e)条;《联邦法规汇编》第8编第264.1(b)条。但该要求并非本规则的产物,而是《移民与国籍法》(INA)及长期施行的相关法规中外国人登记要求的产物。
If an alien's evidence of registration is the Form I-862, Notice to Appear, the alien must carry that evidence of registration and always have that evidence in their personal possession as required by the law. See8 U.S.C. 1304(e); 8 CFR 264.1(b). But that requirement is not a function of this rule; it is a function of the alien registration requirements of the INA and longstanding regulations.
2. 对未成年人的适用性
2. Applicability to Minors
a. 适用于未成年人的法律考量
a. Legal Considerations of Applicability to Minors
评论意见:多位评论者讨论了将登记要求适用于未成年人所涉及的法律问题。评论者表示,将临时最终规则(IFR)适用于未成年人会引发诸多担忧,并“暴露出本规则所依据法律的过时性质”。这些评论者指出,自《外国人登记法》制定以来的数十年间,我们对童年和青春期的理解已经发生了演变,包括后续出台的法律已承认儿童所具有的独特而重大的脆弱性和差异性。为支持其关于IFR偏离了当前有关童年和青春期的法律与科学认知这一立场,评论者援引了若干判例法为例,如罗珀诉西蒙斯案(Roper v. Simmons),543 U.S. 551(2005年);高尔特案(In re Gault),387 U.S. 1(1967年);以及德维森案(Matter of Devison),22 I&N Dec. 1362(移民上诉委员会,2000年)。一位评论者呼应上述意见,表示移民法长期以来一直承认未成年人法律与成年人法律之间存在区别,未成年人不应被要求达到与成年人相同的标准。
Comment: Multiple commenters discussed the legal implications of applying registration requirements to minors. Commenters said the application of the IFR to minors would raise numerous concerns and “belies the outdated nature of the law on which this rule is based.” These commenters remarked that in the decades since the Alien Registration Act was written, our understanding of childhood and adolescence has evolved, including intervening law that recognizes the unique, significant vulnerabilities and differences of children. To support their position on the IFR's perceived departure from the prevailing law and science of childhood and adolescence, commenters cited examples of case law, such as Roper v. Simmons, 543 U.S. 551 (2005); In re Gault, 387 U.S. 1 (1967); and Matter of Devison, 22 I&N Dec. 1362 (BIA 2000). A commenter, echoing these remarks, said that immigration law has long recognized a distinction between juvenile and adult law, and children have not been held to the same standards as adults.
多位评论者写道,要求14岁至18岁之间的儿童进行登记并按捺指纹,有悖于美国法律体系中长期存在的规范。评论者写道,青少年司法专家一致认为(印刷页第39284页)青少年司法政策及相关利益方应避免使青少年接触刑事青少年司法系统。因此,评论者推论,期望一个处于初中或高中年龄段的儿童了解、理解并完成向联邦政府登记这一复杂程序及其影响,否则将面临因未能遵守而被卷入美国刑事青少年司法系统的风险,这种做法“过度繁重且严苛,同时也不恰当、缺乏依据”。一位评论者援引《少年犯罪法》以及《美国法典》第18编第5031条和第5032条,指出虽然各州自行确定其成年年龄,但根据联邦法律,未满18岁的个人通常被视为过于年幼而无法承担完全的刑事责任,一般通过各州少年法庭进行处理,只有在涉及暴力犯罪的特定情形下才会按成人程序起诉。该评论者表示担忧,违反登记和生物识别要求的行为可能会使儿童因未能遵守其可能并不完全理解的行政法规而被归类为“少年违法者”。该评论者指出,对于14岁至18岁的外国人未成年人而言,遵守登记要求的前提假设是这些青少年完全了解自己的移民身份,并能够独立采取法律行动。该评论者最后得出结论,认为因未遵守登记和生物识别要求而将外国人儿童导入少年或联邦法院系统,可能会损害儿童的福祉,并给未成年人施加不恰当的法律负担。
Multiple commenters wrote that requiring children between the ages of 14 and 18 years old to register and be fingerprinted runs contrary to longstanding norms in the U.S. legal system. The commenters wrote that juvenile justice experts agree that ( printed page 39284) juvenile justice policies and stakeholders should avoid exposing youth to the criminal juvenile justice system. Thus, the commenters reasoned, expecting that a child of middle- or high-school age would be aware of, understand, and complete the complex process and implications of registration with the Federal Government or face exposure to the U.S. criminal juvenile system for failure to comply would be “unduly burdensome and harsh, as well as inappropriate and unfounded.” A commenter, citing the Juvenile Delinquency Act and 18 U.S.C. 5031 and 5032, remarked that while States determine their own age of majority, under Federal law, individuals under 18 are typically considered too young to bear full criminal responsibility and are generally processed through State juvenile courts, with adult prosecution occurring only in specific circumstances involving violent crimes. The commenter expressed concern that violations of registration and biometric requirements could potentially classify children as “juvenile delinquents” for failing to comply with administrative regulations they may not fully understand. The commenter pointed out that for alien minors aged 14 to 18, compliance with registration requirements assumes these adolescents have complete knowledge of their immigration status and can independently take legal action. The commenter concluded that channeling alien children into juvenile or Federal court systems as a consequence of noncompliance with registration and biometric requirements could potentially compromise children's wellbeing and place inappropriate legal burdens on minors.
一位评论者表示,IFR规定的登记要求将对儿童的安全、健康和福祉及其家庭完整性产生负面影响。该评论者认为,这将违背各州法院所遵循的普遍标准,以及移民法诸多方面所体现的原则,这些标准和原则均要求考虑儿童的最佳利益。具体而言,该评论者写道,美国全部50个州及哥伦比亚特区均要求法院在涉及儿童监护权、安置或其他重大生活问题的决定中考虑儿童的最佳利益(例如儿童的安全与福祉、所表达的意愿、健康状况,以及其享有的家庭完整、自由、发展和身份认同的权利)。该评论者提供了详细的意见和实例,包括移民事务执行办公室(EOIR)要求移民法官“在法庭上有未成年当事人或证人在场时,采用与其年龄相适应的程序”的指南,并指出IFR中并未体现出同样的、对儿童最佳利益的关注与认知。
A commenter stated that the registration requirements under the IFR would negatively impact children's safety, health and well-being, and their family integrity. The commenter suggested that this would defy universal standards found in State courts as well as multiple aspects of immigration law, which consider a child's best interests. Specifically, the commenter wrote that all 50 States and the District of Columbia require courts to consider a child's best interests ( e.g., the child's safety and well-being; expressed interests; health; and their rights to family integrity, liberty, development, and identity) in decisions about the child's custody, placement, or other critical life issues. While providing detailed remarks and examples, including Executive Office for Immigration Review (EOIR) guidance requiring immigration judges to “employ age-appropriate procedures whenever a juvenile respondent or witness is present in the courtroom,” the commenter stated that this same awareness of what is best for children is not reflected in the IFR.
答复:根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条明文规定,8 U.S.C. 1302,国会意图使外国人登记要求(ARR)适用于14岁及以上的外国人。国土安全部(DHS)有责任执行和实施该法律。参见INA第103(a)(1)条,8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1);参见第14159号行政命令第7条,90 FR 8443(2025年1月20日)。正如临时最终规则(IFR)中所解释的那样,该临时最终规则并未在《移民与国籍法》已有义务之外施加任何新的登记或指纹采集义务,包括对年满14岁儿童的义务。应确保14岁以下外国儿童完成登记的,是其父母或法定监护人,而非儿童本人。
Response: Congress, by the express terms of section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, intended that the ARR apply to aliens 14 years of age or older. It is the duty of DHS to administer and enforce that law. See INA sec. 103(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1); seeE.O. 14159 sec. 7, 90 FR 8443 (Jan. 20, 2025). As explained in the IFR, the IFR did not impose any new registration or fingerprinting obligations separate from the obligations already contained in the INA, including for children who turn 14 years of age. Parents and legal guardians of aliens under the age of 14, not the children themselves, must ensure that those alien children are registered.
b. 未成年人获取信息、理解能力及发育方面的考量
b. Information Access, Comprehension, and Developmental Considerations for Minors
意见:有评论者表示担忧,认为该临时最终规则将使儿童承受“侵入性”的报告要求。另一位评论者同样写道,要求14岁及以上非美国公民登记、提供指纹并列明其现居地址的规定属于“越权”行为。该评论者认为,儿童的权利应受法律保护,且许多外国人已经完成登记(例如通过I-94表、就业授权文件(EAD)或非移民或移民签证)。因此,该评论者得出结论认为,该政策不会提供任何额外保护,反而会侵犯儿童权利并制造恐惧。
Comment: Commenters expressed concern that the IFR would subject children to “invasive” reporting requirements. Another commenter similarly wrote that the requirement that non-U.S. citizens aged 14 and older register, provide fingerprints, and list their current address is an “overstep.” The commenter reasoned that children's rights should be protected by the law, and many aliens are already registered ( e.g., via Form I-94, an EAD, or nonimmigrant or immigrant visa). Thus, the commenter concluded, this policy would not provide any additional protection, but, instead, would encroach on the rights of children and produce fear.
众多评论者认为对未成年人的报告要求不恰当或不必要。许多评论者表示,期望14至18岁的儿童理解并履行登记和按捺指纹的要求,或妥善保管或随身携带登记证明,在发育阶段上是不现实的。评论者指出,该临时最终规则对儿童设定了不切实际的高标准,不能期望年仅14岁的儿童理解并履行登记要求或随身携带登记证明,也不能期望他们意识到登记的必要性,以及不遵守规定所带来的后果性影响。另一位评论者普遍指出,无论移民身份如何,儿童都不应被当作成年人对待。
Numerous commenters regarded reporting requirements for minors as inappropriate or unnecessary. Many commenters said it is not developmentally appropriate to expect children ages 14 to 18 to understand and fulfill the requirement to register and be fingerprinted or to safeguard or always carry proof of registration on their person. Commenters stated that the IFR holds children to unrealistically high standard, and that children as young as 14 cannot be expected to understand and fulfill the registration requirement or carry proof of registration—nor can they be expected to be aware of the need to register, in addition to the consequential implications of noncompliance. Another commenter generally remarked that children should not be treated as adults, regardless of their immigration status.
一位评论者援引关于儿童和青少年发育的研究,表示根据已知的发育情况,对儿童施加如此重大的要求是“不公平且不明智的”。其他评论者援引了大量科学研究以及联邦量刑委员会内部的标准,以证明儿童和青少年的大脑尚未完全发育到足以使其具备必要推理能力的程度,从而无法遵守登记要求,或无法有意义地计算某些行为的长期影响。
A commenter cited research on the development of children and adolescents and said it would be “unfair and unwise” to place such a consequential requirement on children, based on what is known about their development. Other commenters cited numerous scientific studies and standards within the Federal Sentencing Commission to demonstrate that the brains of children and adolescents have not developed fully in a way that provides them the necessary reasoning skills to comply with the registration requirements or meaningfully calculate the long-term effects of certain actions.
另一位评论者同样写道,无人陪伴未成年人项目的年龄限制表明政府理解18岁是一个合理的门槛,在此年龄之上才可期望儿童有能力独立处理自己的事务。因此,该评论者得出结论认为,该临时最终规则对14岁儿童的适用,忽视了对青少年期的现代理解,以及青少年理解登记要求的程序及违反该要求所带来后果的能力。另一位评论者同样就受该临时最终规则约束的未成年人提出发育方面的担忧,并进一步指出,对成年人而言,该临时最终规则所带来的选择与后果已经足够难以完全理解;若期望儿童作出此类决定,将是“违背良知的”。
Another commenter likewise wrote that the age limit for the unaccompanied minors program illustrates the government's understanding that 18 years old is a reasonable threshold at which to expect that a child is capable of handling their affairs independently. Thus, the commenter concluded, the IFR's application to 14-year-olds ignores the modern understanding of adolescence and a teenager's ability to understand not only the process but also the repercussions of violating the registration requirement. A commenter, similarly raising developmental concerns for minors subject to the IFR, further reasoned that for adults, the choices and consequences presented by the IFR are difficult enough to fully understand; to expect children to make such a decision would be “unconscionable.”
多位评论者同样表示担忧,登记所需的信息可能是未成年人无法获取或无法理解的。评论者具体指出,14岁或以上的儿童进入美国时可能年纪很小,可能无法轻易获取其移民历史信息,例如其入境日期。另一位评论者解释说,有关移民历史、入境日期及父母信息的问题,儿童可能难以知晓或回忆起来,而关于犯罪记录的问题可能被儿童误解,包括那些曾遭受犯罪分子剥削或贩运的儿童。
Multiple commenters similarly expressed concern that the information required for registration may be unavailable or incomprehensible to minors, and specifically, commenters remarked that children 14 years or older may have been very young when they entered the United States and may not easily be able to access their immigration history, such as their date of arrival. Another commenter explained that questions regarding immigration history, dates of entry, and parental information may be difficult for children to know or recall, and questions about criminal history may be misunderstood by a child, including children who have been exploited or trafficked by criminal actors.
评论者还写道,G-325R表格询问的复杂问题是未成年人不太可能理解的,例如有关在美国过去和未来活动的问题。一位评论者推论称,登记程序高度复杂,“没有任何出错或遗漏的余地”。然而,该评论者表示,由于填写该表格的许多人是未成年人,且可能英语水平有限,此类错误可能对这一群体造成严重后果。(印刷页第39285页)
Commenters additionally wrote that the Form G-325R asks complex questions that minors would be unlikely to comprehend, such as questions about past and future activities in the United States. A commenter reasoned that the registration process is highly complex, leaving “no room for errors or gaps”; however, the commenter said, as many of those completing the form would be minors and may have limited English proficiency, such errors could result in serious consequences for that population. ( printed page 39285)
一位评论者表示,有关移民历史、入境日期和父母信息的问题,儿童可能难以知晓或回忆,而有关犯罪历史的问题可能被儿童误解,包括那些曾遭受犯罪分子剥削或贩运的儿童,这可能会削弱儿童的合法权利。该评论者表示,许多此类问题可能与儿童可能已在政府备案的法律救济申请中所要求的信息重复。
A commenter stated that questions regarding immigration history, dates of entry, and parental information may be difficult for children to know or recall, and questions about criminal history may be misunderstood by a child, including children who have been exploited or trafficked by criminal actors, potentially abridging children's legal rights. The commenter said that many of these questions may be duplicative of information requested on applications for legal relief that a child may already have on file with the government.
一位评论者对该规则对未成年人的影响表示担忧,写道临时最终规则(IFR)并未就G-325R表格上众多强制性问题提供与发展阶段相适应的信息或指导。另一位评论者同样指出,对于14岁以下儿童及其监护人而言,缺乏针对特定年龄的说明或便利措施,增加了无意中不合规的风险。
A commenter, expressing concerns about impacts of the rule on minors, wrote that the IFR provides no provisions for developmentally appropriate information or guidance related to the many mandatory questions on Form G-325R. A commenter similarly noted that for children under 14 and their guardians, there is a lack of age-specific instruction or accommodation, increasing the risk of mistaken noncompliance.
一位评论者写道,要求移民未成年人向联邦政府登记可能导致不必要的财政和健康成本,同时使未成年人面临被针对、逮捕和拘留的风险。
A commenter wrote that requiring immigrant minors to register with the Federal Government could lead to unnecessary fiscal and health costs, while exposing minors to targeting, arrests, and detention.
回应:美国国土安全部(DHS)指出,许多外国人已经完成登记,因此不直接受本临时最终规则或新的一般登记选项的影响。该法规要求,除有限例外情形外,所有在美国停留30天或以上的14岁以上外国人必须申请登记,并在30天期限届满前按要求进行指纹采集。同样,14岁以下外国人的父母和法定监护人必须确保这些外国人完成登记。在外国人年满14周岁后的30天内,所有先前已登记的外国人必须申请重新登记并进行指纹采集。国土安全部通过本临时最终规则及最终规则,并未改变国会为外国人设立的现行法定登记要求。改变该法定登记要求属于国会的职权范围及其政策选择。
Response: DHS notes that many aliens are already registered and, thus, are not directly affected by this IFR or the new general registration option. The statute requires, with limited exceptions, that all aliens over the age 14 who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted, if required, before the expiration of 30 days. Similarly, parents and legal guardians of aliens below the age of 14 must ensure that those aliens are registered. Within 30 days of an alien reaching his or her 14th birthday, all previously registered aliens must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted. DHS, with the IFR and final rule, has not changed the existing statutory registration requirements established by Congress for aliens. It is within Congress' control and its policy choice to change the statutory registration requirement.
关于G-325R表格上问题的复杂性,14岁或以上的外国人已经接受启动驱逐程序通知的送达,并且必须在其本人的移民福利申请上签字。见8 CFR 103.2(a)(2)、103.8(c)(2)(ii)。G-325R表格上的问题与8 CFR 264.1(a)中其他登记表格所询问的问题类似,包括I-485表格,即登记永久居留权或调整身份申请表。关于14岁以下的外国人,父母或法定监护人有义务为14岁以下外国人办理登记,使用父母或法定监护人为该外国儿童创建的USCIS在线账户。
With regard to the complexity of questions on the Form G-325R, aliens who are 14 years or older already receive service of notices to initiate removal proceedings, and must sign their own applications for immigration benefits. See8 CFR 103.2(a)(2), 103.8(c)(2)(ii). The questions on the Form G-325R are similar to the questions asked on other registration forms in 8 CFR 264.1(a), including the Form I-485, Application to Register Permanent Residence or Adjust Status. With regard to aliens under 14 years of age, the parent or legal guardian has the duty to register aliens under 14, using an USCIS online account that the parent or legal guardian creates for the alien child.
此外,18岁以下的外国人并非被强制要求随时携带登记证明。移民与国籍法(INA)第264(e)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(e)规定的法定携带要求仅适用于在美国年满18周岁及以上的外国人,包括但不限于使用这一新程序进行登记的人。该法规并不要求18岁以下儿童携带登记证明。
Additionally, aliens under the age of 18 are not forced to carry proof of registration at all times. The statutory carry requirement under section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e) applies only to aliens 18 years of age and over in the United States, including but not limited to those who register using this new process. The statute does not require children under the age of 18 to carry proof of the registration.
评论:另一位评论者认为,将本临时最终规则适用于未成年人是错误的做法。该评论者质疑对移民儿童进行指纹采集与“保护美国人民免受入侵”之间有何关联,并要求提供在美国移民儿童所犯罪行的统计数据。该评论者进一步质疑,既然儿童即将被安置寄养,为何还需要对其进行指纹采集,并询问对归化儿童是否也采取同样做法。
Comment: A different commenter suggested that the application of the IFR to minors is misguided. The commenter questioned how fingerprinting migrant children falls under “Protecting the American People Against Invasion,” and requested statistics of crimes committed by migrant children in the United States. The commenter further questioned why a child would need fingerprinting as they are being placed into foster care and asked whether the same is done with naturalized children.
答复:根据法律规定,大多数14岁以上并在美国境内停留30天或以上的外国人必须申请登记,并且除基于年龄或非移民身份的若干例外情形外,还须按捺指纹。本规则与《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条,即《美国法典》第8编第1302条以及第14159号行政命令(E.O. 14159)所规定的要求相一致,仅规定了一项适用于所有尚未登记的外国人(无论其移民身份如何)的一般性登记选项。国土安全部(DHS)通过临时最终规则(IFR)及本最终规则,并未创设或修改《移民与国籍法》第262条,即《美国法典》第8编第1302条中规定的外国人向政府登记义务的法定要求。国土安全部有义务忠实执行国会制定的法律,包括外国人登记要求。参见《移民与国籍法》第103(a)条,《美国法典》第8编第1103(a)条。临时最终规则确立了一项适用于所有尚未登记的外国人(无论其移民身份如何)的一般性登记选项。
Response: The statute requires most aliens over the age of 14 who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer are required to apply for registration and with some exceptions based on age or nonimmigrant status, be fingerprinted. This rule is consistent with the requirements outlined in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 and E.O. 14159 and simply prescribe a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not created or modified the statutory requirements of an alien's duty to register with the government under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. DHS has an obligation to faithfully execute the laws established by Congress, including the alien registration requirement. See INA sec. 103(a), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a). The IFR established a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status.
评论:有评论者还表示担忧,如果儿童被迫随时携带登记证明,可能会使其被边缘化并与其公民同龄人分离,一位评论者称这将对儿童发展造成不利影响。
Comment: Commenters also expressed concern that children who are forced to carry proof of registration at all times could be marginalized and separated from their citizen peers, which a commenter said would have detrimental developmental impacts.
答复:国土安全部不认同评论者所描述的这种情形及其影响。未满18岁的外国人并不被要求始终携带登记证明。《移民与国籍法》第264(e)条,《美国法典》第8编第1304(e)条所规定的法定携带要求,仅适用于在美国境内年满18周岁及以上的外国人,包括但不限于通过此新程序进行登记的人员。该法律并不要求未满18岁的儿童携带登记证明。
Response: DHS disagrees with the characterization and the impact described by commenters. Aliens under the age of 18 are not forced to always carry proof of registration. The statutory carry requirement under section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e) applies only to aliens 18 years of age and over in the United States, including but not limited to those who register using this new process. The statute does not require children under the age of 18 to carry proof of the registration.
c. 该规则对未成年人适用的不一致性或清晰度问题
c. Inconsistencies or Clarity of the Rule for Minors
评论:一位评论者询问,持签证入境美国的未满14周岁的儿童,是否需要根据《移民与国籍法》第262条,《美国法典》第8编第1302条重新登记,包括在其年满14周岁时履行新的登记及按捺指纹要求。
Comment: A commenter asked whether children under 14 years of age who enter the United States on a visa are required to re-register under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, including the new requirement to register and be fingerprinted when they turn 14 years old.
答复:根据法律规定,除少数例外情形外,所有此前已登记的外国人须在其年满14周岁生日之日起30天内申请重新登记并按捺指纹。参见《移民与国籍法》第262(b)条,《美国法典》第8编第1302(b)条。
Response: The statute, with limited exceptions, requires that within 30 days of an alien reaching his or her 14th birthday, all previously registered aliens must apply for re-registration and be fingerprinted. See INA sec. 262(b), 8 U.S.C. 1302(b).
评论:一位评论者批评临时最终规则令人困惑,质疑生物识别信息采集要求对未满14周岁未成年人的适用性,并写道,据报道边境管理当局发布了一项政策,规定对所有未成年人采集生物识别信息,而不论临时最终规则中对未满14周岁者的豁免规定。
Comment: A commenter criticized the IFR as confusing, questioned the applicability of the biometrics requirement to minors under 14, and wrote that border authorities reportedly issued a policy providing for the collection of biometrics from all minors, despite the waiver in the IFR for those under 14.
答复:法律要求未满14周岁外国人的父母或法定监护人为其办理登记。这些外国人无需亲自到场参加生物识别服务预约以完成登记,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)将在其父母或法定监护人完成登记要求后,提供登记证明。美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)采集生物识别信息的行为不在本规则适用范围之内。
Response: The statute requires parents and legal guardians of aliens under the age of 14 to register those aliens. These aliens are not required to appear for a biometric services appointment to register, and USCIS will provide evidence of registration after the parent or legal guardian complies with the registration requirement. The collection of biometrics by CBP is outside the scope of the rule.
评论:一位评论者指出,临时最终规则中存在大量含糊不清之处,需要国土安全部作出进一步澄清,包括登记要求在多大程度上具有溯及力,以及该要求是否涵盖在临时最终规则公布或生效前超过30天已入境美国的人员,或是否涵盖在临时最终规则公布或生效前超过30天已年满14周岁、须履行登记要求的儿童。该评论者表示,如果该规则不具有溯及力,那么尚不清楚国土安全部设定的适用起始日期究竟是临时最终规则的公布日期,即2025年3月12日,还是其生效日期,即2025年4月11日。
Comment: A commenter stated the IFR is replete with ambiguities that require more clarification from DHS, including the extent to which the registration requirements are retroactive, and whether they cover people who entered the United States more than 30 days before the IFR was published or takes effect, or whether they cover children subject to registration upon turning 14 who turned 14 more than 30 days before the IFR was published or takes effect. The commenter said, if it is not retroactive, then it is unclear whether DHS is setting the applicability date at the IFR publication date of March 12, 2025, or the IFR effective date of April 11, 2025.
答复:美国国土安全部(DHS)通过临时最终规则和本最终规则,并未为在美国境内停留30天或以上的大多数外国人设立新的要求,也未修改其遵守登记要求的法律义务。本规则制定行动建立了一项通用登记选项,以确保所有外国人均有登记途径。(第39286页印刷版)临时最终规则的生效日期为2025年4月11日,因此已就特朗普总统关于执法优先事项的指示再次提供了充分的执法优先事项通知。参见90 FR 11793(2025年3月12日);另见美国国土安全部新闻稿,“诺姆部长提醒外国公民根据现行联邦法律进行登记,否则将面临法律处罚”(2025年4月11日),https://www.dhs.gov/news/2025/04/11/secretary-noem-reminds-foreign-nationals-register-or-face-legal-penalties。无论如何,自临时最终规则发布以来已经过去了许多个月。
Response: DHS, with the IFR and this final rule, has not created new requirements or modified the legal obligations that most aliens who are in the United States for 30 days or longer to comply with the registration requirements. This rulemaking has ( printed page 39286) established a general registration option to ensure that all aliens have a way to register. The IFR had an effective date of April 11, 2025, and thus provided sufficient notice again of the enforcement priority pursuant to President Trump's directing on the enforcement priority. See90 FR 11793 (Mar. 12, 2025); see also DHS, Press Release, “Secretary Noem Reminds Foreign Nationals to Register under Longstanding Federal Law or Face Legal Penalties” (Apr. 11, 2025), https://www.dhs.gov/​news/​2025/​04/​11/​secretary-noem-reminds-foreign-nationals-register-or-face-legal-penalties. In any event, many months have passed since the IFR's publication.
评论:有评论者指出,根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第221条、《美国法典》第8编第1201条已获发移民签证或非移民签证的14岁以下儿童已经完成登记,不应再次要求登记。
Comment: Commenters stated that children under the age of 14 who were issued an immigrant or a nonimmigrant visa were already registered pursuant to section 221 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1201 and should not have to register again.
答复:美国国土安全部同意,未满14岁且在获准进入美国之前已获发移民签证或非移民签证的外国人,依据《移民与国籍法》第262条、《美国法典》第8编第1302条,视为已完成登记。但是,根据《移民与国籍法》第262(b)条、《美国法典》第8编第1302(b)条的规定,这些外国人在年满14岁后30日内仍须重新登记并按捺指纹。
Response: DHS agrees that an alien who has not turned 14 years old and was issued an immigrant visa or nonimmigrant visa prior to admission to the United States is considered registered under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. However, these aliens will have to re-register and be fingerprinted within 30 days after reaching 14 years of age, in accordance with section 262(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(b).
评论:一位评论者指出,目前尚不清楚所有在14岁以下已获发I-551表格的儿童,只要遵守现行规定,即在年满14岁后30日内提交I-90表格(更换永久居民卡申请表)以申请新的绿卡,是否即可豁免该登记程序。
Comment: A commenter stated that it is unclear whether all children who received Form I-551 while under the age of 14 are exempt from the process as long as they comply with the existing rule that they file a Form I-90, Application to Replace Permanent Resident Card, within 30 days of turning 14 to apply for a new Green Card.
该评论者还询问,若儿童因其现有绿卡在其年满16岁之前即到期,从而未在年满14岁后30日内提交I-90表格,是否需要在年满14岁时填写G-325R表格。这些儿童所适用的是一项监管例外规定,该规定允许其在卡片到期前6个月内提交常规绿卡延期申请之前,无需提交I-90表格(并按捺指纹)。
The commenter also asked whether children who do not file a Form I-90 within 30 days of turning 14 because their current Green Card expires before they turn 16, would need to complete Form G-325R when they turn 14. These children are using the regulatory exception that allows them not to submit a Form I-90 (and be fingerprinted) until they file their routine Green Card extension application within 6 months of the card's expiration.
答复:美国国土安全部指出,根据8 CFR 264.1(g)的规定,年满14岁且为合法永久居民(LPR)的外国人必须申请登记并按捺指纹,除非获得豁免。参见《移民与国籍法》第262(a)条、《美国法典》第8编第1302(a)条;另见8 CFR 264.1(g)。正如I-90表格说明中所述,此类外国人提交I-90表格即是为履行其依据该法规向政府登记的义务。[50]此类外国人无需提交G-325R表格。美国国土安全部还在本序言第五节中征求公众意见,拟修订8 CFR 264.5(b)(8)的法规文本,使其与《移民与国籍法》第262(b)条、《美国法典》第8编第1302(b)条保持一致,该条款要求年满14岁的外国人无论此前是否已登记,均须进行登记。I-90表格的说明在这一点上表述清晰且准确。
Response: DHS notes that in accordance with 8 CFR 264.1(g), an alien who reaches 14 years of age and who is an LPR must apply for registration and to be fingerprinted, unless waived. See INA sec. 262(a), 8 U.S.C. 1302(a); see8 CFR 264.1(g). As noted in the Form I-90 instructions, such an alien submits the Form I-90 to comply with his or her duty to register with the government under the statute.[50] Such an alien would not submit the Form G-325R. DHS is also soliciting comments in section V of this preamble on updating the regulatory text in 8 CFR 264.5(b)(8) to align with section 262(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(b), which requires aliens who turn 14 to register irrespective of whether they were registered previously. The instructions of Form I-90 are clear on this point, and accurate.
评论:少数评论者就临时最终规则中关于未成年人规定的表述不明确之处提出意见。评论者指出,该规则在一处规定,若儿童持有所列表格之一,则“无需再次登记”,而在另一处则规定,所有“非公民”儿童在年满14岁时均须登记,无论此前是否已登记。其中几位评论者引用该规则原文指出,尽管临时最终规则解释称,凡“此前已依据8 CFR 264.1(a)完成登记者,或……持有依据8 CFR 264.1(b)规定的登记证明者,无需再次登记”,但该规则在其他部分却规定,受影响人群包括“非公民”,“无论此前是否已登记,凡在美国境内年满14岁者,均须在其14岁生日后30日内登记”。
Comment: A few commenters addressed ambiguities in the IFR's discussion of the provisions for minors. The commenters noted that the rule states in one section that if children have one of the forms listed, they “need not register again,” while in another section, the rule states that all “noncitizen” children must register when they turn 14, whether previously registered or not. A couple of these commenters quoted the rule, stating that while the IFR explains that someone “who has previously registered consistent with 8 CFR 264.1(a), or. . .who has evidence of registration consistent with 8 CFR 264.1(b), need not register again,” it elsewhere states that affected populations include a “noncitizen” “whether previously registered or not, who turns 14 years old in the United States and therefore must register within 30 days after their 14th birthday.”
答复:该法规要求在美国境内停留30天或以上的14岁以下外国人,须由其父母或法定监护人在该30天期满前为其办理登记。对于这些14岁以下的外国人,登记并无指纹要求。多年来,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)及其前身原移民归化局(INS)一直严格按照移民与国籍法(INA)第262(b)条,即8 U.S.C. 1302(b)的字面规定予以执行(例如,在合法永久居民年满14周岁的情形中)。移民与国籍法第262(b)条第二句的宽泛措辞(“任何外国人在美国境内年满十四周岁时,应……”)要求此类外国人须在其十四岁生日后30天内重新登记并按捺指纹(除非获得豁免)。自1952年《外国人登记要求》(ARR)最初纳入移民与国籍法以来,移民与国籍法第262条,即8 U.S.C. 1302(b)中的这一要求一直存在,未经国会修订。实际上,该要求(使用相同措辞)最初见于1940年《外国人登记法》(公法第76-670号)。
Response: The statute requires aliens under the age of 14 who remain in the United States for 30 days or longer to be registered by their parents or legal guardians before the expiration of the 30 days. There is no fingerprint requirement for the registration of these aliens under the age of 14. USCIS and the former INS have consistently implemented the plain language of section 262(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(b) (for example, in the context of LPRs turning 14 years of age) over the years. The broad language of the second sentence of section 262(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(b) (“whenever any alien attains his fourteenth birthday in the United States he shall”) compels such aliens to register again and to be fingerprinted (unless waived) within 30 days after their 14th birthday. This requirement has been in place in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(b), without amendment by Congress, since the ARR was initially placed in the INA in 1952. Indeed, the requirement (using this language) initially appeared in the Alien Registration Act of 1940, Public Law 76-670.
意见:一位评论者指出,临时最终规则(IFR)并未说明针对可能无法满足登记要求或无法保留登记证明的儿童,将如何适用所列举的刑事处罚。
Comment: A commenter said the IFR does not address how the enumerated criminal penalties would be assessed to children who may not be able to meet the requirements to register or maintain proof of registration.
评论者指出,该规则对以下情形未作说明:未与父母或法定监护人同住的儿童,或与存在虐待或疏忽行为且未代其办理登记的监护人同住的儿童,将面临何种后果。
Commenters noted that the rule is silent on the implications for a child who is not living with a parent or legal guardian, or who lives with one who is abusive or neglectful and does not register on the child's behalf.
答复:美国国土安全部(DHS)指出,外国人登记要求,包括涉及14岁以下儿童、近期在美国境内年满14周岁者,以及14岁及以上者的相关规定,连同相关刑事处罚,自1940年以来一直是我国法律的一部分。本临时最终规则及最终规则只是增加了履行现行法定登记要求的另一种方式。本临时最终规则并未创设或修改与登记要求相关的义务及后果,包括刑事处罚。登记要求系国会数十年前确立,因此属于长期存在的法律要求。尽管国土安全部在刑事条款的执法方面尊重其合作伙伴美国司法部(DOJ)的职权,但国土安全部指出,对于14岁以下儿童,儿童本人并不因未能或拒绝登记而承担刑事处罚,而是其父母和或法定监护人可能面临刑事处罚。对于14岁及以上者,国土安全部指出,移民与国籍法第266(a)条,即8 U.S.C. 1306(a)仅对“故意”未能或拒绝登记的行为予以处罚。
Response: DHS notes that the alien registration requirement, including the provisions relating to children under 14 years of age, those who have recently turned 14 years of age within the United States, or those 14 years of age or older, as well as the related criminal penalties, has been a part of our laws since 1940. The IFR and the final rule add another method for compliance with the existing statutory registration requirements. This IFR did not create or modify the obligations and consequences related to the registration requirement, including the criminal penalties. The registration requirement was established by Congress many decades ago and is thus a longstanding legal requirement. While DHS defers to its partners at the DOJ regarding the enforcement of the criminal provisions, DHS notes that for children under 14 years of age, the children themselves face no criminal penalties for a failure or refusal to register, but rather the parents and or the legal guardians could face criminal penalties. For those 14 years of age or older, DHS notes that only a “willful” failure or refusal to register is penalized in section 266(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1306(a).
意见:一位评论者指出,临时最终规则未考虑执法部门在实施随身携带要求时,将如何区分18岁以下儿童与18岁及以上青少年。该评论者询问,这是否隐含地为须登记的青少年儿童施加了单独的要求,即须随时随身携带其年龄证明,并询问何种年龄证明方可满足要求。
Comment: A commenter said that the IFR does not consider how law enforcement implementing the carry requirement would distinguish between children under 18 and youth 18 and over. The commenter asked whether this implicitly imposes a separate carry requirement for adolescent children subject to registration to always carry proof of their age on their person and asked what kind of proof of age would suffice.
答复:国土安全部指出,临时最终规则及本规则并未改变移民与国籍法第264(e)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(e)项下有关随身携带要求的程序。有关移民与国籍法第264(e)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(e)执法方面的意见超出本次规则制定的范围。然而,一般而言,负责执行随身携带要求的国土安全部各机构均可查阅国土安全部的数据库(见原文第39287页),该数据库不仅载有国土安全部是否已向特定外国人签发登记证明的信息,还载有该外国人的年龄信息。此外,18岁以下的外国人并不被强制要求随时随身携带登记证明。移民与国籍法第264(e)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(e)所规定的法定随身携带要求,仅适用于在美国境内年满18岁及以上的外国人,包括但不限于通过本新程序办理登记的人员。该法规并未要求18岁以下儿童随身携带登记证明。
Response: DHS notes that the IFR and this rule have not changed procedures related to the carry requirements under section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e). The comments regarding the enforcement of section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e) is out of scope of this rulemaking. In general, however, DHS agencies enforcing the carry requirement have access to DHS ( printed page 39287) databases, which contain not only information about whether DHS has issued evidence of registration to a specific alien, but also the alien's age. Additionally, aliens under the age of 18 are not forced to carry proof of registration at all times. The statutory carry requirement under section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e) applies only to aliens 18 years of age and over in the United States, including but not limited to those who register using this new process. The statute does not require children under the age of 18 to carry proof of the registration.
d.未成年人的安全问题
d. Safety Concerns for Minors
评论:在援引报告和轶事证据的同时,一位评论者还表示担忧,登记要求的不确定性可能使未成年人面临潜在的虐待风险。具体而言,该评论者表示,临时最终规则(IFR)下拟议的对外国人的“普遍登记要求”将增加不法分子冒充联邦探员利用弱势移民,尤其是未成年人的途径,同时也会阻碍对登记要求的遵守。
Comment: While citing reports and anecdotal evidence, a commenter additionally expressed concern that uncertainty about registration requirements could expose minors to potential abuse. Specifically, the commenter said that a “universal registration requirement” for aliens as proposed under the IFR would increase the vectors by which bad actors impersonating Federal agents can exploit vulnerable migrants, particularly minors, while simultaneously hindering compliance with registration requirements.
一位评论者表示担忧,询问年仅14岁的“无证”未成年人将为监禁他们提供途径,并补充说拘留设施对心理和身体健康有害,甚至可能致命。
A commenter expressed concern that questioning “undocumented” minors as young as 14 years old would provide an avenue to incarcerate them, adding that detention facilities are detrimental to mental and physical health and even fatal.
一位评论者在讨论家庭安全与稳定风险的同时,对父母和法定监护人的登记要求表达了安全方面的担忧。该评论者对临时最终规则(IFR)下的家庭分离表示担忧,指出通过使未能登记或缺乏登记证明的父母遭受拘留或刑事起诉,该临时最终规则将使家庭分离和对儿童的心理伤害制度化。该评论者补充说,该临时最终规则要求父母自愿提供有关其子女的信息,这可能使其子女面临移民执法行动,包括拘留和驱逐出境,从而影响父母指导子女教育、养育和监督的权力。该评论者总结认为,这些对家庭福祉、自主权和完整性的影响将违反1999年《财政部总拨款法》第654条。
A commenter expressed safety concerns related to the registration requirement for parents and legal guardians while discussing the risks of family safety and stability. The commenter expressed concern with family separation under the IFR, stating that by subjecting parents who fail to register or who lack proof of registration to detention or criminal prosecution, the IFR would institutionalize family separation and psychological harm to children. The commenter added that the IFR requires parents to volunteer information about their children that could expose their children to immigration enforcement, including detention and deportation, impacting the authority of parents to direct the education, nurture, and supervision of their children. The commenter concluded that these impacts on family well-being, autonomy and integrity would violate section 654 of the Treasury General Appropriations Act of 1999.
答复:如临时最终规则所述,国土安全部(DHS)已确定,根据1999年《财政与政府总拨款法》第654条[51],本法规的实施不会对家庭福祉产生负面影响,也不会对作为一种制度的家庭的自主权和完整性产生任何影响。参见90 FR 11793, 11799(2025年3月12日)。登记要求既不影响家庭的稳定性或安全性,特别是在婚姻承诺方面,也不影响家庭的整体福祉。相反,正如特朗普总统的行政命令中所述,执行国家移民法律对美国及在美境内人员的国家安全和公共安全至关重要。参见第14159号行政命令,第1节。
Response: As stated in the IFR, DHS has determined that the implementation of this regulation will not negatively affect family well-being in accordance with section 654 of the Treasury and General Government Appropriations Act of 1999 [51] and will not have any impact on the autonomy and integrity of the family as an institution. See90 FR 11793, 11799 (Mar. 12, 2025). The registration requirement neither impacts the stability or the safety of the family, particularly in terms of the marital commitment, nor the well-being of a family overall. To the contrary, as outlined in President Trump's E.O., enforcing the Nation's immigration laws is critically important to the national security and public safety of the United States and individuals present within the United States. SeeE.O. 14159, sec. 1.
e. 无人陪伴外国儿童
e. Unaccompanied Alien Children
评论:一位评论者就临时最终规则提出了非常详细的意见,指出无人陪伴外国儿童(UAC)是世界上最脆弱的群体之一,每年有成千上万的儿童逃离暴力、迫害、虐待和贩卖。该评论者指出,国会在《贩卖受害者保护再授权法》(TVPRA)中为无人陪伴外国儿童制定了具体的程序保护措施,以防止其被遣返回贩卖和其他伤害之中。该评论者写道,TVPRA为无人陪伴外国儿童提供了保障措施,包括豁免快速遣返程序、被安排进入完整的移民驱逐程序、庇护申请首先由美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)以面谈形式审理的权利,以及豁免庇护申请的一年期限和安全第三国限制。该评论者表示担忧,临时最终规则未能考虑这些现有保护措施以及无人陪伴外国儿童的特殊需求。此外,该评论者指出,临时最终规则并未专门提及无人陪伴外国儿童、其法律保护,或为该群体提供豁免。该评论者还警告说,国土安全部未能考虑临时最终规则对无人陪伴外国儿童产生影响的具体方式,可能因不遵守规定而造成重大伤害。
Comment: A commenter, offering highly detailed remarks on the IFR, stated that UACs are among the world's most vulnerable populations, with thousands fleeing violence, persecution, abuse, and trafficking each year. The commenter noted that Congress created specific procedural protections for UACs in the TVPRA to prevent their return to trafficking and other harm. The commenter wrote that the TVPRA provides safeguards for UACs, including exemption from expedited removal, placement in full immigration removal proceedings, the right to have asylum applications first considered by USCIS in an interview setting, and exemption from the 1-year filing deadline for asylum and from the safe third country bar. The commenter expressed concern that the IFR fails to consider these existing protections and the unique needs of UACs. Furthermore, the commenter observed that the IFR does not specifically mention UACs, their legal protections, or provide an exemption for this population. The commenter additionally warned that DHS's failure to consider the specific ways in which the IFR would impact UACs could cause significant harm as a result of noncompliance.
答复:国土安全部指出,在无人陪伴外国儿童问题背景下提及的各项现有保护措施是由国会通过立法确立的。尽管已颁布了若干为无人陪伴外国儿童提供保护的法律,但国会并未对《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条至第266条,即《美国法典》第8编第1302条至第1306条中有关无人陪伴外国儿童和外国人登记要求的规定作出任何修改。然而,国土安全部指出,关于未能或拒绝登记的刑事条款不适用于14岁以下的外国人,对于14岁及以上的外国人,则要求该未能或拒绝登记的行为须为故意行为。参见《移民与国籍法》第266(a)条,《美国法典》第8编第1306(a)条。此外,《移民与国籍法》第264(e)条,《美国法典》第8编第1304(e)条中规定的随身携带要求,仅适用于已由国土安全部签发登记证明的18岁及以上的外国人。
Response: DHS notes that the various existing protections mentioned in the context of UACs were established by Congress through legislation. While various pieces of legislation providing protections for UACs have been enacted, Congress has not made any changes to sections 262 through 266 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302 through 1306, regarding UACs and the alien registration requirement. However, DHS notes that the criminal provision relating to a failure or refusal to register does not apply to aliens under 14 years of age, and for aliens 14 years of age or older requires that such a failure or refusal to register be willful. See INA sec. 266(a), 8 U.S.C. 1306(a). In addition, the carry requirement of section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e), only applies to aliens 18 years of age or older who have been issued evidence of registration by DHS.
评论:一名评论者表示担忧,该规则未说明在美国没有父母或法定监护人的无人陪伴儿童(UAC)将如何遵守初始登记要求;谁将负责为处于难民安置办公室(ORR)监护下或被释放给非父母/非监护人担保人的儿童办理登记;以及年满18岁的无人陪伴儿童将如何获知其此前是否已登记或如何提供此类登记的证明。另一名评论者也对处于难民安置办公室监护下的人员的合规问题表示担忧。一名评论者表示,对于无人陪伴儿童以及此前处于联邦政府监护下的人员而言,获取登记所需信息可能尤为困难。
Comment: A commenter expressed concern that the rule does not address whether and how UACs who do not have parents or legal guardians in the United States would comply with the initial registration requirement; who would be responsible for registering children in ORR custody or released to a nonparental/non-guardian sponsor; and how UACs turning 18 would learn if they were previously registered or provide evidence of such registration. Another commenter also raised concerns about compliance for those in government custody with ORR. A commenter said that it may be particularly challenging for UACs and those previously in Federal Government custody to obtain the information required for registration.
一名评论者指出,该临时最终规则(IFR)在现有文本下将使庇护所提供者和担保人对处于难民安置办公室监护下的儿童是否需要登记、何时登记以及由谁负责登记产生困惑。该评论者警告称,由于该规则在谁有责任为其登记以及儿童处于政府监护期间应如何合规方面存在含糊之处,这种困惑可能使儿童面临延长拘留以及日后被逮捕的风险。该评论者进一步表示担忧,临时最终规则未为处于政府监护下的儿童提供合规机制。他们解释说,难民安置办公室庇护所内的儿童极少被允许离开,因此在满足登记要求方面将面临结构性障碍,包括无法基本获取相关规则,也无法获得指纹采集和生物识别信息采集服务。最后,该评论者警告称,临时最终规则使处于州寄养机构监护下的寄养儿童面临被逮捕和驱逐出境的风险。
A commenter stated that the IFR, as written, would cause confusion among shelter providers and sponsors about whether and when children in ORR custody need to register, and who is responsible for registration. The commenter warned that this confusion could expose children to prolonged detention and later arrest due to the rule's vagueness as to who has responsibility for registering them and how to comply when a child is in government custody. The commenter further expressed concern that the IFR does not provide children with a mechanism to comply while in government custody. They explained that children in ORR shelters are rarely permitted to leave and would face structural barriers to complying with the registration requirements, including not having basic access to the rules or the ability to obtain fingerprinting and biometrics. Finally, the commenter warned that the IFR leaves foster children who are in the custody of State foster agencies vulnerable to the risk of arrest and deportation.
多名评论者表示担忧,临时最终规则在谁应负责为被释放给非父母、非法定监护人担保人的无人陪伴儿童办理登记方面缺乏明确规定。一名评论者表示担忧,该规则在适用于无人陪伴儿童及其担保人方面缺乏明确性,这可能使其更难以判断是否需要填写G-325R表格以向联邦政府登记。该评论者写道,根据该临时最终规则,14岁以下外国人儿童的父母和法定监护人负有为其子女登记的义务,但该规则未考虑到许多14岁以下的无人陪伴儿童被释放给非其父母或法定监护人的担保人这一事实。该评论者援引美国卫生与公众服务部(HHS)的数据称,从2024年11月30日至2025年2月28日期间,在难民安置办公室监护下的无人陪伴儿童中,有49.7%被释放给了既非父母也非法定监护人的担保人。该评论者从为众多无人陪伴儿童提供法律服务的机构角度发表评论,对谁应负责为此类个人登记以及未在14岁前登记可能面临的处罚缺乏明确性表示深切担忧,并要求国土安全部(DHS)就如何确保幼年移民儿童遵守该临时最终规则提供进一步指导。
Several commenters expressed concern that the IFR lacks clarity regarding who would be required to register an unaccompanied child released to a sponsor who was neither a parent nor a legal guardian. A commenter voiced concern that the rule lacks clarity regarding its application to UACs and their sponsors, which they said may make it less likely that they would be able to determine whether they are required to complete Form G-325R to register with the Federal ( printed page 39288) Government. The commenter wrote, under the IFR, parents and legal guardians of alien children under the age of 14 have a duty to register their child, but the IFR does not account for the fact that many UACs under the age of 14 are released to a sponsor who is not their parent or legal guardian. Citing HHS data, the commenter said that, from November 30, 2024, to February 28, 2025, 49.7 percent of UACs in ORR custody were released to a sponsor who was neither a parent nor a legal guardian. Commenting from the viewpoint of a legal services provider for many UACs, the commenter expressed deep concerns about a lack of clarity around who is required to register such individuals and potential penalties for failing to register before the age of 14 and requested additional guidance from DHS around how to ensure young immigrant children comply with the IFR.
一名评论者指出,该临时最终规则未说明登记要求是否以及如何适用于被安置给非父母、非监护人担保人的14岁以下儿童。该评论者担忧,儿童可能被不一致地登记或登记信息不准确,或被迫与他们原本可能并无联系的父母互动,这可能对那些寻求保护、躲避虐待、遗弃、忽视或类似父母不当对待的儿童造成伤害风险。此外,该评论者表示,目前尚不清楚无人陪伴儿童或担保人是否会在难民安置办公室照管期间或释放时通过法律情况介绍获知登记要求,也不清楚在非父母、非监护人担保人照管下年满14岁的儿童将如何获知相关的自行登记要求。该评论者还表示担忧,儿童的登记状态可能会随着其提交或收到移民表格、年龄增长或其他因素而发生意外变化,从而造成困惑和障碍,特别是对于即将年满18岁、被要求随时携带登记证明否则将面临重大处罚的无人陪伴儿童而言,这种情况尤为突出。
A commenter stated that the IFR does not address if and how the registration requirement would be applied to children under the age of 14 placed with non-parental/non-guardian sponsors. The commenter expressed concern that children could be registered inconsistently or with inaccurate information or be compelled to interact with parents with whom they may not otherwise be in contact, potentially risking harm to children seeking protection from abuse, abandonment, neglect, or similar parental mistreatment. Furthermore, the commenter said it is unclear whether UACs or sponsors would be advised of registration requirements through legal orientations during ORR care or upon release, and how children turning 14 while in the care of a non-parent/non-guardian sponsor would learn of related requirements to register themselves. The commenter also expressed concern that a child's registration status could vary unexpectedly over time based on their submission or receipt of immigration forms, their age, or other factors, creating confusion and barriers, particularly for UACs turning 18, who are expected to carry proof of registration at all times or face significant penalties.
一位评论者表示,该规则将对无人陪伴未成年人(UAC)的潜在担保人产生寒蝉效应,这些人可能担心如果尚未登记就会成为被针对的对象,或者担心一旦同意担保14岁以下的无人陪伴未成年人,他们将承担确保该儿童完成登记的法律义务。
A commenter said the rule would have a chilling effect on potential sponsors of UACs, who may fear that they would be targeted if they have not registered, or that, by agreeing to sponsor an unaccompanied child under the age of 14, they would assume a legal obligation to ensure that child is registered.
另一位评论者写道,关于临时最终规则(IFR)对无人陪伴未成年人适用性缺乏明确性,将加剧移民儿童对执法部门坦诚相告或配合调查的不情愿,这对作为人口贩运或其他犯罪行为受害者或目击者的儿童造成特别的伤害,使儿童和社区更不安全。
Another commenter wrote that a lack of clarity around the applicability of the IFR to UACs would increase reluctance among immigrant children to confide in or cooperate with law enforcement, causing particular harm to children who are victims of or witnesses to trafficking or other crimes, making children and communities less safe.
一位评论者援引多项研究和报告指出,移民儿童,包括该评论者所服务的许多无人陪伴未成年人,曾遭受贫困、人口贩运或暴力造成的创伤,这些创伤影响其认知发展、成熟程度和记忆能力。该评论者表示,这些状况可能使这些儿童更难应对登记程序。一位来自医疗保健行业的评论者指出,为移民儿童提供诊疗的儿科医生报告称,对于如何遵守该临时最终规则存在极大的困惑,因为作为无人陪伴未成年人的患者及其家庭成员或照护者不清楚自己是否受该临时最终规则约束。该评论者表示,其会员缺乏来自国土安全部(DHS)关于如何就此为患者提供最佳建议的明确指引。
A commenter, citing multiple studies and reports, stated that immigrant children, including many UACs the commenter serves, have suffered trauma—from poverty, trafficking, or violence—that affects cognitive development, maturity, and memory. The commenter said that these conditions could make responding to the registration process more difficult for these children. A commenter in the healthcare sector noted that pediatricians who provide care to immigrant children are reporting great confusion over how to comply with the IFR, as patients who are UACs and their family members or caregivers do not understand whether they are subject to the IFR. The commenter said its members lack clarity from DHS on how best to advise these patients.
多位评论者表示担忧,如果无人陪伴未成年人的担保人或家人因未能登记或未能随身携带登记证明而被刑事起诉,这些儿童可能面临长期的家庭分离和严重的再创伤。同样,一位评论者对该临时最终规则可能对愿意担任担保人的人产生的寒蝉效应表示担忧,因为与儿童同住的成年人将因遵守登记要求而承担刑事责任。该评论者警告称,这将导致儿童在难民安置办公室(ORR)监护下滞留更长时间,而在该机构中教育课程有限,且某些发展需求,例如拥抱,是被禁止的。另一位评论者补充表示,针对未遵守登记和指纹采集要求者的优先执法行动,可能影响无人陪伴未成年人的潜在担保人,包括儿童的父母和其他家庭成员,从而妨碍他们在儿童离开难民安置办公室监护后照顾儿童的能力。该评论者表示,因此,儿童可能面临在难民安置办公室监护下滞留更长时间,或被释放给更疏远、可能不太适合的担保人的局面。
Commenters expressed concern that UACs may face prolonged family separation and profound re-traumatization if their sponsors or family are criminally prosecuted for failure to register or carry proof of registration. Similarly, a commenter voiced concern about chilling effects on those willing to be sponsors as a result of the IFR, as adults living with children would be held criminally responsible for complying with the registration requirement. This, the commenter cautioned, would lead to children languishing for longer periods in ORR custody, where educational curricula are limited, and certain developmental needs—such as hugs—are prohibited. A commenter additionally commented that prioritized enforcement against those who fail to comply with registration and fingerprinting requirements could impact potential sponsors of UACs, including children's parents and other family members, impeding their ability to care for children once they leave ORR custody. Consequently, the commenter said, children could face prolonged time in ORR care or face release to more distant and potentially less suitable sponsors.
一位评论者表示,该部门未就因不遵守规定而被逮捕的未成年人将会面临何种处置提供任何明确说明,包括他们是否可能被送回联邦监护,例如难民安置办公室的监护。该评论者及其他人援引多项报告,进一步对处于难民安置办公室监护下的儿童表示担忧,原因在于该规则的复杂性和语言要求,评论者警告称这可能导致对14岁及以上儿童进行不必要的登记,并对儿童及其家庭采取执法行动。评论者表示,这一点尤其令人担忧,因为难民安置办公室已开始削减为无人陪伴未成年人提供的法律服务,并已发布其自身的临时最终规则,要求难民安置办公室员工向移民及海关执法局(ICE)共享担保人信息。
A commenter said the Department offers no clarity about what would happen to minors who are arrested for noncompliance, including whether they may be returned to Federal custody, such as under ORR. The commenter and others, citing multiple reports, expressed additional concern for children in ORR custody due to the rule's complexity and language requirements, which the commenters warned could lead to unnecessary registrations of children 14 and older and enforcement actions against children and their families. The commenters said this is particularly concerning, as ORR has begun dismantling legal services for UACs and has issued its own IFR for ORR employees to share information about sponsors to ICE.
一位评论者在讨论详细关切时写道,临时最终规则(IFR)对于国土安全部(DHS)是否打算将登记要求适用于处于政府监管下的无人陪伴未成年人(UAC)这一问题保持沉默。该评论者表示,同样不明确的是,难民安置署(ORR)是否必须为其监护下的儿童填写登记表格,ORR将如何做出此类决定,将使用何种实际地址,如何避免登记重复,以及其他与无人陪伴未成年人相关的考虑因素。该评论者指出,在ORR监护下的平均滞留时间超过30天,部分儿童可能在长期寄养安置中滞留数月甚至更久,这意味着许多无人陪伴未成年人在被要求遵守登记规定时可能仍处于政府监护之下。该评论者援引《ORR政策指南》解释称,ORR目前采取某些措施以确保儿童在被监护期间按时出席已安排的移民听证会,并协助其获得法律情况介绍和筛查;然而,该评论者指出,除非儿童或其法律服务提供者提出请求,否则ORR通常不得就儿童的移民案件向其他联邦机构作出陈述或分享信息。该评论者表示,这些保障措施体现了对未经授权的信息共享可能对儿童权利、人道主义保护申请的公正裁决以及整体安全造成的有害和不利影响的认识。
A commenter, discussing detailed concerns, wrote that the IFR is silent on whether DHS intends to apply the registration requirement to UACs in government custody. The commenter said it is also unclear whether ORR must complete registration forms for children in its care, how ORR would make such determinations, what physical address would be used, how to avoid duplication of registration, and other considerations for UACs. The commenter noted that average lengths of stay in ORR custody exceed 30 days, and some children may remain in long-term foster care placements for months or longer, meaning many UACs may still be in government care when they are required to comply with registration provisions. Citing the ORR Policy Guide, the commenter explained that ORR currently undertakes certain actions to ensure a child's attendance at immigration hearings scheduled while the child is in custody and facilitates access to legal orientations and screenings; however, the commenter noted that ORR generally must not make representations or share information about a child's immigration case with other Federal agencies unless requested by the child or their legal service provider. The commenter said that these safeguards recognize the harmful and prejudicial effect that unauthorized information sharing could have on a child's rights, fair adjudication of applications for humanitarian protection, and overall safety.
该评论者对G-325R表格包含众多可能影响儿童移民案件的问题表示关切,这些问题也可能包含在儿童正在填写或已提交的其他法律申请中。该评论者指出,由ORR代表儿童填写该表格将是不恰当且有损其权益的,尤其是在儿童无法咨询法律顾问的情况下。
The commenter expressed concern that Form G-325R includes numerous questions with potential bearing on a child's immigration case that may also be contained within other legal applications the child is completing or has submitted. The commenter stated it would be inappropriate and prejudicial for ORR to complete this form on a child's behalf, particularly without the child's ability to consult with legal counsel.
一位评论者对基于不遵守IFR中所述要求而可能导致无人陪伴未成年人被监禁表示关切,称这引发了本应由(国土安全)部予以考虑但在IFR中未予处理的严重问题。该评论者表示,因此无人陪伴未成年人有可能(印刷页第39289页)重新被拘留于ORR监护之下,或被关押于移民及海关执法局(ICE)或其他执法机构的设施中。该评论者质疑,此类逮捕行为将如何符合《防止人口贩运保护法》(TVPRA)、《弗洛雷斯和解协议》(Flores Settlement)以及有关儿童和青少年待遇条件与程序保护的少年司法改革要求。
A commenter expressed concern about the potential for imprisonment of UACs based on noncompliance with requirements discussed in the IFR, stating that this raises serious issues that the Department should have considered but does not address in the IFR. The commenter stated it would therefore be ( printed page 39289) possible for UACs to face re-detention in ORR custody or in ICE or other law enforcement facilities. The commenter questioned how any such arrests would comport with the TVPRA, Flores Settlement, and juvenile justice reforms regarding conditions and procedural protections for children and youth.
回应:临时最终规则(IFR)和本最终规则的适用范围有限,为所有身份不明的外国人建立了一个通用的登记选项,不论其移民身份如何。国会于1940年建立外国人登记要求时,并未针对须登记的14岁以下外国人儿童在无父母或法定监护人陪同下身处美国境内的情形作出规定。然而,该成文法及登记程序并不要求父母或法定监护人本人身处美国境内,方可依照《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262(b)条、《美国法典》第8编第1302(b)条的规定为14岁以下的外国人儿童办理登记。对于由美国卫生与公众服务部(HHS)监管,或已被释放给非父母或非法定监护人赞助人的14岁以下无人陪伴儿童(UAC),情况同样如此;该成文法仍将登记无人陪伴儿童的义务置于父母或法定监护人身上。该成文法并未将登记无人陪伴儿童的义务置于美国难民安置办公室(ORR)或任何非父母或非法定监护人赞助人身上。此外,一般而言,无人陪伴儿童在被移交至美国难民安置办公室看管、监护及安置之前,会由美国国土安全部(DHS)送达I-862表格《出庭通知书》,并被置于驱逐出境程序中。参见《美国法典》第8编第1232(a)(5)(D)条及《联邦法规汇编》第8编第236.3(f)(1)条。一旦国土安全部签发I-862表格,无人陪伴儿童即被视为已完成登记。参见《联邦法规汇编》第8编第264.1(b)条。关于已满14岁的无人陪伴儿童,国土安全部指出,他们已经亲自收到启动驱逐出境程序的I-862表格《出庭通知书》的送达,此项送达依照《联邦法规汇编》第8编第264.1(b)条构成登记的证据,且他们必须亲自签署本人的移民福利申请。参见《联邦法规汇编》第8编第103.8(c)(2)(ii)条;参见第8编第103.2(a)(2)条。国土安全部根据特朗普总统在第14159号行政命令中的指示,已就《移民与国籍法》第262条、《美国法典》第8编第1302条所述外国人登记及(如要求)按捺指纹的义务进行了公开宣传。除通过制定规则的行动宣传外国人登记要求的相关信息外,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)还设立了专门网站,提供有关外国人登记要求(ARR)的信息,并设立了ARR工具,以帮助外国人判断其是否须办理登记。
Response: The IFR and this final rule are limited in scope and establish a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. When Congress established the alien registration requirement in 1940, it did not address scenarios in which an alien child under 14 years of age required to register would be present in the United States without a parent or legal guardian. However, the statute and the registration processes does not require a parent or legal guardian to themselves be present in the United States in order to register an alien child under 14 years of age as required under section 262(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(b). The same is true for UACs under 14 years of age in HHS custody or who have been released to a nonparental or non-legal guardian sponsor; the statute still places duty to register the UAC on the parent or legal guardian. The statute does not place a duty to register a UAC on ORR or on any nonparental or non-legal guardian sponsor. Also, in general, UACs are served Form I-862, Notice to Appear, and placed in removal proceedings by DHS before the UAC is transferred to ORR care, custody, and placement. See8 U.S.C. 1232(a)(5)(D) and 8 CFR 236.3(f)(1). UACs are considered to be registered once the Form I-862 is issued by DHS. See8 CFR 264.1(b). With regard to UACs who are 14 years of age or older, DHS notes that they already receive personal service of notices to initiate removal proceedings on Form I-862, Notice to Appear, which constitutes as evidence of registration in 8 CFR 264.1(b), and must sign their own applications for immigration benefits. See8 CFR 103.8(c)(2)(ii); see8 CFR 103.2(a)(2). DHS, as directed by President Trump in E.O. 14159, has publicized information about the duty of aliens described in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, to register and be fingerprinted (if required). In addition to the rulemaking actions to publicize information on the alien registration requirement, USCIS established a dedicated website with information on the ARR and an ARR Tool that may help aliens determine whether they must register.
此外,如前所述,有关未登记或拒绝登记的刑事处罚条款不适用于14岁以下的外国人。尽管国土安全部在刑事条款的执行方面听从其合作伙伴美国司法部(DOJ)的意见,但国土安全部指出,对于已满14岁的外国人,依照《移民与国籍法》第266(a)条、《美国法典》第8编第1306(a)条的规定,只有“故意”未登记或拒绝登记或按捺指纹的行为才会受到处罚。
Also, as mentioned previously, the criminal provision relating to a failure or refusal to register does not apply to aliens under 14 years of age. While DHS defers to its partners at DOJ regarding the enforcement of the criminal provisions, DHS notes that for aliens 14 years of age or older only a “willful” failure or refusal to register or be fingerprinted is penalized in section 266(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1306(a).
如果任何外国人不确定自己是否已登记,该外国人可使用美国公民及移民服务局设立的ARR工具,以确定该外国人是否必须登记。[52]此外,《移民与国籍法》第264(e)条、《美国法典》第8编第1304(e)条所规定的随身携带要求,仅适用于已由国土安全部签发登记证明文件、且年满18周岁的外国人。
If any alien is unsure whether they are registered, an alien may use the ARR Tool established by USCIS, to determine if an alien must register.[52] In addition, the carry requirement of section 264(e) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(e), only applies to aliens 18 years of age or older who have been issued evidence of registration by DHS.
评论意见:在无人陪伴儿童被安置给赞助人之后,应向该儿童送达出庭通知书(I-862表格),《联邦法规汇编》第8编第264.1(b)条将此列为满足登记要求的方式;然而,多位评论者就无人陪伴儿童通过出庭通知书满足登记要求一事,提出了令人困惑之处及实际操作方面的担忧。评论者表示,尽管该规则对已被送达出庭通知书者予以豁免,但这对许多无人陪伴儿童及其家庭而言会造成困惑。他们写道,无人陪伴儿童因其年龄、脆弱性及缺乏法律行为能力,可能并不知晓自己已收到的具体文件,同时他们也可能根本未曾收到出庭通知书。部分评论者援引一份报告指出,直到最近,美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)一直奉行一项政策,即将无人陪伴儿童出庭通知书向移民法院的提交推迟至其被安置给赞助人之后。此外,评论者分析认为,无人陪伴青少年收到出庭通知书的情况,也因儿童与家庭的地址变更,以及政府方面的信息错误和不当送达而受到阻碍。
Comment: Following the placement of an unaccompanied child with a sponsor, the child is to be provided with an NTA (Form I-862), which 8 CFR 264.1(b) lists as satisfying the registration requirements; however, multiple commenters discussed confusion and practical concerns about UACs fulfilling the registration requirements with an NTA. Commenters said that, while the rule exempts those who have been served an NTA, this would be confusing for many UACs and their families. They wrote that UACs not only may be unaware of the specific documentation they have received by nature of their age, vulnerability, and lack of legal competency; but they also may not have received an NTA. While citing a report, some commenters wrote that, until recently, ICE maintained a policy of deferring filings with immigration courts of UACs' NTAs until their placement with sponsors. Furthermore, the commenter reasoned, receipt of NTAs for unaccompanied youth has also been hampered by address changes amongst children and families, and by information errors and improper service by the government.
有评论者指出,律师们报告了许多案例,表明国土安全部(DHS)在将已向无人陪伴儿童(UAC)签发的出庭通知书(NTA)提交给美国司法部(DOJ)下属的行政复议办公室(EOIR)时,存在未提交或延迟提交的情况。该评论者表示,在表格未及时提交给行政复议办公室的情况下,不应要求无人陪伴儿童提交G-325R表格;相反,一旦他们已被签发出庭通知书,就应被视为已完成登记。该评论者对临时最终规则(IFR)未考虑到这种情形表示担忧,并要求澄清在未将I-862表格提交给行政复议办公室的情况下签发该表格是否满足登记要求。
A commenter remarked that lawyers have reported many cases where DHS either fails to file or delays in filing NTAs that have been issued to UACs with the DOJ's EOIR. The commenter said that, in cases where the form is not filed with EOIR in a timely manner, an unaccompanied child should not be expected to file Form G-325R; instead, once they have been issued an NTA, they should be considered registered. The commenter expressed concern that the IFR does not contemplate this scenario and requested clarity as to whether the issuance of Form I-862 without the filing of said form with EOIR would satisfy the registration requirements.
答复:临时最终规则及本规则的适用范围有限,仅为所有未登记的外国人(无论其移民身份如何)设立一个通用的登记选项。相关法规规定I-862表格构成8 CFR 264.1(b)项下的登记证明,持有该登记证明的外国人被视为已履行《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262(a)条,即8 U.S.C. 1302(a)条规定的向政府登记的义务。如果无人陪伴儿童或其担保人不确定该儿童是否已登记,该儿童或其担保人可使用美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)设立的“年龄登记要求”(ARR)工具,以确定某外国人是否必须登记。[53]
Response: The IFR and this rule are limited in scope and establish a general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status. The regulations identify a Form I-862 as constituting evidence of registration under 8 CFR 264.1(b), and an alien with this evidence of registration is considered to have complied with their duty to register with the government under section 262(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(a). If a UAC or his or her sponsor is unsure whether the UAC is registered, the UAC or his or her sponsor may use the ARR Tool established by USCIS, to determine if an alien must register.[53]
评论:有评论者对要求承认少年裁决(包括已被封存的裁决)表示担忧,该评论者指出,这可能产生儿童无法理解的移民后果。
Comment: A commenter expressed concern with the requirement to admit juvenile adjudications, even those that were sealed, which the commenter said could have immigration consequences children would not understand.
答复:根据《移民与国籍法》第262(a)条,即8 U.S.C. 1302(a)条的规定,用于登记外国人的表格“应包含关于该外国人警方记录和犯罪记录(如有)的询问事项”。参见《移民与国籍法》第264(a)条,即8 U.S.C. 1304(a)条。许多用于申请移民福利的表格都要求提供申请人的犯罪历史信息,即使犯罪行为发生在申请人未成年时亦然。例如参见I-485表格《调整身份或登记永久居留申请书》第9部分第23题;I-589表格《庇护及暂缓递解申请书》B部分第2题。
Response: Under section 262(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(a), forms designated to register aliens “shall contain queries with respect to . . . the police and criminal record, if any, of such alien.” See INA sec. 264(a), 8 U.S.C. 1304(a). Many forms used to seek immigration benefits request information about the applicant's criminal history, even if the criminal activity occurred when the applicant is a minor. See, e.g., Form I-485, Application to Register Permanent Residence or Adjust Status, Part 9, Question 23; Form I-589, Application for Asylum and for Withholding of Removal, Part B, Question 2.
评论:有评论者对无人陪伴儿童(包括在难民安置办公室(ORR)监管下的儿童)所面临的困惑和合规挑战表示担忧,并建议至少应对目前或曾经处于联邦移民监管下的儿童设立明确的豁免条款。该评论者认为,这些儿童已经由政府机构处理,理应已被签发出庭通知书,尽管由于政府的做法和政策,该通知书可能并未在其本人手中;而且这些儿童必然已提供过个人生物信息和生物特征信息,因此无需再次登记。另一位评论者敦促国土安全部撤销该临时最终规则,并至少将无人陪伴儿童排除在相关条款适用范围之外,以防止该群体再次遭受人口贩运及其他伤害。该评论者建议,国土安全部不应增加无人陪伴儿童(原文第39290页)面对已知伤害的障碍,而是可以完全豁免无人陪伴儿童,或将儿童提交的其他法律申请视为登记证明。
Comment: A commenter expressed his concerns about the confusion and compliance challenges for UACs, including those in ORR custody, and suggested that there should, at the least, be a clear carveout for children who are currently or have previously been in Federal immigration custody. The commenter reasoned that these children have already been processed by government agencies and should have been issued an NTA, although it may not be in their possession due to government practices and policies, and that these children have necessarily provided biographic and biometric information, rendering further registration unnecessary. Another commenter urged DHS to rescind the IFR, and, at minimum, exempt UACs from the provisions, to prevent the return of this population to trafficking and other harm. The commenter suggested, rather than increasing barriers for UACs facing known harm, DHS could have exempted UACs ( printed page 39290) altogether or considered children's other legal applications as evidence of registration.
答复:该法律为外国人登记要求规定了有限的豁免情形。[54] 临时最终规则及本最终规则均未创设或修改国会就14岁以下外国人、在美国境内刚满14岁的外国人,以及14岁或以上但在移民法意义上仍属“儿童”的外国人所设立的法定要求。
Response: The statute provides limited exemptions [54] to the alien registration requirements. The IFR and this final rule have not created or modified the statutory requirements established by Congress that apply to aliens under 14 years of age, those who have recently turned 14 years of age within the United States, and those 14 years of age or older who are still a “child” for immigration purposes.
国土安全部同意,已被签发I-862表格出庭通知书的外国人拥有符合8 CFR 264.1(b)规定的登记证明,因此无需完成临时最终规则及本最终规则所规定的通用登记选项。一般而言,外国人在以下情形下已属于已登记:
DHS agrees that aliens issued an NTA on a Form I-862 have evidence of registration in accordance with 8 CFR 264.1(b) and would not complete the general registration option as provided in the IFR and this final rule. An alien is, in general, already registered if the alien:
曾获签发移民签证或非移民签证,并在其最近一次入境时凭该签证获准进入美国的;
Was issued an immigrant or nonimmigrant visa and at his or her most recent arrival was admitted into the United States using that visa;
已被签发8 CFR 264.1(b)项下指定为登记证明之一的文件的;或
Has been issued one of the documents designated as evidence of registration under8 CFR 264.1(b); or
已提交8 CFR 264.1(a)项下指定的表格之一并已提供指纹(除非获得豁免),但尚未被签发8 CFR 264.1(b)项下指定的登记证明表格之一的。
Submitted one of the forms designated at8 CFR 264.1(a) and provided fingerprints (unless waived) but was not issued one of the forms of evidence of registration designated at 8 CFR 264.1(b).
f. 特殊移民青少年
f. Special Immigrant Juveniles
评论:一位评论者表示担忧,申请I-360表格以寻求特殊移民青少年(SIJ)身份的儿童将难以评估自己是否已满足登记要求。该评论者表示,在提交基于SIJ身份的I-360表格后,申请人通常无需参加生物识别信息采集预约;然而,那些在获得基于SIJ身份的暂缓遣返裁定后提交I-765表格的申请人,则需要参加生物识别信息采集预约,尽管并非总是需要采集指纹。该评论者表示,许多此类儿童将不确定自己是否已登记,并且不太可能自行完成G-325R表格。他们敦促联邦政府考虑申请SIJ身份儿童的年龄和特殊脆弱性,并像对所有14岁以下儿童豁免指纹采集要求一样,豁免提交I-360表格儿童的指纹采集要求。
Comment: A commenter expressed concern that children who file Form I-360, seeking SIJ status, would experience difficulties assessing whether they have met the registration requirements. The commenter said, after filing a SIJ-based Form I-360, applicants generally are not required to attend biometrics appointments; however, those who file Form I-765, after they receive a SIJ-based deferred action determination are required to attend a biometrics appointment, although they are not always fingerprinted. The commenter said that many of these children would be uncertain as to whether they are registered and would be unlikely to complete Form G-325R on their own. They urged the Federal Government to take the age and particular vulnerabilities of children who file for SIJ status into account and waive the requirement for children who file Form I-360 to be fingerprinted, just as it waives the requirement for all children under the age of 14.
回应:按照特朗普总统在第14159号行政命令中的指示,美国国土安全部(DHS)已公布有关《移民与国籍法》(INA)第262条、《美国法典》第8编第1302条所述外国人登记及(如要求)采集指纹义务的信息。除通过规则制定行动公布外国人登记要求相关信息外,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)还建立了专门网站,提供有关外国人登记要求(ARR)的信息,以及一个外国人登记要求工具,可帮助外国人确定自己是否必须登记。如果任何外国人不确定自己是否已登记,可使用USCIS建立的外国人登记要求工具来确定该外国人是否必须登记。[55]
Response: DHS, as directed by President Trump in E.O. 14159, has publicized information about the duty of aliens described in section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, to register and be fingerprinted (if required). In addition to the rulemaking actions to publicize information on the alien registration requirement, USCIS established a dedicated website with information on the ARR and an ARR Tool that may help aliens determine whether they must register. If any alien is unsure whether they are registered, an alien may use the ARR Tool established by USCIS, to determine if an alien must register.[55]
根据8 CFR 264.1(b)条规定被签发指定作为登记证明文件之一的外国人,包括被签发I-766表格《就业许可文件》的外国人,已完成登记。但是,已获签发登记证明文件、且在美国境内年满14周岁的外国人,必须在其14周岁生日后30日内申请重新登记,并在要求时接受指纹采集。此类外国人可提交G-325R表格以重新登记并接受指纹采集。
An alien issued one of the documents designated as evidence of registration under 8 CFR 264.1(b), including a Form I-766, Employment Authorization Document, has already registered. However, an alien who was issued evidence of registration and turns 14 years old in the United States must apply for reregistration within 30 days after the 14th birthday and be fingerprinted, if required. These aliens may submit a Form G-325R to reregister and be fingerprinted.
这些规则制定并未创设或修改国会所设立的、适用于以下外国人的法定要求:14岁以下的外国人、近期在美国境内年满14周岁的外国人,以及14岁或以上但在移民法意义上仍属“儿童”的外国人。
These rulemakings have not created or modified the statutory requirements established by Congress that apply to aliens under 14 years of age, those who have recently turned 14 years of age within the United States, and those 14 years of age or older who are still a “child” for immigration purposes.
The statute requires aliens 14 years or older to be fingerprinted. See INA sec. 262, 8 U.S.C. 1302. Children under the age of 14 do not have to be fingerprinted for purposes of the registration statute. See INA sec. 262(b), 8 U.S.C. 1302(b). Pursuant to section 262(c) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302(c), the Secretary may, in the Secretary's discretion and on the basis of reciprocity pursuant to regulations as the Secretary may prescribe, waive the requirement for fingerprinting in the case of any nonimmigrant. See INA sec. 262(c), 8 U.S.C. 1302(c). That authority was exercised through long-standing regulations at 8 CFR 264.1(e). The purpose of the IFR was to amend DHS regulations at 8 CFR 264.1(a) and (b) to designate a new registration form and an additional documentation that may serve as evidence of alien registration. The IFR did not alter the existing fingerprinting waiver scheme as outlined in 8 CFR 264.1(e), which is beyond the scope of the IFR and this final rule.
g. Child Survivors
Comment: A commenter expressed particular concern that the registration process could be manipulated by abusers seeking to control, coerce, or intimidate immigrant child victims. The commenter said that although there is an exception to criminal liability for lack of willfulness in failing to register, this exception requires a sophisticated understanding of criminal law, which children between 14 and 18 should not be expected to have, especially if they do not have access to counsel.
Response: DHS agrees that the criminal provision relating to a failure or refusal to register does not apply to aliens under 14 years of age and that, for aliens 14 years of age or older, it requires that such a failure or refusal to register be “willful.” INA sec. 266(a), 8 U.S.C. 1306(a), but DHS defers to DOJ regarding enforcement of the criminal provisions.
3. Applicability to Canadian Citizens
a. Burdens and Redundant Requirements for Canadians
Comment: Many commenters expressed concerns about the application to Canadian visitors, with commenters warning that the requirements would deter Canadians from visiting or returning to the United States.
A commenter specifically expressed concern about applicability of the IFR to nonimmigrant Canadian tourists and business travelers, who were not issued an electronic Form I-94 upon processing at a land port of entry, to submit Form G-325R if they plan to be physically present in the United States for 30 days or longer.
A commenter expressed concern that the IFR creates an unnecessary burden on Canadian tourists to the United States. The commenter reasoned that Canadian citizens entering the United States by land typically do not receive an electronic Form I-94, unlike those arriving by air or sea. As a result, the commenter said that hundreds of thousands of Canadian travelers who enter the United States for extended stays of 30 days or longer through land ports of entry would be required to register with USCIS. The commenter said this requirement is redundant given the extensive information sharing between Canada and the United States, including the coordinated Entry/Exit information system established under the Beyond the Border security agreement in 2011, which permits the ( printed page 39291) sharing of biographic entry data on all travelers who cross the land border. The commenter also remarked that the United States has access to Canadian criminal record and public safety information through the Canadian Police Information Centre system.
Commenters expressed concerns with the applicability of the IFR to Canadian visitors who entered the United States at land ports of entry and were not issued evidence of registration, reasoning that it would be overly burdensome for them to fulfill the requirement to create an individual electronic account, complete a form asking for more information than they would have otherwise had to provide for admission into the United States, and then appear for a biometrics appointment at an ASC. Furthermore, the commenters wrote that the Department could better meet the IFR's compliance objectives and increase efficiency by capturing all of the information at one's time of entry, rather than waiting 30 days for such individuals to self-report. A commenter specifically expressed concern that the fingerprinting proposal would lead Canadians to exit en masse and suggested that “a form and a passport” is sufficient.
Response: DHS agrees that aliens who are Canadian citizens and admitted at land ports of entry as nonimmigrant visitors for business or pleasure are generally not issued a Form I-94. Under 8 CFR 212.1, a visa is generally not required for these aliens and under 8 CFR 235.1(h), the Form I-94 is not required. An alien who was admitted without the issuance of Form I-94 and without issuance of a nonimmigrant visa under section 221 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1201, who remains for 30 days or more in the United States must register in order to comply with section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. Such an alien may use the general registration option available to all unregistered aliens regardless of immigration status or one of the other paths to registration available under the regulation.
Regarding the commenters' suggestion that it would be reasonable for Canadian visitors to register in connection with their admission to the United States as nonimmigrants, rather than after their admission, DHS agrees. While most Canadian citizens seeking to be admitted at land ports of entry as nonimmigrant visitors for business or pleasure, or for direct transit through the United States, are not subject to the Form I-94 requirement under 8 CFR 235.1(h)(1)(i), they may choose to request a Form I-94 from CBP in advance of their travel at https://i94.cbp.dhs.gov/​home. Aliens issued Form I-94 upon admission as nonimmigrants are considered registered under 8 CFR 264.1, and a Canadian citizen who opts for this approach has fulfilled his or her duty to register under section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302, and generally, does not have to register again during his or her visit to the United States. DHS agrees with these and other commenters who have suggested that DHS should consider whether there are other processes that include robust information collection and thorough screening and vetting, including the provision of biometrics, that may be suitable ways for aliens to comply with the registration requirement of section 262 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1302. DHS has updated the list of prescribed registration forms as well as evidence of registration as a part of this final rule. See section IV.A.2 of this preamble. DHS is also seeking comments on the possible addition of other forms. See section V. of this preamble.
A nonimmigrant who is a Canadian citizen and who files the G-325R may be eligible for a waiver of the registration fingerprinting requirement under 8 CFR 264.1(e)(2) or, in more limited circumstances, 8 CFR 264.1(e)(1). Once an alien submits the Form G-325R, USCIS reviews the information and any DHS records to determine if an alien is already registered or if fingerprints are required. If a Canadian nonimmigrant who submits Form G-325R is not already registered and is not required to be fingerprinted, USCIS will provide the alien with evidence of registration through his or her USCIS online account without the scheduling of a biometrics appointment. Under section 264(a) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1304(a), forms for registration of aliens should collect information related to an alien, including activities in which the alien has been and intends to be engaged. Further, as mentioned in the IFR and this rule, the information an alien provides in the registration process and the biometric collection, if required, provides DHS with additional information about an alien's criminal record, including whether the alien is a known or suspected terrorist. DHS prioritizes the removal of aliens who pose threats to public safety and national security and this information helps DHS address such threats.
b. Requests for Exemptions
Comment: A commenter stated that Canadians should be exempt from the rule, reasoning that requiring registration for Canadians who plan to stay in the United States longer than 30 days adds an unnecessary burden for both Canadian visitors and DHS. The commenter further reasoned that Canadians pose very little to no risk to national security and have close relations with the United States. Another commenter wrote that Canada and the United States “have always had open borders” and warned that this would put a strain on relationships across the borders, particularly for Canadians needing access to U.S. medical facilities. The commenter further expressed concern that the rule would stop travel on the Alaska Highway or entry into Canada.
Other commenters discussed the historical and practical reasons for insulating Canadian visitors from registration. Citing research, the commenters stated that, by 1952, Canadians visiting the United States for less than 6 months were exempt from registration requirements, even though there was no clear authorization in the statute for these exemptions. Additionally, the commenters said that, for Canadian visitors present in the United States for 30 days or more, CBP has long waived the need to obtain an admission stamp in their passport or a Form I-94 admission record. Furthermore, the commenters reasoned that CBP admits many Canadian nonimmigrants to the United States at the land border without a Form I-94 admission record. Furthermore, due to CBP's implementation of stampless entry, the commenters said Canadian nonimmigrants are unlikely to be provided an admission stamp that references an entry date, category of admission, and end date. The commenters said that land border entries have always been treated differently due to processing time concerns for pedestrian, commercial, and passenger vehicular traffic, reasoning that, due to CBP's pragmatic approach, Canadians are accustomed to being allowed to remain in the United States for up to 6 months after driving into the United States via a land port of entry. The commenters ultimately expressed concern that the IFR does not maintain these exemptions despite Canadians' low security risk and visa exemptions in most nonimmigrant categories. The commenters said that enforcing the provisional Form I-94 process at land borders would burden limited CBP staff while yielding minimal security benefits.
A commenter similarly recommended exempting Canadian travelers who have not been issued an electronic Form I-94 from the general registration requirement, reasoning that Canadian citizens entering the United States at the ( printed page 39292) land border should be considered “registered” when they are inspected and admitted by CBP officers. The commenter further reasoned that the E.O. was intended to apply to unlawfully present individuals, not tourists temporarily visiting the United States, and noted that the registration form itself does not permit users to enter a foreign address, such as a Canadian residential address.
Response: DHS has an obligation to faithfully execute the laws established by Congress, including the alien registration requirement. See INA sec. 103(a), 8 U.S.C. 1103(a).
